### 76. `RD_ALifeLine_1cf6469169bbc1af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0026*
- **EN:** "It's how my stomach works, it thinks it's getting more"
- **TR:** 'Benim midem öyle işliyor, daha çok yiyor sanıyor'

### 77. `RD_ALifeLine_15236f775265be38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'I made a sandwich'
- **TR:** 'Sandviç yaptım'

### 78. `RD_ALifeLine_0d8a41183beb9f82` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Out of what'
- **TR:** 'Neyden'

### 79. `RD_ALifeLine_308a973f70c7313b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Bread and a feeling'
- **TR:** 'Ekmek ve biraz da his'

### 80. `RD_ALifeLine_4747761e5a94e3de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Give me half the feeling'
- **TR:** 'Hissin yarısını bana ver'

### 81. `RD_ALifeLine_000b1fa8447475bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'Know what I remember about my brother?'
- **TR:** 'Kardeşimle ilgili ne hatırlıyorum biliyor musun?'

### 82. `RD_ALifeLine_48cf09af3b873d70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0031*
- **EN:** "He'd split his sandwich without asking if I was hungry"
- **TR:** 'Aç mıyım diye sormadan sandviçini ikiye bölerdi'

### 83. `RD_ALifeLine_558fb58f348915ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'Even when he needed it?'
- **TR:** 'Kendinin de ihtiyacı varken mi?'

### 84. `RD_ALifeLine_23d70af51cb3be2c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0031*
- **EN:** "Especially then. That's what stays with me"
- **TR:** 'Hele o zaman. Aklımda kalan bu.'

### 85. `RD_ALifeLine_1a3c0ff417243eec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0032*
- **EN:** "Anybody else's mouth taste like pennies"
- **TR:** 'Başka kimsenin ağzında bozuk para tadı var mı?'

### 86. `RD_ALifeLine_6f4626033bcdf36c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'Since Saturday'
- **TR:** 'Cumartesiden beri'

### 87. `RD_ALifeLine_5164c8761c80fb86` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'I thought that was just me'
- **TR:** 'Bir tek bende sanıyordum'

### 88. `RD_ALifeLine_0edc55cd6fb0e7eb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'We got any of that Kool-Aid powder left?'
- **TR:** 'Kool-Aid tozundan kaldı mı bizde?'

### 89. `RD_ALifeLine_3facd8db0526084e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'The red'
- **TR:** 'Kırmızısı var'

### 90. `RD_ALifeLine_102252223303f13a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0033*
- **EN:** "Red's fine, I just wanna taste something"
- **TR:** 'Kırmızı da olur, tek istediğim ağzıma bir tat gelsin'

### 91. `RD_ALifeLine_3973d5e052957c4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'Put it in the canteen, make it look like blood, scare the neighbors'
- **TR:** 'Mataraya kat, kan gibi dursun, komşuların ödü patlasın'

### 92. `RD_ALifeLine_506f8c803b57a4ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0034*
- **EN:** 'You ever just stand in front of an open fridge'
- **TR:** 'Hiç açık buzdolabının önünde öylece dikildin mi'

### 93. `RD_ALifeLine_32ee0d290559136f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0034*
- **EN:** "Even though it's off, I stand there, I feel nothing"
- **TR:** 'Kapalı olduğunu bile bile duruyorum orada, hiçbir şey hissetmiyorum'

### 94. `RD_ALifeLine_79653363010c32c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0034*
- **EN:** 'Yeah, no cold, just the smell'
- **TR:** 'Evet, soğuk yok, sadece koku'

### 95. `RD_ALifeLine_7153de6c5b8854de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'Used to say, last cigarette'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden "son sigara" derdim'

### 96. `RD_ALifeLine_7cddb7891313c774` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'Every day?'
- **TR:** 'Her gün mü?'

### 97. `RD_ALifeLine_42cce77f1ba68188` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'Every day I found another excuse'
- **TR:** 'Her gün yeni bir bahane buldum'

### 98. `RD_ALifeLine_4fc5482c68445d2c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'And somebody asking for a drag'
- **TR:** 'Bir de biri bir fırt isterse?'

### 99. `RD_ALifeLine_4bbee4ba5b8796a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'Fine, but I watched their lips like they were stealing my wallet'
- **TR:** 'Veririm ama dudaklarına cüzdanımı çalıyormuş gibi bakardım'

### 100. `RD_ALifeLine_4fb7897f127c4e10` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0041*
- **EN:** "Skoal's all I got left and I'm out of cups"
- **TR:** "Elimde Skoal'dan başka bir şey kalmadı, kabım da yok"

### 101. `RD_ALifeLine_783da9c55adf5f64` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'Use a can'
- **TR:** 'Konserve kutusu kullan'

### 102. `RD_ALifeLine_7dcca9f62a44e920` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0041*
- **EN:** "The can's got beans in it"
- **TR:** 'Kutunun içinde fasulye var'

### 103. `RD_ALifeLine_57e54a557d1df697` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'Then eat the beans'
- **TR:** 'O zaman fasulyeyi ye'

### 104. `RD_ALifeLine_7b5f88f27a6310f7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'Found a cigar in the drawer'
- **TR:** 'Çekmecede bir puro buldum'

### 105. `RD_ALifeLine_5b626a1f6e9a5f19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'White Owl, probably older than you'
- **TR:** 'White Owl, muhtemelen senden yaşlı'

### 106. `RD_ALifeLine_0c1d45d33b6f0251` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'Light it, I wanna see your face'
- **TR:** 'Yak şunu, yüzünü görmek istiyorum'

### 107. `RD_ALifeLine_172e2e556a5a3d7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0045*
- **EN:** 'Four matches left'
- **TR:** 'Dört kibrit kaldı'

### 108. `RD_ALifeLine_2bd1e07b6133af21` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0045*
- **EN:** 'And one lighter that works when it feels like it'
- **TR:** 'Bir de keyfi yerindeyken yanan çakmak'

### 109. `RD_ALifeLine_0124a72f5d9b013a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0045*
- **EN:** 'So nobody waste any'
- **TR:** 'O zaman kimse israf etmesin'

### 110. `RD_ALifeLine_1397819d4454995d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0046*
- **EN:** "That's pipe tobacco, smells just like my granddaddy"
- **TR:** 'Pipo tütünü bu, tıpkı dedem gibi kokuyor'

### 111. `RD_ALifeLine_30b8d6586942d848` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'You want it?'
- **TR:** 'İster misin?'

### 112. `RD_ALifeLine_3ee8ec8122a35e46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'Just let me smell it for a minute'
- **TR:** 'Bir dakika sadece kokusunu çekeyim'

### 113. `RD_ALifeLine_53ce37971e2749be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0047*

- **TR:** 'Babam Winston içerdi'

### 114. `RD_ALifeLine_0d10812a3f6c09a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Mine did too'
- **TR:** 'Benimki de'

### 115. `RD_ALifeLine_71de537e41624bcd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Bought a pack at the gas station once just to smell it'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde sırf kokusu için benzincide bir paket aldım'

### 116. `RD_ALifeLine_02c2ed0a7412e42a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'It smelled like my dad'
- **TR:** 'Babam gibi kokuyordu'

### 117. `RD_ALifeLine_109b50295a48be4e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Yeah, it would'
- **TR:** 'Kokar tabii'

### 118. `RD_ALifeLine_77a39f2d57818f6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0048*
- **EN:** "You can't smoke in here"
- **TR:** 'Burada sigara içilmez'

### 119. `RD_ALifeLine_45b11a0a5e119971` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0048*
- **EN:** "We're in a house with no power"
- **TR:** 'Elektriği bile olmayan bir evdeyiz'

### 120. `RD_ALifeLine_4337f5d25a415523` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0048*
- **EN:** "It's a house with a baby"
- **TR:** 'Bebek olan bir ev bu'

### 121. `RD_ALifeLine_73bdd1024e54aff1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0048*
- **EN:** "Where's the baby"
- **TR:** 'Bebek nerede'

### 122. `RD_ALifeLine_1339b00a4d641a50` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0048*
- **EN:** "...there's no baby, it's the principle"
- **TR:** '...bebek yok, mesele prensip'

### 123. `RD_ALifeLine_5e4a7d0b06b1b1fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0051*
- **EN:** 'I keep reaching for the fan switch'
- **TR:** 'Sürekli vantilatör düğmesine uzanıyorum'

### 124. `RD_ALifeLine_7d583ff416de58d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0051*
- **EN:** "There's no power"
- **TR:** 'Elektrik yok'

### 125. `RD_ALifeLine_5847a14d4b6d0829` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0051*
- **EN:** "I know there's no power, my hand doesn't know"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum yok, elim bilmiyor'

### 126. `RD_ALifeLine_06a57087737866c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0051*
- **EN:** 'Mine either'
- **TR:** 'Benimki de'

### 127. `RD_ALifeLine_2f46ca547beec3be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0051*
- **EN:** "It's the little things that get you"
- **TR:** 'Asıl koyan küçük şeyler'

### 128. `RD_ALifeLine_78f3eab56f4759fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'Would you pay a hundred bucks for a bag of ice'
- **TR:** 'Bir torba buz için yüz dolar verir miydin'

### 129. `RD_ALifeLine_2063822b2cb41c6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'From the Kwik-Mart?'
- **TR:** "Kwik-Mart'tan mı?"

### 130. `RD_ALifeLine_1da0ae12696fa41d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'From anywhere'
- **TR:** 'Neresi olursa'

### 131. `RD_ALifeLine_72cbf0336565c9e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0052*
- **EN:** "Two hundred, and I'd let you keep the bag"
- **TR:** 'İki yüz veririm, torbayı da sana bırakırım'

### 132. `RD_ALifeLine_0abb77573fc3b3f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0053*
- **EN:** "There's one mosquito, ONE, and it's been in my left ear for an hour!"
- **TR:** 'Tek bir sivrisinek var, TEK, ve bir saattir sol kulağımda!'

### 133. `RD_ALifeLine_7be95eab02efe149` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0053*
- **EN:** 'Slap it'
- **TR:** 'Vur şuna'

### 134. `RD_ALifeLine_6e3b545359d8e519` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0053*
- **EN:** "I can't, it's a relationship now"

### 135. `RD_ALifeLine_55f1e84e01702cf1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0053*
- **EN:** "Slap yourself then, I'm trying to sleep"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kendine vur, ben uyumaya çalışıyorum'

### 136. `RD_ALifeLine_5508a22442291c99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0055*
- **EN:** 'Check my back, I felt something'
- **TR:** 'Sırtıma bak, bir şey hissettim'

### 137. `RD_ALifeLine_116f1ab94a674f9a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0055*
- **EN:** 'Is it a tick?'
- **TR:** 'Kene mi?'

### 138. `RD_ALifeLine_2c16ee033e53fbdc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0055*
- **EN:** "It's a tick"
- **TR:** 'Kene'

### 139. `RD_ALifeLine_797ba79139a6fb28` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0055*
- **EN:** 'GET IT OFF!'
- **TR:** 'ÇIKAR ŞUNU!'

### 140. `RD_ALifeLine_5ba9db906955e7c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0055*
- **EN:** "Stop wiggling or I'm taking skin with it"
- **TR:** 'Kıpırdama, yoksa deriyle birlikte koparırım'

### 141. `RD_ALifeLine_30f03e91571eef58` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0061*
- **EN:** 'Smell what'
- **TR:** 'Neyin kokusu'

### 142. `RD_ALifeLine_528d0c14183475f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0061*
- **EN:** "Never mind, forget it, it's nothing"

### 143. `RD_ALifeLine_25ac729c425d1319` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0061*

### 144. `RD_ALifeLine_60f17cef5a9c288a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0061*

### 145. `RD_ALifeLine_4c5c0ca65d28b08b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0062*

### 146. `RD_ALifeLine_2e065a6245f84e02` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0062*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0062*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0062*
- **EN:** 'We had powder'
- **TR:** 'Vardı'

### 149. `RD_ALifeLine_1e67a71e7262723a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0062*
- **EN:** "Check the bag, there's a can of something"

### 150. `RD_ALifeLine_3dff883d71d2117d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0063*
- **EN:** 'Can you imagine a ceiling fan right now'
- **TR:** 'Şu an bir tavan vantilatörü hayal edebiliyor musun'

### 151. `RD_ALifeLine_1861bbdb0a29e8be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0063*
- **EN:** "I'm imagining it"
- **TR:** 'Ediyorum'

### 152. `RD_ALifeLine_591eb49d46ba6355` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0063*
- **EN:** 'A little chain, you pull it, click click click'

### 153. `RD_ALifeLine_16083b453a779e93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0063*
- **EN:** "Stop, you're making it worse"

### 154. `RD_ALifeLine_37acf1ba4cf402a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0064*
- **EN:** 'You ever been this sweaty?'
- **TR:** 'Hiç bu kadar terlemiş miydin?'

### 155. `RD_ALifeLine_2dce515460254d20` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0064*
- **EN:** "Once, church picnic, Mamaw's sun tea was in a jar on the counter and I drank the whole gallon"

- *Not: 1 gallon -> kavanozdaki bütün çay*

### 156. `RD_ALifeLine_6bf9a22b7be626e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0064*

### 157. `RD_ALifeLine_626ff0461725bd9f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0064*
- **EN:** 'Nothing, I just thought of the tea'
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir alakası yok, çay aklıma geldi'

### 158. `RD_ALifeLine_012d297831be1c3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0064*

### 159. `RD_ALifeLine_3d5ea79156b42392` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0066*

### 160. `RD_ALifeLine_4b4c7d85444a98b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0066*

### 167. `RD_ALifeLine_18f7811015ab17a2` — Claude (yeni)

- **EN:** 'Tell her what?'

### 168. `RD_ALifeLine_1933caf117c32b91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0067*
- **EN:** "I really don't remember"
- **TR:** 'Hiç hatırlamıyorum, inan bana'

### 169. `RD_ALifeLine_2ed05c8205d2ef5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0068*

### 170. `RD_ALifeLine_767bea055325b262` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'What for'
- **TR:** 'Ne için'

### 171. `RD_ALifeLine_03daf08173c227b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'I dunno, to leave, I think'

### 172. `RD_ALifeLine_7f98d6f663166ffa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'Where would you go'
- **TR:** 'Nereye giderdin'

### 173. `RD_ALifeLine_555f625518e10639` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0068*
- **EN:** "That's the thing, nowhere, I just wake up reaching"
- **TR:** 'Mesele de bu, hiçbir yere. Ben sadece uzanarak uyanıyorum'

### 174. `RD_ALifeLine_4ad8a59e7d047943` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0070*

### 175. `RD_ALifeLine_4ea2890b180d252f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0070*
- **EN:** 'Over there, hush, listen'

### 176. `RD_ALifeLine_7875d18b77d18226` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0070*

### 177. `RD_ALifeLine_1bd11c224aa02767` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0070*
- **EN:** "Pipes don't cool, the water's off"

### 178. `RD_ALifeLine_4dc49063324dde37` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0070*
- **EN:** "Well then I don't know"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bilmiyorum'

### 179. `RD_ALifeLine_304d6abe3e0a41e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'I miss the sound of the refrigerator'

### 180. `RD_ALifeLine_354abf0423228c56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'The what'
- **TR:** 'Neyini'

### 181. `RD_ALifeLine_3a0bca007cbb145b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0071*
- **EN:** "The motor, you know, the hum, you never hear it till it's gone"

### 182. `RD_ALifeLine_7846a1d52b3b52aa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'Yeah, the whole house is wrong without it'

### 183. `RD_ALifeLine_658913f376f160eb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0071*

### 184. `RD_ALifeLine_350b801c405c9ef5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0072*

### 185. `RD_ALifeLine_1b78391876a1d3b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0072*
- **EN:** "It's you, you hum when you're nervous"

### 186. `RD_ALifeLine_2288f4f2203f4fcc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0072*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0073*

### 188. `RD_ALifeLine_547510655ee8b424` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0073*
- **EN:** 'Twice?'

### 189. `RD_ALifeLine_3ede301a00f139b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0073*
- **EN:** "I'm a heavy sleeper, once won't do it"
- **TR:** 'Ağır uyurum, bir kere yetmez'

### 190. `RD_ALifeLine_1bbdd3ac0eb5f44a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0073*
- **EN:** 'Twice then, and a third time if you cuss at me'

### 191. `RD_ALifeLine_5eb41f7e31d98ae3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0074*

### 192. `RD_ALifeLine_670466432d3fec7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0074*

### 193. `RD_ALifeLine_62c526e751aa1765` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0074*

### 195. `RD_ALifeLine_1a294a636d0ccb0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0075*

### 198. `RD_ALifeLine_046ac9142e4da621` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0075*

### 200. `RD_ALifeLine_56d94de82c08616c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0076*

### 201. `RD_ALifeLine_017fd24028a45bd2` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0076*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0082*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0090*

### 262. `RD_ALifeLine_24d0d29001257d59` — Claude (yeni)

- **TR:** 'O karttan hep nefret ettim'

### 263. `RD_ALifeLine_28c35719575efd63` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0090*

### 264. `RD_ALifeLine_4d68679a6e12bf35` — Claude (yeni)

### 265. `RD_ALifeLine_7c8e8bdb5da445f7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'What'
- **TR:** 'Ne'

### 266. `RD_ALifeLine_37942c677b6e7d00` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'Stick your knife in the floor between your fingers, fast as you can'
- **TR:** 'Bıçağını parmaklarının arasına, yere, olabildiğince hızlı sapla'

### 267. `RD_ALifeLine_4effd6cd74304a91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0091*
- **EN:** "I'm not doing that"
- **TR:** 'Yapmam ben onu'

### 268. `RD_ALifeLine_0e9b868f7a779e19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'Mumblety-peg, everybody did it when I was little'
- **TR:** 'Mumblety-peg, ben küçükken herkes oynardı'

### 269. `RD_ALifeLine_41c9ede813783330` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'Everybody lost a finger when you were little'
- **TR:** 'Sen küçükken herkes bir parmağını kaybetti'

### 270. `RD_ALifeLine_0a5fa247776354e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'Would you rather eat a raw possum or drink a warm Budweiser?'

### 271. `RD_ALifeLine_7c3ebfb43b65f4f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'Warm Budweiser'
- **TR:** 'Ilık Budweiser'

### 272. `RD_ALifeLine_1be4a3466286e19d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0092*
- **EN:** "That's too easy, you've done it before"
- **TR:** 'Çok kolay, daha önce yaptın'

### 273. `RD_ALifeLine_765c406602a3c75b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'I did it at a fish fry, I was fine'
- **TR:** 'Balık şenliğinde yaptım, bir şeyim olmadı'

### 274. `RD_ALifeLine_40a9eb5344a3420e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'Twice, I was there'
- **TR:** 'İki kere, ben oradaydım'

### 275. `RD_ALifeLine_6fe7bb0d245b8f8a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0093*
- **EN:** 'County fair last August, I won a goldfish'
- **TR:** 'Geçen ağustosta ilçe fuarında bir japon balığı kazandım'

### 276. `RD_ALifeLine_3ec3c22d138c75eb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0093*
- **EN:** 'Did it live?'
- **TR:** 'Yaşadı mı?'

### 277. `RD_ALifeLine_7fb3e072425871d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0093*
- **EN:** 'Three whole days'
- **TR:** 'Tam üç gün'

### 278. `RD_ALifeLine_24cdf75b4069c67c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0093*
- **EN:** "That's a good run for a fair goldfish"

### 279. `RD_ALifeLine_28c939ce6570afe6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0093*

### 281. `RD_ALifeLine_6473b3755c026bff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0094*

### 282. `RD_ALifeLine_76c01c9127901631` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'Every Saturday in high school'
- **TR:** 'Lisede her cumartesi'

### 283. `RD_ALifeLine_5698ebd67d9e4750` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'We never watched the movie'
- **TR:** 'Filmi hiç izlemedik'

### 284. `RD_ALifeLine_7f351f3f5f3bc61e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'Nobody watched the movie'
- **TR:** 'Kimse izlemedi'

### 285. `RD_ALifeLine_6a85b9d06d84cb2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0094*
- **EN:** "I watched the movie, I was the designated driver and I'm still mad about it"
- **TR:** 'Ben izledim, içmeyen şoför bendim, hala bozuluyorum buna'

### 286. `RD_ALifeLine_453af9c42cd148bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0095*

### 287. `RD_ALifeLine_69e24d274cc26826` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0095*
- **EN:** 'He could pick up a hot coal and not say a word'

### 288. `RD_ALifeLine_6895c62d0ad2961b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0095*
- **EN:** "He'd have hated all this"

### 289. `RD_ALifeLine_6b5c324d09763f5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0095*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0096*

### 291. `RD_ALifeLine_5a1873ad60d5102b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0096*

### 292. `RD_ALifeLine_2a7624524d894d70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0096*
- **EN:** 'And you loved it'
- **TR:** 'Sen de bayılırdın'

### 293. `RD_ALifeLine_2a10cde136eaf992` — Claude (yeni)

### 294. `RD_ALifeLine_26a9df6e04a5f38c` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0097*

### 318. `RD_ALifeLine_6e6a99ba258d4442` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0102*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0103*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0103*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0103*

### 336. `RD_ALifeLine_6a5b8af945ea1150` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0107*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0107*

### 338. `RD_ALifeLine_37881bbe246361be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0107*

### 339. `RD_ALifeLine_613abb82771a665b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0108*

### 340. `RD_ALifeLine_0b8832d3173027a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0108*
- **EN:** 'Who'
- **TR:** 'Kim?'

### 341. `RD_ALifeLine_7f2ce3c56c52b06b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0108*

- **TR:** 'Kızarmış tavuğun yanında dikilip sen parça alırken seni izleyen adam. Sanki ona borcun varmış gibi.'

### 342. `RD_ALifeLine_12eaa11b52481964` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0108*

### 343. `RD_ALifeLine_54a9d37e22f47847` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0109*
- **EN:** "You know what I'd do if I could go back to any one day"
- **TR:** 'Geri dönebilsem hangi günü seçerdim, biliyor musun?'

### 344. `RD_ALifeLine_500dfe4166ac1a6a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0109*

### 345. `RD_ALifeLine_25d33da07865ceda` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0109*

### 346. `RD_ALifeLine_349c99de428449ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0110*
- **EN:** 'Remember phone calls with the cord stretched all the way into the closet'
- **TR:** 'Telefon kablosunu dolabın içine kadar uzatıp konuştuğumuz günleri hatırlıyor musun?'

### 347. `RD_ALifeLine_238ebe3b573c54de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0110*
- **EN:** 'Privacy'
- **TR:** 'Mahremiyet!'

### 348. `RD_ALifeLine_3b1c76722de837b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0110*
- **EN:** 'My mother stood outside the closet door breathing'
- **TR:** 'Annem dolap kapısının önünde dikilip soluk alıp dururdu.'

### 349. `RD_ALifeLine_34803d961b48a306` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0110*
- **EN:** 'Mine too, I could hear her'
- **TR:** 'Benimki de, sesini duyabiliyordum.'

### 350. `RD_ALifeLine_571c5535626121dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0111*

### 351. `RD_ALifeLine_70d2c1ce6e09ddce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0111*

### 352. `RD_ALifeLine_588ee66262f5f361` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0111*

### 354. `RD_ALifeLine_428e474735fe01a2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0111*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0112*

### 356. `RD_ALifeLine_4b95e1ee6cb7ea6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0112*

### 357. `RD_ALifeLine_1e4e96e814b902a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0112*

### 358. `RD_ALifeLine_16b49b490b67b877` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0112*

### 359. `RD_ALifeLine_6f5af66224b63557` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0113*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0113*

- **TR:** "'89'da Kroger'ın çekilişinde hindi kazandım."

### 369. `RD_ALifeLine_797b3d332ed784e7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0115*
- **EN:** 'A frozen one, and I had to carry it home on the bus'
- **TR:** 'Dondurulmuş. Eve otobüsle taşımak zorunda kaldım.'

### 370. `RD_ALifeLine_0fdce7951f31fde4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0115*
- **EN:** "A frozen turkey on a bus, that's a very specific memory"
- **TR:** 'Otobüste dondurulmuş hindi, çok özel bir anı bu.'

### 371. `RD_ALifeLine_20ad1c726036fb5c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0115*
- **EN:** "It's my proudest day"
- **TR:** 'Hayatımın gurur günü.'

### 372. `RD_ALifeLine_41b8a4f51adeb0ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0115*
- **EN:** "Frozen turkey on a bus, that's a country song"
- **TR:** 'Otobüste dondurulmuş hindi, country şarkısı olur bu.'

### 373. `RD_ALifeLine_6fa4a11375bcd57f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0116*
- **EN:** 'Summer job at the pool, I got paid four dollars an hour to yell at kids'
- **TR:** 'Yaz işim havuzdaydı, çocuklara bağırmak için saatine dört dolar alıyordum.'

### 374. `RD_ALifeLine_679f4e2e69077f59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0116*
- **EN:** 'You were good at it'
- **TR:** 'İyiydin bu işte.'

### 375. `RD_ALifeLine_0720b66c5e3689ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0116*
- **EN:** 'I was great at it, I miss it so much'
- **TR:** 'Harikaydım hem. Öyle özledim ki.'

### 376. `RD_ALifeLine_7aa59314096b3231` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0117*
- **EN:** 'Do you remember what it sounded like before?'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden nasıl bir sesti hatırlıyor musun?'

### 377. `RD_ALifeLine_74b52e4414c2949c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0117*
- **EN:** 'Before what'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden ne?'

### 378. `RD_ALifeLine_7220181f5e474779` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0117*
- **EN:** 'This, the sound, the trucks on Dixie Highway at night'
- **TR:** "Şunu diyorum, o sesi. Geceleri Dixie Highway'den geçen kamyonların sesini."

### 379. `RD_ALifeLine_4d2ce0ff600188db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0117*
- **EN:** 'Yeah, I could fall asleep to that'
- **TR:** 'Evet, o sesle uyuyabilirdim.'

### 380. `RD_ALifeLine_771458530c30c1a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0117*
- **EN:** "I'd give a lot for a truck to go by"
- **TR:** 'Bir kamyon geçsin diye neler vermezdim.'

### 381. `RD_ALifeLine_0eaabaae1e0215ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0117*
- **EN:** 'A truck, a horn, anything with an engine'
- **TR:** 'Bir kamyon, bir korna, motoru olan herhangi bir şey.'

### 382. `RD_ALifeLine_65a17b1265cd98b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0118*
- **EN:** "My boy's baseball card collection is in the attic"
- **TR:** 'Oğlumun beyzbol kartı koleksiyonu tavan arasında.'

### 383. `RD_ALifeLine_27e0ae901fab5874` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0118*
- **EN:** "A Reds rookie card, he'd kill me if I lost it"
- **TR:** 'Bir Reds çaylak kartı var, kaybedersem beni öldürür.'

### 384. `RD_ALifeLine_767b7a5d7d93998b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0118*
- **EN:** "You didn't lose it, you left it"
- **TR:** 'Kaybetmedin ki, bıraktın.'

### 385. `RD_ALifeLine_30760e64093ca569` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0118*
- **EN:** "It's the same thing when you're ten"
- **TR:** 'On yaşındayken ikisi aynı şey.'

### 386. `RD_ALifeLine_37b6117716d2a33f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0119*
- **EN:** 'Church picnic, the three-legged race, I fell on Pastor'
- **TR:** "Kilise pikniği, üç ayaklı yarış, Papaz'ın üstüne düştüm."

### 387. `RD_ALifeLine_5caf015843aaee88` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0119*
- **EN:** 'You did not'
- **TR:** 'Yapma ya!'

### 388. `RD_ALifeLine_7651979d17d93bbd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0119*
- **EN:** 'Flat on top of him, I heard his glasses crunch'
- **TR:** 'Üstüne yayıldım, gözlüğünün çıtırtısını duydum.'

### 389. `RD_ALifeLine_58bfd23a090dab2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0119*
- **EN:** 'Did he forgive you?'
- **TR:** 'Affetti mi seni?'

### 390. `RD_ALifeLine_5e2f1c6675c266e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0119*
- **EN:** "He said he'd been wanting new glasses"
- **TR:** 'Zaten yeni gözlük almak istiyormuş, dedi.'

### 391. `RD_ALifeLine_480641722ae0731d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0119*
- **EN:** 'Pastor was a saint, he took that and a sermon in the same day'
- **TR:** 'Papaz azizin tekiydi, aynı gün hem bunu hem bir vaazı kaldırdı.'

### 392. `RD_ALifeLine_432675b569bd8e40` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0120*
- **EN:** 'Me and my brother once drove to Nashville on a dare'
- **TR:** "Kardeşimle bir keresinde Nashville'e kadar gittik, iddiaya girmiştik."

### 393. `RD_ALifeLine_402f2b296e41b910` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0120*
- **EN:** 'And it was a Tuesday, nothing was open, we ate gas station chicken and drove home'
- **TR:** 'Salı günüydü, hiçbir yer açık değildi, benzinlikte tavuk yiyip geri döndük.'

### 394. `RD_ALifeLine_665cfbf76588924a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0120*
- **EN:** "That's a good trip"
- **TR:** 'İyi gezi bu.'

### 395. `RD_ALifeLine_59d88af6307bfffb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0121*
- **EN:** "I'd give a month's pay for one more night shift at the plant, with the break room and the bad coffee"
- **TR:** 'Fabrikada bir gece vardiyası daha, mola odası, kötü kahve... Bunun için bir aylık maaşımı verirdim.'

### 396. `RD_ALifeLine_0b85a47e5a5729ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0121*
- **EN:** 'You hated that plant'
- **TR:** 'O fabrikadan nefret ediyordun.'

### 397. `RD_ALifeLine_15cae9c87ee033ff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0121*
- **EN:** "I hated every minute of it, and I'd be there right now"
- **TR:** 'Her dakikasından nefret ettim, şu an olsam orada olurdum.'

### 398. `RD_ALifeLine_22d03e875164e13f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0121*
- **EN:** "We all would, don't lie..."
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz olurduk, yalan söyleme...'

### 399. `RD_ALifeLine_6aa646b34206d80a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0122*
- **EN:** "Remember when the whole street came out for the Fourth, every house had a flag and somebody's uncle had a cannon"
- **TR:** "Hatırlıyor musun, Dört Temmuz'da bütün sokak dışarı dökülürdü, her evde bayrak, birinin amcasında da top olurdu."

### 400. `RD_ALifeLine_72d9208a1d5bd40b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0122*
- **EN:** 'It was a lawn mower with a pipe'
- **TR:** 'Boru takılmış bir çim biçme makinesiydi o.'

### 401. `RD_ALifeLine_0bbb123817baf750` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0122*
- **EN:** 'It was a very loud lawn mower with a pipe'
- **TR:** 'Çok gürültülü, borulu bir çim biçme makinesiydi.'

### 402. `RD_ALifeLine_1bd5205e290ee4d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0123*
- **EN:** "I could drive to my sister's with my eyes closed, you take the left at the Shell, the road goes by the old cemetery"
- **TR:** "Kardeşimin evine gözüm kapalı giderim. Shell'den sola dönersin, yol eski mezarlığın yanından geçer."

### 403. `RD_ALifeLine_77ee901b003b9c34` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0123*
- **EN:** 'And then'
- **TR:** 'Sonra?'

### 404. `RD_ALifeLine_6dd5c342288d77d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0123*
- **EN:** "And then it's a hill, a long one, and her house is on the left, with the green shutters"
- **TR:** 'Sonra bir yokuş, upuzun, evi de solda, yeşil panjurlu.'

### 405. `RD_ALifeLine_66bd1e160dbd5370` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0123*
- **EN:** "You're telling it like you're already there"
- **TR:** 'Sanki şu an oradaymışsın gibi anlatıyorsun.'

### 406. `RD_ALifeLine_5b830652007cfd9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0124*
- **EN:** "Tried Mama's number before the line died"
- **TR:** 'Hat kesilmeden annemin numarasını denedim.'

### 407. `RD_ALifeLine_521b9347374dc945` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0124*
- **EN:** 'Anybody pick up?'
- **TR:** 'Açan oldu mu?'

### 408. `RD_ALifeLine_377811657ea472a2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0124*
- **EN:** 'It rang, just rang, like normal, like she was out back hanging wash'
- **TR:** 'Çaldı, sadece çaldı. Her zamanki gibi, sanki arka bahçede çamaşır asıyormuş gibi.'

### 409. `RD_ALifeLine_7e23e6a36553e536` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0124*
- **EN:** "That's something"
- **TR:** 'Bu da bir şey.'

### 410. `RD_ALifeLine_2c924dca51d6c5f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0125*
- **EN:** "My brother's at Fort Knox, they'd have called, right?"
- **TR:** "Kardeşim Fort Knox'ta, arasalardı, değil mi?"

### 411. `RD_ALifeLine_6484006a51719454` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0125*
- **EN:** 'Called how'
- **TR:** 'Nasıl arasınlar?'

### 412. `RD_ALifeLine_1d4b2b1c5568aa60` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0125*
- **EN:** "I-- I don't know, somebody, a letter, anything"
- **TR:** 'Bi-- bilmem, biri, bir mektup, herhangi bir şey!'

### 413. `RD_ALifeLine_0b8c6d08730e4cc2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0125*
- **EN:** "There's no mail"
- **TR:** 'Posta yok.'

### 414. `RD_ALifeLine_68b5a63477c57b59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0125*
- **EN:** "I KNOW there's no mail!"
- **TR:** 'Posta olmadığını BİLİYORUM!'

### 415. `RD_ALifeLine_37eb8a731c8acadb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0125*
- **EN:** "Somebody'll come through, a runner, somebody"
- **TR:** 'Biri uğrar mutlaka, bir ulak, kim olursa.'

### 416. `RD_ALifeLine_034a89e30cbb1df7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0126*
- **EN:** "Last I knew, my girl was at her dad's in Lexington"
- **TR:** "Bildiğim kadarıyla kızım Lexington'da babasındaydı."

### 417. `RD_ALifeLine_6e885a22219688e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0126*
- **EN:** "That's what, a hundred miles?"
- **TR:** 'Orası ne kadar, yüz altmış kilometre mi?'
- *Not: 100 miles -> 160 km*

### 418. `RD_ALifeLine_16b8a48c1ccfefbf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0126*
- **EN:** "About a hundred and ten from the old house. Feels longer when I can't call"
- **TR:** 'Eski evden yaklaşık yüz yetmiş yedi kilometre. Arayamayınca daha uzun geliyor.'
- *Not: 110 miles -> 177 km*

### 419. `RD_ALifeLine_13f92a8b0d2982e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0126*
- **EN:** 'Does she know where we are'
- **TR:** 'Bizim nerede olduğumuzu biliyor mu?'

### 420. `RD_ALifeLine_3fe30c1b47345bd9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0126*
- **EN:** "She knew where I'd try to go. That's the best I can say"
- **TR:** 'Nereye gitmeye çalışacağımı bilir. Elimde bundan fazlası yok.'

### 421. `RD_ALifeLine_0803e2e272c81da8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0127*
- **EN:** "My sister's due in September"
- **TR:** 'Kardeşim eylülde doğuracak.'

### 422. `RD_ALifeLine_14e918f307f62096` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0127*
- **EN:** 'Louisville, apartment, third floor, no elevator'
- **TR:** "Louisville'de, apartman dairesi, üçüncü kat, asansör yok."

### 423. `RD_ALifeLine_5e6c80df528dddd3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0127*
- **EN:** 'She sounds tough'
- **TR:** 'Sağlam biriymiş.'

### 424. `RD_ALifeLine_0f78cd1940e7e04e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0127*
- **EN:** "You don't know her"
- **TR:** 'Sen tanımıyorsun onu.'

### 425. `RD_ALifeLine_259a91eb1b697b9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0127*
- **EN:** "No, I'm trying to help"
- **TR:** 'Yok, ben sadece yardım etmeye çalışıyorum.'

### 426. `RD_ALifeLine_57a51aab4f0039cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0127*
- **EN:** "It helps, don't stop"
- **TR:** 'İşe yarıyor, bırakma.'

### 427. `RD_ALifeLine_0bc875181ef4baee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0128*
- **EN:** "Dad's got three oxygen tanks and one of them was half empty"
- **TR:** 'Babamın üç oksijen tüpü var, birinin yarısı boş.'

### 428. `RD_ALifeLine_364e0329166cda18` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0128*
- **EN:** 'How long does that-'
- **TR:** 'Bu ne kadar yeter--'

### 429. `RD_ALifeLine_3b57708300ec5e67` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0128*
- **EN:** "Don't do the math, I did the math"
- **TR:** 'Hesap yapma, ben yaptım.'

### 430. `RD_ALifeLine_4ac1cbc848324e15` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0129*
- **EN:** "I filled the dog's bowl all the way before we left"
- **TR:** 'Gitmeden önce köpeğin kabını ağzına kadar doldurdum.'

### 431. `RD_ALifeLine_7b4d65221c781d1f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0129*
- **EN:** "He's a smart dog"
- **TR:** 'Akıllı köpektir o.'

### 432. `RD_ALifeLine_61d2572a751d3478` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0129*
- **EN:** "He's a stupid dog, he ate a sock last week"
- **TR:** 'Aptal köpek o, geçen hafta çorap yedi.'

### 433. `RD_ALifeLine_049527d1352163f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0129*
- **EN:** "Then he'll eat the bowl and be fine"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kabı da yer, bir şey olmaz.'

### 434. `RD_ALifeLine_1b650719652dc84d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0129*
- **EN:** "He'll find the neighbors' trash..."
- **TR:** 'Komşuların çöpünü bulur...'

### 435. `RD_ALifeLine_6a88ee9c50af2066` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0130*
- **EN:** "I didn't even say goodbye, I yelled see you at supper and went to work"
- **TR:** 'Hoşça kal bile demedim, "akşam yemeğinde görüşürüz" diye bağırıp işe gittim.'

### 436. `RD_ALifeLine_124fe5e15a0de106` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0130*
- **EN:** 'Everybody did that'
- **TR:** 'Herkes öyle yaptı.'

### 437. `RD_ALifeLine_35afb54f4afc9004` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0130*
- **EN:** "I know, but it's all I think about"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, ama aklımda sadece bu var.'

### 438. `RD_ALifeLine_03509fa13fe0275b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0131*
- **EN:** "Where would your people go if the house wasn't safe?"
- **TR:** 'Ev güvenli olmasa senin ailen nereye giderdi?'

### 439. `RD_ALifeLine_4532750a1221109b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0131*
- **EN:** "Aunt Peg's farm, past Rineyville"
- **TR:** "Peg Teyze'nin çiftliği, Rineyville'in ötesinde."

### 440. `RD_ALifeLine_579768076eb8413a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0131*
- **EN:** "That's where mine would go too. Same sort of farm"
- **TR:** 'Benimkiler de oraya giderdi. Aynı türden bir çiftlik.'

### 441. `RD_ALifeLine_510a4180623ae59c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0131*
- **EN:** 'How many aunts have farms around here?'
- **TR:** 'Buralarda kaç teyzenin çiftliği var ki?'

### 442. `RD_ALifeLine_0b933fc934fbd7f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0132*
- **EN:** "The answering machine, the last message was my mom saying call me back, it's nothing, just call me"
- **TR:** 'Telesekreter, son mesaj annemden: beni geri ara, bir şey yok, sadece ara.'

### 443. `RD_ALifeLine_47955fe27a1b0d1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0132*
- **EN:** 'I was gonna call her Sunday'
- **TR:** 'Pazar arayacaktım.'

### 444. `RD_ALifeLine_2326b8391aa5dbae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0133*
- **EN:** 'I keep thinking about my truck sitting in the lot at work'
- **TR:** 'Aklım hep iş yerinin otoparkında duran kamyonetimde.'

### 445. `RD_ALifeLine_322197036f1400e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0133*
- **EN:** 'Your truck?'
- **TR:** 'Kamyonetin mi?'

### 446. `RD_ALifeLine_217bf4b1089e0ec8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0133*
- **EN:** "It's just sitting there, getting hot, I don't know why I keep thinking about it"
- **TR:** 'Orada öylece duruyor, ısınıyor. Niye sürekli onu düşünüyorum bilmiyorum.'

### 447. `RD_ALifeLine_11aacab33e48171f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0133*
- **EN:** "It's a truck, it's fine"
- **TR:** 'Kamyonet işte, bir şeyi yoktur.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 448. `RD_ALifeLine_64cf849a00371ef4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0133*
- **EN:** "I know it's a truck"
- **TR:** 'Kamyonet olduğunu biliyorum.'

### 449. `RD_ALifeLine_7958b47903ef2c7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0134*
- **EN:** 'My mother takes blood pressure pills, twice a day'
- **TR:** 'Annem tansiyon hapı kullanıyor, günde iki kere.'

### 450. `RD_ALifeLine_2164d0b17ff95569` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0134*
- **EN:** 'How many does she have?'
- **TR:** 'Kaç tane kaldı?'

### 451. `RD_ALifeLine_0ba82e6326c7525c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0134*
- **EN:** "That's the thing, I don't know, I never counted"
- **TR:** 'Mesele de o, bilmiyorum, hiç saymadım.'

### 452. `RD_ALifeLine_7f4e180b4aba9088` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0134*
- **EN:** "She'll know to stretch them"
- **TR:** 'Uzatmayı bilir o.'

### 453. `RD_ALifeLine_372edd2272e7d58d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0134*
- **EN:** 'Mothers always know...'
- **TR:** 'Anneler hep bilir...'

### 454. `RD_ALifeLine_2455205f0b4e43c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0135*
- **EN:** 'My ex called last Wednesday, left a message, wanted to talk'
- **TR:** 'Eskim geçen çarşamba aradı, mesaj bıraktı, konuşmak istiyormuş.'

### 455. `RD_ALifeLine_308fdf6f79fb13da` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0135*
- **EN:** 'You call back?'
- **TR:** 'Geri aradın mı?'

### 456. `RD_ALifeLine_21efbdb156f5eaf2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0135*
- **EN:** "I thought I'd wait till the weekend, make him sweat"
- **TR:** 'Hafta sonuna kadar bekleyeyim, bir terlesin bakalım dedim.'

### 457. `RD_ALifeLine_73dceef93fa2d960` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0135*
- **EN:** 'Oh, no...'
- **TR:** 'Ah, hayır...'

### 458. `RD_ALifeLine_37f2c0d837dfee75` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0136*
- **EN:** 'Last I heard, both my kids were with their grandmother in Brandenburg'
- **TR:** "Bildiğim kadarıyla iki çocuğum da Brandenburg'da büyükannelerinin yanındaydı."

### 459. `RD_ALifeLine_64de287710d089da` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0136*
- **EN:** 'I remember Brandenburg being quiet'
- **TR:** 'Brandenburg sessizdi, hatırlıyorum.'

### 460. `RD_ALifeLine_0102b29064ba8b47` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0136*
- **EN:** "It was quiet, I don't know what it is now"
- **TR:** 'Sessizdi, şimdi ne durumda bilmiyorum.'

### 461. `RD_ALifeLine_77e3b32839be373e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0136*
- **EN:** "I wish somebody could bring you news. Quiet doesn't tell us enough"
- **TR:** 'Keşke biri sana haber getirebilse. Sessizlik bize pek bir şey söylemiyor.'

### 462. `RD_ALifeLine_7c7692056b5e5277` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0136*
- **EN:** 'No. But I keep listening anyway'
- **TR:** 'Hayır, söylemiyor. Ama ben yine de dinlemeye devam ediyorum.'

### 463. `RD_ALifeLine_45d391f816c0b1e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0137*
- **EN:** 'I can still smell the hospital on my hands'
- **TR:** 'Ellerimde hastane kokusunu hala alıyorum.'

### 464. `RD_ALifeLine_1dce446932566b20` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0137*
- **EN:** 'Wash them'
- **TR:** 'Yıka.'

### 465. `RD_ALifeLine_1a37e01e2b983bc3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0137*
- **EN:** 'I did, three times, with the little bar of soap'
- **TR:** 'Yıkadım, üç kere, şu küçücük sabunla.'

### 466. `RD_ALifeLine_3937c17658f023c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0137*
- **EN:** "Then it's in your head"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kafanda.'

### 467. `RD_ALifeLine_4c87378f29202f38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0138*
- **EN:** 'My cousin was getting married in two weeks'
- **TR:** 'Kuzenim iki hafta sonra evlenecekti.'

### 468. `RD_ALifeLine_66aa0ae76ebf123a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0138*
- **EN:** "I already bought the gift, it's in my trunk, a toaster, a stupid toaster"
- **TR:** 'Hediyesini aldım bile, bagajda, bir tost makinesi, salak bir tost makinesi.'

### 469. `RD_ALifeLine_15bad3d068eb3648` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0138*
- **EN:** 'You could still give it to him'
- **TR:** 'Yine de verebilirsin.'

### 470. `RD_ALifeLine_32ae58f244d5f974` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0138*
- **EN:** 'I could give it to him on the other side'
- **TR:** 'Öbür tarafta veririm artık.'

### 471. `RD_ALifeLine_095919162110cfea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0138*
- **EN:** "A toaster's a good gift"
- **TR:** 'Tost makinesi güzel hediyedir.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 472. `RD_ALifeLine_607434105c9f7e3a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0139*
- **EN:** "I think my daddy's still at the house, he won't leave it"
- **TR:** 'Babam hala evdedir herhalde, çıkmaz oradan.'

### 473. `RD_ALifeLine_53db1d2853da2190` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0139*
- **EN:** "He'd never leave"
- **TR:** 'Hiç bırakmazdı.'

### 474. `RD_ALifeLine_1f4fb2241d4300e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0139*
- **EN:** "He said they'd have to carry him out"
- **TR:** 'Beni ancak taşıyarak çıkarırlar demişti.'

### 475. `RD_ALifeLine_3b2da4f00e6891fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0139*
- **EN:** "I'd say that to a man with a shotgun, too"
- **TR:** 'Ben de pompalı tüfekli bir adama bunu derdim.'

### 476. `RD_ALifeLine_204da22754f3660f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0140*
- **EN:** "I wrote down everybody's number in the front of my Bible"
- **TR:** "Herkesin numarasını İncil'imin ilk sayfasına yazdım."

### 477. `RD_ALifeLine_4f1314f11920fbd9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0140*
- **EN:** 'When the lines come back'
- **TR:** 'Hatlar geri gelince.'

### 478. `RD_ALifeLine_3f1958c64e9fe0f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0140*
- **EN:** "When the lines come back, I'll have them, I'll start with Aunt Dot, she's the loudest"
- **TR:** "Hatlar geri gelince hepsi elimde olacak. Dot Teyze'den başlarım, o en gürültücüsü."

### 479. `RD_ALifeLine_75dfc51134bebd05` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0140*
- **EN:** "She'll pick up on the first ring"
- **TR:** 'İlk çalışta açar.'

### 480. `RD_ALifeLine_4579653d753b66d6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0141*
- **EN:** '...maybe later'
- **TR:** '...Belki sonra.'

### 481. `RD_ALifeLine_4d298e9308351df1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0142*
- **EN:** 'I never learned how to make her chicken and dumplings'
- **TR:** 'Onun tavuklu hamur köftelerini yapmayı hiç öğrenemedim.'

### 482. `RD_ALifeLine_5755339b7572b795` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0142*
- **EN:** "I always said I'd get the recipe, there was time"
- **TR:** 'Hep tarifini alırım derdim. Zaman var sanıyordum.'

### 483. `RD_ALifeLine_0994e79d65fcec38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0142*
- **EN:** 'She had a recipe card?'
- **TR:** 'Tarif kartı mı vardı?'

### 484. `RD_ALifeLine_05b280e522f19b24` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0142*
- **EN:** "It's in a tin in her kitchen, I know exactly which drawer"
- **TR:** 'Mutfağında bir teneke kutuda, hangi çekmecede olduğunu tam biliyorum.'

### 485. `RD_ALifeLine_1822b70010aa2c1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0142*
- **EN:** "Then you'll go get it"
- **TR:** 'O zaman gidip alırsın.'

### 486. `RD_ALifeLine_02ece2bc24097ed1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0143*
- **EN:** "I counted the days wrong, it's been nine, not eight"
- **TR:** 'Günleri yanlış saymışım, dokuz gün olmuş, sekiz değil.'

### 487. `RD_ALifeLine_7bdce22239a2783d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0143*
- **EN:** "It matters, I don't know why it matters, I just can't lose count of the days"
- **TR:** 'Önemi var, neden önemi var bilmiyorum, sadece günlerin hesabını kaybedemem.'

### 488. `RD_ALifeLine_5a2242586cb4bcb7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0144*
- **EN:** "My boy's birthday was Tuesday"
- **TR:** 'Oğlumun doğum günü salıydı.'

### 489. `RD_ALifeLine_3302d6b81e93d618` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0144*
- **EN:** "I made him a card out of a cereal box, I was gonna mail it, there's no mail"
- **TR:** 'Ona tahıl kutusundan bir kart yaptım, postalayacaktım, posta yok.'

### 490. `RD_ALifeLine_34cfdfc47ee8dafa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0144*
- **EN:** 'Give it to him when you see him'
- **TR:** 'Onu gördüğünde ver.'

### 491. `RD_ALifeLine_5be853fd660dfb9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0145*
- **EN:** "I got my mother's ring in my pocket, I took it off her kitchen counter"
- **TR:** 'Annemin yüzüğü cebimde, mutfak tezgahından aldım.'

### 492. `RD_ALifeLine_4c4735fe34ba5ea2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0145*
- **EN:** "Because she'd have wanted me to have it, and because I didn't want it left there"
- **TR:** 'Çünkü benim olmasını isterdi. Bir de orada kalsın istemedim.'

### 493. `RD_ALifeLine_03e7be121ea087ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0148*
- **EN:** "I'm not sleeping next to a Cardinals fan"
- **TR:** 'Bir Cardinals taraftarının yanında uyumam.'

### 494. `RD_ALifeLine_3e973f0a73d8fcce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0148*
- **EN:** "I don't even like basketball"
- **TR:** 'Ben basketbolu bile sevmem.'

### 495. `RD_ALifeLine_653804a86385b480` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0148*
- **EN:** "That's exactly what a Cardinals fan would say"
- **TR:** 'Bir Cardinals taraftarı tam bunu söylerdi.'

### 496. `RD_ALifeLine_5e4291a70b121aec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0148*
- **EN:** "Put 'em by the door"
- **TR:** 'İkisi de kapının yanında yatsın.'

### 497. `RD_ALifeLine_06a2b1d85c5bb572` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0149*
- **EN:** "Preacher said it's a judgment"
- **TR:** 'Papaz bunun bir ceza olduğunu söyledi.'

### 498. `RD_ALifeLine_4800b6b54d94e885` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0149*
- **EN:** 'On who'
- **TR:** 'Kime?'

### 499. `RD_ALifeLine_618844105d175ff0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0149*
- **EN:** 'On all of us'
- **TR:** 'Hepimize.'

### 500. `RD_ALifeLine_0863a2fd3a2bc0a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0149*
- **EN:** "Then God's got bad aim, my grandmother never hurt anybody"
- **TR:** "O zaman Allah'ın nişanı bozukmuş. Ninem hayatında kimseye zarar vermedi."

### 501. `RD_ALifeLine_24fb326a3ea9cd7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0149*
- **EN:** "Y'all want to do this now?"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi mi başlayacaksınız buna?'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 502. `RD_ALifeLine_595af7ca1d282c24` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0149*
- **EN:** "Let 'em, it's something to do"
- **TR:** 'Bırak yapsınlar, bir uğraş işte.'

### 503. `RD_ALifeLine_5bf404f544842d12` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0153*
- **EN:** 'You burnt it'
- **TR:** 'Yakmışsın.'

### 504. `RD_ALifeLine_36873cb1534a4b97` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0153*
- **EN:** "It's crispy"
- **TR:** 'Çıtır olmuş.'

### 505. `RD_ALifeLine_1c7e2e6478ff8684` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0153*
- **EN:** "It's goddamn charcoal"
- **TR:** 'Kahrolası kömür bu!'

### 506. `RD_ALifeLine_2313ee036e8e2025` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0153*
- **EN:** "Then you don't have to chew"
- **TR:** 'O zaman çiğnemene gerek kalmaz.'

### 507. `RD_ALifeLine_77d4368d4ec5f4d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0153*
- **EN:** "It's not even food, what was it"
- **TR:** 'Yemeğe benzemiyor bile, neydi bu?'

### 508. `RD_ALifeLine_706e76dc190a7045` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0154*
- **EN:** 'We should go'
- **TR:** 'Gitmeliyiz.'

### 509. `RD_ALifeLine_203a84bb2e8489c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0154*
- **EN:** "We're not going anywhere"
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir yere gitmiyoruz.'

### 510. `RD_ALifeLine_24fbf589627f2b35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0154*
- **EN:** "We've been sitting here for three days!"
- **TR:** 'Üç gündür burada oturuyoruz!'

### 511. `RD_ALifeLine_6a573a2e074e52b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0154*
- **EN:** "And we're alive, and I'd like to stay that way"
- **TR:** 'Ama hayattayız, böyle kalmak da istiyorum.'

### 512. `RD_ALifeLine_6a6914666182fbb0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0154*
- **EN:** "Everybody pipe down, you'll bring the whole street"
- **TR:** 'Hepiniz kesin sesinizi, bütün sokağı başımıza toplayacaksınız!'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 513. `RD_ALifeLine_1bc9770a27d7938a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0156*
- **EN:** "A man's dead and you're laughing"
- **TR:** 'Adam ölmüş, sen gülüyorsun.'

### 514. `RD_ALifeLine_6ffc13f3480e9581` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0156*
- **EN:** "It's the hat I'm laughing at"
- **TR:** 'Güldüğüm şapkaya.'

### 515. `RD_ALifeLine_45fa08780c8b4072` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0156*
- **EN:** 'What hat?'
- **TR:** 'Ne şapkası?'

### 516. `RD_ALifeLine_7aec36423a970d2d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0156*
- **EN:** "He had a hat, a little feather in it, he'd wear it to the bank"
- **TR:** 'Adamın bir şapkası vardı, üstünde küçük bir tüy, bankaya onunla giderdi.'

### 517. `RD_ALifeLine_5e97e73d5c618e9a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0157*
- **EN:** "Don't touch the radio"
- **TR:** 'Radyoya dokunma.'

### 518. `RD_ALifeLine_2be755f611a7bb84` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0157*
- **EN:** "I'm just checking the dial"
- **TR:** 'Sadece kadrana bakıyorum.'

### 519. `RD_ALifeLine_47eb68c52db0b3ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0157*
- **EN:** 'Every time you check the dial it eats batteries'
- **TR:** 'Kadranı her kurcalayışında pil bitiyor.'

### 520. `RD_ALifeLine_4ab0fe95162d6eba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0157*
- **EN:** "It's a radio, it's supposed to eat batteries"
- **TR:** 'Radyo bu, pil yemesi lazım.'

### 521. `RD_ALifeLine_4eeddfcd4f905675` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0158*
- **EN:** 'My mother always said, wash your hands'
- **TR:** 'Annem hep derdi: Ellerini yıka.'

### 522. `RD_ALifeLine_2c954b283d0690ad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0158*
- **EN:** "Even when there wasn't much water?"
- **TR:** 'Su azken bile mi?'

### 523. `RD_ALifeLine_11cc791a42acc385` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0158*
- **EN:** "Especially then. She'd get upset if we'd forgotten to save any"
- **TR:** 'Özellikle o zaman. Yıkanmak için su ayırmayı unutmuşsak çok kızardı.'

### 524. `RD_ALifeLine_51d0c3b0626208cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0158*
- **EN:** 'Hard choosing between that and drinking'
- **TR:** 'Bununla içmek arasında seçim yapmak zor.'

### 525. `RD_ALifeLine_4157a76d45a2c99c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0158*
- **EN:** 'I know. Being told off was easier than having to make the choice'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum. Azar yemek, o seçimi yapmaktan kolaydı.'

### 526. `RD_ALifeLine_2d63047730ca73ae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'I told you to check the back'
- **TR:** 'Sana arkaya bak demiştim.'

### 527. `RD_ALifeLine_57e06f2f0b54fb86` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'I checked the back'
- **TR:** 'Arkaya baktım.'

### 528. `RD_ALifeLine_1e9324032f1f1f03` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'It was unlocked'
- **TR:** 'Kilitli değildi.'

### 529. `RD_ALifeLine_58ca4f8b515a1321` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'I thought it was locked'
- **TR:** 'Kilitli sandım.'

### 530. `RD_ALifeLine_3073dd044ec34e78` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'You THOUGHT?!'
- **TR:** 'SANDIN MI?!'

### 531. `RD_ALifeLine_140cc7fb1973322f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'Lord, here we go'
- **TR:** "Allah'ım, yine başlıyoruz."

### 532. `RD_ALifeLine_6e6e386b4b67d58f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0160*
- **EN:** 'Every time you say when this is over'
- **TR:** 'Her "bu bitince" dediğinde...'

### 533. `RD_ALifeLine_047cd50f024905d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0160*
- **EN:** 'Stop saying it'
- **TR:** 'Bunu demeyi bırak.'

### 534. `RD_ALifeLine_58dc4e697af50261` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0160*
- **EN:** "I'm just trying to-"
- **TR:** 'Ben sadece--'

### 535. `RD_ALifeLine_21df2f6142041e78` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0160*
- **EN:** 'Let it go, both of you'
- **TR:** 'Bırakın artık, ikiniz de.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 536. `RD_ALifeLine_72d6940735887179` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0161*
- **EN:** 'Can we pray?'
- **TR:** 'Dua edebilir miyiz?'

### 537. `RD_ALifeLine_3f22f55d5f93bf6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0161*
- **EN:** 'Pray quietly'
- **TR:** 'Sessiz et.'

### 538. `RD_ALifeLine_00f2b8946b9d3808` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0161*
- **EN:** 'I always pray quietly'
- **TR:** 'Ben hep sessiz ederim.'

### 539. `RD_ALifeLine_6020378646efeafc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0161*
- **EN:** "You pray like you're calling hogs"
- **TR:** 'Sen domuz çağırır gibi dua ediyorsun.'

### 540. `RD_ALifeLine_1371195e0778a6bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0161*
- **EN:** 'Amen to that'
- **TR:** 'Buna amin.'

### 541. `RD_ALifeLine_4ac6858024af0125` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0162*
- **EN:** "That jacket's mine"
- **TR:** 'O ceket benim.'

### 542. `RD_ALifeLine_4fac08195045d304` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0162*
- **EN:** 'It was on the floor'
- **TR:** 'Yerdeydi.'

### 543. `RD_ALifeLine_5e9ee0b852f3f2db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0162*
- **EN:** "It was on the floor because I put it on the floor, that's where I keep it"
- **TR:** 'Yerdeydi çünkü ben yere koydum, hep oraya koyarım.'

### 544. `RD_ALifeLine_5d3e25a03e0c57a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0162*
- **EN:** 'I was cold'
- **TR:** 'Üşüyordum.'

### 545. `RD_ALifeLine_0c505429238f53b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0162*
- **EN:** "It's ninety degrees!"
- **TR:** 'Otuz iki derece var!'
- *Not: 90 °F -> 32 °C*

### 546. `RD_ALifeLine_06c5649431693c35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0162*
- **EN:** 'At night it was cold'
- **TR:** 'Geceleri soğuktu.'

### 547. `RD_ALifeLine_0fd6b79b6f59c285` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0165*
- **EN:** "My boss called, said I'm late"
- **TR:** 'Patronum aradı, geciktim diyor.'

### 548. `RD_ALifeLine_0d25919f35a28d46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0165*
- **EN:** 'Did you tell him'
- **TR:** 'Ona söyledin mi?'

### 549. `RD_ALifeLine_2c3a327c04c16dc7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0165*
- **EN:** "I told him I'd be in Monday, to the house, with a shotgun"
- **TR:** 'Pazartesi geleceğimi söyledim. Evine. Pompalıyla.'

### 550. `RD_ALifeLine_65a6c531299c99c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0165*
- **EN:** "I did not, I said sorry, I'll be in Monday"
- **TR:** 'Yapmadım, özür dilerim, pazartesi gelirim dedim.'

### 551. `RD_ALifeLine_2c77e24154bba3bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0166*
- **EN:** 'Would anybody like to hear the weather?'
- **TR:** 'Hava durumunu duymak isteyen var mı?'

### 552. `RD_ALifeLine_3ccc18f67b5fddea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0166*
- **EN:** 'Hot, with a chance of hot'
- **TR:** 'Sıcak, sıcak yağış ihtimali var.'

### 553. `RD_ALifeLine_14711aad5879eeda` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0166*
- **EN:** "That's the best forecast I've heard all week"
- **TR:** 'Bu hafta duyduğum en iyi tahmin.'

### 554. `RD_ALifeLine_1c6e5e5f63ff622a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0167*
- **EN:** "No telemarketers, that's one good thing"
- **TR:** 'Telefonla pazarlamacı yok, bir iyi yanı bu.'

### 555. `RD_ALifeLine_441059e73c8cfe93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0167*
- **EN:** 'Two, no jury duty, I already said that'
- **TR:** 'İki, jüri görevi de yok, bunu zaten söylemiştim.'

### 556. `RD_ALifeLine_7753b3b328c90c56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0167*
- **EN:** 'Three, nobody asks how my day was'
- **TR:** 'Üç, kimse günün nasıl geçti diye sormuyor.'

### 557. `RD_ALifeLine_1a4e42bd0479219c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0168*
- **EN:** "I'm writing a letter to the editor"
- **TR:** 'Editöre mektup yazıyorum.'

### 558. `RD_ALifeLine_68ade9fb2f12c16f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0168*
- **EN:** 'They shut the newspaper down'
- **TR:** 'Gazeteyi kapatmışlar.'

### 559. `RD_ALifeLine_578ae4d528830be2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0168*
- **EN:** "Then it's a letter to nobody, so I can say whatever I want about the Army"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kimseye yazılmış bir mektup oluyor, Ordu hakkında ne istersem söylerim.'

### 560. `RD_ALifeLine_63129cef78b100e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0168*
- **EN:** 'Read it to me'
- **TR:** 'Oku bana.'

### 561. `RD_ALifeLine_6c03cc2023fb6713` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0169*
- **EN:** "My cousin's in the Guard and he couldn't find his own tent"
- **TR:** "Kuzenim Ulusal Muhafız'da, kendi çadırını bile bulamamış."

### 562. `RD_ALifeLine_43fdbd2d1e717649` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0169*
- **EN:** "That's comforting"
- **TR:** 'Çok rahatlattın.'

### 563. `RD_ALifeLine_025b765f5048091a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0169*
- **EN:** "I mean they're supposed to come get us"
- **TR:** 'Yani bizi almaya gelmeleri lazım ya.'

### 564. `RD_ALifeLine_3140e4c16938ae01` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0169*
- **EN:** "Don't pin it on your cousin"
- **TR:** 'Bunu kuzenine yıkma.'

### 565. `RD_ALifeLine_5b1869bf2b2c2037` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0170*
- **EN:** "We've never been this clean and this filthy at the same time"
- **TR:** 'Hem hiç bu kadar temiz hem bu kadar pis olmamıştık.'

### 566. `RD_ALifeLine_0ae5464c3baa1cf3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0170*
- **EN:** 'What does that mean'
- **TR:** 'Ne demek bu?'

### 567. `RD_ALifeLine_4d7bb2866568b282` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0170*
- **EN:** 'I washed my face and my feet and I still feel dirty'
- **TR:** 'Yüzümü de ayaklarımı da yıkadım, hala pis hissediyorum.'

### 568. `RD_ALifeLine_731f413553862d27` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0170*
- **EN:** "Wet isn't clean"
- **TR:** 'Islak olmak temiz olmak değil.'

### 569. `RD_ALifeLine_6f19cb1272a8aac1` — Claude (yeni)
