*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0171*
- **EN:** "Hey, if I turn into one of them, don't let me eat the last can"
- **TR:** 'Hey, onlardan birine dönüşürsem son konserveyi yememe izin verme.'

### 570. `RD_ALifeLine_4ef69b6105e33148` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0171*
- **EN:** "I'll save you a spoon"
- **TR:** 'Sana bir kaşık ayırırım.'

### 571. `RD_ALifeLine_6033a77d46ac94e7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0172*
- **EN:** 'I miss Monday'
- **TR:** 'Pazartesiyi özledim.'

### 572. `RD_ALifeLine_5baf7c265d8a1df5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0172*
- **EN:** 'You hate Monday'
- **TR:** 'Sen pazartesiden nefret edersin.'

### 573. `RD_ALifeLine_6c2483a0028dc8c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0172*
- **EN:** "I miss hating Monday, I'd hate it so good right now"
- **TR:** 'Pazartesiden nefret etmeyi özledim, şu an öyle güzel nefret ederdim ki.'

### 574. `RD_ALifeLine_2f6d5d4719484de1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0172*
- **EN:** "I'd go to the dentist, I'd get a root canal"
- **TR:** 'Ben dişçiye giderdim, kanal tedavisi olurdum.'

### 575. `RD_ALifeLine_213c1f1e5e7a29ae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0173*
- **EN:** 'You two sound like an old married couple'
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de yaşlı karı koca gibi konuşuyorsunuz.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 576. `RD_ALifeLine_5668d90a27ab4e0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0173*
- **EN:** "It's right here, it's all out loud"
- **TR:** 'Dibimizde oluyor bu, hepsi sesli sesli.'

### 577. `RD_ALifeLine_7a32370e7ea0d89b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0174*
- **EN:** "Swear to God that's what he said"
- **TR:** 'Yemin ederim, adam böyle dedi.'

### 578. `RD_ALifeLine_5c26ea761f55b037` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0174*
- **EN:** 'The guy at the hardware store'
- **TR:** 'Nalburdaki adam.'

### 579. `RD_ALifeLine_6031b2f20e466ae3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0174*
- **EN:** "Y'all been on that for twenty minutes"
- **TR:** 'Yirmi dakikadır aynı konudasınız.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 580. `RD_ALifeLine_44e6a61176042bf0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0174*
- **EN:** "It's a story, it takes a while"
- **TR:** 'Hikaye bu, zaman alır.'

### 581. `RD_ALifeLine_406d7189322712b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0175*
- **EN:** "You're lying"
- **TR:** 'Yalan söylüyorsun.'

### 582. `RD_ALifeLine_59dda0057147015d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0175*
- **EN:** "I'm not"
- **TR:** 'Söylemiyorum.'

### 583. `RD_ALifeLine_18f4319604865bec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0175*
- **EN:** "Your eyebrow's doing that thing again"
- **TR:** 'Kaşın yine aynı şeyi yapıyor.'

### 584. `RD_ALifeLine_06e9ee150bb0fbf8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0176*
- **EN:** 'It was three or four of them, maybe five'
- **TR:** 'Üç dört tane vardı, belki beş.'

### 585. `RD_ALifeLine_44f25a1f47ce3415` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0176*
- **EN:** 'It was two'
- **TR:** 'İki tane vardı.'

### 586. `RD_ALifeLine_6c28ea6c2cd1f81e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0176*
- **EN:** 'It FELT like five!'
- **TR:** 'Bana BEŞ gibi geldi!'

### 587. `RD_ALifeLine_5a71c83a6b61e4d7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0176*
- **EN:** 'It felt like a lot, every time somebody tells it'
- **TR:** 'Anlatan kim olursa olsun çok gibi geliyor.'

### 588. `RD_ALifeLine_490627172eb4d88c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0177*
- **EN:** 'Just kiss and make up already'
- **TR:** 'Hadi barışın artık.'

### 589. `RD_ALifeLine_07e0251954e58613` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0177*
- **EN:** "We're not fighting"
- **TR:** 'Kavga etmiyoruz.'

### 590. `RD_ALifeLine_01ce36b915eacf90` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0177*
- **EN:** "You've been quiet for an hour, that's a fight"
- **TR:** 'Bir saattir ağzını açmadın, bu kavgadır.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 591. `RD_ALifeLine_54d6d9160a9d5c23` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0177*
- **EN:** "That's a nap"
- **TR:** 'Ona şekerleme derler.'

### 592. `RD_ALifeLine_6e9ecd962bd5bc9d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0178*
- **EN:** "I'm telling you, it's the best fish I ever caught"
- **TR:** 'Diyorum sana, hayatımda tuttuğum en iyi balık.'

### 593. `RD_ALifeLine_358b91b8294f46f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0178*
- **EN:** 'How big'
- **TR:** 'Ne kadar büyük?'

### 594. `RD_ALifeLine_7e66310817a99c4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0178*
- **EN:** 'This big, a good two feet'
- **TR:** 'Bu kadar, iyi bir altmış santim.'
- *Not: 2 feet -> 60 cm*

### 595. `RD_ALifeLine_141983827d084936` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0178*
- **EN:** "That's three inches, I was there"
- **TR:** 'O yedi santim ancak, ben oradaydım.'
- *Not: 3 inches -> 7,5 cm*

### 596. `RD_ALifeLine_6663f48c12b52297` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0179*
- **EN:** "Keep it down, you're gonna wake the dead"
- **TR:** 'Sesini kıs, ölüleri bile uyandıracaksın.'

### 597. `RD_ALifeLine_4a2860a51d30ae11` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0179*
- **EN:** 'Fine, a little'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, biraz komik.'

### 598. `RD_ALifeLine_01103d2f1368fa84` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'You remember the song?'
- **TR:** 'O şarkıyı hatırlıyor musun?'

### 599. `RD_ALifeLine_0b13dfc2649596b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'Which one'
- **TR:** 'Hangisi?'

### 600. `RD_ALifeLine_34df6bea2c378080` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'The one on the radio, the day, you know'
- **TR:** 'Radyoda çalan, o gün, bilirsin.'

### 601. `RD_ALifeLine_216ab9a9175549c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0180*
- **EN:** "I remember, but I'm not singing it"
- **TR:** 'Hatırlıyorum ama söylemeyeceğim.'

### 602. `RD_ALifeLine_39f8881819ebb747` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'Nobody asked you to sing'
- **TR:** 'Kimse senden söylemeni istemedi.'

### 603. `RD_ALifeLine_24d94cd636c4674d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'You were thinking it'
- **TR:** 'İçinden geçiriyordun ama.'

### 604. `RD_ALifeLine_233a66a06fb54953` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0181*
- **EN:** 'Who cut the cards'
- **TR:** 'Desteyi kim kesti?'

### 605. `RD_ALifeLine_52057210061c5323` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0181*
- **EN:** 'You did'
- **TR:** 'Sen.'

### 606. `RD_ALifeLine_469506ef6ef0bb9d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0181*
- **EN:** 'I did not'
- **TR:** 'Ben değilim.'

### 607. `RD_ALifeLine_5ccc3a7c20f6c2a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0181*
- **EN:** "You both did, there's four cards on the floor"
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de kestiniz, yerde dört kart duruyor.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 608. `RD_ALifeLine_6ac8cf6c2cae1cf2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0181*
- **EN:** "I'm just here for the beans"
- **TR:** 'Ben sadece fasulye için geldim.'

### 609. `RD_ALifeLine_7ef3a6bc65f59613` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0182*
- **EN:** 'I think I saw a deer'
- **TR:** 'Sanırım bir geyik gördüm.'

### 610. `RD_ALifeLine_613a7112564fbba3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0182*
- **EN:** 'A deer?'
- **TR:** 'Geyik mi?'

### 611. `RD_ALifeLine_07c2cad8191fb8cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0182*
- **EN:** 'Over by the old fence, plain as day'
- **TR:** 'Eski çitin yanında, apaçık gördüm.'

### 612. `RD_ALifeLine_4969b98a24b48e26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0182*
- **EN:** 'It was a dog'
- **TR:** 'Köpekti.'

### 613. `RD_ALifeLine_2abfac08347121c2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0182*
- **EN:** 'It was big, it was a deer'
- **TR:** 'Büyüktü, geyikti.'

### 614. `RD_ALifeLine_4b89a66660a57367` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0182*
- **EN:** 'It was a big dog'
- **TR:** 'Büyük bir köpekti.'

### 615. `RD_ALifeLine_6abe8a6609ec50d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0183*
- **EN:** "And then he said, I'll take the whole case"
- **TR:** 'Sonra dedi ki, bütün kolisini alırım.'

### 616. `RD_ALifeLine_30bbea6641cd41a4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0183*
- **EN:** 'The whole case of what'
- **TR:** 'Neyin kolisi?'

### 617. `RD_ALifeLine_0c3237585d90f2e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0183*
- **EN:** 'I never found out, he left'
- **TR:** 'Hiç öğrenemedim, gitti.'

### 618. `RD_ALifeLine_5efaf0fb76daef1c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0183*
- **EN:** 'You tell that story like it has an ending'
- **TR:** 'Hikayeyi sanki sonu varmış gibi anlatıyorsun.'

### 619. `RD_ALifeLine_288bb0c838659bbd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0185*
- **EN:** "I told her, I said, you can't park there"
- **TR:** 'Ona dedim ki, burada park edemezsin.'

### 620. `RD_ALifeLine_3c3462844b4893a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0185*
- **EN:** "What'd she say"
- **TR:** 'Ne dedi?'

### 621. `RD_ALifeLine_4c6254e07d153fce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0185*
- **EN:** 'She said watch me'
- **TR:** 'Bak, görürsün" dedi.'

### 622. `RD_ALifeLine_034e5865570c0d10` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0185*
- **EN:** "And that's why she's not here anymore"
- **TR:** 'İşte o yüzden artık yok aramızda.'

### 623. `RD_ALifeLine_567665d208d001b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0186*
- **EN:** "Go on, deny it. You're smiling"
- **TR:** 'Hadi inkar et bakalım. Gülümsüyorsun.'

### 624. `RD_ALifeLine_524d9dbe5e3da4a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0186*
- **EN:** "You're a little bit smiling"
- **TR:** 'Azıcık gülüyorsun.'

### 625. `RD_ALifeLine_288ce740327fd546` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0186*
- **EN:** "It's gas, don't flatter yourself"
- **TR:** 'Gaz o, havalara girme.'

### 626. `RD_ALifeLine_5de367067675f1ab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0187*
- **EN:** 'Somebody get me a beer'
- **TR:** 'Biri bana bira getirsin.'

### 627. `RD_ALifeLine_3e6b416c563e30f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0187*
- **EN:** "We don't have beer"
- **TR:** 'Bira yok.'

### 628. `RD_ALifeLine_50435bc145c93fe1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0187*
- **EN:** 'Then get me something that used to be near beer'
- **TR:** 'O zaman eskiden alkolsüz biraya benzeyen bir şey getir.'

### 629. `RD_ALifeLine_3fde9a544febc183` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0187*
- **EN:** "There's a jar of pickled eggs in the pantry"
- **TR:** 'Kilerde bir kavanoz turşu yumurta var.'

### 630. `RD_ALifeLine_21fb78f85d6b5be6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0188*
- **EN:** "Somebody's laughing, over there"
- **TR:** 'Biri gülüyor, şu tarafta.'

### 631. `RD_ALifeLine_3c10aeef478e85d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0188*
- **EN:** 'About time, honestly'
- **TR:** 'Hadi be, zamanı gelmişti valla.'

### 632. `RD_ALifeLine_3d0ef9b8163a8254` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0001*
- **EN:** 'Nothing out here'
- **TR:** 'Dışarıda hiçbir şey yok.'

### 633. `RD_ALifeLine_35f0e1194ad1d1b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0001*
- **EN:** "Nothing's good"
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şey iyidir.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 634. `RD_ALifeLine_7994c34d13c02a94` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0001*
- **EN:** 'Same nothing as an hour ago'
- **TR:** 'Bir saat öncesiyle aynı hiçbir şey.'

### 635. `RD_ALifeLine_1cc20fe135a39d96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0001*
- **EN:** "Then it's holding"
- **TR:** 'Demek ki dayanıyor.'

### 636. `RD_ALifeLine_1066642f00773f98` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0002*
- **EN:** "Dog's barking again, way off"
- **TR:** 'Köpek yine havlıyor, çok uzakta.'

### 637. `RD_ALifeLine_10dbad8930b8a789` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0002*
- **EN:** "Far, I can barely hear it, it's not coming this way"
- **TR:** 'Uzakta, zor duyuyorum, bu tarafa gelmiyor.'

### 638. `RD_ALifeLine_04b8e10c3ea0c0b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0002*
- **EN:** 'Tell me when it is'
- **TR:** 'Gelince söyle.'

### 639. `RD_ALifeLine_6386d35e2814b23f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0002*
- **EN:** "I'll tell you, I'm not sleeping anyway..."
- **TR:** 'Söylerim, zaten uyumuyorum...'

### 640. `RD_ALifeLine_3b44e9411a386d37` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0003*
- **EN:** 'Counted forty-one crows so far'
- **TR:** 'Şimdiye kadar kırk bir karga saydım.'

### 641. `RD_ALifeLine_2250f3162f732447` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0003*
- **EN:** 'Why are you counting crows'
- **TR:** 'Niye karga sayıyorsun?'

### 642. `RD_ALifeLine_75cf19a35f1159d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0003*
- **EN:** "It's that or fall asleep standing up"
- **TR:** 'Ya bu ya ayakta uyuyakalacağım.'

### 643. `RD_ALifeLine_382229244d67b26c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0003*
- **EN:** 'Forty-two, one just landed'
- **TR:** 'Kırk iki, biri az önce kondu.'

### 644. `RD_ALifeLine_038a28574fd06777` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0003*
- **EN:** "That one's mine, I named it"
- **TR:** 'O benimki, adını da ben koydum.'

### 645. `RD_ALifeLine_20fb6b55344cf9b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0004*
- **EN:** "What's the worst trade you ever offered?"
- **TR:** 'Şimdiye kadar teklif ettiğin en kötü takas neydi?'

### 646. `RD_ALifeLine_34af5efd3bcc879d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0004*
- **EN:** 'Trade for what?'
- **TR:** 'Neyin takası?'

### 647. `RD_ALifeLine_3b7cd8bf2f50f11f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0004*
- **EN:** 'Offered a chair and my last cigarette to get out of a shift'
- **TR:** 'Bir vardiyadan kurtulmak için sandalye ve son sigaramı teklif ettim.'

### 648. `RD_ALifeLine_2470c18b77c01586` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0004*
- **EN:** 'What kind of chair?'
- **TR:** 'Nasıl bir sandalye?'

### 649. `RD_ALifeLine_06272d411942a6cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0004*
- **EN:** 'Three legs. But it did come with a cigarette'
- **TR:** 'Üç ayaklı. Ama yanında sigara da vardı.'

### 650. `RD_ALifeLine_79d0429c20e5bad9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0004*
- **EN:** "I'd have told you to keep it. You'd have looked miserable enough already"
- **TR:** 'Ben sana tut onu derdim. Zaten yeterince sefil görünüyordun.'

### 651. `RD_ALifeLine_706d4a822b3d564b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0005*
- **EN:** 'Shh, hold on'
- **TR:** 'Şşt, bir dakika.'

### 652. `RD_ALifeLine_40d438990e61d131` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0005*
- **EN:** 'Listen'
- **TR:** 'Dinle.'

### 653. `RD_ALifeLine_694dbf4a051f4608` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0005*
- **EN:** "I don't hear any--"
- **TR:** 'Ben hiçbir şey duy--'

### 654. `RD_ALifeLine_144ed0de438a7a9d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0005*
- **EN:** '...nothing, never mind, my ears are doing it again'
- **TR:** '...Yok bir şey, boşver, kulaklarım yine bana oyun oynuyor.'

### 655. `RD_ALifeLine_6906725916b15bf3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0005*
- **EN:** "Mine too, it's that ringing"
- **TR:** 'Benimkiler de, o çınlama.'

### 656. `RD_ALifeLine_18336b601fe9612e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0006*
- **EN:** "Two hours, who's up after me"
- **TR:** 'İki saat, benden sonra kim?'

### 657. `RD_ALifeLine_239b60742b80d036` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0006*
- **EN:** 'You said that yesterday and then you slept through it'
- **TR:** 'Dün de öyle demiştin, sonra uyuyup kaldın.'

### 658. `RD_ALifeLine_5c6b360a10766612` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0006*
- **EN:** "I'll take it for real"
- **TR:** 'Bu sefer gerçekten alırım.'

### 659. `RD_ALifeLine_5297e1f97bd6269b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0006*
- **EN:** "I'll take it, nobody's waking that one up"
- **TR:** 'Ben alırım, şunu kimse uyandıramaz.'

### 660. `RD_ALifeLine_5a5095e914b36bc0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0006*
- **EN:** "I'll take whatever's left"
- **TR:** 'Ben, geriye ne kalırsa onu alırım.'

### 661. `RD_ALifeLine_774ed63316a96a09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0007*
- **EN:** 'Quiet as a church out here'
- **TR:** 'Burası kilise gibi sessiz'

### 662. `RD_ALifeLine_028864bc3e763601` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0007*
- **EN:** "That's what bothers me"
- **TR:** 'Beni rahatsız eden de o zaten'

### 663. `RD_ALifeLine_70d2fa6f67f54bb4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0007*
- **EN:** 'Church bothers you?'
- **TR:** 'Kilise mi rahatsız ediyor seni?'

### 664. `RD_ALifeLine_0c1ff73b50da75fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0007*
- **EN:** 'Church on Sunday, quiet like that, and then somebody coughs'
- **TR:** 'Pazar günü kilise, öyle sessiz... sonra biri öksürüyor'

### 665. `RD_ALifeLine_4f14a41066ea8ab6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0007*
- **EN:** 'Somebody always coughs'
- **TR:** 'Biri hep öksürür'

### 666. `RD_ALifeLine_0ad0309628d2fa5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0008*
- **EN:** 'Water over here, please'
- **TR:** 'Şuraya su ver, lütfen'

### 667. `RD_ALifeLine_155a86c049c15578` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0008*
- **EN:** "You've got a canteen"
- **TR:** 'Matara var ya sende'

### 668. `RD_ALifeLine_1b4fe3fc370fb4b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0008*
- **EN:** "It's empty and I'm up here dying"
- **TR:** 'Boş, ben de burada ölüyorum'

### 669. `RD_ALifeLine_181a2a1d01c66375` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0008*
- **EN:** "Dying, right, you've been up there ten minutes"
- **TR:** 'Ölüyorsun tabii, daha on dakikadır oradasın'

### 670. `RD_ALifeLine_2d08d42a56a963f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0008*
- **EN:** "Bring the water up, you're gonna fall over"
- **TR:** 'Suyu yukarı çıkar, şimdi düşeceksin'

### 671. `RD_ALifeLine_4c139bf84ba12a6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0009*
- **EN:** 'Movement, left side'
- **TR:** 'Hareket var, sol tarafta'

### 672. `RD_ALifeLine_13aafcae2213ddb9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0009*
- **EN:** "It's a cat, never mind, it's just a cat"
- **TR:** 'Kedi çıktı, boş ver, sadece kedi'

### 673. `RD_ALifeLine_148fb76f3c00c7f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0009*
- **EN:** "It's always a cat"
- **TR:** 'Hep kedi çıkar'

### 674. `RD_ALifeLine_4a0305b13ee0a90f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0009*
- **EN:** "Until it's not a cat"
- **TR:** 'Kedi olmadığı güne kadar'

### 675. `RD_ALifeLine_1ce3c3cd7e1f482e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0009*
- **EN:** "Hush, let 'em watch"
- **TR:** 'Şşt, bırak baksınlar'

### 676. `RD_ALifeLine_6b52e3a17141d30a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0010*
- **EN:** 'My eyes are doing that thing where everything moves'
- **TR:** 'Gözüm öyle bir şey yapıyor ki, her şey hareket ediyor'

### 677. `RD_ALifeLine_5656c3ce61eb13a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0010*
- **EN:** "That's tired"
- **TR:** 'O yorgunluk'

### 678. `RD_ALifeLine_5e4e37a25441afb1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0010*
- **EN:** "It's moving a lot"
- **TR:** 'Çok hareket ediyor ama'

### 679. `RD_ALifeLine_2ec8d28c452532ae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0010*
- **EN:** "Sit down, I'll take it"
- **TR:** 'Otur, ben bakarım'

### 680. `RD_ALifeLine_3e12cb855beb36b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0010*
- **EN:** 'Sit down before you fall down'
- **TR:** 'Düşmeden otur'

### 681. `RD_ALifeLine_3a813a52710cd05a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0011*
- **EN:** "I think the neighbor's light is on"
- **TR:** 'Komşunun ışığı yanıyor galiba'

### 682. `RD_ALifeLine_51dcf4ea131aa9a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0011*
- **EN:** 'Which neighbor'
- **TR:** 'Hangi komşu'

### 683. `RD_ALifeLine_1708a267158f6b7b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0011*
- **EN:** "The one across, second window, it wasn't on before"
- **TR:** 'Karşıdaki, ikinci pencere. Az önce yanmıyordu'

### 684. `RD_ALifeLine_3589b43e1a2ad096` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0011*
- **EN:** 'Is it flickering?'
- **TR:** 'Titriyor mu?'

### 685. `RD_ALifeLine_19909fa17f30747e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0011*
- **EN:** "No, steady, it's just on"
- **TR:** 'Yok, sabit, sadece yanıyor'

### 686. `RD_ALifeLine_29166a593c7bc0f7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0011*
- **EN:** "That's a person"
- **TR:** 'O bir insan'

### 687. `RD_ALifeLine_66d522bb1c6fcadc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0012*
- **EN:** "Somebody's chimney's going, you can see the line"
- **TR:** 'Birinin bacası tütüyor, çizgiyi görebiliyorsun'

### 688. `RD_ALifeLine_0e60432d6b137ed2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0012*
- **EN:** 'Whose'
- **TR:** 'Kimin'

### 689. `RD_ALifeLine_2e6a4f807539fde0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0012*
- **EN:** "I don't know, a few houses down"
- **TR:** 'Bilmem, birkaç ev ileride'

### 690. `RD_ALifeLine_525b001815a7064a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0012*
- **EN:** "Then we're not the only ones"
- **TR:** 'Demek bir tek biz değiliz'

### 691. `RD_ALifeLine_2ea906e90f897116` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0012*
- **EN:** "No, we're not"
- **TR:** 'Evet, değiliz'

### 692. `RD_ALifeLine_7eccf8c1461ecccd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0012*
- **EN:** "Don't wave"
- **TR:** 'El sallama'

### 693. `RD_ALifeLine_594eaf57597fad68` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0013*
- **EN:** 'You okay up there'
- **TR:** 'İyi misin orada'

### 694. `RD_ALifeLine_4a56fbab31da23e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0013*
- **EN:** "You've been quiet"
- **TR:** 'Sessizsin'

### 695. `RD_ALifeLine_728a23e5487c67e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0013*
- **EN:** "I'm watching"
- **TR:** 'Gözlüyorum'

### 696. `RD_ALifeLine_252a30c704dad9f0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0013*
- **EN:** "You're not watching, you're thinking"
- **TR:** 'Gözlemiyorsun, düşünüyorsun'

### 697. `RD_ALifeLine_476bf5fe75aaf47f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0013*
- **EN:** '...both'
- **TR:** '...İkisi de'

### 698. `RD_ALifeLine_7d9a4fe528e68292` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0013*
- **EN:** "Leave the post a minute, I've got it"
- **TR:** 'Nöbeti bir dakika bırak, ben bakıyorum'

### 699. `RD_ALifeLine_76467d4616393c7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0014*
- **EN:** 'Can I ask a stupid question'
- **TR:** 'Aptalca bir soru sorabilir miyim'

### 700. `RD_ALifeLine_641b0e5e06757662` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0014*
- **EN:** 'If one of them comes up to the door, I mean really close, do I wake everybody or just you'
- **TR:** 'Biri kapıya kadar gelirse, yani iyice yaklaşırsa, herkesi mi uyandırayım, sadece seni mi'

### 701. `RD_ALifeLine_5edd2f497dc60858` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0014*
- **EN:** 'Just me, then me and the baseball bat will see'
- **TR:** 'Sadece beni. Sonra ben ve beyzbol sopası bir bakarız'

### 702. `RD_ALifeLine_797d9b73093b8b9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0014*
- **EN:** 'Wake me too, I like a show'
- **TR:** 'Beni de uyandır, gösteriyi severim'

### 703. `RD_ALifeLine_480565020597d681` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0015*
- **EN:** 'Half hour left on my shift'
- **TR:** 'Nöbetimin bitmesine yarım saat var'

### 704. `RD_ALifeLine_5260a59b0f2fec10` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0015*
- **EN:** "It's been a half hour for an hour"
- **TR:** 'Bir saattir yarım saat kalıyor'

### 705. `RD_ALifeLine_13aa392353f8b66b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0015*
- **EN:** "It's been twenty minutes, I've been counting"
- **TR:** 'Yirmi dakika oldu, sayıyorum'

### 706. `RD_ALifeLine_3fafb52621dd7113` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0015*
- **EN:** "Twenty minutes, and we've all heard about it"
- **TR:** 'Yirmi dakika, hepimiz de duyduk'

### 707. `RD_ALifeLine_226e331217de1f6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0015*
- **EN:** "Somebody give 'em a watch"
- **TR:** 'Biri şuna bir saat versin'

### 708. `RD_ALifeLine_2655935868c5f766` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0016*
- **EN:** "Sun's coming up, kinda orange"
- **TR:** 'Güneş doğuyor, biraz turuncu'

### 709. `RD_ALifeLine_2bc58b6f545b3068` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0016*
- **EN:** 'Is it going to be hot'
- **TR:** 'Sıcak mı olacak'

### 710. `RD_ALifeLine_2cf41eb07ac8ba04` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0016*
- **EN:** "It's already hot, it was hot at midnight"
- **TR:** 'Şimdiden sıcak, gece yarısı da sıcaktı'

### 711. `RD_ALifeLine_6b6554a42f7b8248` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0016*
- **EN:** "It's summer, hot's the weather"
- **TR:** 'Yaz bu, hava dediğin sıcak olur'

### 712. `RD_ALifeLine_3c0945a549bed6e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0017*
- **EN:** "You didn't eat yesterday either"
- **TR:** 'Dün de bir şey yemedin'

### 713. `RD_ALifeLine_4f7fe95e364419e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0017*
- **EN:** 'Leave the can next to them'
- **TR:** 'Konserveyi yanlarına bırak'

### 714. `RD_ALifeLine_0c54a8871f729b77` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0018*
- **EN:** "You've said maybe ten words today"
- **TR:** 'Bugün en fazla on kelime ettin'

### 715. `RD_ALifeLine_4661900c63ce5e36` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0018*
- **EN:** 'Smartass'
- **TR:** 'Çok bilmişsin'

### 716. `RD_ALifeLine_6903c23c31c8deaa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0018*
- **EN:** "That's the most you've said since Friday"
- **TR:** "Cuma'dan beri en çok konuştuğun an bu"

### 717. `RD_ALifeLine_69dc9b5c08d58bdd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0019*
- **EN:** 'Where do you keep going?'
- **TR:** 'Hep nereye gidiyorsun?'

### 718. `RD_ALifeLine_039087746f29a74f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0019*
- **EN:** 'Nowhere'
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir yere'

### 719. `RD_ALifeLine_1b178ea76608a5b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0019*
- **EN:** 'You get up and walk to the end of the room and sit back down'
- **TR:** 'Kalkıp odanın ucuna yürüyorsun, sonra geri oturuyorsun'

### 720. `RD_ALifeLine_2fb2a35e48f48520` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0019*
- **EN:** "I'm stretching"
- **TR:** 'Geriniyorum'

### 721. `RD_ALifeLine_4c4da65417e570c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0019*
- **EN:** 'For an hour?'
- **TR:** 'Bir saattir mi?'

### 722. `RD_ALifeLine_77ab3a9b7f4b7281` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0019*
- **EN:** 'Let it be'
- **TR:** 'Bırak ya'

### 723. `RD_ALifeLine_6a0a92d22ea7613c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0020*
- **EN:** 'You mind if I sit here?'
- **TR:** 'Buraya oturmamda sakınca var mı?'

### 724. `RD_ALifeLine_23d16a992cf2f517` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0020*
- **EN:** "I won't talk"
- **TR:** 'Konuşmam'

### 725. `RD_ALifeLine_7bb55c3912e7d905` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0020*
- **EN:** '...thanks'
- **TR:** '...Sağ ol'

### 726. `RD_ALifeLine_39f4f5165476effd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0020*
- **EN:** "That's the nicest conversation I've heard in days"
- **TR:** 'Günlerdir duyduğum en güzel konuşma bu'

### 727. `RD_ALifeLine_2f872c4c3ef27ac7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** 'How are your hands?'
- **TR:** 'Ellerin nasıl?'

### 728. `RD_ALifeLine_0d1a39034604aad1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** 'Fine...'
- **TR:** 'İyi...'

### 729. `RD_ALifeLine_62787ee751f84be0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** "They're shaking"
- **TR:** 'Titriyorlar'

### 730. `RD_ALifeLine_5f353f8a00df45ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** "It's the coffee"
- **TR:** 'Kahveden'

### 731. `RD_ALifeLine_094bd2e97a30c41f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** "We don't have coffee"
- **TR:** 'Kahvemiz yok ki'

### 732. `RD_ALifeLine_62ddbae42ba515f3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** "Then it's something else"
- **TR:** 'O zaman başka bir şeyden'

### 733. `RD_ALifeLine_5b824375439b0430` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0021*
- **EN:** "Give 'em a minute"
- **TR:** 'Bir dakika ver ona'

### 734. `RD_ALifeLine_13accb5f3f2143e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0022*
- **EN:** "It's not nothing, you've been staring at that wall all day"
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şey değil, bütün gün o duvara bakıp durdun'

### 735. `RD_ALifeLine_555b0d2e1355783a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0022*
- **EN:** "Leave 'em, they'll talk when they talk"
- **TR:** 'Bırak, konuşacaksa konuşur'

### 736. `RD_ALifeLine_10930ed73be62992` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0023*
- **EN:** 'You said you were gonna tell me what happened'
- **TR:** 'Ne olduğunu anlatacaktın bana'

### 737. `RD_ALifeLine_7eabde3317a665b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0023*
- **EN:** "Later's been three days"
- **TR:** 'Sonra dediğin üç gün oldu'

### 738. `RD_ALifeLine_52ee6c45540c1693` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0023*
- **EN:** "Then it's still later"
- **TR:** 'Demek ki hala sonra'

### 739. `RD_ALifeLine_60e6130e489918e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0023*
- **EN:** "Let it go, you're gonna scratch it raw"
- **TR:** 'Bırak artık, kanatacaksın'

### 740. `RD_ALifeLine_2aa1ac8a00df7d96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0024*
- **EN:** 'Somebody once gave me a button'
- **TR:** 'Biri bana bir düğme vermişti'

### 741. `RD_ALifeLine_464f863c6657cc6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0024*
- **EN:** 'Found it on the floor. Said it was a nice button'
- **TR:** 'Yerde bulmuş, güzel bir düğme demişti'

### 742. `RD_ALifeLine_4edd3b8b3df655f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0024*
- **EN:** 'It was to them. They wanted me to have something'
- **TR:** 'Onlara öyleydi. Bende bir şey olsun istemişlerdi'

### 743. `RD_ALifeLine_4c9a1ecc358b442b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0024*
- **EN:** "That's the sweetest thing I've heard all week"
- **TR:** 'Bu hafta duyduğum en tatlı şey bu'

### 744. `RD_ALifeLine_2d0a9ca540b1ad5c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0025*
- **EN:** "Don't talk to 'em, they're thinking"
- **TR:** 'Onunla konuşma, düşünüyor'

### 745. `RD_ALifeLine_68dadcde019ba620` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0025*
- **EN:** 'About what'
- **TR:** 'Neyi'

### 746. `RD_ALifeLine_4f141c2375eb92ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0025*
- **EN:** "I don't know, that's why I'm not talking"
- **TR:** 'Bilmiyorum, o yüzden konuşmuyorum'

### 747. `RD_ALifeLine_4c4f4cf35f453010` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0025*
- **EN:** "That's rude"
- **TR:** 'Bu kabalık'

### 748. `RD_ALifeLine_379a9eb7722d2b36` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0025*
- **EN:** 'Polite is leaving thinking people alone'
- **TR:** 'Kibarlık, düşünen insanı rahat bırakmaktır'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 749. `RD_ALifeLine_035c17125da2c25c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0026*
- **EN:** 'You gonna sit in that corner all day?'
- **TR:** 'Bütün gün o köşede mi oturacaksın?'

### 750. `RD_ALifeLine_6fb7394f5c725403` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0026*
- **EN:** 'Probably...'
- **TR:** 'Muhtemelen...'

### 751. `RD_ALifeLine_0fbb459616179e5c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0026*
- **EN:** 'Why that corner'
- **TR:** 'Neden o köşe'

### 752. `RD_ALifeLine_1c4fffd92b90f6b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0026*
- **EN:** "It's got two walls, and nobody comes at me from behind"
- **TR:** 'İki duvarı var, arkamdan kimse gelemez'

### 753. `RD_ALifeLine_1ca1caba02c8433e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0027*
- **EN:** "I'm not even here"
- **TR:** 'Ben burada bile değilim'

### 754. `RD_ALifeLine_14aaf9fd53e4ba9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0027*
- **EN:** "You're right there"
- **TR:** 'Tam şuradasın'

### 755. `RD_ALifeLine_3c1858125e94bcd5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0027*
- **EN:** "I'm not here, I'm reading"
- **TR:** 'Burada değilim, okuyorum'

### 756. `RD_ALifeLine_34cb615b16fd8daa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0027*
- **EN:** "You've been on that page for an hour"
- **TR:** 'Bir saattir aynı sayfadasın'

### 757. `RD_ALifeLine_0a8ad9cb554ab617` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0027*
- **EN:** "It's a good page"
- **TR:** 'İyi bir sayfa'

### 758. `RD_ALifeLine_011f216a34bc83b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0027*
- **EN:** "What's it say"
- **TR:** 'Ne yazıyor'

### 759. `RD_ALifeLine_195c6ea110b4aa61` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0028*
- **EN:** 'I used to fight my brother for the corner seat'
- **TR:** 'Köşedeki koltuk için kardeşimle kavga ederdim'

### 760. `RD_ALifeLine_52711a5969a1c0cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0028*
- **EN:** 'Whole room to sit in?'
- **TR:** 'Oturacak koca oda varken?'

### 761. `RD_ALifeLine_2490712631070503` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0028*
- **EN:** 'Had the best draft'
- **TR:** 'En iyi cereyan oradaydı'

### 762. `RD_ALifeLine_37123a5b4061216d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0028*
- **EN:** "We'd argue about a draft neither of us could feel"
- **TR:** 'İkimizin de hissedemediği bir cereyan için tartışırdık'

### 763. `RD_ALifeLine_588cf72906bf1e8f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0028*
- **EN:** 'Then Mama would tell us to share. Plenty of space, two fools'
- **TR:** 'Sonra annem paylaşın derdi. Yer bol, iki ahmak'

### 764. `RD_ALifeLine_617d85e20122ce09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0029*
- **EN:** 'Here, hold this'
- **TR:** 'Al, tut şunu'

### 765. `RD_ALifeLine_0555af677f233d6f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0029*
- **EN:** 'What is it'
- **TR:** 'Ne bu'

### 766. `RD_ALifeLine_459f006e1c825812` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0029*
- **EN:** "Cold coffee, from yesterday, it's all I've got"
- **TR:** 'Soğuk kahve, dünden kalma. Elimdeki tek şey bu'

### 767. `RD_ALifeLine_7d039fd24f832f98` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0029*
- **EN:** "Give 'em a blanket too"
- **TR:** 'Ona bir de battaniye ver'

### 768. `RD_ALifeLine_5b0ab2e05514ff08` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0030*
- **EN:** 'I killed something today'
- **TR:** 'Bugün bir şey öldürdüm'

### 769. `RD_ALifeLine_03f994372c53495b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0030*
- **EN:** 'It was already dead'
- **TR:** 'Zaten ölüydü'

### 770. `RD_ALifeLine_6222da554850a613` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0030*
- **EN:** 'It had a face'
- **TR:** 'Yüzü vardı'

### 771. `RD_ALifeLine_750491d368276d07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0030*
- **EN:** 'I know it did...'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum vardı...'

### 772. `RD_ALifeLine_03e5fa82448dd935` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0031*
- **EN:** "I know you're fine, come here anyway..."
- **TR:** 'İyi olduğunu biliyorum, yine de gel...'

### 773. `RD_ALifeLine_68d93d790135066d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0031*
- **EN:** 'About time'
- **TR:** 'Hadi be, nihayet'

### 774. `RD_ALifeLine_48bfda190fc08fec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0032*
- **EN:** "I-- I can't stop thinking about the sound"
- **TR:** 'Ben-- o sesi aklımdan çıkaramıyorum'

### 775. `RD_ALifeLine_0609dd172e396acf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0032*
- **EN:** 'What sound'
- **TR:** 'Ne sesi'

### 776. `RD_ALifeLine_13e8a9f21b6c4ac3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0032*
- **EN:** 'When the door closed, the sound the lock made'
- **TR:** 'Kapı kapanırken, kilidin çıkardığı ses'

### 777. `RD_ALifeLine_20c07fc404eb2afe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0032*
- **EN:** 'You did what you had to'
- **TR:** 'Yapman gerekeni yaptın'

### 778. `RD_ALifeLine_4db1120e63f24aac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0032*
- **EN:** "Don't say that, I hate that"
- **TR:** 'Deme bunu, nefret ediyorum'

### 779. `RD_ALifeLine_2bf187147fc96ef2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0032*
- **EN:** "It's okay, you're allowed"
- **TR:** 'Sorun değil, hakkın var'

### 780. `RD_ALifeLine_4479d8276e6d2bef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0033*
- **EN:** 'You want me to sit with you?'
- **TR:** 'Yanında oturayım mı?'

### 781. `RD_ALifeLine_213bf85a1a912d06` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0033*
- **EN:** "No, yeah, I don't know"
- **TR:** 'Yok, evet, bilmiyorum'

### 782. `RD_ALifeLine_4a8804c8638a62c2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0033*
- **EN:** "I'll sit, you don't have to say anything"
- **TR:** 'Oturayım, bir şey söylemek zorunda değilsin'

### 783. `RD_ALifeLine_45b0661a35d6b0e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0033*
- **EN:** 'Okay...'
- **TR:** 'Tamam...'

### 784. `RD_ALifeLine_30ff12b5172a2cd7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0034*
- **EN:** "Hold my hand, I'm dizzy"
- **TR:** 'Elimi tut, başım dönüyor'

### 785. `RD_ALifeLine_0b8821f5172f1c0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0034*
- **EN:** 'Sit'
- **TR:** 'Otur'

### 786. `RD_ALifeLine_6c024f3764a970ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0034*
- **EN:** "I'll throw up"
- **TR:** 'Kusacağım'

### 787. `RD_ALifeLine_0414d5be649e854a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0034*
- **EN:** "Then throw up, I'll hold your hair"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kus, saçını tutarım'

### 788. `RD_ALifeLine_700edca906d2050e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0035*
- **EN:** 'You cried in your sleep'
- **TR:** 'Uykunda ağladın'

### 789. `RD_ALifeLine_34a3592318667ed5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0035*
- **EN:** 'I did?'
- **TR:** 'Öyle mi?'

### 790. `RD_ALifeLine_320810ec2650d715` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0035*
- **EN:** "A little, I didn't say anything, I just put the blanket back"
- **TR:** 'Biraz. Bir şey demedim, battaniyeyi geri örttüm'

### 791. `RD_ALifeLine_29fe603543e84ac2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0035*
- **EN:** 'I thought that was you'
- **TR:** 'Ben de sen sandım'

### 792. `RD_ALifeLine_00b955865d98e373` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0035*
- **EN:** "I heard too, I'm sorry, I didn't want to wake anybody"
- **TR:** 'Ben de duydum, kusura bakma, kimseyi uyandırmak istemedim'

### 793. `RD_ALifeLine_0d57abc71e22aa68` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0036*
- **EN:** "I'm sorry about earlier"
- **TR:** 'Az önceki için üzgünüm'

### 794. `RD_ALifeLine_2ce232de2f3d6673` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0036*
- **EN:** 'No, I was out of line'
- **TR:** 'Yok, haddimi aştım'

### 795. `RD_ALifeLine_7dc2a8d42288735e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0036*
- **EN:** "We're all out of line"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz aşıyoruz'

### 796. `RD_ALifeLine_0022b45566c6472a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0036*
- **EN:** "I'm still sorry"
- **TR:** 'Yine de üzgünüm'

### 797. `RD_ALifeLine_118c92a21c1a7480` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0036*
- **EN:** "Take the apology, it's rare..."
- **TR:** 'Özrü kabul et, nadir bulunur...'

### 798. `RD_ALifeLine_60693708732f8a01` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0037*
- **EN:** 'It was a dumb thing to be mad about, the peaches'
- **TR:** 'Şeftali yüzünden kızmak aptalcaydı'

### 799. `RD_ALifeLine_2755849c692bcde0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0037*
- **EN:** "It wasn't about the peaches"
- **TR:** 'Mesele şeftali değildi'

### 800. `RD_ALifeLine_6e0c24fa161b96ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0037*
- **EN:** 'I know...'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum...'

### 801. `RD_ALifeLine_711800da40ef2dab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0038*
- **EN:** 'I keep checking the lock'
- **TR:** 'Kilidi kontrol edip duruyorum'

### 802. `RD_ALifeLine_4b4bf3097577ec02` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0038*
- **EN:** "It's locked"
- **TR:** 'Kilitli'

### 803. `RD_ALifeLine_0ab1d0f97b23d29e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0038*
- **EN:** "I know, I know it's locked, I just- one more time"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, kilitli olduğunu biliyorum, sadece-- bir kez daha'

### 804. `RD_ALifeLine_085b52350faf40d9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0038*
- **EN:** 'Go ahead, check it'
- **TR:** 'Hadi, kontrol et'

### 805. `RD_ALifeLine_0cd5af7620f25f79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0038*
- **EN:** "I'll check it with you"
- **TR:** 'Seninle ben de bakarım'

### 806. `RD_ALifeLine_4bf3d2536bc95997` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0039*
- **EN:** 'Somebody said something nice about me today'
- **TR:** 'Bugün biri hakkımda güzel bir şey söyledi'

### 807. `RD_ALifeLine_4c7878a233ba1a6f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0039*
- **EN:** 'You said I made a decent fire'
- **TR:** 'Düzgün bir ateş yaktığımı söyledin'

### 808. `RD_ALifeLine_0a4262725afdc39d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0039*
- **EN:** 'It was a decent fire'
- **TR:** 'Düzgün bir ateşti'

### 809. `RD_ALifeLine_0ae92918039ccf90` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0039*
- **EN:** 'It was a very decent fire'
- **TR:** 'Hem de çok düzgün bir ateşti'

### 810. `RD_ALifeLine_77cb812c32e4c6e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0040*
- **EN:** "If I don't make it"
- **TR:** 'Ben yaşayamazsam'

### 811. `RD_ALifeLine_3dd250c64a300f2e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0040*
- **EN:** "You'll make it"
- **TR:** 'Yaşarsın'

### 812. `RD_ALifeLine_010c48f340ecba03` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0040*
- **EN:** "If I don't, tell my brother the- never mind, he knows"
- **TR:** 'Yaşamazsam kardeşime söyle ki-- boş ver, o biliyor'

### 813. `RD_ALifeLine_73c131da3725e22e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0040*
- **EN:** 'Tell him yourself'
- **TR:** 'Kendin söylersin'

### 814. `RD_ALifeLine_4b1d18413f09368f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0041*

### 815. `RD_ALifeLine_08ca261f78688890` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0041*

### 816. `RD_ALifeLine_3887362177e820c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0041*
- **EN:** "It's a very small softball"
- **TR:** 'Çok küçük bir softbol topu'

### 817. `RD_ALifeLine_645184581f05d7f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0041*
- **EN:** 'Let me wrap it'
- **TR:** 'Sarayım'

### 818. `RD_ALifeLine_13a8fd806e083874` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0041*
- **EN:** "Wrap it, but don't make it a thing"
- **TR:** 'Sar ama olay çıkarma'

### 819. `RD_ALifeLine_2617ba245ede2822` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0041*
- **EN:** "I'll hold the foot"
- **TR:** 'Ayağı ben tutarım'

### 820. `RD_ALifeLine_7abddc526f02ada5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0042*
- **EN:** 'I keep remembering not knowing what to say to the kid'
- **TR:** 'Çocuğa ne diyeceğimi bilememek aklımdan çıkmıyor'

### 821. `RD_ALifeLine_5e98ec466b81590d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0042*
- **EN:** 'She was asleep then'
- **TR:** 'O zaman uyuyordu'

### 822. `RD_ALifeLine_5fdb7bf77bc140d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0042*
- **EN:** "I knew she'd wake up and ask"

### 823. `RD_ALifeLine_47156a2b286bdadc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0042*
- **EN:** "I told you to say you didn't know"
- **TR:** 'Sana bilmediğini söylemeni söylemiştim'

### 824. `RD_ALifeLine_76b1b82964d4d09d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0042*
- **EN:** 'It felt like the worst thing I could say'
- **TR:** 'Söyleyebileceğim en kötü şey gibi geldi'

### 825. `RD_ALifeLine_115f911c16de7c6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0043*

- **TR:** 'Buzdolabında birinin market listesi duruyor'

### 826. `RD_ALifeLine_2aa9d21462b5c7aa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0043*
- **EN:** "Don't read it"
- **TR:** 'Okuma'

### 827. `RD_ALifeLine_5ac9eefa1e7a3739` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0043*

### 828. `RD_ALifeLine_0f52ddeb77a7d546` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0043*
- **EN:** 'They had a cat'

### 829. `RD_ALifeLine_4f2356bb38637ea0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0043*

### 830. `RD_ALifeLine_6403e5df73f63660` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0044*

### 831. `RD_ALifeLine_73bf25bd4e9cf7fa` — Claude (yeni)

### 832. `RD_ALifeLine_334980470691b836` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0044*

### 838. `RD_ALifeLine_79b3ef4a3ab3da61` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0046*

### 842. `RD_ALifeLine_19a86f692e0e81c4` — Claude (yeni)

### 843. `RD_ALifeLine_505b5ea843b4245d` — Claude (yeni)

### 844. `RD_ALifeLine_0be9d08f08161020` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0047*

### 845. `RD_ALifeLine_0afd490c7050a33a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0047*

### 847. `RD_ALifeLine_076dbcdd3b78f191` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0047*

### 849. `RD_ALifeLine_210c50117236d842` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0048*

### 850. `RD_ALifeLine_65ec56654b91477e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0048*

### 851. `RD_ALifeLine_13ede7753289c126` — Claude (yeni)

### 852. `RD_ALifeLine_623403e153d3de70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0048*
- **EN:** 'What about Kevin'
- **TR:** "Kevin'e ne olmuş"

### 853. `RD_ALifeLine_51f06a682bb32e6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0049*
- **EN:** 'Whoever lived here had nine cats, I can tell by the smell'

### 854. `RD_ALifeLine_3f95056d655ee72b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0049*
- **EN:** 'I can tell by the cats'

### 855. `RD_ALifeLine_6a17eb285204a029` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0049*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0050*

### 859. `RD_ALifeLine_5ba9464b4fcb9864` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0050*

### 860. `RD_ALifeLine_2fe18d2b3491f24b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0050*
- **EN:** 'Leave it open'

### 861. `RD_ALifeLine_3eea748b353a4715` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0050*
- **EN:** "I'll leave it, I'm not touching that"

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0051*

### 863. `RD_ALifeLine_4f9817f075e916a1` — Claude (yeni)

### 864. `RD_ALifeLine_2469ec1227d4f7f6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0051*
- **EN:** 'I tried them.. they still worked'

### 865. `RD_ALifeLine_575d3d8e65bb32a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0051*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0051*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0052*

### 869. `RD_ALifeLine_08e975f41fdf59b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0052*

### 871. `RD_ALifeLine_65face4c0d8ee255` — Claude (yeni)

### 873. `RD_ALifeLine_2ce36bbd0aff1c59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0053*

### 874. `RD_ALifeLine_17071c586f624782` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0053*

### 877. `RD_ALifeLine_60f9aaea099b23d0` — Claude (yeni)

- **EN:** "Somebody's gotta"
- **TR:** 'Birinin yapması lazım'

### 878. `RD_ALifeLine_7c3f5adf63e2bc06` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0054*
- **EN:** 'Nobody goes out to pee alone'
- **TR:** 'Kimse tek başına çişe çıkmıyor'

### 879. `RD_ALifeLine_2261d09e7cc8b0e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0054*
- **EN:** 'Take somebody to pee?'
- **TR:** 'Çişe birini mi götüreceğiz?'

### 880. `RD_ALifeLine_69e3dfe13b75d455` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0054*
- **EN:** "That's the dumbest rule I ever heard"
- **TR:** 'Duyduğum en aptalca kural'

### 881. `RD_ALifeLine_6ec50b216cbda4b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0054*
- **EN:** "Then you'll be wanting me to hold your hand"
- **TR:** 'O zaman elini de tutayım istersin'

### 882. `RD_ALifeLine_17932b817529ad0a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0054*
- **EN:** "I'll go with you, I need to go anyway..."
- **TR:** 'Ben seninle gelirim, zaten gitmem lazım...'

### 883. `RD_ALifeLine_767b488e282dee0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0055*
- **EN:** 'Boots by the door, laces open, in case we have to go'
- **TR:** 'Çizmeler kapının yanında, bağcıklar açık, gitmemiz gerekirse diye'

### 884. `RD_ALifeLine_633179b9218a36f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0055*
- **EN:** "What if we don't have to go"
- **TR:** 'Ya gitmemiz gerekmezse'

### 885. `RD_ALifeLine_75d519930c4b3eac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0055*
- **EN:** "Then you've got your boots by the door, relax"
- **TR:** 'O zaman çizmelerin kapının yanında, rahat ol'

### 886. `RD_ALifeLine_78f4465079d38b42` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0055*
- **EN:** "Mine are on my feet, I'm not taking chances"
- **TR:** 'Benimkiler ayağımda, şansa bırakmam'

### 887. `RD_ALifeLine_7721b5b53723a7f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0056*
- **EN:** "I'm sleeping by the door"
- **TR:** 'Kapının yanında yatıyorum'

### 888. `RD_ALifeLine_1c58a037546ca01f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0056*
- **EN:** "You're not, you snore"
- **TR:** 'Yatamazsın, horluyorsun'

### 889. `RD_ALifeLine_64c90a1e0b7b365f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0056*
- **EN:** "That's why, if something comes in, it goes right past me and gets you"
- **TR:** 'Ondan işte. İçeri bir şey girerse yanımdan geçip seni bulur'

### 890. `RD_ALifeLine_1ef995505fc1d413` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0056*
- **EN:** "You're a real friend"
- **TR:** 'Ne iyi arkadaşsın'

### 891. `RD_ALifeLine_0057c6c94e546dc5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0056*
- **EN:** "Put me by the door, I don't snore"
- **TR:** 'Beni kapının yanına yatır, ben horlamam'

### 892. `RD_ALifeLine_7bc57d6c2ec63b66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0057*
- **EN:** 'Wake me if anything happens'
- **TR:** 'Bir şey olursa uyandır beni'

### 893. `RD_ALifeLine_0fb439974db3d0c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0057*
- **EN:** 'Like what'
- **TR:** 'Ne gibi'

### 894. `RD_ALifeLine_26d0cfc63b59c88a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0057*
- **EN:** 'If the sun comes up should I wake you'
- **TR:** 'Güneş doğarsa uyandırayım mı'

### 895. `RD_ALifeLine_5b0a0bfd0169e338` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0057*
- **EN:** "Only if there's coffee"
- **TR:** 'Sadece kahve varsa'

### 896. `RD_ALifeLine_0aaa1d3f502f1a6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0058*
- **EN:** 'No fire tonight'
- **TR:** 'Bu gece ateş yok'

### 897. `RD_ALifeLine_1c6596262281125b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0058*
- **EN:** "It's ninety degrees, why would we"
- **TR:** 'Hava otuz iki derece, niye yakalım'
- *Not: 90°F -> 32 °C*

### 898. `RD_ALifeLine_72c17a60507f289f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0058*
- **EN:** 'Somebody always wants one'
- **TR:** 'Birileri hep ister'

### 899. `RD_ALifeLine_4e3d5f4968d112af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0058*
- **EN:** 'Nobody wants one'
- **TR:** 'Kimse istemez'

### 900. `RD_ALifeLine_1d1e21250bd0f19b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0058*
- **EN:** "Somebody's gonna light one, I can feel it"
- **TR:** 'Biri mutlaka yakacak, hissediyorum'

### 901. `RD_ALifeLine_7cbd5f66726cdc34` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0059*
- **EN:** 'Put the water where we can find it in the dark'
- **TR:** 'Suyu karanlıkta bulabileceğimiz bir yere koy'

### 902. `RD_ALifeLine_2df2729143620f59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0059*
- **EN:** "Where's that"
- **TR:** 'Nereye'

### 903. `RD_ALifeLine_7412c0e34a2f99ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0059*
- **EN:** 'Right here, next to my head'
- **TR:** 'Tam şuraya, başımın yanına'

### 904. `RD_ALifeLine_5a1cad3477d835b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0059*
- **EN:** "It's next to your head so you can drink it all"
- **TR:** 'Başının yanında olsun ki hepsini içesin'

### 905. `RD_ALifeLine_666abc6a1d81d4e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0059*
- **EN:** "That's a coincidence"
- **TR:** 'Tesadüf'

### 906. `RD_ALifeLine_0ad45b3153156a31` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0060*
- **EN:** 'Quiet hour after dark, no talking, no humming'
- **TR:** 'Akşamdan sonra sessiz saat, konuşma yok, mırıldanma yok'

### 907. `RD_ALifeLine_0819b18f059d6e80` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0060*
- **EN:** 'You mean me'
- **TR:** 'Beni diyorsun'

### 908. `RD_ALifeLine_10bdbc7431155eed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0060*
- **EN:** 'I mean everybody, but yes, you'
- **TR:** 'Herkesi diyorum ama evet, seni'

### 909. `RD_ALifeLine_520584a9061d6574` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0060*
- **EN:** 'You hum'
- **TR:** 'Mırıldanıyorsun'

### 910. `RD_ALifeLine_412ad6f137ea4109` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0061*
- **EN:** 'Tomorrow we move'
- **TR:** 'Yarın taşınıyoruz'

### 911. `RD_ALifeLine_23802f8e3ec26630` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0061*
- **EN:** 'Not decided'
- **TR:** 'Daha karar vermedik'

### 912. `RD_ALifeLine_2fec6863672051bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0061*
- **EN:** "Then decide, I can't sleep on not decided"
- **TR:** 'O zaman ver, belli olmayan şeyin üstüne uyuyamam'

### 913. `RD_ALifeLine_60c4dc924a6f4ea2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0061*
- **EN:** 'Let me sleep on it'
- **TR:** 'Bırak da bir gece düşüneyim'

### 914. `RD_ALifeLine_0d6111dd058b6fbe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0062*
- **EN:** 'Check the back door twice'
- **TR:** 'Arka kapıyı iki kez kontrol et'

### 915. `RD_ALifeLine_5ab5cc7431d5690d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0062*
- **EN:** 'I checked it once'
- **TR:** 'Bir kez baktım'

### 916. `RD_ALifeLine_24130baa485beaf9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0062*
- **EN:** "That's how it works, you check it twice"
- **TR:** 'Olay bu, iki kez bakacaksın'

### 917. `RD_ALifeLine_3800fa963692f771` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0062*
- **EN:** 'Says the guy who locks the same door twice'
- **TR:** 'Aynı kapıyı iki kez kilitleyen adam söylüyor'

### 918. `RD_ALifeLine_11d67fbc060158dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0062*
- **EN:** "It's a good way to work"
- **TR:** 'İyi bir çalışma şekli'

### 919. `RD_ALifeLine_7b40459c7c867422` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0063*
- **EN:** 'What if somebody knocks'
- **TR:** 'Ya biri kapıyı çalarsa'

### 920. `RD_ALifeLine_16831cde5cfa2024` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0063*
- **EN:** "Nobody's gonna knock"
- **TR:** 'Kimse çalmaz'

### 921. `RD_ALifeLine_196ebbc454ba0877` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0063*
- **EN:** 'What if they do'
- **TR:** 'Ya çalarsa'

### 922. `RD_ALifeLine_6d132ecc20fb2cb3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0063*
- **EN:** "Then we don't answer"
- **TR:** 'O zaman açmayız'

### 923. `RD_ALifeLine_44de81cc50940d4e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0063*
- **EN:** 'And if they keep knocking?'
- **TR:** 'Ya çalmaya devam ederlerse?'

### 924. `RD_ALifeLine_3c8039821be9bc78` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0063*
- **EN:** 'Then we answer with the shotgun'
- **TR:** 'O zaman pompalıyla açarız'

### 925. `RD_ALifeLine_0d5ca3fd45c517e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0064*
- **EN:** 'Try the other end of the dial'
- **TR:** 'Kadranın öbür ucunu dene'

### 926. `RD_ALifeLine_0bdc02ad17924335` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0064*
- **EN:** "I've tried both ends and the middle"
- **TR:** 'İki ucunu da, ortasını da denedim'

### 927. `RD_ALifeLine_3e6465b93f3dd0a5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0064*
- **EN:** 'Hold it higher'
- **TR:** 'Daha yukarı tut'

### 928. `RD_ALifeLine_3cf8e2d22cf64d94` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0064*
- **EN:** "That's not how radios work"
- **TR:** 'Radyo öyle çalışmaz'

### 929. `RD_ALifeLine_26d144e96cf947e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0065*
- **EN:** 'Four D batteries, half dead'
- **TR:** 'Dört tane D pil, yarısı bitik'

### 930. `RD_ALifeLine_04d49f1e50176ea0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0065*
- **EN:** 'So half a radio'
- **TR:** 'Yani yarım radyo'

### 931. `RD_ALifeLine_58cf9ce6178601b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0065*
- **EN:** "Don't waste it on music"
- **TR:** 'Müziğe harcama'

### 932. `RD_ALifeLine_689e4623494b0259` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0065*
- **EN:** "It's only worth anything if it's talking"
- **TR:** 'Ancak konuşuyorsa bir işe yarar'

### 933. `RD_ALifeLine_43ad8eca7350600e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0066*
- **EN:** "Got one tape in the Walkman, side A is my cousin's mixtape and side B is her talking between the songs"
- **TR:** "Walkman'de bir kaset var. A yüzü kuzenimin karışık kaseti, B yüzünde şarkıların arasında konuşuyor"

### 934. `RD_ALifeLine_4a5552022eb3de14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0066*
- **EN:** 'Play B'
- **TR:** "B'yi çal"

### 935. `RD_ALifeLine_12997c7455ff375d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0066*
- **EN:** 'Play B for a minute, just a minute'
- **TR:** "Sadece bir dakika, bir dakikacık B'yi çal"

### 936. `RD_ALifeLine_6d35cee24eef07be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0066*
- **EN:** "Do it, I won't tell anybody"
- **TR:** 'Yap, kimseye söylemem'

### 937. `RD_ALifeLine_0e7a38556e8c5d45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0067*
- **EN:** 'At night you can get Nashville on the AM'
- **TR:** "Geceleri AM'den Nashville'i yakalayabiliyorsun"

### 938. `RD_ALifeLine_7b0ba6a968d72d76` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0067*
- **EN:** 'Nashville?'
- **TR:** 'Nashville mi?'

### 939. `RD_ALifeLine_5261420469c0d098` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0067*
- **EN:** 'Skips right off the sky, I used to listen in the truck on long hauls'
- **TR:** 'Gökyüzünden sekip geliyor, uzun yolda kamyonda dinlerdim'

### 940. `RD_ALifeLine_28e6b9512106e232` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0067*
- **EN:** 'Any luck'
- **TR:** 'Bir şey çıktı mı'

### 941. `RD_ALifeLine_5a32b92124de5e2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0067*
- **EN:** 'A man selling used trailers, still, in the middle of all this'
- **TR:** 'Kullanılmış römork satan bir adam, hala, bunca şeyin ortasında'

### 942. `RD_ALifeLine_30b1cc1d5e1fec32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0067*
- **EN:** "Now that's service"
- **TR:** 'Buna hizmet denir'

### 943. `RD_ALifeLine_3313f728711375ec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0068*
- **EN:** 'Somebody keeps keying a CB on channel nineteen and not saying anything'
- **TR:** "Biri CB'de on dokuzuncu kanalı açıp açıp hiçbir şey demiyor"

### 944. `RD_ALifeLine_1a66093a505b4f93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0068*
- **EN:** 'Just breathing?'
- **TR:** 'Sadece nefes mi?'

### 945. `RD_ALifeLine_3876f8d662347620` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0068*
- **EN:** "Just the click, click, click, like they want to talk and can't"
- **TR:** 'Sadece tık, tık, tık, sanki konuşmak istiyorlar da konuşamıyorlar'

### 946. `RD_ALifeLine_559641d7642321f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0068*
- **EN:** 'Say something back'
- **TR:** 'Sen bir şey söyle'

### 947. `RD_ALifeLine_0676f9955d85b13d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0068*
- **EN:** 'I did, nobody answered'
- **TR:** 'Söyledim, kimse cevap vermedi'

### 948. `RD_ALifeLine_5f5c8eae2f298140` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0069*
- **EN:** 'Radio said stay indoors, then it quit'
- **TR:** 'Radyo içeride kalın dedi, sonra sustu'

### 949. `RD_ALifeLine_70e212113be1fb0b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0069*
- **EN:** 'Did it say for how long?'
- **TR:** 'Ne kadar süreyle dedi mi?'

### 950. `RD_ALifeLine_04a8bd707ba86df2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0069*
- **EN:** 'It said until further notice, then it quit'
- **TR:** 'Bir sonraki duyuruya kadar dedi, sonra sustu'

### 951. `RD_ALifeLine_5c79022d774844e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0069*
- **EN:** "Further notice, that's good, that's funny"

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0070*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0070*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0071*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0072*

### 963. `RD_ALifeLine_2fec72a470a4580d` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0072*

### 965. `RD_ALifeLine_233fbf9e39a7b012` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0072*

### 966. `RD_ALifeLine_32c5072b3bfeed26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0072*

### 967. `RD_ALifeLine_24854f74125cb7ae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0073*
- **EN:** 'Remember the ball game on the radio, the whole street had it on'

### 968. `RD_ALifeLine_4293064c3b3686d7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0073*

### 969. `RD_ALifeLine_58225f6954ed19dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0073*
- **EN:** 'Every window open, every radio on'

### 970. `RD_ALifeLine_4971af755f8f06d3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0073*

### 971. `RD_ALifeLine_2b0e43aa376301c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0074*

### 972. `RD_ALifeLine_633228c566d54dcf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0074*
- **EN:** "It's not loaded"
- **TR:** 'Dolu değil'

### 973. `RD_ALifeLine_527401e87f8636c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0074*
