*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0085*
- **EN:** "We don't have whiskey"
- **TR:** 'Viskimiz yok ki'

### 1027. `RD_ALifeLine_6a59584028f90105` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0085*
- **EN:** 'We might have a little whiskey'
- **TR:** 'Azıcık viski olabilir aslında'

### 1028. `RD_ALifeLine_2134d6f5671dcbd9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0086*
- **EN:** 'Once dried my underwear on a picture frame'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde iç çamaşırımı bir tablo çerçevesinde kurutmuştum'

### 1029. `RD_ALifeLine_0cd416d5324ac676` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0086*
- **EN:** "Please tell me it wasn't a family portrait"
- **TR:** 'Aile portresi değildi, de bana'

### 1030. `RD_ALifeLine_6086c31639c3cd00` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0086*
- **EN:** "Only thing at the right height. Didn't think about it till afterward"
- **TR:** 'Doğru yükseklikte tek şey oydu. Ta sonradan aklıma geldi'

### 1031. `RD_ALifeLine_3ecabfea0f65108d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0086*
- **EN:** 'Bet the family looked pleased'
- **TR:** 'Aile memnun kalmıştır herhalde'

### 1032. `RD_ALifeLine_0d68bbcd4b948926` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0087*
- **EN:** 'Lord, we thank you for this food and for the can opener'
- **TR:** 'Rabbim, bu yemek için sana şükrediyoruz, bir de konserve açacağı için'

### 1033. `RD_ALifeLine_5a50d5eb0aa1391a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0087*
- **EN:** 'And for Doyle not eating the whole can'
- **TR:** 'Bir de Doyle bütün kutuyu tek başına yemediği için'

### 1034. `RD_ALifeLine_08592c145b651c1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0087*
- **EN:** "Amen, and I'm sorry"
- **TR:** 'Amin... bir de özür dilerim'

### 1035. `RD_ALifeLine_7c40446f612afd5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0088*
- **EN:** "Nobody's asking you to pray, just be quiet while I do"
- **TR:** 'Sana dua et demiyorum, ben ederken sus yeter'

### 1036. `RD_ALifeLine_47ff2ab8465311c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0088*
- **EN:** "I'm quiet"
- **TR:** 'Susuyorum'

### 1037. `RD_ALifeLine_7013e59752112169` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0088*
- **EN:** "You're breathing at me"
- **TR:** 'Üstüme nefes alıp duruyorsun'

### 1038. `RD_ALifeLine_07ce062f0e8ed424` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0088*
- **EN:** "I'm breathing at everybody"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin üstüne alıyorum zaten'

### 1039. `RD_ALifeLine_6a84be342d372e56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0089*
- **EN:** "I haven't been to church since Easter"
- **TR:** 'Paskalyadan beri kiliseye gitmedim'

### 1040. `RD_ALifeLine_1b4d6c2f24b09411` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0089*
- **EN:** 'Nobody has'
- **TR:** 'Kimse gitmedi'

### 1041. `RD_ALifeLine_42a042e466eab526` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0089*
- **EN:** 'Easter, I wore a yellow dress, I felt like a lemon'
- **TR:** 'Paskalyada sarı elbise giymiştim, limon gibi hissetmiştim'

### 1042. `RD_ALifeLine_7baab50f610eedec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0089*
- **EN:** 'Pastor said you were a ray of sunshine'
- **TR:** 'Papaz "Tam bir güneş ışığısın" demişti'

### 1043. `RD_ALifeLine_6f2d3a1e5257f955` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0089*
- **EN:** 'Pastor lied'
- **TR:** 'Papaz yalan söylemiş'

### 1044. `RD_ALifeLine_40b68de93c2693e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0089*
- **EN:** "Pastor lied about a lot of things, that's why it was a good sermon"
- **TR:** 'Papaz bir sürü konuda yalan söylerdi, vaazlar da o yüzden güzeldi'

### 1045. `RD_ALifeLine_08346c800db8d338` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0090*
- **EN:** "God's gonna have some explaining to do"
- **TR:** "Allah'ın açıklaması gereken çok şey var"

### 1046. `RD_ALifeLine_0f2e19c028b39f2d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0090*
- **EN:** "I'm not afraid of Him"
- **TR:** 'Ondan korkmuyorum'

### 1047. `RD_ALifeLine_6252bccc525826a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0090*
- **EN:** "I know, that's what scares me"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, beni korkutan da o'

### 1048. `RD_ALifeLine_4f3fea195c8346c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0091*
- **EN:** 'Do you know that hymn about the river?'
- **TR:** 'Şu nehirle ilgili ilahiyi biliyor musun?'

### 1049. `RD_ALifeLine_75645ed054d865f3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0091*
- **EN:** 'The one at funerals?'
- **TR:** 'Cenazelerde okunanı mı?'

### 1050. `RD_ALifeLine_47dea95c5bc9d8ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0091*
- **EN:** 'The one at everything, I only know the first line'
- **TR:** 'Her şeyde okunanı. Ben sadece ilk dizesini biliyorum'

### 1051. `RD_ALifeLine_70a7a78e11c32ba8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0091*
- **EN:** "Hum the first line, I'll try the second"
- **TR:** 'İlk dizeyi mırılda, ikincisini ben denerim'

### 1052. `RD_ALifeLine_04e565f67ceb8045` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0091*
- **EN:** 'I know the third'
- **TR:** 'Ben üçüncüyü biliyorum'

### 1053. `RD_ALifeLine_5ab7b65730169b0d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0092*
- **EN:** 'I prayed for rain'
- **TR:** 'Yağmur için dua ettim'

### 1054. `RD_ALifeLine_48e2801102d70c56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0092*
- **EN:** "It's a hundred degrees"
- **TR:** 'Hava otuz sekiz derece'
- *Not: 100°F -> 38 °C*

### 1055. `RD_ALifeLine_7dd2310247ad7ccb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0092*
- **EN:** 'I prayed for a little rain, a drizzle'
- **TR:** 'Azıcık yağmur istedim, bir çisenti'

### 1056. `RD_ALifeLine_14b57e3846e1171d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0092*
- **EN:** "God doesn't do drizzle"
- **TR:** 'Allah çise yapmaz'

### 1057. `RD_ALifeLine_6dd6d204455850dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0092*
- **EN:** "He did it in '87"
- **TR:** "87'de yapmıştı"

### 1058. `RD_ALifeLine_1ae8f22b553ee32d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0093*
- **EN:** "I prayed for my mother and then I prayed for my mother's dog"
- **TR:** 'Annem için dua ettim, sonra annemin köpeği için'

### 1059. `RD_ALifeLine_6833075160f96454` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0093*
- **EN:** "That's a good prayer"
- **TR:** 'Güzel dua'

### 1060. `RD_ALifeLine_75cb64b71067a1a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0093*
- **EN:** 'I felt bad about the dog, he was an idiot'
- **TR:** 'Köpeğe de üzüldüm, o aptalın tekiydi'

### 1061. `RD_ALifeLine_452cc73d1dbff4fe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0093*
- **EN:** "He's still a dog"
- **TR:** 'Yine de köpek işte'

### 1062. `RD_ALifeLine_016228b044bda72c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0093*
- **EN:** "He's still a good dog"
- **TR:** 'Yine de iyi bir köpek'

### 1063. `RD_ALifeLine_7ba0b9d83a6ad3ff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0094*
- **EN:** "Dog's whining out there"
- **TR:** 'Dışarıda bir köpek sızlanıyor'

### 1064. `RD_ALifeLine_7e0d5e3024621e7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0094*
- **EN:** 'Whose dog'
- **TR:** 'Kimin köpeği?'

### 1065. `RD_ALifeLine_5db93315364b84e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0094*
- **EN:** "I don't know, it's just whining, it's been at it an hour"

### 1066. `RD_ALifeLine_1e714f8e6509ac9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0094*
- **EN:** "Maybe he's lost"
- **TR:** 'Belki kaybolmuştur'

### 1067. `RD_ALifeLine_7718768b101abb14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0094*
- **EN:** "Maybe he's hungry"
- **TR:** 'Belki açtır'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 1068. `RD_ALifeLine_1757f8d42dec79a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0094*
- **EN:** "We're all hungry"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz açız'

### 1069. `RD_ALifeLine_67b53f41300ff2b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0095*
- **EN:** "If we had a dog I'd name him Biscuit"
- **TR:** 'Köpeğimiz olsa adını Bisküvi koyardım'

### 1070. `RD_ALifeLine_4f7d929d7071b58b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0095*
- **EN:** 'Why Biscuit'
- **TR:** 'Niye Bisküvi?'

### 1071. `RD_ALifeLine_5092a0c0305712aa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0095*

- **TR:** 'Çünkü şu an bir bisküviye razıyım'

### 1072. `RD_ALifeLine_4ec695bf3f00bf7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0095*
- **EN:** "That's a good dog name"
- **TR:** 'İyi köpek adı'

### 1073. `RD_ALifeLine_58e21cae31629591` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0096*
- **EN:** 'I used to read cereal boxes while everybody else read the paper'
- **TR:** 'Herkes gazete okurken ben mısır gevreği kutularını okurdum'

### 1074. `RD_ALifeLine_2670176f350c2667` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0096*
- **EN:** 'What were you looking for?'
- **TR:** 'Ne arıyordun?'

### 1075. `RD_ALifeLine_7f95580f5b4cc0f3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0096*
- **EN:** "The maze. Same one every morning, and I'd still do it"
- **TR:** 'Labirenti. Her sabah aynısı, yine de çözerdim'

### 1076. `RD_ALifeLine_0b83070654579516` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0096*
- **EN:** 'That was for kids'
- **TR:** 'O çocuklar içindi'

### 1077. `RD_ALifeLine_65055ce540ea7429` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0096*
- **EN:** 'I was half asleep. It was about my level'
- **TR:** 'Yarı uykuluydum. Tam benim seviyemdi'

### 1078. `RD_ALifeLine_6b3bfa8943fbe042` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0096*
- **EN:** "I'd take a maze over the news now"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi haberlerle labirent arasında kalsam labirenti seçerim'

### 1079. `RD_ALifeLine_2276277c07ee9b88` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0097*
- **EN:** 'TV Guide from last week, what was on that Friday'

### 1080. `RD_ALifeLine_32738eb60f22f2b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0097*
- **EN:** "I don't wanna know"

### 1081. `RD_ALifeLine_635006fa2e6a003b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0097*

### 1082. `RD_ALifeLine_32c57f52450bab26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0097*

### 1083. `RD_ALifeLine_39d81f8863321230` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0097*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0098*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0101*
- **EN:** "If there's an afterlife I hope they've got air conditioning"
- **TR:** 'Ahiret varsa umarım klimaları vardır'

### 1099. `RD_ALifeLine_3572ed515656fa55` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0101*
- **EN:** "That's a bold assumption"
- **TR:** 'Cesur bir varsayım'

### 1100. `RD_ALifeLine_7dd4a9bc36ca0dc2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0101*
- **EN:** "You think they don't"
- **TR:** 'Yoksa mı sanıyorsun?'

### 1101. `RD_ALifeLine_769b0a366dfc9fc3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0101*
- **EN:** 'I think it depends which one you get'
- **TR:** 'Bence hangisine düştüğüne bağlı'

### 1102. `RD_ALifeLine_78623b3e2d5d7820` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0101*
- **EN:** "I'd take the one with ice"
- **TR:** 'Ben buzlu olanı alırım'

### 1103. `RD_ALifeLine_4dbab44c38bf9a12` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0102*
- **EN:** 'Nobody told me what to do, nobody came, I keep waiting for somebody in a uniform to show up and say go home'
- **TR:** 'Kimse bana ne yapacağımı söylemedi, kimse gelmedi. Hala üniformalı biri çıkıp gelecek, eve dönün diyecek diye bekliyorum'

### 1104. `RD_ALifeLine_3c284c0a4c2a255e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0102*
- **EN:** "Nobody's coming"
- **TR:** 'Kimse gelmeyecek'

### 1105. `RD_ALifeLine_2a924c8f55ed6284` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0102*
- **EN:** "I know that, I KNOW that, I'm just tired of waiting for it to be true!"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, BİLİYORUM! Sadece bunun gerçekten içime işlemesini bekleyip durmaktan yoruldum!'

### 1106. `RD_ALifeLine_6934431d03f57bd0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0103*
- **EN:** "I haven't cried yet and I'm waiting for it, like a sneeze"
- **TR:** 'Daha ağlamadım, hapşırık gibi bekliyorum'

### 1107. `RD_ALifeLine_7fee0461710a1554` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0103*
- **EN:** "It'll come"
- **TR:** 'Gelir'

### 1108. `RD_ALifeLine_2012ed093255409f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0103*
- **EN:** "It's been nine days, it'll come at a stupid time"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz gün oldu, aptalca bir anda gelecek'

### 1109. `RD_ALifeLine_45d3f31c4e7b8ee3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0103*
- **EN:** "It'll be over a can opener or something"
- **TR:** 'Konserve açacağına falan ağlarsın muhtemelen'

### 1110. `RD_ALifeLine_6fef56942fcd8416` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0105*
- **EN:** "I'd trade everything I own for one hour of air conditioning and a Pepsi"
- **TR:** "Sahip olduğum her şeyi bir saatlik klima ve bir Pepsi'ye değişirim"

### 1111. `RD_ALifeLine_35b06ca862e4afda` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0105*
- **EN:** 'You own nothing'
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şeyin yok ki'

### 1112. `RD_ALifeLine_4ab61a381d840b77` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0105*
- **EN:** "I own the hour, I'm very motivated"
- **TR:** 'O bir saati şimdiden sahiplendim, hevesim büyük'

### 1113. `RD_ALifeLine_7adfec2617ec9b99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0125*
- **EN:** "Wednesday night service, I'd give anything"
- **TR:** 'Çarşamba akşamı ayini için neler vermezdim'

### 1114. `RD_ALifeLine_1bf01bc163768842` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0125*
- **EN:** 'The potluck?'
- **TR:** 'Ortak yemek için mi?'

### 1115. `RD_ALifeLine_63e8358e6198fa82` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0125*
- **EN:** 'The singing, honestly, and then the potluck'
- **TR:** 'Dürüst olayım, ilahiler için, sonra da ortak yemek için'

### 1116. `RD_ALifeLine_4194fcd75caf5d5b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0125*
- **EN:** "And the pastor's wife in the green dress"
- **TR:** 'Bir de papazın yeşil elbiseli karısı'

### 1117. `RD_ALifeLine_627e03e31489a18d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0126*
- **EN:** 'I pray for everybody by name, every night, it takes forty minutes'
- **TR:** 'Herkes için isim isim dua ediyorum, her gece, kırk dakika sürüyor'

### 1118. `RD_ALifeLine_237880013ed19236` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0126*
- **EN:** 'I get up to Delbert, fall asleep, start over'
- **TR:** "Delbert'a geliyorum, uyuyakalıyorum, baştan başlıyorum"

### 1119. `RD_ALifeLine_1627dd51394b36db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0127*
- **EN:** 'Brother Dale used to say the Lord works in mysterious ways'
- **TR:** 'Dale Kardeş hep derdi ki, Rab gizemli yollarla iş görür'

### 1120. `RD_ALifeLine_4df964b81f63bf43` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0127*
- **EN:** "And I'd like to know what the Lord is working on, because it's a mess"
- **TR:** "Rab'bin neyin üstünde çalıştığını çok merak ediyorum, çünkü ortalık felaket"

### 1121. `RD_ALifeLine_711c8a17444a893f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0128*
- **EN:** 'Somebody start a hymn'
- **TR:** 'Biri bir ilahi başlatsın'

### 1122. `RD_ALifeLine_07fcc72f4329bc43` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0128*
- **EN:** "I don't know any"
- **TR:** 'Ben hiç bilmem'

### 1123. `RD_ALifeLine_2c1118cb553e9b2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0128*
- **EN:** 'You know one, you know the one about the bridge'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorsun, köprüyle ilgili olanı biliyorsun'

### 1124. `RD_ALifeLine_75e12a9413ffc3a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0128*
- **EN:** 'Bridge over what'
- **TR:** 'Ne üstünde köprü?'

### 1125. `RD_ALifeLine_58c7457d18646f4d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0128*
- **EN:** "I'll start it, you'll know it"
- **TR:** 'Ben başlarım, sen tanırsın'

### 1126. `RD_ALifeLine_604c25636a27a6c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0129*
- **EN:** "Preacher's wife used to bring a casserole to every funeral"
- **TR:** 'Papazın karısı her cenazeye bir güveç getirirdi'

### 1127. `RD_ALifeLine_377ac0e65d61a682` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0129*
- **EN:** "There'd be six casseroles and nobody ate them"
- **TR:** 'Altı güveç olurdu, kimse yemezdi'

### 1128. `RD_ALifeLine_33001a9264258578` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0129*
- **EN:** 'Everybody ate them...'
- **TR:** 'Herkes yerdi...'

### 1129. `RD_ALifeLine_7c13648f6d66434e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0143*
- **EN:** 'How do you decide who to let stay?'
- **TR:** 'Kimin kalacağına nasıl karar veriyorsun?'

### 1130. `RD_ALifeLine_71d7368a09438931` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0143*
- **EN:** "I talk to them. That's about all I've got"
- **TR:** 'Konuşuyorum onlarla. Elimdeki tek şey bu'

### 1131. `RD_ALifeLine_57dc3f7a3ec70cc2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0143*
- **EN:** 'People can tell you anything'
- **TR:** 'İnsan sana her şeyi söyleyebilir'

### 1132. `RD_ALifeLine_5cc353c91e6f910c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0143*
- **EN:** 'I know. I pay more attention to what they do afterward'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum. Ben sonrasında ne yaptıklarına bakıyorum'

### 1133. `RD_ALifeLine_78ee67a37af0707d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0143*
- **EN:** 'And I still keep my things close. Both can be true'
- **TR:** 'Ben yine de eşyalarımı yakınımda tutuyorum. İkisi de doğru olabilir'

### 1134. `RD_ALifeLine_2f38bf443c4fa3b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0144*
- **EN:** "Don't let anybody know I'm this scared"
- **TR:** 'Kimse bu kadar korktuğumu bilmesin'

### 1135. `RD_ALifeLine_65b7256a6c3981f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0144*
- **EN:** "We're scared too. Nobody needs to hear it from me"
- **TR:** 'Biz de korkuyoruz. Kimsenin bunu benden duymasına gerek yok'

### 1136. `RD_ALifeLine_5e76f2040a8cc2d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0144*
- **EN:** "I mean, don't tell them I said it. Please"
- **TR:** 'Yani, söylediğimi söylemeyin. Lütfen'

### 1137. `RD_ALifeLine_5926306a08b58326` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0144*
- **EN:** 'Said what?'
- **TR:** 'Ne demişsin?'

### 1138. `RD_ALifeLine_09a945b05e40c311` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0145*
- **EN:** "Where's Lonnie?"
- **TR:** 'Lonnie nerede?'

### 1139. `RD_ALifeLine_1a35ceb043de5506` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0145*
- **EN:** 'Out where'
- **TR:** 'Nereye çıktı?'

### 1140. `RD_ALifeLine_4991f896410561a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0145*
- **EN:** "I don't know, he said he needed air"
- **TR:** 'Bilmiyorum, hava alması lazımmış dedi'

### 1141. `RD_ALifeLine_65b8a00e166d103f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0145*
- **EN:** "Air's a lot of places"
- **TR:** 'Hava dediğin bir sürü yer'

### 1142. `RD_ALifeLine_7e802d693e4e3e83` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0146*
- **EN:** "Somebody's been taking from the food, and I'm not saying who, but it's somebody with a belly"
- **TR:** 'Biri yiyeceklerden çalıyor, kim olduğunu söylemiyorum ama göbekli biri'

### 1143. `RD_ALifeLine_398f90c635d4158c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0146*
- **EN:** "I'm just saying it's the one with the belly"
- **TR:** 'Kimseyi söylemiyorum, sadece göbekli olan diyorum'

### 1144. `RD_ALifeLine_0882196f6a817d14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0146*
- **EN:** "Everybody's got a belly right now"
- **TR:** 'Şu sıra herkeste göbek var'

### 1145. `RD_ALifeLine_4b964fd25c9d6165` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0147*
- **EN:** "I heard the Army's got a camp up in Louisville"
- **TR:** "Ordunun Louisville'de bir kampı varmış"

### 1146. `RD_ALifeLine_680cfa0134d60978` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0147*
- **EN:** 'From who'
- **TR:** 'Kimden duydun?'

### 1147. `RD_ALifeLine_2ef47a013d27d389` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0147*
- **EN:** "A guy on the road, he said they've got tents and hot meals"
- **TR:** 'Yolda bir adam söyledi, çadırları, sıcak yemekleri varmış'

### 1148. `RD_ALifeLine_6fc841d173784107` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0147*
- **EN:** 'A guy on the road'
- **TR:** 'Yolda bir adam'

### 1149. `RD_ALifeLine_2b2407216ad145f7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0147*
- **EN:** "I'd walk there for a hot meal"
- **TR:** 'Sıcak yemek için oraya yürürüm'

### 1150. `RD_ALifeLine_20e9e89034c637d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0148*
- **EN:** 'Somebody said it started at the chemical plant'
- **TR:** 'Biri başlangıcın kimyasal tesiste olduğunu söyledi'

### 1151. `RD_ALifeLine_40f555af0c7f78d6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0148*
- **EN:** 'Somebody says a lot'
- **TR:** 'Biri bir sürü şey söylüyor'

### 1152. `RD_ALifeLine_1bd88fea6d93e8d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0148*
- **EN:** 'They said there was a leak and by Friday people were sick'
- **TR:** "Sızıntı olmuş, Cuma'ya kadar insanlar hastalanmış diyorlar"

### 1153. `RD_ALifeLine_290e5b9d54251a69` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0148*
- **EN:** "It started everywhere at once, that's what I heard"
- **TR:** 'Her yerde birden başlamış, ben öyle duydum'

### 1154. `RD_ALifeLine_2369091c2958a822` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0149*
- **EN:** "Somebody said the President's in a bunker"
- **TR:** "Biri Başkan'ın bir sığınakta olduğunu söyledi"

### 1155. `RD_ALifeLine_11f3c21411b48e2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0149*
- **EN:** "I don't know, the one under whatever's under Washington"
- **TR:** "Bilmem, Washington'ın altındaki her ne varsa onun da altındaki"

### 1156. `RD_ALifeLine_37ea959532f37d4c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0149*
- **EN:** "That's a lot of bunkers"
- **TR:** 'Bir sürü sığınak var yani'

### 1157. `RD_ALifeLine_101e08093b892662` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0150*
- **EN:** "You know what, I'm glad you're all here"
- **TR:** 'Ya, hepinizin burada olmasına sevindim'
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1158. `RD_ALifeLine_18daf5994ad70750` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0150*
- **EN:** "Don't get sappy"
- **TR:** 'Duygusallaşma'

### 1159. `RD_ALifeLine_2b1c327272739204` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0150*
- **EN:** "I'm not getting sappy, I'm just saying it in a flat voice"
- **TR:** 'Duygusallaşmıyorum, düz bir sesle söylüyorum sadece'

### 1160. `RD_ALifeLine_381988da48836f64` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0150*
- **EN:** "That's the worst kind of sappy"
- **TR:** 'En kötü duygusallık o'

### 1161. `RD_ALifeLine_71f30b2d76d2296b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0014*
- **EN:** 'We got a little bit of sugar'
- **TR:** 'Azıcık şekerimiz var'

### 1162. `RD_ALifeLine_118508524443974e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0014*
- **EN:** "Let's save it"
- **TR:** 'Saklayalım'

### 1163. `RD_ALifeLine_0b182734029f0905` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0014*
- **EN:** "I'll make tea, I've got a little pot"
- **TR:** 'Çay yaparım, küçük bir çaydanlığım var'

### 1164. `RD_ALifeLine_307027bc5997c0b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0014*
- **EN:** "We don't have tea"
- **TR:** 'Çayımız yok ki'

### 1165. `RD_ALifeLine_111e16e85e9f68af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0014*
- **EN:** 'I have tea'
- **TR:** 'Bende var'

### 1166. `RD_ALifeLine_0cba5c614f657250` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0018*
- **EN:** "Every little noise and my heart's going like a drum, I can't make it stop"
- **TR:** 'En ufak sese kalbim davul gibi atıyor, durduramıyorum'

### 1167. `RD_ALifeLine_6d8bd70f2c6c06cf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0018*
- **EN:** 'Breathe'
- **TR:** 'Nefes al'

### 1168. `RD_ALifeLine_02c8f3d314ff16c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0018*
- **EN:** "I AM breathing, that's why I can hear it"
- **TR:** 'Nefes ALIYORUM, kalbimi de o yüzden duyuyorum!'

### 1169. `RD_ALifeLine_2ced13037535567b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0018*
- **EN:** "Come sit down, you're white as a sheet"
- **TR:** 'Gel otur, bembeyaz kesilmişsin'

### 1170. `RD_ALifeLine_340403752be002c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0019*
- **EN:** 'Go check the back, again, please'
- **TR:** 'Arkayı kontrol et, bir daha, lütfen'

### 1171. `RD_ALifeLine_1a2d180136e4d232` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0019*
- **EN:** 'I checked it ten minutes ago'
- **TR:** 'On dakika önce baktım'

### 1172. `RD_ALifeLine_41b5d58f0d751c52` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0019*
- **EN:** "Check it again, please, I won't be able to sit if you don't"
- **TR:** 'Bir daha bak lütfen, sen bakmazsan oturamam'

### 1173. `RD_ALifeLine_186e544a21e4641f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0019*
- **EN:** "...fine, but I'm not gonna enjoy it"
- **TR:** '...Tamam, ama hoşuma gitmeyecek'

### 1174. `RD_ALifeLine_5b8b3894798baa33` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0020*
- **EN:** 'What if they can hear us breathing'
- **TR:** 'Ya nefes alışımızı duyuyorlarsa?'

### 1175. `RD_ALifeLine_2aaa53cf478b90e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0020*
- **EN:** "They can't"
- **TR:** 'Duymazlar'

### 1176. `RD_ALifeLine_35a995ea35ea0fa0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0020*
- **EN:** 'How do you know that? Nobody knows that!'
- **TR:** 'Nereden biliyorsun? Bunu kimse bilmiyor!'

### 1177. `RD_ALifeLine_27cfc3b53199b398` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0020*
- **EN:** 'Hush, both of you, just hush'
- **TR:** 'Susun, ikiniz de, hadi susun artık'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1178. `RD_ALifeLine_70a2cef725dc1ace` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0021*
- **EN:** "I-- I can't do this, I can't, I'm not built for this"
- **TR:** 'Ben-- ben yapamam bunu, yapamam, bu iş bana göre değil'

### 1179. `RD_ALifeLine_0bac140441841b26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0021*
- **EN:** "Nobody's built for it"
- **TR:** 'Kimseye göre değil'

### 1180. `RD_ALifeLine_747265196ef79643` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0021*
- **EN:** "You are, you're calm, how are you calm"
- **TR:** 'Sen öylesin, sakinsin, nasıl oluyor da sakinsin?'

### 1181. `RD_ALifeLine_27b2001572447561` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0021*
- **EN:** "I'm not calm, I'm just not moving"
- **TR:** 'Sakin değilim, sadece kıpırdamıyorum'

### 1182. `RD_ALifeLine_719ba85b77ffe596` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0022*
- **EN:** 'I keep seeing Mrs. Hadley from next door, in her window'
- **TR:** 'Yan evden Bayan Hadley gözümün önünden gitmiyor, pencerede'

### 1183. `RD_ALifeLine_4ee1a9bc7665a095` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0022*
- **EN:** 'She was just standing there, in her nightgown, waving, like she wanted me to come over'
- **TR:** 'Orada öyle duruyordu, geceliğiyle, el sallıyordu, sanki yanına gitmemi istiyormuş gibi'

### 1184. `RD_ALifeLine_641c870d4e8b5ebd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0022*
- **EN:** "She wasn't waving at you"
- **TR:** 'Sana el sallamıyordu'

### 1185. `RD_ALifeLine_4533399958c3d69a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0024*
- **EN:** 'I keep saving the good coat for my mother'
- **TR:** 'İyi paltoyu annem için saklayıp duruyorum'

### 1186. `RD_ALifeLine_5d8e214e5f4eeb01` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0024*
- **EN:** "She'd want you to wear it"
- **TR:** 'Senin giymeni isterdi'

### 1187. `RD_ALifeLine_6ea6e45d06f38155` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0024*
- **EN:** "She's not here, and I'm saving it, it's stupid, it's a coat"
- **TR:** 'O burada değil, ben de saklıyorum, saçma, bir palto sonuçta'

### 1188. `RD_ALifeLine_75b94b853cc367ad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0024*
- **EN:** "It's a good coat, wear it"
- **TR:** 'İyi bir palto, giy onu'

### 1189. `RD_ALifeLine_1db3ef9a4139350b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0026*
- **EN:** 'They told us stay inside, they told us stay calm, and then they cut the lines'
- **TR:** 'Evde kalın dediler, sakin olun dediler, sonra da hatları kestiler'

### 1190. `RD_ALifeLine_3702242f768c4ca2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0026*
- **EN:** "Some bureaucrat in Frankfort is eating a sandwich right now and he doesn't know my name"
- **TR:** "Frankfort'taki bir bürokrat şu an sandviç yiyordur, benim adımı bile bilmiyor"

### 1191. `RD_ALifeLine_7c5fa19e394dbfca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0026*
- **EN:** "He doesn't know any of our names"
- **TR:** 'Hiçbirimizin adını bilmiyor'

### 1192. `RD_ALifeLine_14f8f87b44c43dfd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0028*
- **EN:** "I always said this county would go to hell, I just thought it'd take longer"
- **TR:** 'Ben hep bu ilçe cehenneme gidecek derdim, sadece bu kadar çabuk olacağını düşünmemiştim'

### 1193. `RD_ALifeLine_36315eb37fe1f613` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0028*
- **EN:** 'It was a good county'
- **TR:** 'Güzel bir ilçeydi'

### 1194. `RD_ALifeLine_5061739a1d5ce7f0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0028*
- **EN:** "It had its moments, I'd say it had a decent Dairy Queen"
- **TR:** "Güzel anları vardı, mesela adam gibi bir Dairy Queen'i vardı"

### 1195. `RD_ALifeLine_5ef689ce5f89642f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0028*
- **EN:** "I'll drink to that, if we had anything to drink"
- **TR:** 'Ona içerim, içecek bir şeyimiz olsaydı'

### 1196. `RD_ALifeLine_61bd508c0ed61d4c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0030*
- **EN:** "I can smell my own feet from over here, and I'm sitting on them"
- **TR:** 'Ayaklarımın kokusunu şuradan alıyorum, üstelik üstlerine oturmuşum'

### 1197. `RD_ALifeLine_58591c61003c3c6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0030*
- **EN:** "That's physically impossible"
- **TR:** 'Bu fiziksel olarak imkansız'

### 1198. `RD_ALifeLine_152a7a0a6a797217` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0030*
- **EN:** "Then tell my nose, it's very confused"
- **TR:** 'O zaman burnuma söyle, çok kafası karışmış'

### 1199. `RD_ALifeLine_0ec292de1367b82d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0033*
- **EN:** 'I miss my bed, I miss my stupid lumpy bed, it had a dip in the middle exactly where my body went'
- **TR:** 'Yatağımı özledim, o aptal yumru yumru yatağımı, tam gövdemin geldiği yerde bir çukuru vardı'

### 1200. `RD_ALifeLine_5b9b9b1d679977f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0033*
- **EN:** "I'd marry my pillow, I'd have its babies"
- **TR:** 'Yastığımla evlenirim, ondan çocuk bile yaparım'

### 1201. `RD_ALifeLine_74deb90e20c77f19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0033*
- **EN:** 'Calm down'
- **TR:** 'Sakin ol'

### 1202. `RD_ALifeLine_0ede2a053b6a7284` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0034*
- **EN:** "What if nobody's coming"
- **TR:** 'Ya kimse gelmezse?'

### 1203. `RD_ALifeLine_48295b4a7653276c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0034*
- **EN:** "I'm just asking, what if it's just us"
- **TR:** 'Sadece soruyorum, ya bir tek biz varsak?'

### 1204. `RD_ALifeLine_34933b7d348cad54` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0034*
- **EN:** "Then it's just us, eat your beans"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir tek biziz, fasulyeni ye'

### 1205. `RD_ALifeLine_32590f0d7fcffb2b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0036*
- **EN:** 'You know what I miss? Cutting the grass'
- **TR:** 'Neyi özlüyorum biliyor musun? Çim biçmeyi'

### 1206. `RD_ALifeLine_7b6637032f7cd6e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0036*
- **EN:** 'Nobody misses cutting the grass'
- **TR:** 'Çim biçmeyi kimse özlemez'

### 1207. `RD_ALifeLine_55809175310ccc8b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0036*
- **EN:** "I do, I'd give a hundred bucks to push a mower around the yard for an hour"
- **TR:** 'Ben özlerim, bir saat bahçede çim biçme makinesi sürmek için yüz dolar veririm'

### 1208. `RD_ALifeLine_4fee2fd63a689d18` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0036*
- **EN:** "Come push mine, I'll watch..."
- **TR:** 'Gel benimkini it, ben seyrederim...'

### 1209. `RD_ALifeLine_7560ffd91c24061d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0037*
- **EN:** "If I'd known this was the last week I'd have eaten the whole pie"
- **TR:** 'Bunun son hafta olduğunu bilseydim bütün turtayı yerdim'

### 1210. `RD_ALifeLine_6d904f5f78ad2355` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0037*
- **EN:** 'Which pie?'
- **TR:** 'Hangi turta?'

### 1211. `RD_ALifeLine_53ccad93408f7796` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0037*
- **EN:** 'The one in the fridge, the lemon meringue, I was being good, I was saving it'
- **TR:** 'Buzdolabındaki, limonlu beze kremalı olan. Uslu duruyordum, saklıyordum'

### 1212. `RD_ALifeLine_090cc9777487fba3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0037*
- **EN:** 'Never save the pie'
- **TR:** 'Turtayı asla saklama'

### 1213. `RD_ALifeLine_7f6e15f833d5bc58` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0039*
- **EN:** "I'm not gonna sit here and be told what to do by somebody I met on Saturday"
- **TR:** 'Cumartesi günü tanıştığım biri bana ne yapacağımı söyleyecek diye burada oturmam'

### 1214. `RD_ALifeLine_06212d2f6241a2bb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0039*
- **EN:** "I'm trying to help"
- **TR:** 'Yardım etmeye çalışıyorum'

### 1215. `RD_ALifeLine_69f8d92c006ea42b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0039*
- **EN:** "You're trying to be in charge, there's a difference"
- **TR:** 'Başa geçmeye çalışıyorsun, fark var'

### 1216. `RD_ALifeLine_1084a8384523867a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0040*
- **EN:** 'Whose idea was it to stay in this house anyway?'
- **TR:** 'Zaten bu evde kalma fikri kimin?'

### 1217. `RD_ALifeLine_715058637db571c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0040*
- **EN:** "That wasn't mine, that was Pete's thing"
- **TR:** "Benim değildi, Pete'in işiydi"

### 1218. `RD_ALifeLine_4005910b75c09369` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0040*
- **EN:** "Pete's not here, so it was somebody's idea, shut up"
- **TR:** 'Pete burada yok, yani birinin fikriydi, sus artık'

### 1219. `RD_ALifeLine_36cafd7f2990d762` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0042*
- **EN:** "I had to tell the kids Daddy wasn't coming home, I had to say it in the kitchen"
- **TR:** 'Çocuklara babalarının eve gelmeyeceğini söylemem gerekti, mutfakta söyledim'

### 1220. `RD_ALifeLine_37b2bcb415f34a19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0042*
- **EN:** "I didn't know the words, I used the wrong words, I said gone"
- **TR:** 'Kelimeleri bilemedim, yanlış kelimeleri kullandım, gitti dedim'

### 1221. `RD_ALifeLine_2fed4dc362828ac7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0042*
- **EN:** "Gone's all right, gone's what it is"
- **TR:** 'Gitti demen yanlış değil, olan bu'

### 1222. `RD_ALifeLine_1dfa8b762eff14fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0043*
- **EN:** 'Did you ever fix that thing with your brother?'
- **TR:** 'Kardeşinle olan o şeyi hallettin mi?'

### 1223. `RD_ALifeLine_0617ebe83ed3dd7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0043*
- **EN:** 'What thing'
- **TR:** 'Hangi şey?'

### 1224. `RD_ALifeLine_24a8893d4d9d6b5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0043*
- **EN:** 'The fight, at the funeral'
- **TR:** 'Kavga, cenazedeki'

### 1225. `RD_ALifeLine_01b1ce9d30120293` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0043*
- **EN:** "Then do it, if he's out there"
- **TR:** 'O zaman hallet, dışarıdaysa'

### 1226. `RD_ALifeLine_7cc2758d0ce08ef2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0044*
- **EN:** 'I think I heard somebody say my name outside'
- **TR:** 'Sanki dışarıda biri adımı söyledi'

### 1227. `RD_ALifeLine_63e9fb56121c4ad0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0044*
- **EN:** "There's nobody out there"
- **TR:** 'Dışarıda kimse yok'

### 1228. `RD_ALifeLine_5122a9353f9d9f7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0044*
- **EN:** 'I heard it, clear as day, Dale, Dale'
- **TR:** 'Net duydum, Dale, Dale diye'

### 1229. `RD_ALifeLine_74d894b41c471c83` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0044*
- **EN:** "It's nothing, there's a loose board"
- **TR:** 'Bir şey değil, gevşek bir tahta var'

### 1230. `RD_ALifeLine_0dde3f3f12949c6f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0046*
- **EN:** "I'm sick of being told to be quiet, I'm gonna scream"
- **TR:** 'Sürekli sessiz ol denmesinden bıktım, bağıracağım'

### 1231. `RD_ALifeLine_7010059a70bbd56b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0046*
- **EN:** "I'm-- I'm gonna scream into this jacket, give me a minute"
- **TR:** 'Ben-- şu ceketin içine bağıracağım, bir dakika verin'

### 1232. `RD_ALifeLine_00f6115f222504e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0046*
- **EN:** "Scream into the bucket, it'll be more satisfying"
- **TR:** 'Kovaya bağır, daha tatmin edici olur'

### 1233. `RD_ALifeLine_6e2af3ba4c6335ef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0048*
- **EN:** "I'm glad you're here, I didn't think I'd say it, but I'm glad"
- **TR:** 'Burada olmana sevindim, söyleyeceğimi sanmazdım ama sevindim'

### 1234. `RD_ALifeLine_600c24545cb9c6b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0048*
- **EN:** "You're gonna make me cry and I've got no water to spare"
- **TR:** 'Beni ağlatacaksın, harcayacak suyum yok'

### 1235. `RD_ALifeLine_65d03f4c258aea5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0048*
- **EN:** "I'll cry for both of us, I've got a canteen"
- **TR:** 'İkimiz yerine ben ağlarım, matara bende'

### 1236. `RD_ALifeLine_2ccdb3b8759b2957` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0050*
- **EN:** "My hands won't stop shaking, look at them"
- **TR:** 'Ellerim titremeyi bırakmıyor, bak şunlara'

### 1237. `RD_ALifeLine_46cc5a5702bf4404` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0050*
- **EN:** 'Hold them still'
- **TR:** 'Sabit tut'

### 1238. `RD_ALifeLine_66a21bf009349d67` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0050*
- **EN:** "I'm TRYING, they've got a mind of their own!"
- **TR:** 'DENİYORUM, kendi kafalarına göre takılıyorlar!'

### 1239. `RD_ALifeLine_2cfde8b605209fc5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0050*
- **EN:** 'Put them in your pockets'
- **TR:** 'Cebine sok'

### 1240. `RD_ALifeLine_2790dc021f34e3a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0052*
- **EN:** "My wife used to hum while she did dishes, and I'd tell her to stop"
- **TR:** 'Karım bulaşık yıkarken mırıldanırdı, ben de sus derdim'

### 1241. `RD_ALifeLine_3a02481e337ec698` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0052*
- **EN:** 'You told her to stop?'
- **TR:** 'Sus mu dedin?'

### 1242. `RD_ALifeLine_4d98acbd2e2244bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0052*
- **EN:** "Every day, every damn day, and now I'd pay a hundred dollars to hear it"
- **TR:** 'Her gün, her kahrolası gün, şimdi onu bir kez duymak için yüz dolar öderim'

### 1243. `RD_ALifeLine_682de9fa60eecc52` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0053*
- **EN:** "I said I'd call her back, I was in the middle of a game"
- **TR:** 'Onu sonra ararım dedim, maçın ortasındaydım'

### 1244. `RD_ALifeLine_66bb546f5e4ff02a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0053*
- **EN:** 'The ballgame?'
- **TR:** 'Maç mıydı?'

### 1245. `RD_ALifeLine_6bd984c42de5366d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0053*
- **EN:** 'I said gimme a minute, Mom, and I never called her back'
- **TR:** 'Bir dakika ver anne dedim, ve onu bir daha hiç aramadım'

### 1246. `RD_ALifeLine_7ef01f6b12503727` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0053*
- **EN:** 'She knew...'
- **TR:** 'O biliyordu...'

### 1247. `RD_ALifeLine_31a8c86229876a3c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0055*
- **EN:** 'We should name the bucket'
- **TR:** 'Kovaya bir isim verelim'

### 1248. `RD_ALifeLine_7e3d70b350594add` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0055*
- **EN:** 'We should not'
- **TR:** 'Vermeyelim'

### 1249. `RD_ALifeLine_3f1c92c6538e6b19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0055*
- **EN:** "Gerald, it's a Gerald"
- **TR:** 'Gerald, bu bir Gerald'

### 1250. `RD_ALifeLine_0b0b9d435ce47199` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0055*
- **EN:** "Gerald's gonna need emptying"
- **TR:** "Gerald'ı boşaltmak gerekecek"

### 1251. `RD_ALifeLine_5216d7f646675c2c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0059*
- **EN:** "I haven't slept in so long I just saw a cow in the kitchen"
- **TR:** 'O kadar uzun zamandır uyumuyorum ki mutfakta bir inek gördüm'

### 1252. `RD_ALifeLine_4decd2da7defd6d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0059*
- **EN:** "There's no cow"
- **TR:** 'İnek yok'

### 1253. `RD_ALifeLine_151e62c47d2ab981` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0059*
- **EN:** 'It looked right at me, it had a hat on'
- **TR:** 'Doğruca bana baktı, şapkası vardı'

### 1254. `RD_ALifeLine_6f2c9a756fd58f37` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0059*
- **EN:** "Go to sleep, I'll watch the cow"
- **TR:** 'Sen uyu, ineği ben gözlerim'

### 1255. `RD_ALifeLine_764c184428d85f3f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0061*
- **EN:** "I never minded the dark before. Forty-four years old, and now I... I don't want it dark"
- **TR:** 'Karanlıktan hiç rahatsız olmazdım. Kırk dört yaşındayım, şimdi ben... karanlık olsun istemiyorum'

### 1256. `RD_ALifeLine_17d7ea453de3c25b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0061*
- **EN:** "You aren't the only one"
- **TR:** 'Bir tek sen değilsin'

### 1257. `RD_ALifeLine_4bdc110a1f95525a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0061*
- **EN:** "It's what I can hear in it. That's what gets me"
- **TR:** 'İçinde duyduklarım. Beni bitiren o'

### 1258. `RD_ALifeLine_0ae7ab2008451d5b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0061*
- **EN:** "Then light a candle. You don't have to ask us"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir mum yak. Bize sormana gerek yok'

### 1259. `RD_ALifeLine_482dc5893cfdeea7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0063*
- **EN:** 'I saw a black cat cross the road today'
- **TR:** 'Bugün yolda bir kara kedi geçti'

### 1260. `RD_ALifeLine_7b77233b49d0ee80` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0063*
- **EN:** 'Bad luck'
- **TR:** 'Uğursuzluk'

### 1261. `RD_ALifeLine_1da43e320e418ee4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0063*
- **EN:** "No, it's good luck, it means we're out of bad luck"
- **TR:** 'Hayır, uğur, yani kötü şansımız bitti demek'

### 1262. `RD_ALifeLine_46c2c2ec2ecf807b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0063*
- **EN:** "That's not how luck works"
- **TR:** 'Şans öyle işlemez'

### 1263. `RD_ALifeLine_38ec65ed25a174d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0066*
- **EN:** 'What did you do on the first day?'
- **TR:** 'İlk gün ne yaptın?'

### 1264. `RD_ALifeLine_0ca45a8d3e012f09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0066*
- **EN:** 'Locked the doors, sat on the bed, held the phone, waited'
- **TR:** 'Kapıları kilitledim, yatağa oturdum, telefonu tuttum, bekledim'

### 1265. `RD_ALifeLine_788313e86e707809` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0066*
- **EN:** 'Same, I held the phone for eight hours, and it never rang'
- **TR:** 'Ben de, sekiz saat boyunca telefonu tuttum, bir kere çalmadı'

### 1266. `RD_ALifeLine_644ffa0c115b184a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0066*
- **EN:** 'I held mine for three days, and then I put it down'
- **TR:** 'Ben benimkini üç gün tuttum, sonra bıraktım'

### 1267. `RD_ALifeLine_5718bbd975da59c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0068*
- **EN:** 'The kid asked me where Mama is'
- **TR:** 'Çocuk bana annesi nerede diye sordu'

### 1268. `RD_ALifeLine_47670ffe5d3b3c32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0068*
- **EN:** 'What did you say?'
- **TR:** 'Ne dedin?'

### 1269. `RD_ALifeLine_53db91d30b7122a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0068*
- **EN:** "I said she's resting, she's resting somewhere, and I couldn't look at her"
- **TR:** 'Dinleniyor dedim, bir yerde dinleniyor, ona bakamadım'

### 1270. `RD_ALifeLine_7f87e8397265c4f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0070*
- **EN:** 'I found a candy bar, an actual candy bar, a Snickers'
- **TR:** 'Bir çikolata buldum, gerçek bir çikolata, bir Snickers'

### 1271. `RD_ALifeLine_135e8e4a2e08fa09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0070*
- **EN:** 'Cut in four, one for each of us, and nobody say a word'
- **TR:** 'Dörde böldük, hepimize bir parça, kimse tek kelime etmesin'

### 1272. `RD_ALifeLine_03fde6550ddf7240` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0070*
- **EN:** "Best Snickers I've ever had, and I haven't even had it yet"
- **TR:** 'Hayatımda yediğim en iyi Snickers, daha yemedim bile'

### 1273. `RD_ALifeLine_6b9fbf8c0830b852` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0072*
- **EN:** 'You ever hear a house sigh?'
- **TR:** 'Hiç bir evin iç çektiğini duydun mu?'

### 1274. `RD_ALifeLine_0ab58a69695c1e6b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0072*
- **EN:** "This one does, every so often, like it's tired too"
- **TR:** 'Bu ev çekiyor, arada bir, o da yorgunmuş gibi'

### 1275. `RD_ALifeLine_5a92d14c4d23886c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0072*
- **EN:** "That's the roof cooling..."
- **TR:** 'Çatı soğuyor da ondan...'

### 1276. `RD_ALifeLine_3794aff04af08dc5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0075*
- **EN:** "My little brother's nineteen, he's in the Navy, he's somewhere in the Gulf"
- **TR:** "Küçük kardeşim on dokuz yaşında, Donanma'da, Körfez'de bir yerde"

### 1277. `RD_ALifeLine_48e0fbef7e13b901` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0075*
- **EN:** "He'll be fine"
- **TR:** 'İyi olur'

### 1278. `RD_ALifeLine_7234e2eb61b690ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0075*
- **EN:** "He doesn't know any of this, he's probably eating pancakes on a boat"
- **TR:** 'Bunların hiçbirini bilmiyor, muhtemelen bir teknede pankek yiyor'

### 1279. `RD_ALifeLine_49b188752133b19f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0075*
- **EN:** 'Let him eat pancakes'
- **TR:** 'Bırak yesin pankeki'

### 1280. `RD_ALifeLine_65baf234048d8f67` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0077*
- **EN:** "Mom's roses must be dead by now, nobody watering"
- **TR:** 'Annemin gülleri şimdiye ölmüştür, kimse sulamıyor'

### 1281. `RD_ALifeLine_5232f90938338301` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0077*
- **EN:** 'Maybe it rained'
- **TR:** 'Belki yağmur yağmıştır'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 1282. `RD_ALifeLine_73b2d0a106a628c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0077*
- **EN:** "It hasn't rained, I've been watching the sky for two weeks"
- **TR:** 'Yağmadı, iki haftadır gökyüzünü izliyorum'

### 1283. `RD_ALifeLine_26055efc21696b63` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0079*
- **EN:** "I've decided I'm gonna start a farm"
- **TR:** 'Çiftlik kurmaya karar verdim'

### 1284. `RD_ALifeLine_615ca73878e556bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0079*
- **EN:** 'A farm?'
- **TR:** 'Çiftlik mi?'

### 1285. `RD_ALifeLine_35196c981ee2fd66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0079*
- **EN:** "Chickens, a few goats, I've got a plan, it's a good plan"
- **TR:** 'Tavuklar, birkaç keçi, bir planım var, iyi bir plan'

### 1286. `RD_ALifeLine_425aaf4536fc6966` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0079*
- **EN:** "The first goat's gonna eat the plan"
- **TR:** 'İlk keçi planı yer'

### 1287. `RD_ALifeLine_5ef8ab734780172d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0081*
- **EN:** "You snore, but it's the sound of somebody being alive, so I don't mind"
- **TR:** 'Horluyorsun ama bu birinin hayatta olduğunun sesi, o yüzden sorun etmiyorum'

### 1288. `RD_ALifeLine_2bd8a2170188ef77` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0081*
- **EN:** "That's the nicest way anyone's ever told me I snore"
- **TR:** 'Birinin bana horladığımı söylemesinin en nazik yolu bu'

### 1289. `RD_ALifeLine_4055e40e7098808b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0081*
- **EN:** "It's the only way I could say it"
- **TR:** 'Ben ancak böyle söyleyebilirdim'

### 1290. `RD_ALifeLine_2e1b8aec16d0e245` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0083*
- **EN:** 'I used to sit on the porch every night, with a cigarette and the crickets'
- **TR:** 'Her gece verandada otururdum, sigaramla ve cırcır böcekleriyle'

### 1291. `RD_ALifeLine_79a2754f0584170b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0083*
- **EN:** "Now the crickets are the only thing left, and I don't have the cigarette"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi geriye sadece cırcır böcekleri kaldı, sigaram da yok'

### 1292. `RD_ALifeLine_1b0ca5cd657c5e7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0083*
- **EN:** 'Wish I could offer the porch back. The cigarette was only part of it'
- **TR:** 'Keşke verandayı sana geri verebilseydim. Sigara işin sadece bir parçasıydı'

### 1293. `RD_ALifeLine_5acad6a123ad4de5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0086*
- **EN:** "What if there's nowhere to go?"
- **TR:** 'Ya gidecek yer yoksa?'

### 1294. `RD_ALifeLine_7cf292712b7751e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0086*
- **EN:** "There's somewhere"
- **TR:** 'Var'

### 1295. `RD_ALifeLine_7f4d91cf68cbbe3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0086*
- **EN:** "What if there isn't? What if it's all like this?"
- **TR:** 'Ya yoksa? Ya her yer böyleyse?'

### 1296. `RD_ALifeLine_0641d3ec41af9d1a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0086*
- **EN:** "Then we're not gonna find out sitting here"
- **TR:** 'O zaman burada oturarak öğrenemeyiz'

### 1297. `RD_ALifeLine_1103999b15349f07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0088*
- **EN:** "Nicest house I ever slept in, I couldn't enjoy it"
- **TR:** 'Hayatımda yattığım en güzel ev, zevk alamadım'

### 1298. `RD_ALifeLine_5bbabd0a78327629` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0088*
- **EN:** 'Too worried?'
- **TR:** 'Çok mu endişeliydin?'

### 1299. `RD_ALifeLine_4817390a454422d6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0088*
- **EN:** 'Had a bathtub with feet. Nearly cried looking at it'
- **TR:** 'Ayaklı bir küvet vardı. Bakarken neredeyse ağlıyordum'

### 1300. `RD_ALifeLine_483f039611944e55` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0088*
- **EN:** "I'd have cried too. Nice things can make you miss your own place worse"
- **TR:** 'Ben de ağlardım. Güzel şeyler insanın kendi yerini daha çok özletir'

### 1301. `RD_ALifeLine_671dd2ac31919179` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0090*
- **EN:** "We should make a list of everybody's favorite things, when this is over, we'll do them all"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin en sevdiği şeylerin bir listesini yapalım, bu bittiğinde hepsini yaparız'

### 1302. `RD_ALifeLine_3495505b5be2bb38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0090*
- **EN:** 'A list of what?'
- **TR:** 'Neyin listesi?'

### 1303. `RD_ALifeLine_535425d751f0f561` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0090*
- **EN:** "Favorite foods, favorite songs, favorite places, I'll start"
- **TR:** 'En sevdiğimiz yemekler, şarkılar, yerler, ben başlıyorum'

### 1304. `RD_ALifeLine_0dcd3fd251a41aeb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0090*
- **EN:** 'Start with the foods'
- **TR:** 'Yemeklerle başla'

### 1305. `RD_ALifeLine_0be318772b1bb402` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0092*
- **EN:** "It's weird, I miss the news, even the bad news"
- **TR:** 'Garip ama haberleri özlüyorum, kötü haberleri bile'

### 1306. `RD_ALifeLine_2099aaee409be647` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0092*
- **EN:** 'The weatherman, the man who reads the school closings'
- **TR:** 'Hava durumu spikeri, okul tatili duyurularını okuyan adam'

### 1307. `RD_ALifeLine_44a3da1442f94ffb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0092*
- **EN:** 'The man with the farm report'
- **TR:** 'Tarım haberlerini okuyan adam'

### 1308. `RD_ALifeLine_5e4904214d12add1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0094*
- **EN:** "Let's play a game, whoever says 'when this is over' pays a can"
- **TR:** "Bir oyun oynayalım, kim 'bu bittiğinde' derse bir konserve öder"

### 1309. `RD_ALifeLine_6146611351f29f71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0094*
- **EN:** "We don't have cans to pay"
- **TR:** 'Ödeyecek konservemiz yok ki'

### 1310. `RD_ALifeLine_75fce602147a0e57` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0094*
- **EN:** "That's the genius, nobody can pay, and nobody can say it"
- **TR:** 'İşin dahiliği bu, kimse ödeyemez, kimse de diyemez'

### 1311. `RD_ALifeLine_3dcce5a31b3d8acd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0094*
- **EN:** 'You just said it'
- **TR:** 'Az önce dedin'

### 1312. `RD_ALifeLine_7ee3d2a225ca0be7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0097*
- **EN:** "I can't believe this is happening, it's like a bad dream, I keep waiting to wake up and it's Friday morning"
- **TR:** 'Bunun gerçekten olduğuna inanamıyorum, kötü bir rüya gibi, uyanacağım ve Cuma sabahı olacak diye bekleyip duruyorum'

### 1313. `RD_ALifeLine_34125974676e7b23` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0097*
- **EN:** "And then I smell it, and I know I'm awake"
- **TR:** 'Sonra kokuyu alıyorum, ve uyanık olduğumu anlıyorum'

### 1314. `RD_ALifeLine_154ef7015bd3e849` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0099*
- **EN:** "Nobody told me it'd be like this, the TV said stay calm, stay calm, and I'm sitting here SHAKING"
- **TR:** 'Kimse bana böyle olacağını söylemedi, televizyon sakin olun, sakin olun dedi, ben de burada TİTRİYORUM'

### 1315. `RD_ALifeLine_4133f29902fd38d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0099*
- **EN:** 'Stay calm, they said, stay in your homes, and I did, and look!'
- **TR:** 'Sakin olun dediler, evlerinizde kalın dediler, ben de kaldım, şimdi bak!'

### 1316. `RD_ALifeLine_7cb909cd45e496e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0099*
- **EN:** "Look at what? We're alive"
- **TR:** 'Neye bakayım? Hayattayız'

### 1317. `RD_ALifeLine_1cc5dd0f38ead84f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0101*
- **EN:** "Shut up, shut up, just shut up a second, I'm trying to remember something"
- **TR:** 'Sus, sus, bir saniye sus, bir şey hatırlamaya çalışıyorum!'

### 1318. `RD_ALifeLine_0bb7f51d338da100` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0101*
- **EN:** "It's gone now, it was important, it was my sister's phone number"
- **TR:** 'Uçtu gitti. Önemliydi ya, kardeşimin telefon numarasıydı.'

### 1319. `RD_ALifeLine_2855260e5776ede5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0101*
- **EN:** "It'll come back, don't force it"
- **TR:** 'Geri gelir, zorlama.'

### 1320. `RD_ALifeLine_41d6e96e49290f60` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0103*
- **EN:** 'Why does everything smell like feet?!'
- **TR:** 'Neden her yer ayak kokuyor ya?!'

### 1321. `RD_ALifeLine_5121c005747b62be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0103*
- **EN:** "It's all of us"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz kokuyoruz.'

### 1322. `RD_ALifeLine_13b5a1010b6b7106` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0103*
- **EN:** "It's all of us AND the house, the house smells like feet and a dead mouse"
- **TR:** 'Hem hepimiz HEM de ev! Ev ayak kokuyor, bir de ölü fare.'

### 1323. `RD_ALifeLine_531aab5e1ee3a4e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0103*
- **EN:** "It's mostly the dead mouse"
- **TR:** 'Asıl koku ölü fareden.'

### 1324. `RD_ALifeLine_0a30d9b31fe46985` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0105*
- **EN:** 'Swear to God I heard a car, an engine, a real one-- hold on'
- **TR:** 'Yemin ederim bir araba sesi duydum, motor sesi, hem de gerçek bir-- dur bir dakika.'

### 1325. `RD_ALifeLine_3cf37ca14c066d9c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0105*
- **EN:** "Gone, it's gone, swear to-- no, I mean, I'm sure"
- **TR:** 'Gitti, kayboldu, yemin ederim-- yok, yani... eminim.'

### 1326. `RD_ALifeLine_0e2a8dc57bafbfd2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0105*
- **EN:** "You said 'swear to God' twice, calm down"
- **TR:** 'İki kere "yemin ederim" dedin, sakin ol.'

### 1327. `RD_ALifeLine_31a3d4bc5bc83714` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0107*
- **EN:** "I keep patting my pocket for my phone, and then I remember, and it's like losing it all over again"
- **TR:** 'Telefonum için cebimi yoklayıp duruyorum, sonra aklıma geliyor, sanki bir daha kaybediyorum.'

### 1328. `RD_ALifeLine_12d5df3f7e300205` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0107*
- **EN:** 'Which phone?'
- **TR:** 'Hangi telefon?'

### 1329. `RD_ALifeLine_1faf31975b7a6812` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0107*
- **EN:** 'Any phone, the one in the kitchen, the one at work, all of them'
- **TR:** 'Hepsi. Mutfaktaki, işteki, hepsi.'

### 1330. `RD_ALifeLine_255e477527fc2f1e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0107*
- **EN:** 'Me too, every time'
- **TR:** 'Ben de, her seferinde.'

### 1331. `RD_ALifeLine_6b8af9616cfcae65` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0109*
- **EN:** "If I turn into one of those things I'm going to bite the guy who woke me up first"
- **TR:** 'Bunlardan birine dönüşürsem ısıracağım ilk kişi beni uyandıran olacak.'

### 1332. `RD_ALifeLine_633399c8394cdc99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0109*
- **EN:** "Starting with whoever's breathing like that"
- **TR:** 'Önce de şöyle nefes alanla başlarım.'

### 1333. `RD_ALifeLine_0d90196809828721` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0109*
- **EN:** "That's me, go ahead"
- **TR:** 'O benim, buyur.'

### 1334. `RD_ALifeLine_3cc53942645351e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0111*
- **EN:** "I wish I'd called more, you know? I wish I'd just called, just to say nothing"
- **TR:** 'Keşke daha çok arasaydım, bilirsin. Keşke sırf boş boş konuşmak için arasaydım.'

### 1335. `RD_ALifeLine_7aba4bb1322593b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0111*
- **EN:** 'We all wish that'
- **TR:** 'Hepimizin dileği bu.'

### 1336. `RD_ALifeLine_547a10ec2196162a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0111*
- **EN:** "I had nothing to say, and I'd say it for an hour, God"
- **TR:** 'Söyleyecek lafım olmazdı ama bir saat konuşurdum. Tanrım.'

### 1337. `RD_ALifeLine_786349f012675d1a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0111*
- **EN:** "I'd listen to your nothing"
- **TR:** 'Ben senin boş lafını dinlerdim.'

### 1338. `RD_ALifeLine_1c1914e075d98ef9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0113*
- **EN:** "I haven't had a cup of coffee in nine days and I think I can see through time"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz gündür kahve içmedim, zamanın içini görebiliyorum galiba.'

### 1339. `RD_ALifeLine_153e6a092876b0f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0113*
- **EN:** "What's it look like?"
- **TR:** 'Nasıl görünüyor?'

### 1340. `RD_ALifeLine_1cd912e3156f66e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0113*
- **EN:** 'Gray, mostly, and a bit of Tuesday'
- **TR:** 'Çoğu gri, bir de azıcık salı.'

### 1341. `RD_ALifeLine_5460f2c77f9bc789` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0113*
- **EN:** 'Get the man a cup of something'
- **TR:** 'Adama bir fincan bir şey ver artık.'

### 1342. `RD_ALifeLine_28971bb402e02c76` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0115*
- **EN:** "My hands smell like the bleach I used on the kitchen floor Friday, and I can't get it off"
- **TR:** 'Ellerim cuma günü mutfak zeminine döktüğüm çamaşır suyu kokuyor, bir türlü çıkmıyor.'

### 1343. `RD_ALifeLine_7d6521703213b1d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0115*
- **EN:** "I washed them, it doesn't go, it's like a-- like a stain on my brain"
- **TR:** 'Yıkadım, geçmiyor, sanki... beynime bir leke oturmuş gibi.'

### 1344. `RD_ALifeLine_4307544b3d74e7ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0117*
- **EN:** 'What do you do if you see one up close?'
- **TR:** 'Yakından bir tanesiyle karşılaşırsan ne yapacaksın?'

### 1345. `RD_ALifeLine_03b7fb7347d12c84` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0117*
- **EN:** "Don't look at it"
- **TR:** 'Ona bakma.'

### 1346. `RD_ALifeLine_3646a62f0a46f409` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0117*
- **EN:** "I'm asking what I DO"
- **TR:** 'Ne YAPACAĞIMI soruyorum!'

### 1347. `RD_ALifeLine_1792e3e3665136d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0117*
- **EN:** "Walk away, slow, and don't turn around"
- **TR:** 'Yavaşça uzaklaş, arkana da bakma.'

### 1348. `RD_ALifeLine_035135852e806cb8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0119*
- **EN:** "I'm just saying, thank you, for not leaving me"
- **TR:** 'Sadece teşekkür etmek istedim, beni bırakıp gitmediğin için.'

### 1349. `RD_ALifeLine_313b641844c0d1d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0119*
- **EN:** 'I know, but thank you, I wanted to say it before I lost my nerve'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum ama yine de sağ ol, cesaretimi kaybetmeden söylemek istedim.'

### 1350. `RD_ALifeLine_4bc7f2e629ebf95f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0119*
- **EN:** 'That was a lot of words for you'
- **TR:** 'Senin için epey uzun bir konuşmaydı bu.'

### 1351. `RD_ALifeLine_18c7250610e1d076` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0121*
- **EN:** 'I would give anything for a decent cup of black coffee and a Marlboro Light'
- **TR:** 'Güzel bir sade kahveyle bir Marlboro Light için her şeyimi veririm.'

### 1352. `RD_ALifeLine_5c05caca02ad7288` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0121*
- **EN:** 'And a newspaper'
- **TR:** 'Bir de gazete.'

### 1353. `RD_ALifeLine_591dfef318270661` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0121*
- **EN:** "And a newspaper, and my mother's meatloaf"
- **TR:** 'Bir gazete de, bir de annemin rulo köftesi.'

### 1354. `RD_ALifeLine_7625239713e5d04a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0121*
- **EN:** "You'd forgive her anything for that meatloaf"
- **TR:** 'O rulo köfte için annene her şeyi affederdin.'

### 1355. `RD_ALifeLine_7803b1267726bcd7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0123*
- **EN:** "I can't breathe in here, I need the door open, please, just a crack"
- **TR:** 'Burada nefes alamıyorum, kapı açılsın, lütfen, azıcık aralık yeter.'

### 1356. `RD_ALifeLine_45d5e72d670a74d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0123*
- **EN:** "You know we can't"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorsun olmaz.'

### 1357. `RD_ALifeLine_177e1f551298a3c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0123*
- **EN:** "Just a crack, I'm gonna pass out, I'm gonna-- okay, okay, I'm okay"
- **TR:** 'Azıcık aralık, bayılacağım, şimdi-- tamam, tamam, iyiyim.'

### 1358. `RD_ALifeLine_301e4ff1326465a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0125*
- **EN:** "We've been eating the same thing for eight days and I am BEGINNING to hate beans"
- **TR:** 'Sekiz gündür aynı şeyi yiyoruz ve fasulyeden nefret ETMEYE başlıyorum.'

### 1359. `RD_ALifeLine_440e8cb24d544ceb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0125*
- **EN:** 'Beginning?'
- **TR:** 'Şimdi mi başlıyorsun?'

### 1360. `RD_ALifeLine_23320679687373d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0125*
- **EN:** "I'm beginning to hate them in a new way, a more personal way"
- **TR:** 'Yeni bir şekilde nefret etmeye başlıyorum, daha kişisel bir şekilde.'

### 1361. `RD_ALifeLine_59746bbe1030225f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0125*
- **EN:** 'They hate you back'
- **TR:** 'Onlar da senden nefret ediyor.'

### 1362. `RD_ALifeLine_3f0442583c6994b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0127*
- **EN:** 'There was a lady at the Piggly Wiggly who always gave me the extra coupon, and I never thanked her'
- **TR:** "Piggly Wiggly'de hep bana fazladan kupon veren bir kadın vardı, hiç teşekkür etmedim."

### 1363. `RD_ALifeLine_31127c63659fd2f3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0127*
- **EN:** 'The one with the red glasses?'
- **TR:** 'Kırmızı gözlüklü olan mı?'

### 1364. `RD_ALifeLine_5c6f74bd4618c67a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0127*
- **EN:** 'The red glasses, yes, and I never even learned her name'
- **TR:** 'Kırmızı gözlüklü, evet, adını bile öğrenmedim.'

### 1365. `RD_ALifeLine_3f40885477a0808b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0127*
- **EN:** 'Her name was Dolores'
- **TR:** "Adı Dolores'ti."

### 1366. `RD_ALifeLine_601d37c40ab84b25` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0129*
- **EN:** 'I thought I saw somebody in the window, I did-- no, I mean, I thought, it was probably the curtain'
- **TR:** 'Pencerede biri var sandım, gerçekten-- yok, yani sandım, herhalde perdeydi.'

### 1367. `RD_ALifeLine_3f4febbe46f7b491` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0129*
- **EN:** "It's a curtain"
- **TR:** 'Perde o.'

### 1368. `RD_ALifeLine_7375740668206813` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0129*
- **EN:** 'It had a hat'
- **TR:** 'Şapkası vardı.'

### 1369. `RD_ALifeLine_20e79e1d74fc869c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0129*
- **EN:** "Don't look at the window anymore"
- **TR:** 'Artık pencereye bakma.'

### 1370. `RD_ALifeLine_0013e7906ad631e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0131*
- **EN:** "You know what? I like you, I didn't think I would, but I do"
- **TR:** 'Bak ne diyeceğim, seni sevdim. Seveceğimi sanmazdım ama sevdim.'

### 1371. `RD_ALifeLine_1e896f1a4053d7a0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0131*
- **EN:** "That's the nicest thing anybody said to me this week"
- **TR:** 'Bu hafta bana söylenen en güzel söz bu.'

### 1372. `RD_ALifeLine_28d098320956db01` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0131*
- **EN:** "Don't let it go to your head"
- **TR:** 'Havalara girme sakın.'

### 1373. `RD_ALifeLine_592710a3781c6a0a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0133*
- **EN:** "You said you'd teach me gin rummy"
- **TR:** 'Bana gin rummy öğretecektin.'

### 1374. `RD_ALifeLine_62f739ce53df3768` — Claude (yeni)
