*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0133*
- **EN:** "I'm teaching you"
- **TR:** 'Öğretiyorum ya.'

### 1375. `RD_ALifeLine_690e71372a09155e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0133*
- **EN:** "You're winning, that's not teaching"
- **TR:** 'Sen kazanıyorsun, bu öğretmek değil.'

### 1376. `RD_ALifeLine_2c6402c02fd64546` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0133*
- **EN:** "That's how I learned"
- **TR:** 'Ben böyle öğrendim.'

### 1377. `RD_ALifeLine_16931c26444a2981` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0133*
- **EN:** "That's how everybody learned"
- **TR:** 'Herkes böyle öğrendi.'

### 1378. `RD_ALifeLine_136edd5e67ec59d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0133*
- **EN:** "Quit whining, both of you, I'm trying to hear the road"
- **TR:** 'Sızlanmayı kesin, ikiniz de, yolu dinlemeye çalışıyorum.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1379. `RD_ALifeLine_2195a1837414885f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0134*
- **EN:** 'Whose deal is it?'
- **TR:** 'Dağıtma sırası kimde?'

### 1380. `RD_ALifeLine_0a3c0b2a1d68b37b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0134*
- **EN:** 'I dealt last'
- **TR:** 'En son ben dağıttım.'

### 1381. `RD_ALifeLine_1381fe620e80e171` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0134*
- **EN:** "Nobody's dealt in an hour, you're both stalling"
- **TR:** 'Bir saattir kimse dağıtmadı, ikiniz de oyalanıyorsunuz.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1382. `RD_ALifeLine_227eaff00f7f5fca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0134*
- **EN:** 'Nothing out there, if anybody cares'
- **TR:** 'Dışarıda bir şey yok, ilgilenen varsa.'

### 1383. `RD_ALifeLine_1f42c85b59d46e1f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0135*
- **EN:** 'Would you trade socks for a cigarette?'
- **TR:** 'Çorabı sigarayla takas eder misin?'

### 1384. `RD_ALifeLine_23e87af206492af0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0135*
- **EN:** 'What kind of cigarette?'
- **TR:** 'Ne sigarası?'

### 1385. `RD_ALifeLine_723a60e903da73f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0135*
- **EN:** 'Any kind, apparently'
- **TR:** 'Fark etmez, anlaşılan.'

### 1386. `RD_ALifeLine_35ecb5083eb9a07e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0135*
- **EN:** 'Not if the socks smelled like a barn'
- **TR:** 'Çoraplar ahır gibi kokuyorsa olmaz.'

### 1387. `RD_ALifeLine_1710cf02755eac54` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0135*
- **EN:** "My socks don't smell like a barn"
- **TR:** 'Benim çoraplar ahır gibi kokmuyor.'

### 1388. `RD_ALifeLine_0ce4a42264ba638e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0135*
- **EN:** 'No. Yours smell like a barn that burned down'
- **TR:** 'Hayır. Seninkiler yanmış ahır gibi kokuyor.'

### 1389. `RD_ALifeLine_68d883002fae6aad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0136*
- **EN:** "Where's Dale?"
- **TR:** 'Dale nerede?'

### 1390. `RD_ALifeLine_74a9bbe20511aef8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0136*
- **EN:** 'Asleep, in the back'
- **TR:** 'Uyuyor, arkada.'

### 1391. `RD_ALifeLine_7a6297e771c5de5b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0136*
- **EN:** "He's been asleep since noon"
- **TR:** 'Öğleden beri uyuyor.'

### 1392. `RD_ALifeLine_7de577301b5d5a61` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0136*
- **EN:** "Leave him, he didn't sleep Saturday either"
- **TR:** 'Bırak, cumartesi de uyumadı.'

### 1393. `RD_ALifeLine_57aea0676cf487dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0136*
- **EN:** "He doesn't sleep, he lies there with his eyes open"
- **TR:** 'Uyumuyor ki, gözleri açık yatıyor.'

### 1394. `RD_ALifeLine_37913ed01a989ea1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0137*
- **EN:** 'Eleven cans of beans'
- **TR:** 'On bir kutu fasulye.'

### 1395. `RD_ALifeLine_2227fff66a980d00` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0137*
- **EN:** 'I count nine'
- **TR:** 'Ben dokuz sayıyorum.'

### 1396. `RD_ALifeLine_65c6641845fa6c5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0137*
- **EN:** 'Eleven, I counted twice'
- **TR:** 'On bir, iki kere saydım.'

### 1397. `RD_ALifeLine_5435da56071f2729` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0137*
- **EN:** "It's nine and two empties"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz tane, bir de iki boş.'

### 1398. `RD_ALifeLine_66926e45358e716b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0137*
- **EN:** 'Nine, I held every one'
- **TR:** 'Dokuz, hepsini elimde tuttum.'

### 1399. `RD_ALifeLine_66efd9ac5f3a182b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0138*
- **EN:** 'Move the dial a hair to the left'
- **TR:** 'Kadranı azıcık sola çevir.'

### 1400. `RD_ALifeLine_4e09a58864397a1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0138*
- **EN:** 'This way?'
- **TR:** 'Bu tarafa mı?'

### 1401. `RD_ALifeLine_612120661311070f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0138*
- **EN:** 'The other left'
- **TR:** 'Öbür sola.'

### 1402. `RD_ALifeLine_42ee7ce50308f819` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0138*
- **EN:** "You've got it, you've got a station"
- **TR:** 'Oldu, bir istasyon yakaladın.'

### 1403. `RD_ALifeLine_6ba55c424ca5fb5c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0138*
- **EN:** "That's a preacher, go back, go back"
- **TR:** 'O bir vaiz, geri dön, geri dön.'

### 1404. `RD_ALifeLine_6a99979f7682c513` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0139*
- **EN:** "What's that song"
- **TR:** 'Bu hangi şarkıydı?'

### 1405. `RD_ALifeLine_4b6e672f7b746641` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0139*
- **EN:** "I don't remember the words"
- **TR:** 'Sözlerini hatırlamıyorum.'

### 1406. `RD_ALifeLine_56fd0c227e23f1ec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0139*
- **EN:** 'It goes like, mm, mm, mm-mm-mm'
- **TR:** 'Şöyle gidiyor, mm, mm, mm-mm-mm.'

### 1407. `RD_ALifeLine_782894be69b35597` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0139*
- **EN:** 'Oh, that one, the one about the road'
- **TR:** 'A, o, yolla ilgili olan.'

### 1408. `RD_ALifeLine_117690e556fbbcfc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0139*
- **EN:** '...mm-mm-mm, I know it, keep going'
- **TR:** '...mm-mm-mm, biliyorum, devam et.'

### 1409. `RD_ALifeLine_2de1ab3a31d5267d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0140*
- **EN:** "Wind doesn't bang"
- **TR:** 'Rüzgar şangırdamaz ki.'

### 1410. `RD_ALifeLine_48645e3622110af7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0140*
- **EN:** 'It was a pot lid, I dropped it'
- **TR:** 'Tencere kapağıydı, ben düşürdüm.'

### 1411. `RD_ALifeLine_32159aa54870164d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0140*
- **EN:** "Quiet back there, I'm trying to listen"
- **TR:** 'Arkadakiler sussun, dinlemeye çalışıyorum.'

### 1412. `RD_ALifeLine_5a22166e23fd528f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0141*
- **EN:** 'Say something'
- **TR:** 'Bir şey söyle.'

### 1413. `RD_ALifeLine_3c7976867bf2f0d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0141*
- **EN:** "Leave 'em alone"
- **TR:** 'Bırak onları.'

### 1414. `RD_ALifeLine_2644b342441b0f6a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0141*
- **EN:** "They haven't said a word since noon"
- **TR:** 'Öğleden beri tek kelime etmediler.'

### 1415. `RD_ALifeLine_2305d0a40bc275cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0141*
- **EN:** "I bet five cans they won't talk"
- **TR:** 'Beş kutuya bahse girerim konuşmazlar.'

### 1416. `RD_ALifeLine_3d6a3e761f690034` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0141*
- **EN:** '...fine, hi'
- **TR:** '...Tamam, merhaba.'

### 1417. `RD_ALifeLine_64f67973153aa110` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0142*
- **EN:** 'My mother could make one cracker feel like supper'
- **TR:** 'Annem tek bir krakerle bile akşam yemeği havası yaratırdı.'

### 1418. `RD_ALifeLine_0fba4c847eaf3441` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0142*
- **EN:** "How'd she do that?"
- **TR:** 'Nasıl yapardı?'

### 1419. `RD_ALifeLine_097a7e182793a83b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0142*
- **EN:** "A little peanut butter, and she'd say she'd made it just for you"
- **TR:** 'Azıcık fıstık ezmesi sürerdi, bir de sırf senin için yaptım derdi.'

### 1420. `RD_ALifeLine_5b432dd2237c6ed8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0142*
- **EN:** "I'd have stared at it all morning"
- **TR:** 'Sabaha kadar ona bakardım.'

### 1421. `RD_ALifeLine_799d9a617448149d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0142*
- **EN:** "It would have been the nicest cracker you'd ever seen"
- **TR:** 'O, hayatında gördüğün en güzel kraker olurdu.'

### 1422. `RD_ALifeLine_2876912d75e13837` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0143*
- **EN:** 'Anybody remember a phone number by heart'
- **TR:** 'Telefon numarasını ezbere bilen var mı?'

### 1423. `RD_ALifeLine_0f932cb042c69e7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0143*
- **EN:** "My mother's"
- **TR:** 'Ben annemin numarasını biliyorum.'

### 1424. `RD_ALifeLine_50997674634a0cc2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0143*
- **EN:** 'Say it'
- **TR:** 'Söyle.'

### 1425. `RD_ALifeLine_4389d9ff6f6e104c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0143*
- **EN:** "Say it, I'll remember it for you"
- **TR:** 'Söyle, ben senin yerine aklımda tutarım.'

### 1426. `RD_ALifeLine_2de0c7aa7e99bf07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0143*
- **EN:** 'Five oh two, five five five'
- **TR:** 'Beş sıfır iki, beş beş beş.'

### 1427. `RD_ALifeLine_3426a85757072050` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0143*
- **EN:** '...zero one four three...'
- **TR:** '...sıfır bir dört üç...'

### 1428. `RD_ALifeLine_05545a29215fbac1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0144*
- **EN:** 'Who left the lantern on'
- **TR:** 'Feneri kim açık bıraktı?'

### 1429. `RD_ALifeLine_1c7d5c69144c3b3a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0144*
- **EN:** "It's the only light we've got"
- **TR:** 'Elimizdeki tek ışık o.'

### 1430. `RD_ALifeLine_17473f1c3f89ed2e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0144*
- **EN:** "It's gonna run out, we need to save it"
- **TR:** 'Bitecek, biraz tasarruf etmeliyiz.'

### 1431. `RD_ALifeLine_55943458467e13e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0144*
- **EN:** 'Then turn it off'
- **TR:** 'O zaman kapat.'

### 1432. `RD_ALifeLine_0aa5ca632473591a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0144*
- **EN:** "Turn it off, I can't see the front"
- **TR:** 'Kapat, ön tarafı göremiyorum.'

### 1433. `RD_ALifeLine_5cb0ca520a9c3725` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0145*
- **EN:** 'How long do you think this is gonna last?'
- **TR:** 'Sence bu iş ne kadar sürecek?'

### 1434. `RD_ALifeLine_698ee33d00d7d326` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0145*
- **EN:** 'Not long'
- **TR:** 'Uzun sürmez.'

### 1435. `RD_ALifeLine_29487e8831b23272` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0145*
- **EN:** "That's what you said Saturday"
- **TR:** 'Cumartesi de bunu demiştin.'

### 1436. `RD_ALifeLine_685d9497274e8eac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0145*
- **EN:** 'Longer than Saturday'
- **TR:** 'Cumartesiden uzun sürer.'

### 1437. `RD_ALifeLine_2c11fef4697304c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0145*
- **EN:** 'Longer than us...'
- **TR:** 'Bizden uzun sürer...'

### 1438. `RD_ALifeLine_53f0f470788c761a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0146*
- **EN:** "I'm going to sleep"
- **TR:** 'Ben uyuyacağım.'

### 1439. `RD_ALifeLine_70bb224b4384b8ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0146*
- **EN:** "It's three in the afternoon"
- **TR:** 'Öğleden sonra üç be.'

### 1440. `RD_ALifeLine_58af0da571b8d2e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0146*
- **EN:** "It's the best time to sleep, it's the hottest"
- **TR:** 'Uyumanın en iyi vakti, en sıcak saat.'

### 1441. `RD_ALifeLine_5e8bd37160dd1ed2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0146*
- **EN:** "Sleep where it's cool, on the floor"
- **TR:** 'Serin yerde uyu, yerde.'

### 1442. `RD_ALifeLine_51e5396e507864e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0146*
- **EN:** "I'm already on the floor, I've been there since noon"
- **TR:** 'Ben zaten yerdeyim, öğleden beri buradayım.'

### 1443. `RD_ALifeLine_59816bec128b8ffb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0147*
- **EN:** "You're all right, you know that"
- **TR:** 'Sen iyi birisin, biliyor musun.'

### 1444. `RD_ALifeLine_25c2536f01f7e938` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0147*
- **EN:** "You're getting a little sappy"
- **TR:** 'Sen biraz duygusallaşmaya başladın.'

### 1445. `RD_ALifeLine_3cabe2555766ac7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0147*
- **EN:** "We're all a little sappy, it's the heat"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz biraz duygusallaştık, bu sıcaktan.'

### 1446. `RD_ALifeLine_11fbb77765ccee45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0148*
- **EN:** 'Do you remember what we were doing this time last week?'
- **TR:** 'Geçen hafta bu saatlerde ne yapıyorduk, hatırlıyor musun?'

### 1447. `RD_ALifeLine_3803e562721aa93a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0148*
- **EN:** 'Working'
- **TR:** 'Çalışıyorduk.'

### 1448. `RD_ALifeLine_1f34b8f3116db7ef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0148*
- **EN:** 'I was at the lake'
- **TR:** 'Ben gölde idim.'

### 1449. `RD_ALifeLine_5a09f9ce301828c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0148*
- **EN:** 'I was at a baby shower'
- **TR:** 'Ben bir bebek partisindeydim.'

### 1450. `RD_ALifeLine_6654cf7068a32adc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0148*
- **EN:** 'I was at home, in a chair, reading the paper'
- **TR:** 'Ben evdeydim, koltukta, gazete okuyordum.'

### 1451. `RD_ALifeLine_6d11d04330e1d62b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0149*
- **EN:** "Light's going"
- **TR:** 'Işık gidiyor.'

### 1452. `RD_ALifeLine_5fee5f932ab6f62e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0149*
- **EN:** 'Orange, then gray'
- **TR:** 'Turuncu, sonra gri.'

### 1453. `RD_ALifeLine_5cc5c16b03c1b1a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0149*
- **EN:** "We should eat before it's dark"
- **TR:** 'Hava kararmadan yemek yiyelim.'

### 1454. `RD_ALifeLine_4ba7da7206b4fc10` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0149*
- **EN:** "I'll bring the cans"
- **TR:** 'Ben kutuları getiririm.'

### 1455. `RD_ALifeLine_67bec10526fbdb66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0150*
- **EN:** "My heel's bleeding through the sock"
- **TR:** 'Topuğum çorabın içinden kanıyor.'

### 1456. `RD_ALifeLine_63f9460e6910b3b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0150*
- **EN:** 'Try the other station'
- **TR:** 'Öbür istasyonu dene.'

### 1457. `RD_ALifeLine_6f9641d768c25462` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0150*
- **EN:** 'Take the sock off, let me see'
- **TR:** 'Çorabı çıkar, bir bakayım.'

### 1458. `RD_ALifeLine_425327dc79d380b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0150*
- **EN:** 'Static, static, static'
- **TR:** 'Cızırtı, cızırtı, cızırtı.'

### 1459. `RD_ALifeLine_3d972f6d08c6348e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0150*
- **EN:** "It's a blister the size of a quarter"
- **TR:** 'Madeni para büyüklüğünde bir su toplaması.'

### 1460. `RD_ALifeLine_027bbb214abe8c66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0150*
- **EN:** "Wait, wait, there's a voice"
- **TR:** 'Bekle, bekle, bir ses var.'

### 1461. `RD_ALifeLine_2d4ff47f03a242a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0151*
- **EN:** 'Pass the beans'
- **TR:** 'Fasulyeyi uzat.'

### 1462. `RD_ALifeLine_08a4fc3d11bca3f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0151*
- **EN:** "I'm trying to sleep, you two"
- **TR:** 'Uyumaya çalışıyorum ya, siz ikiniz de sussanıza.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1463. `RD_ALifeLine_20be488534f6ab7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0151*
- **EN:** "They're right there, by your elbow"
- **TR:** 'Hemen orada, dirseğinin dibinde.'

### 1464. `RD_ALifeLine_66f89ec93c4553ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0151*
- **EN:** "Then sleep, nobody's stopping you"
- **TR:** 'Uyu o zaman, kimse engellemiyor.'

### 1465. `RD_ALifeLine_33de4232216dc472` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0151*
- **EN:** "I'm not reaching, you reach"
- **TR:** 'Ben uzanmam, sen uzan.'

### 1466. `RD_ALifeLine_1f5592031add3bcb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0151*
- **EN:** "It's a house, nothing is far"
- **TR:** 'Ev bu, hiçbir şey uzak değil.'

### 1467. `RD_ALifeLine_10baf5b00927f13c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0152*
- **EN:** 'Read the one about the sparrow'
- **TR:** 'Serçeyle ilgili olanı oku.'

### 1468. `RD_ALifeLine_0923bd72326adf4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0152*
- **EN:** "You got the dog's water yet"
- **TR:** 'Köpeğin suyunu koydun mu?'

### 1469. `RD_ALifeLine_17f3078e58f96146` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0152*
- **EN:** "It's in the bowl, don't fuss"
- **TR:** 'Kasede, abartma.'

### 1470. `RD_ALifeLine_0103d5362a03aac9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0152*
- **EN:** 'Matthew, six, the sparrow'
- **TR:** 'Matta, altı, serçeler.'

### 1471. `RD_ALifeLine_7083dbdb7b8f14e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0152*
- **EN:** "Fine, I'll check it again"
- **TR:** 'Tamam, bir daha bakayım.'

### 1472. `RD_ALifeLine_2970250a04481525` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0153*
- **EN:** 'Mama used to do this with a wooden spoon'
- **TR:** 'Annem bunu tahta kaşıkla yapardı.'

### 1473. `RD_ALifeLine_6fa040c20247d92f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0153*
- **EN:** "It's gonna be dark in an hour"
- **TR:** 'Bir saate hava kararır.'

### 1474. `RD_ALifeLine_7bac185c6b03144c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0153*
- **EN:** 'Do what'
- **TR:** 'Neyi yapardı?'

### 1475. `RD_ALifeLine_183bd1d01e98a33e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0153*
- **EN:** "I know, I'll get the lantern"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, feneri getireyim.'

### 1476. `RD_ALifeLine_1206d62b4f457cc5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0153*
- **EN:** "Stir it, she'd stir it for twenty minutes, till it turned"
- **TR:** 'Karıştırırdı, yirmi dakika karıştırırdı, kıvam alana kadar.'

### 1477. `RD_ALifeLine_7472a58f6d821341` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0153*
- **EN:** "Don't light it yet, save the oil"
- **TR:** 'Daha yakma, yağı idareli kullan.'

### 1478. `RD_ALifeLine_59d5bf861fdbd560` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0154*
- **EN:** 'Your turn, draw'
- **TR:** 'Sıra sende, çek.'

### 1479. `RD_ALifeLine_33d832012539c053` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0154*
- **EN:** 'I count nine cans, not eleven'
- **TR:** 'Ben dokuz kutu sayıyorum, on bir değil.'

### 1480. `RD_ALifeLine_55ba39ce380a7c80` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0154*
- **EN:** "Hold on, I'm thinking"
- **TR:** 'Dur, düşünüyorum.'

### 1481. `RD_ALifeLine_46c9565f0c527ae5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0154*
- **EN:** "Count again, you're counting the empties"
- **TR:** 'Bir daha say, boşları da sayıyorsun.'

### 1482. `RD_ALifeLine_643d053743c491ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0154*
- **EN:** "You've been thinking for five minutes"
- **TR:** 'Beş dakikadır düşünüyorsun.'

### 1483. `RD_ALifeLine_5d850a53577645bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0154*
- **EN:** "I'm not counting the empties"
- **TR:** 'Boşları saymıyorum.'

### 1484. `RD_ALifeLine_41a273a86635786c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0155*
- **EN:** "That's not what I said"
- **TR:** 'Ben onu demedim.'

### 1485. `RD_ALifeLine_4928041f13ae64c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0155*
- **EN:** 'Who ate my last cracker'
- **TR:** 'Son krakerimi kim yedi?'

### 1486. `RD_ALifeLine_6dfacf4252be8e67` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0155*
- **EN:** "It's exactly what you said"
- **TR:** 'Aynen onu dedin.'

### 1487. `RD_ALifeLine_79187ee1029c1db5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0155*
- **EN:** 'Nobody, you probably dropped it'
- **TR:** 'Kimse, herhalde düşürdün.'

### 1488. `RD_ALifeLine_50a0e47a65a1dd0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0155*
- **EN:** "I said I'd think about it"
- **TR:** 'Düşünürüm dedim.'

### 1489. `RD_ALifeLine_693280ad3ae955c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0155*
- **EN:** 'I dropped it? I dropped it where'
- **TR:** 'Ben mi düşürdüm? Nereye düşürmüşüm?'

### 1490. `RD_ALifeLine_624dd44848deb91e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0156*
- **EN:** 'Tell me again how to boil water'
- **TR:** 'Bir daha anlat, su nasıl kaynatılır.'

### 1491. `RD_ALifeLine_14de0a8233d0d919` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0156*
- **EN:** 'I said you need a bigger pot'
- **TR:** 'Daha büyük bir tencere lazım demiştim.'

### 1492. `RD_ALifeLine_739b3011398a58f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0156*
- **EN:** 'Put it on the camp burner, wait for bubbles'
- **TR:** 'Kamp ocağına koy, kabarcık çıkmasını bekle.'

### 1493. `RD_ALifeLine_11b14ffa3bd33d66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0156*
- **EN:** 'This is the biggest pot we have'
- **TR:** 'Bu elimizdeki en büyük tencere.'

### 1494. `RD_ALifeLine_09e69aa37ad7e678` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0156*
- **EN:** "Then it's the biggest pot we've got, quit saying bigger"
- **TR:** 'O zaman elimizdeki en büyük bu, "daha büyük" deyip durma.'

### 1495. `RD_ALifeLine_731986786df889d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0157*
- **EN:** 'I can hear your stomach from here'
- **TR:** 'Midenin sesini buradan duyuyorum.'

### 1496. `RD_ALifeLine_3db811a248cdf7a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0157*
- **EN:** "How long's the batteries last on that thing"
- **TR:** 'Şu şeyin pilleri ne kadar dayanır?'

### 1497. `RD_ALifeLine_29949f4101a08d98` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0157*
- **EN:** "It's the beans"
- **TR:** 'Fasulyenin sesi o.'

### 1498. `RD_ALifeLine_30b0ea1629b35f13` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0157*
- **EN:** 'About six hours, if you stop checking'
- **TR:** 'Yaklaşık altı saat, bakıp durmazsan.'

### 1499. `RD_ALifeLine_195339b849187b3c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0157*
- **EN:** "It's me being hungry, the beans are innocent"
- **TR:** 'Ben açım, fasulyenin suçu yok.'

### 1500. `RD_ALifeLine_3303f3663ae3dcb5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0157*
- **EN:** "Then I'll stop checking"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bakmayı bırakırım.'

### 1501. `RD_ALifeLine_6d955ee334cf7151` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0158*
- **EN:** 'My father used to carry a pocket watch'
- **TR:** 'Babam cep saati taşırdı.'

### 1502. `RD_ALifeLine_6fff875c3cd78f3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0158*
- **EN:** 'The kind you wind?'
- **TR:** 'Kurmalı olandan mı?'

### 1503. `RD_ALifeLine_3ffb4c1e1d8c10b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0158*
- **EN:** 'Mine had one too. Never trusted a battery'
- **TR:** 'Benimkinde de vardı. Pile hiç güvenmezdi.'

### 1504. `RD_ALifeLine_05f3c370291c4c1d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0158*
- **EN:** 'Sounds like my father'
- **TR:** 'Babama benziyor.'

### 1505. `RD_ALifeLine_47dc34906ce2a557` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0158*
- **EN:** "He'd wind it every morning at seven"
- **TR:** 'Her sabah yedide kurardı.'

### 1506. `RD_ALifeLine_3584725d63d5f8bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0158*
- **EN:** 'A little thing to set the day by'
- **TR:** 'Günü ona göre ayarlamak için ufak bir şey.'

### 1507. `RD_ALifeLine_6c35cc4868e02e33` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0159*
- **EN:** "That's the third time you've said sorry"
- **TR:** 'Bu üçüncü özür dileyişin.'

### 1508. `RD_ALifeLine_43e83efc3740ab8a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0159*
- **EN:** 'Are we out of matches'
- **TR:** 'Kibritimiz bitti mi?'

### 1509. `RD_ALifeLine_2bec9a0619202e0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0159*
- **EN:** "I'm just sorry"
- **TR:** 'Sadece üzgünüm.'

### 1510. `RD_ALifeLine_405715f01ebc2149` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0159*
- **EN:** "Two left, don't ask"
- **TR:** 'İki tane kaldı, sorma.'

### 1511. `RD_ALifeLine_5f7399406ae52419` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0159*
- **EN:** "You've got nothing to be sorry about"
- **TR:** 'Üzülecek bir şeyin yok.'

### 1512. `RD_ALifeLine_22897e2b65a1139b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0159*
- **EN:** "Then I won't ask"
- **TR:** 'O zaman sormam.'

### 1513. `RD_ALifeLine_7ca397c91487de4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0160*
- **EN:** 'Did you hear what I said about your boots'
- **TR:** 'Botların hakkında dediğimi duydun mu?'

### 1514. `RD_ALifeLine_2a6dc0c7713c5fa0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0160*
- **EN:** "There's a bat in here"
- **TR:** 'Burada bir yarasa var.'

### 1515. `RD_ALifeLine_430819f1140c4b46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0160*
- **EN:** 'What about my boots'
- **TR:** 'Botlarım ne olmuş?'

### 1516. `RD_ALifeLine_51ab41423833dca3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0160*
- **EN:** "A bat? That's a moth"
- **TR:** 'Yarasa mı? O bir güve.'

### 1517. `RD_ALifeLine_6c8014ba6b629cd0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0160*
- **EN:** 'They smell like a wet dog'
- **TR:** 'Islak köpek gibi kokuyorlar.'

### 1518. `RD_ALifeLine_01be9b875108e4d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0160*
- **EN:** "It's a big moth"
- **TR:** 'Büyük bir güve.'

### 1519. `RD_ALifeLine_69b5d56a30210d6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0161*
- **EN:** 'I wish I could call my sister'
- **TR:** 'Keşke kardeşimi arayabilsem.'

### 1520. `RD_ALifeLine_274e7aed5d509103` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0161*
- **EN:** "There's a light across the street"
- **TR:** 'Karşıda bir ışık var.'

### 1521. `RD_ALifeLine_433ea4231744c97c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0161*
- **EN:** 'Do you know her number'
- **TR:** 'Numarasını biliyor musun?'

### 1522. `RD_ALifeLine_114c88ea3e1b74c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0161*
- **EN:** 'By heart, since I was six'
- **TR:** 'Ezbere, altı yaşımdan beri.'

### 1523. `RD_ALifeLine_0ee6767540de0f44` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0161*
- **EN:** "The one with the green door, it's on again"
- **TR:** 'Yeşil kapılı olan, yine açık.'

### 1524. `RD_ALifeLine_40b0099175cc25de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0161*
- **EN:** "Say it out loud, you'll remember it"
- **TR:** 'Yüksek sesle söyle, hatırlarsın.'

### 1525. `RD_ALifeLine_1a5d630014bdaac5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0162*
- **EN:** "Don't tell me how to play"
- **TR:** 'Bana nasıl oynayacağımı söyleme.'

### 1526. `RD_ALifeLine_1728f5647f21f73c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0162*
- **EN:** "Your breathing's too loud"
- **TR:** 'Nefesin çok yüksek.'

### 1527. `RD_ALifeLine_1336b97169eeb961` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0162*
- **EN:** "I'm not telling, I'm suggesting"
- **TR:** 'Söylemiyorum, öneriyorum.'

### 1528. `RD_ALifeLine_0fdddbdd33003f57` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0162*
- **EN:** "I'm not breathing loud"
- **TR:** 'Yüksek sesle nefes almıyorum.'

### 1529. `RD_ALifeLine_5dc2acfb341cb73a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0162*
- **EN:** "You're suggesting I'm stupid"
- **TR:** 'Aptal olduğumu ima ediyorsun.'

### 1530. `RD_ALifeLine_1afabaac19c176fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0162*
- **EN:** "Then stop, I can't hear"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kes, duyamıyorum.'

### 1531. `RD_ALifeLine_62ca9de2122fbc52` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0163*
- **EN:** "You should've seen the house, before"
- **TR:** 'Evi öncesinde görmeliydin.'

### 1532. `RD_ALifeLine_05fee41e73b54c4f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0163*
- **EN:** 'It was a nice house'
- **TR:** 'Güzel bir evdi.'

### 1533. `RD_ALifeLine_39d099e21c099700` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0163*
- **EN:** "I'd like to see a picture"
- **TR:** 'Bir fotoğrafını görmek isterim.'

### 1534. `RD_ALifeLine_58002de6726fc261` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0163*
- **EN:** 'It had a porch swing, and a bird feeder'
- **TR:** 'Ön sundurmada salıncak vardı, bir de kuş yemliği.'

### 1535. `RD_ALifeLine_766b44646285f5ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0163*
- **EN:** "I think there's one in my wallet"
- **TR:** 'Cüzdanımda bir tane olduğunu sanıyorum.'

### 1536. `RD_ALifeLine_620613184cd01c1d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0163*
- **EN:** 'And a dog, a cocker spaniel'
- **TR:** 'Bir de köpek, bir cocker spaniel.'

### 1537. `RD_ALifeLine_1a64661c7ac98a46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0164*
- **EN:** "I'm gonna learn to whistle"
- **TR:** 'Islık çalmayı öğreneceğim.'

### 1538. `RD_ALifeLine_588b694b62bc503c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0164*
- **EN:** 'Check the back door'
- **TR:** 'Arka kapıyı kontrol et.'

### 1539. `RD_ALifeLine_27aaa6bf6dad84d7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0164*
- **EN:** "You've been saying that for ten years"
- **TR:** 'On yıldır bunu söylüyorsun.'

### 1540. `RD_ALifeLine_61e04fb456c5f6a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0164*
- **EN:** "It's checked, it's locked"
- **TR:** 'Kontrol edildi, kilitli.'

### 1541. `RD_ALifeLine_19dcc3ae1df395cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0164*
- **EN:** "It's not whistling if there's no air"
- **TR:** 'Hava olmadan ıslık olmuyor ki.'

### 1542. `RD_ALifeLine_46b08adf4ee56ad4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0164*
- **EN:** 'Then check it again'
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir daha kontrol et.'

### 1543. `RD_ALifeLine_1891a65350720573` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0165*
- **EN:** 'Do you think it hurts, you know, after'
- **TR:** 'Sence acıtıyor mu, bilirsin, sonrasında?'

### 1544. `RD_ALifeLine_08c5a89f5ed0ec87` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0165*
- **EN:** "I can't see anybody out there"
- **TR:** 'Dışarıda kimseyi göremiyorum.'

### 1545. `RD_ALifeLine_77c4d1d06cab894e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0165*
- **EN:** "I don't think about it"
- **TR:** 'Ben düşünmüyorum.'

### 1546. `RD_ALifeLine_3c59db6f1db4e640` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0165*
- **EN:** "There's nobody, we're good"
- **TR:** 'Kimse yok, rahatız.'

### 1547. `RD_ALifeLine_300ea07154e08629` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0165*
- **EN:** 'I think about it every night'
- **TR:** 'Ben her gece düşünüyorum.'

### 1548. `RD_ALifeLine_1db03f7b685cd045` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0165*
- **EN:** 'Then think about something else'
- **TR:** 'O zaman başka bir şey düşün.'

### 1549. `RD_ALifeLine_6661e418359ebb88` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0166*
- **EN:** 'Left on the map, then straight'
- **TR:** 'Haritada sola, sonra düz.'

### 1550. `RD_ALifeLine_1742acc74536abce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0166*
- **EN:** 'Anybody hear that?'
- **TR:** 'Bunu duyan var mı?'

### 1551. `RD_ALifeLine_627b649f5333f2e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0166*
- **EN:** 'Left at the church'
- **TR:** 'Kilisede sola.'

### 1552. `RD_ALifeLine_2947c8210a35ccfd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0166*
- **EN:** 'Hear what'
- **TR:** 'Neyi duymak?'

### 1553. `RD_ALifeLine_10c5338428813e38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0166*
- **EN:** 'And then the ridge, past the ridge'
- **TR:** 'Sonra tepe, tepeyi geç.'

### 1554. `RD_ALifeLine_648be41e64b7f8ab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0166*
- **EN:** "Left at the church, then straight, I've got it"
- **TR:** 'Kilisede sola, sonra düz, anladım.'

### 1555. `RD_ALifeLine_3a11fb596b8891f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0167*
- **EN:** 'Quit looking at my cards'
- **TR:** 'Kartlarıma bakmayı kes.'

### 1556. `RD_ALifeLine_3bb8f5344e0480b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0167*
- **EN:** 'What are you reading'
- **TR:** 'Ne okuyorsun?'

### 1557. `RD_ALifeLine_152114390abc902b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0167*
- **EN:** "I'm looking at the ceiling"
- **TR:** 'Tavana bakıyorum.'

### 1558. `RD_ALifeLine_03f1578b22ce6d35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0167*
- **EN:** "The back of this can, it's got a recipe"
- **TR:** 'Şu kutunun arkası, tarif var.'

### 1559. `RD_ALifeLine_429b2bbe79aa4280` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0167*
- **EN:** "You've been looking at the ceiling right over my cards"
- **TR:** 'Tam kartlarımın üstünden tavana bakıyorsun.'

### 1560. `RD_ALifeLine_1ef0163a0881c97f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0167*
- **EN:** 'For what, canned beans'
- **TR:** 'Ne diye? Konserve fasulye için mi?'

### 1561. `RD_ALifeLine_78d0ed987414381c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0168*
- **EN:** "It's just so quiet at night, you can hear your own heart"
- **TR:** 'Geceleri o kadar sessiz ki kendi kalp atışını duyuyorsun.'

### 1562. `RD_ALifeLine_0ff7b00052dc1556` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0168*
- **EN:** 'You okay in the back'
- **TR:** 'Arkada iyi misin?'

### 1563. `RD_ALifeLine_4702bba72eafeaf1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0168*
- **EN:** 'I keep thinking I hear the highway'
- **TR:** 'Sürekli otoyolu duyuyorum sanıyorum.'

### 1564. `RD_ALifeLine_3bf862fd4c64c4cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0168*
- **EN:** "Fine, I'm fine"
- **TR:** 'İyiyim, iyiyim.'

### 1565. `RD_ALifeLine_43ee33966a5e5666` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0168*
- **EN:** "That's the thing, it's the highway that's not there"
- **TR:** 'Mesele de bu, orada olmayan otoyol.'

### 1566. `RD_ALifeLine_123f497e334bf63e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0168*
- **EN:** "Fine means a lot of things, tell me when it's not"
- **TR:** 'İyiyim" çok şey demek, iyi olmadığında söyle.'

### 1567. `RD_ALifeLine_5740fc8019701617` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0169*
- **EN:** 'How many rounds do we have'
- **TR:** 'Kaç mermimiz var?'

### 1568. `RD_ALifeLine_74cd324b75e63c56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0169*
- **EN:** "The light's off in that house"
- **TR:** 'O evdeki ışık kapalı.'

### 1569. `RD_ALifeLine_1f0410216b32ed7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0169*
- **EN:** "Eleven, I think, that's all"
- **TR:** 'On bir galiba, hepsi bu.'

### 1570. `RD_ALifeLine_6a6fdc8621b62d85` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0169*
- **EN:** "Eleven, that's it, that's all of it"
- **TR:** 'On bir, o kadar, hepsi bu.'

### 1571. `RD_ALifeLine_3e3b75dc06d88803` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0169*
- **EN:** 'The one on the left, it was on an hour ago'
- **TR:** 'Soldaki, bir saat önce açıktı.'

### 1572. `RD_ALifeLine_780f9e31313b047d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0170*
- **EN:** 'Come on, one more hand'
- **TR:** 'Hadi, bir el daha.'

### 1573. `RD_ALifeLine_34deb60808329ac5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0170*
- **EN:** "They're all sleeping out back"
- **TR:** 'Hepsi arkada uyuyor.'

### 1574. `RD_ALifeLine_482f05e87962ecb3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0170*
- **EN:** "I'm tired"
- **TR:** 'Yorgunum.'

### 1575. `RD_ALifeLine_15ef013d1aaf086a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0170*
- **EN:** "Don't wake them"
- **TR:** 'Uyandırma onları.'

### 1576. `RD_ALifeLine_3d5ec296529d3985` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0170*
- **EN:** 'One hand, then you can sleep'
- **TR:** 'Bir el, sonra uyursun.'

### 1577. `RD_ALifeLine_5f54e33a09b4519c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0170*
- **EN:** "Quiet, they're finally asleep"
- **TR:** 'Sessiz, sonunda uyudular.'

### 1578. `RD_ALifeLine_62ecc1aa06d340d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0003*
- **EN:** "Officer, do you think they're still looking for us?"
- **TR:** 'Memur, sence bizi hala arıyorlar mı?'

### 1579. `RD_ALifeLine_48b737d55a5c893c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0003*
- **EN:** "Somebody's looking, I just don't know who"
- **TR:** 'Biri arıyor, kim olduğunu bilmiyorum.'

### 1580. `RD_ALifeLine_0b41a09041dab8bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0003*
- **EN:** "That's not very reassuring"
- **TR:** 'Pek rahatlatıcı değil.'

### 1581. `RD_ALifeLine_5bc0bdec6d1a4ec7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0003*
- **EN:** "Wasn't trying to reassure you, I just don't have anything better"
- **TR:** 'Rahatlatmaya çalışmadım, daha iyi bir lafım yok sadece.'

### 1582. `RD_ALifeLine_695e7b9571afde19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0004*
- **EN:** 'Is it true you can write a ticket for anything?'
- **TR:** 'Her şey için ceza kesebildiğin doğru mu?'

### 1583. `RD_ALifeLine_5f59821d41f7d872` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0004*
- **EN:** 'Anything with a reason'
- **TR:** 'Sebebi olan her şey için.'

### 1584. `RD_ALifeLine_53d4d7250572da24` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0004*
- **EN:** 'Busy drinking warm water, no tickets tonight'
- **TR:** 'Ilık su içmekle meşgulüm, bu gece ceza yok.'

### 1585. `RD_ALifeLine_17bae8527164a245` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0004*
- **EN:** 'Write one for the heat'
- **TR:** 'Sıcağa bir tane kes.'

### 1586. `RD_ALifeLine_509600dc6c8f3586` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0004*
- **EN:** 'Fine, ten bucks'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, on dolar.'

### 1587. `RD_ALifeLine_371326436b9d5e5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0006*
- **EN:** 'Can I ask something dumb?'
- **TR:** 'Aptalca bir şey sorabilir miyim?'

### 1588. `RD_ALifeLine_192067e60db18229` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0006*
- **EN:** 'Were you scared Friday?'
- **TR:** 'Cuma günü korktun mu?'

### 1589. `RD_ALifeLine_2b60b1504c3271e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0006*
- **EN:** "Yeah, I was, I still am, I'm just better at not showing it"
- **TR:** 'Evet, korktum, hala korkuyorum, sadece belli etmemekte daha iyiyim.'

### 1590. `RD_ALifeLine_774b00fc465d19f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0007*
- **EN:** 'I thought I heard something upstairs'
- **TR:** 'Yukarıda bir şey duydum sandım.'

### 1591. `RD_ALifeLine_2a0703bc6c4f1ac1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0007*
- **EN:** "It's the house"
- **TR:** 'Ev o.'

### 1592. `RD_ALifeLine_3577e273419d2c54` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0007*
- **EN:** "I'll check it, stay here"
- **TR:** 'Ben bakarım, olduğun yerde kal.'

### 1593. `RD_ALifeLine_102e63c61c5ad8fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0009*
- **EN:** 'A spouse, in Georgia, a dog'
- **TR:** "Eşim Georgia'da, bir de köpeğim var."

### 1594. `RD_ALifeLine_773e005024ab3c1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0009*
- **EN:** "What's the dog's name?"
- **TR:** 'Köpeğin adı ne?'

### 1595. `RD_ALifeLine_2382d5db22df3c35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0009*

### 1596. `RD_ALifeLine_624fb6ae33c4fdd6` — Claude (yeni)

### 1597. `RD_ALifeLine_2945d62b5d03bcae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0001*

### 1598. `RD_ALifeLine_359d9d2a41739454` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0001*

### 1599. `RD_ALifeLine_4bbc5ac71cfe65c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0001*

### 1600. `RD_ALifeLine_74da331961eb3457` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0001*
- **EN:** "It's a can of pig parts, nobody owns it"
- **TR:** 'Domuz parçası dolu bir kutu o, kimsenin değil.'

### 1601. `RD_ALifeLine_26bbc49f5e75eb0d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0001*

### 1602. `RD_ALifeLine_0e82e0a104f56bc8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0003*
- **EN:** 'Half a can each'
- **TR:** 'Kişi başı yarım kutu.'

### 1603. `RD_ALifeLine_665eec6a22551871` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0003*
- **EN:** "I counted twice, that's it"
- **TR:** 'İki kere saydım, bu kadar.'

### 1604. `RD_ALifeLine_4be4a33d11970672` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0003*
- **EN:** "You counted cans, you didn't count how fucking hungry I am"
- **TR:** 'Kutuları saydın, ne kadar siktiğimin aç olduğumu saymadın.'

### 1605. `RD_ALifeLine_23679f3c6e41dd42` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0003*
- **EN:** "Nobody's counting that..."
- **TR:** 'Onu kimse saymıyor...'

### 1606. `RD_ALifeLine_3e322973275dd7d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0007*
- **EN:** "Cold ravioli straight out of the can, I'm eating like a goddamn raccoon"
- **TR:** 'Kutudan soğuk ravioli yiyorum, lanet olası bir rakun gibi.'

### 1607. `RD_ALifeLine_0f40d35b2399b2a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Better than the beans'
- **TR:** 'Fasulyeden iyi.'

### 1608. `RD_ALifeLine_7460d9367ff0895d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Beans are all we got, so eat the beans'
- **TR:** 'Elimizde fasulye var, ye fasulyeyi.'

### 1609. `RD_ALifeLine_505519c67309a685` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0007*
- **EN:** "Don't say beans, I just ate"
- **TR:** 'Fasulye deme, yeni yedim.'

### 1610. `RD_ALifeLine_3e990ff146116595` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0012*
- **EN:** "I'm so hungry my stomach's eating itself"
- **TR:** 'O kadar açım ki midem kendini yiyor.'

### 1611. `RD_ALifeLine_758162964f5ebe7c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0012*
- **EN:** "That's just the beans talking"
- **TR:** 'Fasulyenin sesi o.'

### 1612. `RD_ALifeLine_398d771b513ffd58` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0012*
- **EN:** "No, listen, that's an actual growl"
- **TR:** 'Yok, dinle, gerçek bir hırlama bu.'

### 1613. `RD_ALifeLine_210296204834b227` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0012*
- **EN:** '...Jesus Christ, you sound like a dog in a ditch'
- **TR:** '...Yarabbi, çukurdaki köpek gibi ses çıkarıyorsun.'

### 1614. `RD_ALifeLine_5f828c740394bb6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0012*
- **EN:** 'Somebody feed that thing'
- **TR:** 'Biri şunu doyursun.'

### 1615. `RD_ALifeLine_51164bad6875d71d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0013*
- **EN:** 'Somebody took more than their half'
- **TR:** 'Biri payından fazlasını almış.'

### 1616. `RD_ALifeLine_7ab6ecc94002600f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0013*
- **EN:** "Don't look at ME!"
- **TR:** 'BANA bakma!'

### 1617. `RD_ALifeLine_4e48e00e5e8df873` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0013*
- **EN:** "I'm looking at the fuckin' can, it was full, now it's not"
- **TR:** 'Siktiğimin kutusuna bakıyorum, doluydu, şimdi değil.'

### 1618. `RD_ALifeLine_4477f77c183d59af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0013*

- **TR:** 'Belki sızdırmıştır.'

### 1619. `RD_ALifeLine_4fdfb265400c215d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0013*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0014*

### 1621. `RD_ALifeLine_1af4ef003665ff55` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0014*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0014*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0014*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0018*

### 1625. `RD_ALifeLine_1077ecf7793950de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0018*
- **EN:** 'Cut it off'
- **TR:** 'Kes at.'

### 1626. `RD_ALifeLine_1a0ddcdb26def061` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0018*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0019*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0019*
- **EN:** "Doesn't matter, I'm finishing it"

### 1631. `RD_ALifeLine_4ffeb94076ac4327` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0027*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0027*

### 1634. `RD_ALifeLine_5a2b2388513578bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0027*

### 1635. `RD_ALifeLine_29dabac12bf58ba7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0028*
- **EN:** 'You opened the last can of the good stuff without asking'
- **TR:** 'Son güzel konservenin kapağını sormadan açtın.'

### 1636. `RD_ALifeLine_4230aa760a417637` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0028*
- **EN:** 'It was open already'
- **TR:** 'Zaten açıktı.'

### 1637. `RD_ALifeLine_3f42e1a55837b3df` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0028*
- **EN:** 'It was OPEN because you opened it!'
- **TR:** 'AÇIKTI çünkü sen açtın!'

### 1638. `RD_ALifeLine_068dae3727e2fa06` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0028*
- **EN:** "I'm NOT fighting about goddamn chili!"
- **TR:** 'Lanet olası chili yüzünden kavga ETMEYECEĞİM!'

### 1639. `RD_ALifeLine_6dd057df2789228b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0030*
- **EN:** 'Who the hell packs a can of cranberry sauce?'
- **TR:** 'Kim kızılcık sosu kutusu paketler ya?'

### 1640. `RD_ALifeLine_678469d00c333472` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0030*
- **EN:** 'It was Thanksgiving in my head'
- **TR:** 'Kafamda Şükran Günüydü.'

### 1641. `RD_ALifeLine_4fca4692644a7816` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0030*
- **EN:** "My head's got its own calendar"
- **TR:** 'Benim kafamın kendi takvimi var.'

### 1642. `RD_ALifeLine_312ea0af255e02df` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0030*
- **EN:** "Open it, I'll eat anything"
- **TR:** 'Aç şunu, ben her şeyi yerim.'

### 1643. `RD_ALifeLine_1d61dcbf075a86a0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0035*
- **EN:** "There's a jar of something in the cabinet"
- **TR:** 'Dolapta bir kavanoz bir şey var.'

### 1644. `RD_ALifeLine_4ce75292574322b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0035*
- **EN:** "I can't read the label, it's gone brown, it might be applesauce"
- **TR:** 'Etiketi okuyamıyorum, kahverengileşmiş, elma püresi olabilir.'

### 1645. `RD_ALifeLine_4ca84d0516dc9964` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0035*
- **EN:** 'It might be anything'
- **TR:** 'Her şey olabilir.'

### 1646. `RD_ALifeLine_3be81b97789a2db1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0035*
- **EN:** "It's got a lid, that counts for something"
- **TR:** 'Kapağı var, bu da bir şey sayılır.'

### 1647. `RD_ALifeLine_4edabba03d3a8495` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0037*
- **EN:** "Who's been in my cigarettes?"

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0037*
- **EN:** 'There were nine'
- **TR:** 'Dokuz tane vardı.'

### 1649. `RD_ALifeLine_305444a45a5972b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0037*
- **EN:** 'Maybe you counted wrong'
- **TR:** 'Belki yanlış saymışsındır.'

### 1650. `RD_ALifeLine_05acf1fc2e476a5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0037*
- **EN:** 'I count everything, there were NINE!'
- **TR:** 'Ben her şeyi sayarım, DOKUZ tane vardı!'

### 1651. `RD_ALifeLine_501b1d3351129f59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0037*
- **EN:** 'Check your pocket, hon'
- **TR:** 'Cebine bir bak, tatlım.'

### 1652. `RD_ALifeLine_01e5418b48c35b48` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0038*
- **EN:** "Lighter's out of fluid"
- **TR:** 'Çakmağın gazı bitmiş.'

### 1653. `RD_ALifeLine_6ef8cb646d8fd499` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'Flick it harder'
- **TR:** 'Daha sert çak.'

### 1654. `RD_ALifeLine_4c01d8b5148f344d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0038*
- **EN:** "It's fucking empty, harder doesn't fix empty!"

### 1655. `RD_ALifeLine_4c57c9e43ed583f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'Matches?'
- **TR:** 'Kibrit?'

### 1656. `RD_ALifeLine_547a84885beb8d71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0038*
- **EN:** "Four, and one's wet"

### 1657. `RD_ALifeLine_46cd49de67b34b87` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0039*
- **EN:** 'I quit in May'
- **TR:** 'Mayısta bıraktım.'

### 1658. `RD_ALifeLine_72a525ee3ac7bf7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0039*
- **EN:** "And I'm bumming one off you right now"

### 1659. `RD_ALifeLine_33943d5650012e04` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0039*
- **EN:** "Forty days, that's a record for you"
- **TR:** 'Kırk gün. Senin için rekor bu.'

### 1660. `RD_ALifeLine_1885e80e1fa7f939` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0040*
- **EN:** "Don't blow it at me"
- **TR:** 'Dumanı bana üfleme.'

### 1661. `RD_ALifeLine_246c0a3e679d6f8a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0040*

- **TR:** 'Bir sigara be.'

### 1662. `RD_ALifeLine_7970ac680faf7f09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0040*

### 1663. `RD_ALifeLine_1fb85a454ffa3fc3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0040*
- **EN:** 'Then go sit over there'
- **TR:** 'O zaman git şuraya otur.'

### 1664. `RD_ALifeLine_0ad86d3d24451080` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Phone book pages make decent rolling paper'

### 1665. `RD_ALifeLine_78fcce607b3098fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0043*
- **EN:** "You've done this?"
- **TR:** 'Denedin mi yani?'

### 1666. `RD_ALifeLine_44da24df75074ab7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0043*

### 1667. `RD_ALifeLine_32fd5e811685a44f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0043*
- **EN:** "Great, let's all do it"

### 1668. `RD_ALifeLine_296fbd722640b07e` — Claude (yeni)

- **EN:** "I'm so wired for a smoke I'm gonna bite somebody"
- **TR:** 'Sigaraya öyle acıktım ki birini ısıracağım.'

### 1669. `RD_ALifeLine_779547df622a5604` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0044*
- **EN:** 'Bite a boot'
- **TR:** 'Çizmeyi ısır.'

### 1670. `RD_ALifeLine_18f20f86270c5330` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0044*
- **EN:** "Bite the can, it's closer"
- **TR:** 'Konserveyi ısır, daha yakın.'

### 1671. `RD_ALifeLine_379b696d03d4e2b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0044*
- **EN:** "Y'all are no help"
- **TR:** 'Siz de hiçbir işe yaramıyorsunuz!'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1672. `RD_ALifeLine_292af7de143b4e71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0049*
- **EN:** "It's so fucking hot my shirt's glued to the chair and the water's warm as spit, I can't even sit still"
- **TR:** 'Hava siktiğimin sıcağı, gömleğim sandalyeye yapıştı, su da tükürük gibi ılık. Yerimde duramıyorum bile.'

### 1673. `RD_ALifeLine_746ac5185c4ffabb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'Peel it off then'
- **TR:** 'O zaman sök at.'

### 1674. `RD_ALifeLine_4046cc20326aff13` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'I did, it made a noise'
- **TR:** 'Söktüm, ses çıkardı.'

### 1675. `RD_ALifeLine_773a7e070f0d652e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'We heard'
- **TR:** 'Duyduk.'

### 1676. `RD_ALifeLine_1d27859765d3509e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0050*
- **EN:** "Canteen water's the temperature of spit and I'm drinking it anyway, what the fuck else am I gonna do"
- **TR:** 'Matara suyu tükürük kadar ılık, yine de içiyorum. Başka ne halt edeceğim ki?'

### 1677. `RD_ALifeLine_3a488d0e54177d16` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0050*
- **EN:** 'Drink it!'
- **TR:** 'İç o zaman!'

### 1678. `RD_ALifeLine_6d22a53536af2d9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0050*
- **EN:** 'I am, it tastes like drinking myself'
- **TR:** 'İçiyorum ya, kendimi içiyor gibiyim.'

### 1679. `RD_ALifeLine_4fdb3bc934d3430b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0054*
- **EN:** "I got chigger bites in places I'm not gonna name"
- **TR:** 'Akar ısırdı, hem de adını anmayacağım yerlerimi.'

### 1680. `RD_ALifeLine_49705629660fbf99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0054*
- **EN:** "Then don't name them"
- **TR:** 'O zaman anma.'

### 1681. `RD_ALifeLine_6d6e18267a79dcc6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0054*
- **EN:** 'They itch like hell'
- **TR:** 'Cehennem gibi kaşınıyor!'

### 1682. `RD_ALifeLine_29c89ba85763b895` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0054*
- **EN:** "Stop SCRATCHING, you're making it worse!"
- **TR:** 'KAŞIMA artık, daha da kötüleştiriyorsun!'

### 1683. `RD_ALifeLine_321d43775606c3e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0054*
- **EN:** "Nothing's stopping that, it'll go till Sunday"
- **TR:** 'Onu hiçbir şey durdurmaz, pazara kadar sürer.'

### 1684. `RD_ALifeLine_78ae50d14194f556` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0056*
- **EN:** "Gnats in my eyes, gnats in my mouth, I'm eating fucking gnats"
- **TR:** 'Gözümde tatarcık, ağzımda tatarcık, siktiğimin böceklerini yiyorum!'

### 1685. `RD_ALifeLine_15a6fb3e25918266` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0056*
- **EN:** 'Close your mouth'
- **TR:** 'Ağzını kapat.'

### 1686. `RD_ALifeLine_2b8f9a5556d219c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0056*
- **EN:** "I'd have to stop talking, and where's the fun in that"
- **TR:** 'O zaman susmam lazım, o da hiç eğlenceli değil.'

### 1687. `RD_ALifeLine_4dcc35957f861498` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0056*
- **EN:** "There's the fun"
- **TR:** 'Eğlence de tam orada.'

### 1688. `RD_ALifeLine_51ae763d4a5dcd0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'Roach on the wall the size of my thumb'
- **TR:** 'Duvarda başparmağım kadar bir hamamböceği var.'

### 1689. `RD_ALifeLine_3519e4d87ecffd0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'Kill the bastard'
- **TR:** 'Öldür şu piçi.'

### 1690. `RD_ALifeLine_4b4a056819cc6366` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0057*
- **EN:** "I'm not touching it"
- **TR:** 'Ben dokunmam.'

### 1691. `RD_ALifeLine_7f59153a3a6a5c07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'Then name the bastard, he lives here now'
- **TR:** 'O zaman şu piçe bir isim ver, artık burada oturuyor.'

### 1692. `RD_ALifeLine_5a84e1a2550efe07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'Name him Walt'
- **TR:** 'Adı Walt olsun.'

### 1693. `RD_ALifeLine_3b2e846d24a5c2f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0058*
- **EN:** "Hornet's been in the room since lunch"
- **TR:** 'Eşek arısı öğleden beri odada.'

### 1694. `RD_ALifeLine_5bba5d6f2202dcc8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0058*
- **EN:** 'Leave it alone and it leaves you alone'
- **TR:** 'Sen ona dokunma, o da sana dokunmaz.'

### 1695. `RD_ALifeLine_6affff137a44992a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0058*
- **EN:** "That's not what my cousin said, he got stung on the lip, swelled up like a goddamn tomato"
- **TR:** 'Kuzenim öyle demedi ama. Dudağından soktu, koskoca domates gibi şişti.'

### 1696. `RD_ALifeLine_1de916a90e3c59ad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0058*
- **EN:** "Then don't kiss it"
- **TR:** 'O zaman öpme.'

### 1697. `RD_ALifeLine_71326f480c7bbee2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0059*
- **EN:** 'Who stinks'
- **TR:** 'Kim kokuyor?'

### 1698. `RD_ALifeLine_7e11e27b41dde428` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0059*
- **EN:** 'Everybody stinks'
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz kokuyoruz.'

### 1699. `RD_ALifeLine_06fcd5175cb44fc6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0059*
- **EN:** "No, somebody stinks worse, I can pick it out, it's a goddamn skill"
- **TR:** 'Yok, biri daha beter kokuyor. Ayırt edebiliyorum, bu resmen bir yetenek!'

### 1700. `RD_ALifeLine_71e7e08940a786a4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0059*
- **EN:** "It's your boots"
- **TR:** 'Çizmelerin.'

### 1701. `RD_ALifeLine_765fbc9443a687bb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0059*
- **EN:** "It's NOT my boots!"
- **TR:** 'Çizmelerim DEĞİL!'

### 1702. `RD_ALifeLine_252ddce64d8dff79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0060*
- **EN:** "It's your turn to empty the bucket"
- **TR:** 'Kovayı boşaltma sırası sende.'

### 1703. `RD_ALifeLine_5d11ac1507df5c8d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'I emptied it yesterday'
- **TR:** 'Dün ben boşalttım.'

### 1704. `RD_ALifeLine_5f307c57302da6ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'Yesterday was a whole day ago!'
- **TR:** 'Dün koca bir gün önceydi!'

### 1705. `RD_ALifeLine_120b9fe564eecce4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0060*
- **EN:** "Fine, hold the fucking door, don't let it slam"
- **TR:** 'İyi be, şu siktiğimin kapısını tut, çarpmasın.'

### 1706. `RD_ALifeLine_177da3b20f5903b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'Lord, the things we say now'
- **TR:** 'Yarabbi, ne laflar ediyoruz artık.'

### 1707. `RD_ALifeLine_26737fa67946b788` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0065*
- **EN:** "If I don't get out of this shirt I'm gonna die in it, it's growing on me"
- **TR:** 'Bu gömleği çıkarmazsam içinde gebereceğim, resmen üstümde filizleniyor.'

### 1708. `RD_ALifeLine_118d5c3a183b91c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0065*
- **EN:** 'Take the fucking shirt off, nobody cares'
- **TR:** 'Çıkar şu siktiğimin gömleğini, kimsenin umurunda değil.'

### 1709. `RD_ALifeLine_3273ab4d7a39d847` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0065*
- **EN:** "Then I'm in a house with strangers, half dressed"
- **TR:** 'O zaman yabancılarla dolu bir evde yarı çıplak oturacağım yani.'

### 1710. `RD_ALifeLine_10989db643061901` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0065*
- **EN:** "We've seen worse, believe me, we're all disgusting"
- **TR:** 'Daha beterini gördük, inan bana. Hepimiz iğrenciz.'

### 1711. `RD_ALifeLine_6187bf9c6b434812` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'This floor is killing my back'
- **TR:** 'Bu yer yüzünden belim kopacak.'

### 1712. `RD_ALifeLine_151a44e360ffc9e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'Put your jacket under it'
- **TR:** 'Ceketini altına ser.'

### 1713. `RD_ALifeLine_5802ba1e7264a895` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0069*
- **EN:** "It's under it, my back's still on the floor"
- **TR:** 'Altında zaten, yine de belim yerde.'

### 1714. `RD_ALifeLine_0534185263339920` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0069*
- **EN:** "That's the problem with floors"
- **TR:** 'Yerlerin derdi de bu zaten.'

### 1715. `RD_ALifeLine_3bdfb7834d759318` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0079*
- **EN:** 'Everybody just shut the fuck UP for five minutes!'
- **TR:** 'Hepiniz beş dakika çenenizi KAPATIN, siktir!'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1716. `RD_ALifeLine_3d1689b026f27e5b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0079*
- **EN:** "Nobody's talking"
- **TR:** 'Kimse konuşmuyor.'

### 1717. `RD_ALifeLine_157143e149844928` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0079*
- **EN:** "You're breathing, the WHOLE room's breathing!"
- **TR:** 'Nefes alıyorsun, bütün oda nefes alıyor!'

### 1718. `RD_ALifeLine_67384b156293f198` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0079*
- **EN:** "...somebody's tired"
- **TR:** '...Biri yorulmuş galiba.'

### 1719. `RD_ALifeLine_09e45d79702dfe35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0083*
- **EN:** 'You reneged'
- **TR:** 'Renonse yaptın!'

### 1720. `RD_ALifeLine_0bd6654e147c2f45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0083*
- **EN:** 'You had a heart, you played a spade'
- **TR:** 'Elinde kupa vardı, maça attın.'

### 1721. `RD_ALifeLine_441ddb8f7d076d8c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0083*
- **EN:** 'I mixed it up'
- **TR:** 'Karıştırdım.'

### 1722. `RD_ALifeLine_4001996e7fb2fc79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0083*
- **EN:** 'Mixed it up my ass!'
- **TR:** 'Karıştırmış, bok karıştırmış!'

### 1723. `RD_ALifeLine_1c6dcded13d1871b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_1.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0083*
- **EN:** "Watch your mouth, there's women present"
- **TR:** 'Ağzını topla, burada kadınlar var.'

### 1724. `RD_ALifeLine_22251c8846d4e49d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0146*
- **EN:** 'You left the fucking door open all night!'
- **TR:** 'Siktiğimin kapısını bütün gece açık bıraktın!'

### 1725. `RD_ALifeLine_44f2261047cb0b96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0146*
- **EN:** 'It was hot'
- **TR:** 'Hava sıcaktı.'

### 1726. `RD_ALifeLine_16016ead0f2d4c19` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0146*
- **EN:** "Anything could've walked in!"
- **TR:** 'İçeri her şey girebilirdi!'

### 1727. `RD_ALifeLine_2c060cbb0819b0d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0146*
- **EN:** 'Nothing did'
- **TR:** 'Girmedi ama.'

### 1728. `RD_ALifeLine_56d7461523af9cf0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0146*
- **EN:** "That's not the point"
- **TR:** 'Mesele o değil.'

### 1729. `RD_ALifeLine_6718153568d4658f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0146*
- **EN:** "It's a little bit the point"
- **TR:** 'Biraz da o aslında.'

### 1730. `RD_ALifeLine_4bcde8bc21482057` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0147*
- **EN:** "That's my spot"
- **TR:** 'Orası benim yerim.'

### 1731. `RD_ALifeLine_2df930856fa3fd68` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0147*
- **EN:** "It's a floor"
- **TR:** 'Yer işte.'

### 1732. `RD_ALifeLine_7c6af87b3f9a71cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0147*
- **EN:** "It's the floor I picked, it's got the least dust"
- **TR:** 'Ben seçtim orayı, en az toz orada.'

### 1733. `RD_ALifeLine_4370e0073148a0a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0147*
- **EN:** "There's no dust-free part of a floor, I'm moving over"
- **TR:** 'Yerin tozsuz yeri mi olurmuş, ben kayıyorum.'

### 1734. `RD_ALifeLine_79a4c6ef0b3e01ff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0147*
- **EN:** "You're moving onto my jacket!"
- **TR:** 'Ceketimin üstüne kayıyorsun!'

### 1735. `RD_ALifeLine_357a245e6e0ac755` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0147*
- **EN:** "Y'all are fighting over a jacket on a floor"
- **TR:** 'Yerdeki bir ceket için kavga ediyorsunuz.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1736. `RD_ALifeLine_359ee9700d83de2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0150*
- **EN:** "Don't touch my bag"
- **TR:** 'Çantama dokunma.'

### 1737. `RD_ALifeLine_4634dae34cf8d755` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0150*
- **EN:** "I wasn't touching it"
- **TR:** 'Dokunmuyordum.'

### 1738. `RD_ALifeLine_6509f22e0b614e23` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0150*
- **EN:** 'You had your hand on the strap'
- **TR:** 'Elin kayışındaydı.'

### 1739. `RD_ALifeLine_4a98aa5630684ec8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0150*
- **EN:** 'The strap was in my way!'
- **TR:** 'Kayış önüme çıkmıştı!'

### 1740. `RD_ALifeLine_1763d53720efa8f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0150*
- **EN:** "It's a bag, it's got a lot of ways, and you're in all of them"
- **TR:** 'Çanta bu, her yanı yol, sen de hepsine çıkıyorsun.'

### 1741. `RD_ALifeLine_73d5ed73436defb1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0151*
- **EN:** 'You narrate everything you do, you know that?'
- **TR:** 'Yaptığın her şeyi anlatıyorsun, farkında mısın?'

### 1742. `RD_ALifeLine_7c77dc756ea87500` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0151*
- **EN:** "'Now I'm opening the can, now I'm sitting down'"
- **TR:** "'Şimdi konserveyi açıyorum, şimdi oturuyorum.'"

### 1743. `RD_ALifeLine_0a69cb14336e2cb8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0151*
- **EN:** "...I wasn't aware I was doing it"
- **TR:** '...Yaptığımın farkında değildim.'

### 1744. `RD_ALifeLine_26c716fd71622e48` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0151*
- **EN:** 'Not aware, ladies and gentlemen'
- **TR:** 'Farkında değilmiş, bayanlar baylar!'

### 1745. `RD_ALifeLine_4a2d57b82266cba2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0152*
- **EN:** 'You always leave one swallow in the bottle'
- **TR:** 'Şişede hep bir yudum bırakıyorsun.'

### 1746. `RD_ALifeLine_5918ae39554d2dff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0152*
- **EN:** 'I used to. So?'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden bırakırdım. Ne olmuş?'

### 1747. `RD_ALifeLine_13a3e082004b7642` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0152*
- **EN:** "My brother did that. Handed back a bottle with one swallow instead of admitting he'd finished it"
- **TR:** 'Kardeşim de yapardı. Bitirdiğini itiraf etmek yerine şişeyi bir yudumla geri uzatırdı.'

### 1748. `RD_ALifeLine_0890b5a6764d6e14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0152*
- **EN:** "He'd have called it manners"
- **TR:** 'Ona nezaket derdi.'

### 1749. `RD_ALifeLine_3fcfac6d438881a4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0152*
- **EN:** "I'd have called him a lazy ass"
