- **TR:** 'Ben ona tembel göt derdim.'

### 1750. `RD_ALifeLine_181e4a817b2e3860` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0155*
- **EN:** 'Who made you boss'
- **TR:** 'Seni kim patron yaptı?'

### 1751. `RD_ALifeLine_260a09c771ea9f9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0155*
- **EN:** 'Then why are you giving orders'
- **TR:** 'O zaman neden emir veriyorsun?'

### 1752. `RD_ALifeLine_6d2c374e1f33bef2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0155*
- **EN:** 'Because nobody else was, and somebody has to, so quit crying'
- **TR:** 'Çünkü kimse vermiyordu, birinin vermesi lazım. Hadi ağlamayı kes.'

### 1753. `RD_ALifeLine_49c6b78e6a24c6b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0155*
- **EN:** "Quit crying, that's a point!"
- **TR:** 'Ağlamayı kes, hah, bu doğru!'

### 1754. `RD_ALifeLine_2f177f4b1457b10c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0163*
- **EN:** 'Nobody told us shit and nobody showed up, nobody ever said what to do'
- **TR:** 'Bize kimse bir bok söylemedi, kimse de gelmedi. Ne yapacağımızı bile kimse demedi.'

### 1755. `RD_ALifeLine_1ad3138b468537a2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0163*
- **EN:** "And now I'm sitting in somebody's living room eating out of a can"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi de başkasının oturma odasında konserveden yiyorum.'

### 1756. `RD_ALifeLine_2ad7e2f03b30edec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0163*
- **EN:** 'I pay taxes! I paid taxes Friday!'
- **TR:** 'Vergi veriyorum ben! Cuma günü bile verdim!'

### 1757. `RD_ALifeLine_6beadfb53245ebf3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0163*
- **EN:** "They'll refund you"
- **TR:** 'İade ederler sana.'

### 1758. `RD_ALifeLine_5e19e6fc5ddad48e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0164*
- **EN:** 'The Army said stay inside, and then it left'
- **TR:** 'Ordu evde kalın dedi, sonra da çekip gitti.'

### 1759. `RD_ALifeLine_6b6e0f202d1d3515` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0164*
- **EN:** "They didn't leave, they're regrouping"
- **TR:** 'Gitmediler, yeniden toplanıyorlar.'

### 1760. `RD_ALifeLine_7f8825d00a9b4664` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0164*
- **EN:** 'They left, they put up a fucking line and they left'
- **TR:** 'Gittiler! Kordonu çektiler, siktir olup gittiler!'

### 1761. `RD_ALifeLine_49889b891c18ade2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0184*
- **EN:** 'You eat like a goddamn animal'
- **TR:** 'Lanet bir hayvan gibi yiyorsun.'

### 1762. `RD_ALifeLine_3062884c74a0955f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0184*
- **EN:** 'You chew like one'
- **TR:** 'Sen de öyle çiğniyorsun.'

### 1763. `RD_ALifeLine_4f74875271a0bc42` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_2.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_1_0184*
- **EN:** "You both eat like animals, and I'm sitting right here"
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de hayvan gibi yiyorsunuz, ben de tam burada oturuyorum.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1764. `RD_ALifeLine_14af03e33cf29784` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0076*
- **EN:** 'I got duct tape on both boots'
- **TR:** 'İki çizmeme de gri bant sardım.'

### 1765. `RD_ALifeLine_65894a6a686f0cf1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0076*
- **EN:** "What's holding the duct tape on"
- **TR:** 'Peki o bandı ne tutuyor?'

### 1766. `RD_ALifeLine_090852d615de81eb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0076*
- **EN:** 'Hope, and more duct tape'
- **TR:** 'Umut, bir de daha fazla bant.'

### 1767. `RD_ALifeLine_08ed553e25a5a77d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0076*
- **EN:** "That's about all anybody's got"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin elinde kalan o zaten.'

### 1768. `RD_ALifeLine_07e3d03703e268a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0104*
- **EN:** "If I hear one more person say we'll be fine I'm gonna fucking scream"
- **TR:** "Bir kişi daha 'bir şey olmaz' derse çığlığı basarım, siktir!"

### 1769. `RD_ALifeLine_25d88ed3782336b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0104*
- **EN:** "We'll be fine"
- **TR:** 'Bir şey olmaz.'

### 1770. `RD_ALifeLine_12cca3d14b74a877` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0104*
- **EN:** "I'm gonna scream, I mean it"
- **TR:** 'Bağıracağım, şaka yapmıyorum!'

### 1771. `RD_ALifeLine_175446c706ee85ba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0104*
- **EN:** "Scream into a pillow, then, there's one right there"
- **TR:** 'O zaman yastığa bağır, hemen şurada bir tane var.'

### 1772. `RD_ALifeLine_5ef9505132451ad9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0015*
- **EN:** "It's so damn hot in here I can't think straight, my brain's like warm soup"
- **TR:** 'İçerisi o kadar sıcak ki düşünemiyorum, beynim ılık çorba gibi.'

### 1773. `RD_ALifeLine_26a03e6d376c173f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0015*
- **EN:** "Then don't think"
- **TR:** 'O zaman düşünme.'

### 1774. `RD_ALifeLine_3b9b266c3b508d69` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0015*
- **EN:** "I haven't had a decent night's sleep since the sixth, I'm gonna kill the next person who breathes!"
- **TR:** 'Ayın altısından beri düzgün uyku uyumadım, nefes alan bir sonraki kişiyi öldüreceğim!'

### 1775. `RD_ALifeLine_389b8d094f443f8d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0015*
- **EN:** "That's me, I'm breathing, go ahead"
- **TR:** 'O benim, nefes alıyorum, buyur.'

### 1776. `RD_ALifeLine_1c429a0c3904856a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0016*
- **EN:** 'My ass has been numb for two days from this goddamn floor'
- **TR:** 'Şu lanet yer yüzünden iki gündür kıçım uyuştu.'

### 1777. `RD_ALifeLine_04d7d5a81011e67c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0016*
- **EN:** 'Sit on something'
- **TR:** 'Bir şeyin üstüne otur.'

### 1778. `RD_ALifeLine_132e4b930c8a830f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0016*
- **EN:** "There's nothing to sit on, I've tried the table, the table's worse"
- **TR:** 'Oturacak bir şey yok. Masayı denedim, o daha kötü.'

### 1779. `RD_ALifeLine_25823bc010322962` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0016*
- **EN:** 'Then stand, stop telling me about your ass'
- **TR:** 'O zaman ayakta dur, bana kıçından bahsetmeyi kes.'

### 1780. `RD_ALifeLine_1ae5f03d28e1019e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0017*
- **EN:** 'If I hear that radio static one more fucking time!'
- **TR:** 'O siktiğimin radyo cızırtısını bir daha duyarsam!'

### 1781. `RD_ALifeLine_602404e3105967ec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0017*
- **EN:** "I'm listening for a voice"
- **TR:** 'Bir ses bekliyorum.'

### 1782. `RD_ALifeLine_1888bb3a36c33fbe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0017*
- **EN:** "There's no voice, it's static, it's been static for four days!"
- **TR:** 'Ses yok, cızırtı var, dört gündür cızırtı!'

### 1783. `RD_ALifeLine_1ee3906f250bf86f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0017*
- **EN:** "Turn it down, don't turn it off, I like the hiss"
- **TR:** 'Sesini kıs ama kapatma, hışırtısını seviyorum.'

### 1784. `RD_ALifeLine_622255b619ecea24` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0023*
- **EN:** "I forgot my dad's birthday was Tuesday, I remembered just now, TUESDAY!"
- **TR:** 'Babamın doğum günü salıymış, şimdi aklıma geldi, SALI!'

### 1785. `RD_ALifeLine_073ec48e5f0aa8bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0023*
- **EN:** "It's okay"
- **TR:** 'Sorun değil.'

### 1786. `RD_ALifeLine_3f89445b1b1146be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0023*
- **EN:** "It's not okay, I always called, even when he was a dick, I always called"
- **TR:** 'Değil! Ben hep arardım, babam pislik olduğunda bile hep arardım.'

### 1787. `RD_ALifeLine_4a2fac802c2353a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0023*
- **EN:** "So call him in your head, he'll hear it"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kafanda ara, duyar.'

### 1788. `RD_ALifeLine_14cef016729f6943` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0025*
- **EN:** 'Where the fuck is the Army? I pay taxes! I paid for those tanks!'
- **TR:** 'Ordu nerede, siktir? Vergi veriyorum ben! O tankların parasını ben ödedim!'

### 1789. `RD_ALifeLine_147c26510f18f04e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0025*
- **EN:** "They're coming"
- **TR:** 'Geliyorlar.'

### 1790. `RD_ALifeLine_1d9831726d58d9fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0025*
- **EN:** "Everybody keeps saying they're coming, it's been a goddamn week!"
- **TR:** 'Herkes geliyorlar diyor, koca bir hafta oldu!'

### 1791. `RD_ALifeLine_6de0e9cf30a0ef61` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0025*
- **EN:** "Don't yell, you'll bring the neighborhood"
- **TR:** 'Bağırma, bütün mahalleyi çağırırsın.'

### 1792. `RD_ALifeLine_2b9fa6a2781f34d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0027*
- **EN:** "At least I don't have to go to work Monday"
- **TR:** 'Neyse ki pazartesi işe gitmek zorunda değilim.'

### 1793. `RD_ALifeLine_0da67972380f477b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0027*
- **EN:** "Monday's tomorrow"
- **TR:** 'Pazartesi yarın.'

### 1794. `RD_ALifeLine_7c8570f05b60871e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0027*
- **EN:** 'Fuck'
- **TR:** 'Siktir.'

### 1795. `RD_ALifeLine_42d892092edc81d6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0027*
- **EN:** 'Congratulations, you remembered'
- **TR:** 'Tebrikler, hatırlamışsın.'

### 1796. `RD_ALifeLine_68ae69071d247ed5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0029*
- **EN:** "I'd trade my left arm for a bag of ice"
- **TR:** 'Bir torba buz için sol kolumu verirdim.'

### 1797. `RD_ALifeLine_31a338a27971d976` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0029*
- **EN:** 'You need that arm'
- **TR:** 'O kola ihtiyacın var.'

### 1798. `RD_ALifeLine_7bb3b397163a8b3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0029*
- **EN:** "I'd trade my right arm too, I'd learn to use my feet"
- **TR:** 'Sağ kolumu da verirdim, ayaklarımı kullanmayı öğrenirim.'

### 1799. `RD_ALifeLine_736c8e915a53f9d3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0031*
- **EN:** "Somebody's chewing, I can hear them chewing, STOP!"
- **TR:** 'Biri çiğniyor, çiğnediğini duyuyorum, KES şunu!'

### 1800. `RD_ALifeLine_3713a8e327bd27b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0031*
- **EN:** "It's a cracker"
- **TR:** 'Sade bir kraker bu.'

### 1801. `RD_ALifeLine_0e5fdfaf24b49401` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0031*
- **EN:** "It's a loud goddamn cracker!"
- **TR:** 'Gürültülü, lanet bir kraker!'

### 1802. `RD_ALifeLine_7e8a5fa139a03467` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0031*
- **EN:** 'Chew with your mouth closed, hon'
- **TR:** 'Ağzın kapalı çiğne, tatlım.'

### 1803. `RD_ALifeLine_48c9b77b0ef50c75` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0032*
- **EN:** "If you say 'we've got this' one more time I'll throw you through that window"
- **TR:** "Bir kez daha 'hallederiz' dersen seni şu pencereden dışarı atarım."

### 1804. `RD_ALifeLine_58be23b15483cf26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0032*
- **EN:** "We've got this"
- **TR:** 'Hallederiz.'

### 1805. `RD_ALifeLine_302b83216ed0bdff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0035*
- **EN:** 'Do you ever think about how many are out there?'
- **TR:** 'Dışarıda kaç tane var diye hiç düşünüyor musun?'

### 1806. `RD_ALifeLine_038c1c5a2157369c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0035*
- **EN:** 'Hundreds, thousands, all of them walking around, with nowhere to go'
- **TR:** 'Yüzlerce, binlerce, hepsi dolaşıp duruyor, gidecek yerleri yok.'

### 1807. `RD_ALifeLine_76cb370e14910f9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0035*
- **EN:** "Fuck's sake, I'm trying to eat"
- **TR:** 'Ya siktir, yemek yemeye çalışıyorum!'

### 1808. `RD_ALifeLine_7914fdf620d6e933` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0038*
- **EN:** "I want a cheeseburger so bad I'd sleep with a Burger King, I don't care which one"
- **TR:** "Öyle bir çizburger istiyorum ki bir Burger King'le yatarım, hangisi olursa olsun, umurumda değil."

### 1809. `RD_ALifeLine_02f6ce274810fcca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0038*
- **EN:** "You're a disgusting human being"
- **TR:** 'İğrenç bir insansın.'

### 1810. `RD_ALifeLine_789150af671ec6b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0038*
- **EN:** "I'm a hungry one"
- **TR:** 'Aç bir insanım.'

### 1811. `RD_ALifeLine_5e75744329a841de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0041*
- **EN:** "My ex-husband's the one I think about, can you believe that? That asshole"
- **TR:** 'Aklıma gelen eski kocam, inanabiliyor musun? O göt herif!'

### 1812. `RD_ALifeLine_33a5e58754b1b373` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0041*
- **EN:** "The first one, he had the worst temper, and he'd have known what to do"
- **TR:** 'Birincisi. Sinirli herifin tekiydi ama ne yapacağını bilirdi.'

### 1813. `RD_ALifeLine_54166a4774506976` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0041*
- **EN:** "He'd have shot at something and run"
- **TR:** 'Bir şeye ateş edip kaçardı.'

### 1814. `RD_ALifeLine_14b66443662d976c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0045*
- **EN:** 'Take your goddamn boots off before you come in!'
- **TR:** 'Lanet çizmelerini çıkar, ondan sonra gir!'

### 1815. `RD_ALifeLine_3b3b0faf19ebaf67` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0045*
- **EN:** "It's a dirt floor!"
- **TR:** 'Toprak zemin burası!'

### 1816. `RD_ALifeLine_4c7857bc4a523ac9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0045*
- **EN:** "It's a dirt floor in MY house! I wiped it this morning!"
- **TR:** 'Benim evimdeki toprak zemin bu! Bu sabah sildim!'

### 1817. `RD_ALifeLine_55734ec30d082d1d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0045*
- **EN:** 'You wiped dirt'
- **TR:** 'Toprak sildin yani.'

### 1818. `RD_ALifeLine_4bab1159722114b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0047*
- **EN:** 'Good news, I found a hot dog in the freezer'
- **TR:** 'Müjde, dondurucuda bir sosisli buldum.'

### 1819. `RD_ALifeLine_1afd7483778d3fc4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0047*
- **EN:** "The freezer's off"
- **TR:** 'Dondurucu kapalı.'

### 1820. `RD_ALifeLine_7183b9ed50deb900` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0047*
- **EN:** "It's been off for a week, the hot dog's pretty confident"
- **TR:** 'Bir haftadır kapalı, sosisli de kendinden gayet emin.'

### 1821. `RD_ALifeLine_0696748328e38806` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0047*
- **EN:** "That hot dog's got a face on it, put it back"
- **TR:** 'O sosislinin yüzü var, geri koy gitsin.'

### 1822. `RD_ALifeLine_734d4a1110e15104` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0049*
- **EN:** "I'd give my left nut for a cigarette"
- **TR:** 'Bir sigara için sol taşağımı verirdim.'

### 1823. `RD_ALifeLine_28214dfc1277a0bb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0049*
- **EN:** "That's a lot of nut"
- **TR:** 'Ciddi bir taşak o.'

### 1824. `RD_ALifeLine_1d10c1cd3808f9d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0049*
- **EN:** "It's a mid-sized nut"
- **TR:** 'Orta boy bir taşak.'

### 1825. `RD_ALifeLine_1009db8d0b047a59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0049*
- **EN:** "Take it up with the guy who's got a pack"
- **TR:** 'O iş için paketi olan herifle konuş.'

### 1826. `RD_ALifeLine_1fa8401b52c5a90b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0051*
- **EN:** 'You hear it? The moaning, way out, like cows'
- **TR:** 'Duydun mu? Çok uzaktan inleme sesleri geliyor, inekler gibi.'

### 1827. `RD_ALifeLine_48f2306c0281d82f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0051*
- **EN:** "It's not cows"
- **TR:** 'İnek değil.'

### 1828. `RD_ALifeLine_32945671043bac0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0051*
- **EN:** "I know it's not cows, I'm trying to pretend it's cows"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum inek değil, inekmiş gibi davranmaya çalışıyorum.'

### 1829. `RD_ALifeLine_5fcbfeda7cce3396` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0051*
- **EN:** 'Pretend harder...'
- **TR:** 'Daha çok dene...'

### 1830. `RD_ALifeLine_0e53c60a195cdb8c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0054*
- **EN:** "I read the whole back of the shampoo bottle, it's the most boring thing I've ever read"
- **TR:** 'Şampuan şişesinin arkasını baştan sona okudum, hayatımda okuduğum en sıkıcı şey.'

### 1831. `RD_ALifeLine_5ed74c9c4791da7e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0054*
- **EN:** "Lather, rinse, repeat, it's a goddamn trap"
- **TR:** 'Köpürt, durula, tekrarla. Lanet bir tuzak bu!'

### 1832. `RD_ALifeLine_2e4983410a5e29cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0054*
- **EN:** "You'd be clean forever"
- **TR:** 'Hep tertemiz kalırdın.'

### 1833. `RD_ALifeLine_3e2eedb12fc6e40f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0056*
- **EN:** "Don't you dare tell me to calm down"
- **TR:** 'Sakın sakin ol deme bana.'

### 1834. `RD_ALifeLine_34c2b10f17443f4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0056*
- **EN:** 'You had it on your face'
- **TR:** 'Yüzünde yazıyordu.'

### 1835. `RD_ALifeLine_5d77b9fc0d37d52d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0056*
- **EN:** "Everybody's got it on their face, we've all got it"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin yüzünde yazıyor, hepimizde var.'

### 1836. `RD_ALifeLine_6987540941c21cec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0057*
- **EN:** "Nobody's talking about it, are we gonna just sit here till we die?"
- **TR:** 'Kimse bundan bahsetmiyor. Ölene kadar burada mı oturacağız?'

### 1837. `RD_ALifeLine_4b9c9d756974a368` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0057*
- **EN:** "Nobody's dying"
- **TR:** 'Kimse ölmüyor.'

### 1838. `RD_ALifeLine_69e79e866847d061` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0057*
- **EN:** "We're gonna die in this goddamn living room eating Vienna sausages!"
- **TR:** 'Şu lanet oturma odasında konserve sosis yiye yiye geberip gideceğiz!'

### 1839. `RD_ALifeLine_2b2b04dc2f280c76` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0057*
- **EN:** 'Better than dying in the street'
- **TR:** 'Sokakta ölmekten iyidir.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 1840. `RD_ALifeLine_2895132111cadeb0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0058*
- **EN:** "Dale's been gone too long, it's been three hours"
- **TR:** 'Dale çok uzun süredir yok, üç saat oldu.'

### 1841. `RD_ALifeLine_35cb81335308cb4b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0058*
- **EN:** 'He said an hour'
- **TR:** 'Bir saat demişti.'

### 1842. `RD_ALifeLine_7f0d2bfc209cb12c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0058*
- **EN:** "He said an hour and it's been three, something's wrong, something's wrong"
- **TR:** 'Bir saat dedi, üç saat oldu. Bir şey var, bir şey var!'

### 1843. `RD_ALifeLine_4edc48fa69583020` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0058*
- **EN:** "Don't go looking, that's how we lose two"
- **TR:** 'Bakmaya gitme, böyle iki kişi kaybederiz.'

### 1844. `RD_ALifeLine_4b88c46d3afa5b5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0060*
- **EN:** "Everybody here smells like a goddamn locker room and nobody's saying it"
- **TR:** 'Burada herkes lanet bir soyunma odası gibi kokuyor, kimse de söylemiyor.'

### 1845. `RD_ALifeLine_53b6e8d639654eaa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0060*
- **EN:** "We're all saying it"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz söylüyoruz.'

### 1846. `RD_ALifeLine_3caf5afe603f5ba2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0060*
- **EN:** 'Say it louder!'
- **TR:** 'Daha yüksek söyle!'

### 1847. `RD_ALifeLine_1b19975a3fcd0e99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0062*
- **EN:** "I don't trust the new guy, he sits too quiet"
- **TR:** 'Yeni adama güvenmiyorum, fazla sessiz oturuyor.'

### 1848. `RD_ALifeLine_265c3aad7beec7c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0062*
- **EN:** "He's just tired"
- **TR:** 'Yorgun sadece.'

### 1849. `RD_ALifeLine_56b10ab72ef7b811` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0062*
- **EN:** "Nobody's that quiet, he's watching us, he's counting"
- **TR:** 'Kimse o kadar sessiz olmaz. Bizi izliyor, sayıyor.'

### 1850. `RD_ALifeLine_20fd4377560ae7dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0062*
- **EN:** "He's counting the cans, like you, genius"
- **TR:** 'Konserveleri sayıyor, tıpkı senin gibi, bilmiş.'

### 1851. `RD_ALifeLine_365f55d428fedf94` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0064*
- **EN:** "Forty-one years old. I'm not going to sit here crying. I'm not"
- **TR:** 'Kırk bir yaşındayım. Burada oturup ağlamayacağım. Ağlamayacağım.'

### 1852. `RD_ALifeLine_6498b08076be5350` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0064*
- **EN:** "You don't have to stop on our account"
- **TR:** 'Bizim yüzümüzden durmana gerek yok.'

### 1853. `RD_ALifeLine_0a00ccc56457a123` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0064*
- **EN:** "It's dust. In my eye. That's all, it's... dust"
- **TR:** 'Toz. Gözüme kaçtı. Hepsi bu... toz.'

### 1854. `RD_ALifeLine_17092dca3d19fb16` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0064*
- **EN:** 'Must be the dust, then'
- **TR:** 'Toz olmalı o zaman.'

### 1855. `RD_ALifeLine_1a82d73c2a2f7eb8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0065*
- **EN:** "I'm leaving tomorrow, I'm done with this, I'll take my chances out there"
- **TR:** 'Yarın gidiyorum, bıktım bundan, şansımı dışarıda deneyeceğim.'

### 1856. `RD_ALifeLine_62c289920109f842` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0065*
- **EN:** "You won't last a day"
- **TR:** 'Bir gün dayanamazsın.'

### 1857. `RD_ALifeLine_596df67638640f6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0065*
- **EN:** "I'll last longer than you in here, you're not even trying!"
- **TR:** 'Senin burada geçirdiğinden uzun dayanırım! Sen denemiyorsun bile!'

### 1858. `RD_ALifeLine_3833f49953a1f32c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0067*
- **EN:** "If I live through this I'm never complaining about a goddamn thing again"
- **TR:** 'Bundan sağ çıkarsam bir daha hiçbir şeyden şikayet etmem, lanet olsun.'

### 1859. `RD_ALifeLine_79b19f82384b4c36` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0067*
- **EN:** "You'll complain by Christmas"
- **TR:** 'Noele kalmaz şikayet edersin.'

### 1860. `RD_ALifeLine_5e04bdf21bad563c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0067*
- **EN:** "I'll complain about the cold, just the cold, and gladly"
- **TR:** 'Sadece soğuktan şikayet ederim, o kadar, hem de seve seve.'

### 1861. `RD_ALifeLine_1a011545408b23e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0067*
- **EN:** 'I give it six weeks'
- **TR:** 'Ben altı hafta veriyorum.'

### 1862. `RD_ALifeLine_78647a793e812539` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0069*
- **EN:** "I found a bottle of something in the garage, it's blue"
- **TR:** 'Garajda bir şişe bir şey buldum, mavi.'

### 1863. `RD_ALifeLine_09d9cdda7d7659bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0069*
- **EN:** "I don't know, it's got a skull on it, but it's a nice skull"
- **TR:** 'Bilmiyorum, üstünde kuru kafa var ama güzel bir kuru kafa.'

### 1864. `RD_ALifeLine_4c00d6ea68e9942c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0069*
- **EN:** 'Put it down, slow, very slow'
- **TR:** 'Yavaşça bırak onu, çok yavaşça.'

### 1865. `RD_ALifeLine_6b4125a2310b0fad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0071*
- **EN:** "If you tell me to pray one more time I'm gonna throw this boot at you"
- **TR:** 'Bir kez daha dua et dersen bu çizmeyi suratına fırlatırım.'

### 1866. `RD_ALifeLine_0785a91b76d9bc57` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0071*
- **EN:** "I'm only trying to help"
- **TR:** 'Sadece yardım etmeye çalışıyorum.'

### 1867. `RD_ALifeLine_7fc58b10057099c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0071*
- **EN:** 'Pray quietly, in your head, like a normal person'
- **TR:** 'Sessizce dua et, içinden, normal insanlar gibi.'

### 1868. `RD_ALifeLine_69798def4cbb0e4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0071*
- **EN:** "Let 'em pray loud, it's how they're wired"
- **TR:** 'Bırak sesli etsin, onlar öyle yapılmış.'

### 1869. `RD_ALifeLine_37353de961016dfa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0073*
- **EN:** "Anybody got a toothbrush? I'm gonna lose a tooth, it's rotting right out of my head"
- **TR:** 'Kimsede diş fırçası var mı? Dişim çürüyüp düşecek, kafamın içinden dökülüyor.'

### 1870. `RD_ALifeLine_4c84ee6b01780cfc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0073*
- **EN:** 'Use your finger'
- **TR:** 'Parmağınla fırçala.'

### 1871. `RD_ALifeLine_14668aba521baf33` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0073*
- **EN:** "My finger's disgusting, I just touched a can of corn"
- **TR:** 'Parmağım pis, az önce bir mısır konservesine dokundum.'

### 1872. `RD_ALifeLine_46999c7315e4d586` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0074*
- **EN:** "Stop looking at the door like somebody's gonna walk through it"
- **TR:** 'Kapıya birisi girecekmiş gibi bakmayı kes.'

### 1873. `RD_ALifeLine_434c4d21014f17a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0074*
- **EN:** 'Somebody might'
- **TR:** 'Girebilir.'

### 1874. `RD_ALifeLine_41d7e6451b43ece9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0074*
- **EN:** "And if they do, you'll look at them like that?"
- **TR:** 'Girerse de ona öyle mi bakacaksın?'

### 1875. `RD_ALifeLine_6076e84c2fd76f11` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0074*
- **EN:** 'Just look at them like you want to be friends'
- **TR:** 'Dost olmak istermiş gibi bak yeter.'

### 1876. `RD_ALifeLine_0d114faa6975b05c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0076*
- **EN:** "I'd trade my house for a six-pack and a cold shower"
- **TR:** 'Evimi bir altılı bira ile soğuk bir duşa değişirim.'

### 1877. `RD_ALifeLine_30bc1fb0380faa12` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0076*
- **EN:** 'Your house is a dump'
- **TR:** 'Evin harabe.'

### 1878. `RD_ALifeLine_3ba5bfab13a38f28` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0076*
- **EN:** "It's a dump with a roof, and I'd still trade it"
- **TR:** 'Çatısı olan bir harabe ve yine de verirdim.'

### 1879. `RD_ALifeLine_6cb865f7323c3531` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0078*
- **EN:** "Why does everybody keep saying 'the infection', it's got a name, hasn't it?"
- **TR:** "Herkes neden hep 'enfeksiyon' diyor, bir adı vardır herhalde?"

### 1880. `RD_ALifeLine_3b81bd2a048af432` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0078*
- **EN:** 'Nobody knows it'
- **TR:** 'Kimse bilmiyor.'

### 1881. `RD_ALifeLine_6ed7890d2fad47d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0078*
- **EN:** 'Somebody knows it, somebody in a lab knows it'
- **TR:** 'Biri biliyordur, bir laboratuvardaki biri biliyordur.'

### 1882. `RD_ALifeLine_0f2dc74c51d230b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0078*
- **EN:** "And they're not telling us, that's the damn point"
- **TR:** 'Ve bize söylemiyorlar, mesele de zaten bu.'

### 1883. `RD_ALifeLine_2aa8b3ac4a46b7b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0080*
- **EN:** 'I was gonna leave my wife, you know that? Two weeks ago, I had the bag packed'
- **TR:** 'Karımı bırakacaktım, biliyor muydun? İki hafta önce, çantam hazırdı.'

### 1884. `RD_ALifeLine_7cfe5a3333145b72` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0080*
- **EN:** "And now I'd walk to Louisville on my knees to be shouted at by her"
- **TR:** "Şimdi ise onun bağırışlarını duymak için Louisville'e dizlerimin üstünde yürürüm."

### 1885. `RD_ALifeLine_4307f26226a4d2eb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0080*
- **EN:** 'Then go find her'
- **TR:** 'O zaman git onu bul.'

### 1886. `RD_ALifeLine_02636076354e7251` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0082*
- **EN:** 'Why the hell is it so quiet?'
- **TR:** 'Neden bu kadar sessiz be?'

### 1887. `RD_ALifeLine_6f043cd510a397d7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0082*
- **EN:** "It's night"
- **TR:** 'Gece.'

### 1888. `RD_ALifeLine_358e61c039a4c756` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0082*
- **EN:** "It's never been this quiet, even at night, even in the middle of nowhere"
- **TR:** 'Hiç bu kadar sessiz olmamıştı, gece bile, ücra köşelerde bile.'

### 1889. `RD_ALifeLine_3fa0411c61bd02f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0082*
- **EN:** "I know, it's like somebody turned the world down"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, sanki biri dünyanın sesini kısmış.'

### 1890. `RD_ALifeLine_26d49500386efe3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0084*
- **EN:** "I'd murder for a hot dog from the gas station, the kind that spins"
- **TR:** 'Benzinlikteki o dönen sosisliler için cinayet işlerim.'

### 1891. `RD_ALifeLine_70ef990411b76b09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0084*
- **EN:** "The kind that's been spinning since Tuesday"
- **TR:** 'Salıdan beri dönenlerden mi?'

### 1892. `RD_ALifeLine_50f19fbc53740e58` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0084*
- **EN:** "That's the best kind, they get a crust"
- **TR:** 'En iyisi o, kabuk bağlarlar.'

### 1893. `RD_ALifeLine_3d21490654fa4405` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0084*
- **EN:** "I've got a lighter if you wanna spin one"
- **TR:** 'Çakmağım var, istersen bir tane döndür.'

### 1894. `RD_ALifeLine_610d4f42080d4eba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0085*
- **EN:** "Don't you tell me it's gonna be okay, don't you dare"
- **TR:** 'Sakın bana her şey yoluna girecek deme, sakın!'

### 1895. `RD_ALifeLine_159dba8c4bddf55b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0085*
- **EN:** "Good, because I'll hit you with this spoon"
- **TR:** 'İyi, yoksa bu kaşıkla vururum.'

### 1896. `RD_ALifeLine_64becd7524c617af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0087*
- **EN:** "My old man used to say you never know what you've got till it's gone, and I thought he was full of shit"
- **TR:** "Babam derdi ki 'Elindekinin kıymetini kaybedene kadar bilmezsin', ben de bok attığını sanırdım."

### 1897. `RD_ALifeLine_3709bce24efb6289` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0087*
- **EN:** 'He was'
- **TR:** 'Atıyordu da.'

### 1898. `RD_ALifeLine_17faeedd196c17b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0087*
- **EN:** "He was full of shit and he was right, that's the worst part"
- **TR:** 'Bok atıyordu ve haklıydı, en kötü yanı bu.'

### 1899. `RD_ALifeLine_045acd015a92e0c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0089*
- **EN:** 'I almost shot a cat tonight, Jesus Christ, I thought it was a person'
- **TR:** 'Bu gece neredeyse bir kediyi vuruyordum, Yarabbi, insan sandım.'

### 1900. `RD_ALifeLine_1cc77b9443f9444c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0089*
- **EN:** "You're getting jumpy"
- **TR:** 'Tetik olmaya başladın.'

### 1901. `RD_ALifeLine_49625c0b42210658` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0089*
- **EN:** "I'm getting accurate, I almost hit it"
- **TR:** 'İsabetli oluyorum, neredeyse vuruyordum.'

### 1902. `RD_ALifeLine_65577ec831a9ae30` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0089*
- **EN:** "Don't shoot cats, they're the last smart things we've got"
- **TR:** 'Kedileri vurma, elimizde kalan son akıllı yaratıklar onlar.'

### 1903. `RD_ALifeLine_7d9515b559df6a26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0091*
- **EN:** 'Somebody ate the last of the crackers and left the wrapper in the can'
- **TR:** 'Biri son krakerleri yiyip ambalajı kutunun içinde bırakmış.'

### 1904. `RD_ALifeLine_31327c5c7c378be1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0091*
- **EN:** "It's more than disgusting, it's a message!"
- **TR:** 'İğrençten öte, bu bir mesaj!'

### 1905. `RD_ALifeLine_69e6e9fb76577780` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0091*
- **EN:** "It's a cracker wrapper, calm down"
- **TR:** 'Kraker ambalajı bu, sakin ol.'

### 1906. `RD_ALifeLine_62df36b5113e6622` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0093*
- **EN:** "I keep dreaming about my mother's kitchen, the light's on, and she's got her back to me"
- **TR:** 'Hep annemin mutfağını görüyorum rüyamda, ışık yanıyor, annem bana sırtını dönmüş.'

### 1907. `RD_ALifeLine_1b85945153e7c877` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0093*
- **EN:** 'What does she say?'
- **TR:** 'Ne diyor?'

### 1908. `RD_ALifeLine_7c451bfc062ba86e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0093*
- **EN:** 'She never turns around, I just stand there'
- **TR:** 'Hiç dönmüyor, ben de öylece duruyorum.'

### 1909. `RD_ALifeLine_4e948b98144dce5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0095*
- **EN:** "If I hear one more joke about the damn beans I'm gonna eat the cook"
- **TR:** 'Şu lanet fasulyeyle ilgili bir şaka daha duyarsam aşçıyı yerim.'

### 1910. `RD_ALifeLine_6d21ba081696ea22` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0095*
- **EN:** "That's cannibalism"
- **TR:** 'Bu yamyamlık.'

### 1911. `RD_ALifeLine_6168eec5462f9ce9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0095*
- **EN:** "That's justice"
- **TR:** 'Bu adalet.'

### 1912. `RD_ALifeLine_71cdce53580e684d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0096*
- **EN:** "Goddamn flies, they're all over the food, I'm gonna lose my fuckin'--"
- **TR:** 'Lanet sinekler, yemeğin her yerinde, kafayı siktiğimin--'

### 1913. `RD_ALifeLine_4f282500742e2767` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0096*
- **EN:** "Wave a rag at 'em"
- **TR:** 'Bir bezle kov onları.'

### 1914. `RD_ALifeLine_5b134a766377f14e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0096*
- **EN:** 'I AM waving a rag, they just land on the rag!'
- **TR:** 'Bezi ZATEN sallıyorum, bezin üstüne konuyorlar!'

### 1915. `RD_ALifeLine_34ae76dd7e4ab472` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0096*
- **EN:** 'Then eat in the other room'
- **TR:** 'O zaman öbür odada ye.'

### 1916. `RD_ALifeLine_1bf14e503c4bcf8d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0098*
- **EN:** 'Do you hear that? Shh-- no, wait, nothing, never mind'
- **TR:** 'Duydun mu? Şşt-- yok, dur, bir şey yok, boş ver.'

### 1917. `RD_ALifeLine_2032f8d926ad1eef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0098*
- **EN:** "You've done that nine times"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz kez yaptın bunu.'

### 1918. `RD_ALifeLine_1cc887ba7a60922f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0098*
- **EN:** "I'm sorry, I'm sorry, I can't turn it off!"
- **TR:** 'Özür dilerim, özür dilerim, kapatamıyorum!'

### 1919. `RD_ALifeLine_695a25db7561edb2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0098*
- **EN:** 'Then go sit by the wall and be quiet'
- **TR:** 'O zaman duvarın dibine otur, sessiz ol.'

### 1920. `RD_ALifeLine_302c6190306b0d7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0100*
- **EN:** "Why the hell didn't we leave Thursday? I told you, I TOLD you, the radio said--"
- **TR:** 'Perşembe günü neden çıkmadık be? Sana söyledim, SÖYLEDİM, radyo--'

### 1921. `RD_ALifeLine_68192fdb3e8330c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0100*
- **EN:** 'The radio said a lot of things'
- **TR:** 'Radyo çok şey söyledi.'

### 1922. `RD_ALifeLine_73a1de74436d68fd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0100*
- **EN:** "It said leave, and you said it's a drill!"
- **TR:** 'Gidin dedi, sen de tatbikat dedin!'

### 1923. `RD_ALifeLine_2653de8e435f4489` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0102*
- **EN:** "I'm not crying, it's the, it's the-- fuck, it's just crying, okay, I'm crying"
- **TR:** 'Ağlamıyorum, bu, bu-- siktir, ağlıyorum işte, tamam, ağlıyorum.'

### 1924. `RD_ALifeLine_762325f4773d8020` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0102*
- **EN:** "Nobody's looking"
- **TR:** 'Kimse bakmıyor.'

### 1925. `RD_ALifeLine_680343de519eb7ab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0102*
- **EN:** 'Look away anyway!'
- **TR:** 'Yine de başka yere bak!'

### 1926. `RD_ALifeLine_34a9a5a847064013` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0104*
- **EN:** 'If you see me turn, shoot me in the head and take my boots'
- **TR:** 'Dönüşürsem kafama sık, çizmelerimi de al.'

### 1927. `RD_ALifeLine_7654421d1d1fa941` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0104*
- **EN:** "And don't wear them in front of my mother, she'd kill you"
- **TR:** 'Annemin yanında da giyme, seni öldürür.'

### 1928. `RD_ALifeLine_07eba23214d2e9b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0104*
- **EN:** "She'd have to get in line"
- **TR:** 'Sıraya girmesi gerekir.'

### 1929. `RD_ALifeLine_51d7e16b289222f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0106*
- **EN:** "If one more person tells me to 'stay positive' I'll scream, I'll fuckin' SCREAM"
- **TR:** "Bir kişi daha 'pozitif kal' derse bağırırım, siktiğimin çığlığını ATARIM!"

### 1930. `RD_ALifeLine_5eb1958e678c9ae0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0106*
- **EN:** 'Stay positive'
- **TR:** 'Pozitif ol.'

### 1931. `RD_ALifeLine_027749a13c1ffcf7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0106*
- **EN:** "I'm warning you!"
- **TR:** 'Seni uyarıyorum!'

### 1932. `RD_ALifeLine_38025f9271d969d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0106*
- **EN:** "Positive's all we've got"
- **TR:** 'Elimizde kalan tek şey pozitiflik.'

### 1933. `RD_ALifeLine_3de4ade824aee67f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0108*
- **EN:** 'Who the fuck cooks beans in a pot without a lid and leaves the spoon in?!'
- **TR:** 'Kim siktiğimin fasulyesini kapaksız tencerede pişirip kaşığı içinde bırakır?!'

### 1934. `RD_ALifeLine_3a634de45062732f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0108*
- **EN:** 'I was gonna come back'
- **TR:** 'Geri gelecektim.'

### 1935. `RD_ALifeLine_7247933b5b0458d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0108*
- **EN:** "You're always gonna come back!"
- **TR:** 'Hep geri gelecektin sen!'

### 1936. `RD_ALifeLine_66b683464c42d305` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0110*
- **EN:** "They could be right outside, right now, standing there, and we wouldn't even know"
- **TR:** 'Hemen dışarıda olabilirler, şu an, orada dikiliyor olabilirler, bizim haberimiz bile olmaz.'

### 1937. `RD_ALifeLine_551077a17c91e02d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0110*
- **EN:** "I'm just SAYING, we're sitting here with the lights off like it's a movie!"
- **TR:** 'SÖYLÜYORUM sadece, ışıklar kapalı, film gibi oturuyoruz!'

### 1938. `RD_ALifeLine_7fe1a5e36d52bd23` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0112*
- **EN:** "You think you're better than me because you've got a gun?"
- **TR:** 'Silahın var diye benden üstün olduğunu mu sanıyorsun?'

### 1939. `RD_ALifeLine_63bbf57a32647adc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0112*
- **EN:** "I didn't say that"
- **TR:** 'Ben bunu demedim.'

### 1940. `RD_ALifeLine_4c801fb80822828c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0112*
- **EN:** 'You looked it!'
- **TR:** 'Öyle baktın!'

### 1941. `RD_ALifeLine_4cd1d96b0b729cfd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0114*
- **EN:** "Goddammit, the lantern's out, who touched the lantern?"
- **TR:** 'Lanet olsun, fener söndü, feneri kim elledi?'

### 1942. `RD_ALifeLine_4669e22119f71067` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0114*
- **EN:** 'Nobody touched it, it just turned itself off, great, FANTASTIC'
- **TR:** 'Kimse dokunmadı, kendi kendine söndü, harika, MUHTEŞEM.'

### 1943. `RD_ALifeLine_60b8cc0b5d2816e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0114*
- **EN:** "It's out of oil, you dumbass"
- **TR:** 'Gazyağı bitmiş, salak.'

### 1944. `RD_ALifeLine_7f41f2c90b84c135` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0116*
- **EN:** "I'm so goddamn tired I just said 'thank you' to a chair"
- **TR:** "O kadar yorgunum ki bir sandalyeye 'teşekkürler' dedim."

### 1945. `RD_ALifeLine_65d987e33b4eaa3c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0116*
- **EN:** "Did it say you're welcome?"
- **TR:** 'Rica ederim dedi mi?'

### 1946. `RD_ALifeLine_6f00439c4783b95b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0116*
- **EN:** 'It kind of creaked'
- **TR:** 'Şey, gıcırdadı.'

### 1947. `RD_ALifeLine_31efe99551810e74` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0116*
- **EN:** 'Good manners'
- **TR:** 'İyi terbiye.'

### 1948. `RD_ALifeLine_40c5af101a6c79ae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0118*
- **EN:** "It's been eight days and nobody's coming! Eight! I counted! It's eight!"
- **TR:** 'Sekiz gün oldu, kimse gelmiyor! Sekiz! Saydım! Sekiz!'

### 1949. `RD_ALifeLine_71eb144531a1f757` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0118*
- **EN:** "I know, I know it's eight"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, sekiz olduğunu biliyorum.'

### 1950. `RD_ALifeLine_4f588c1d5d781e57` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0118*
- **EN:** 'Then why are we still sitting here!'
- **TR:** 'O zaman neden hala burada oturuyoruz!'

### 1951. `RD_ALifeLine_6a3794af2a53088f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0120*
- **EN:** "Quit tapping your foot, it's driving me up the goddamn wall"
- **TR:** 'Ayağını yere vurmayı kes, beni çıldırtıyor.'

### 1952. `RD_ALifeLine_2def561a5cb0f7f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0120*
- **EN:** "I'm not tapping"
- **TR:** 'Vurmuyorum.'

### 1953. `RD_ALifeLine_2afc25f207be8534` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0120*
- **EN:** 'Your whole leg is going like a sewing machine!'
- **TR:** 'Bütün bacağın dikiş makinesi gibi çalışıyor!'

### 1954. `RD_ALifeLine_7faa0ac41bb514fe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0120*
- **EN:** 'Both of you, outside'
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de dışarı.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 1955. `RD_ALifeLine_097d10ec192fcb6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0122*
- **EN:** 'I used to be a good person, I did, I coached Little League'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden iyi bir insandım, gerçekten. Çocuk beyzbol takımını çalıştırdım.'

### 1956. `RD_ALifeLine_6455c3ac0543679d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0122*
- **EN:** "You're still a good person"
- **TR:** 'Hala iyi bir insansın.'

### 1957. `RD_ALifeLine_29dbf60e75af4620` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0122*
- **EN:** 'I stepped over a man on the road, I looked right at him, and I stepped over him'
- **TR:** 'Yolda bir adamın üstünden atladım. Yüzüne baktım, yine de üstünden atladım.'

### 1958. `RD_ALifeLine_2ed086f96c329c96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0122*
- **EN:** 'You did what you had to...'
- **TR:** 'Yapman gerekeni yaptın...'

### 1959. `RD_ALifeLine_5f47e6b31c7210c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0124*
- **EN:** "I told you not to sit on the couch, it's got a stain on it, God knows what it is"
- **TR:** 'Kanepeye oturma dedim sana, üstünde leke var, Allah bilir ne o.'

### 1960. `RD_ALifeLine_2dc4a4153b634f4e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0124*
- **EN:** "It's a couch"
- **TR:** 'Kanepe işte.'

### 1961. `RD_ALifeLine_02918acf639767cf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0124*
- **EN:** "It's a goddamn crime scene!"
- **TR:** 'Lanet bir cinayet mahalli bu!'

### 1962. `RD_ALifeLine_107c442c31793c61` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0124*
- **EN:** "It's coffee, from before, I saw them spill it"
- **TR:** 'Kahve o, eskiden dökmüşler, ben gördüm.'

### 1963. `RD_ALifeLine_46ec10e4359f69b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0126*
- **EN:** "Don't you dare use the good bucket for that"
- **TR:** 'Sakın o iş için iyi kovayı kullanma.'

### 1964. `RD_ALifeLine_6466d46c4f3834db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0126*
- **EN:** "It's a bucket"
- **TR:** 'Kova işte.'

### 1965. `RD_ALifeLine_2efe12db5f099f7c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0126*
- **EN:** "It's THE good bucket, it's got a handle!"
- **TR:** 'O İYİ kova, kulpu var!'

### 1966. `RD_ALifeLine_241212d129d3b039` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0128*
- **EN:** "If the world ends and I'm still stuck with you, I want it on record, I didn't pick this"
- **TR:** 'Dünya biter de hala seninle kalırsam, kayıtlara geçsin, bunu ben seçmedim.'

### 1967. `RD_ALifeLine_31e7097376e703ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0128*
- **EN:** 'Write it down, I want a lawyer'
- **TR:** 'Yaz şunu, avukat istiyorum.'

### 1968. `RD_ALifeLine_4514f2cf4c1d2395` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0130*
- **EN:** 'Everybody needs to stop being nice, the nice is gonna get us killed'
- **TR:** 'Herkesin iyi olmayı bırakması lazım, bu iyilik bizi öldürecek.'

### 1969. `RD_ALifeLine_21458a4f699e645f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0130*
- **EN:** "We're not being nice"
- **TR:** 'İyi davranmıyoruz ki.'

### 1970. `RD_ALifeLine_35c8858f2b9c4f91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0130*
- **EN:** 'You offered the guy a SANDWICH, I saw you!'
- **TR:** 'O adama SANDVİÇ ikram ettin, gördüm!'

### 1971. `RD_ALifeLine_5276d2d53fc46465` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0132*
- **EN:** "I've been thinking, if I get out of this alive I'm gonna quit my job and open a fuckin' bait shop"
- **TR:** 'Düşünüyordum da, bundan sağ çıkarsam işimi bırakıp siktiğimin bir balık yemi dükkanını açacağım.'

### 1972. `RD_ALifeLine_2426ff4f3a578f2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0132*
- **EN:** "I don't hate fish, I hate fishermen, they'd be the customers"
- **TR:** 'Balıktan nefret etmiyorum, balıkçılardan nefret ediyorum, müşterilerim onlar olacak.'

### 1973. `RD_ALifeLine_121110f116b07e50` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0132*
- **EN:** "Bait shop and a hot dog stand, that's the plan"
- **TR:** 'Yem dükkanı ve bir sosisli standı, plan bu.'

### 1974. `RD_ALifeLine_74e521c2143c6225` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0044*
- **EN:** "My bike's out of gas"
- **TR:** 'Motorun benzini bitti.'

### 1975. `RD_ALifeLine_6283b83c16100ecf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0044*
- **EN:** "Mine's out of everything"
- **TR:** 'Benimkinin her şeyi bitti.'

### 1976. `RD_ALifeLine_4f5540950de00110` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0044*
- **EN:** 'I keep sitting on it, just to feel it'
- **TR:** 'Hep gidip üstüne oturuyorum, sırf o hissi duymak için.'

### 1977. `RD_ALifeLine_310e620d4bb25f9a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0044*
- **EN:** "That's sad, man"
- **TR:** 'Çok acıklı bu, dostum.'

### 1978. `RD_ALifeLine_2dd379b840c7d40e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0044*
- **EN:** "It's a good feeling, you wouldn't know"
- **TR:** 'İyi bir his o. Sen bilmezsin.'

### 1979. `RD_ALifeLine_327b86b35982f8a0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0045*
- **EN:** 'I miss telling a prospect to get me a beer'
- **TR:** 'Çaylağa "git bana bir bira getir" demeyi özledim.'

### 1980. `RD_ALifeLine_7d6ba83e06a2fa94` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0045*
- **EN:** "Even when we didn't have any?"
- **TR:** 'Hiç bira yokken bile mi?'

### 1981. `RD_ALifeLine_5017da7a32d2d1e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0045*
- **EN:** "Then I'd tell him to get me something wet"
- **TR:** 'O zaman "git bana ıslak bir şey getir" derdim.'

### 1982. `RD_ALifeLine_0287852e1cc7da5b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0045*
- **EN:** "And he'd offer water"
- **TR:** 'O da gidip su getirirdi.'

### 1983. `RD_ALifeLine_0d10776a18ea243e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0045*
- **EN:** "I'd call it an insult and ask for beer again"
- **TR:** 'Hakaret sayıp tekrar bira isterdim.'

### 1984. `RD_ALifeLine_0e39fdc7228ef68e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0045*
- **EN:** "I'd have brought you a bucket. Kept hoping you'd get the joke"
- **TR:** 'Ben sana kova getirirdim. Şakayı anlarsın diye umut edip dururdum.'

### 1985. `RD_ALifeLine_549d45d939619446` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'Pipes, way off'
- **TR:** 'Egzoz sesi, çok uzaktan.'

### 1986. `RD_ALifeLine_5501b0d52cfb2b94` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0046*
- **EN:** "That's a V-twin, I'd know it anywhere"
- **TR:** 'V-twin o, nerede duysam tanırım.'

### 1987. `RD_ALifeLine_4c1a70540be85394` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0046*
- **EN:** "Nobody we know, whoever it is, he's got a nice sound"
- **TR:** 'Bizden biri değil, kim bilir kim. Ama sesi güzelmiş.'

### 1988. `RD_ALifeLine_2c2ad09a6977edab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'Nice sound, wrong road'
- **TR:** 'Ses güzel, yol yanlış.'

### 1989. `RD_ALifeLine_79daabd11296cc1c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Polished that bike every Sunday for six years'
- **TR:** 'O motoru altı yıl boyunca her pazar cilaladım.'

### 1990. `RD_ALifeLine_459d684e67202397` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0047*
- **EN:** "It's the cleanest bike in the county"
- **TR:** 'İlçenin en temiz motoru o.'

### 1991. `RD_ALifeLine_1370d34c54c3f934` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0047*
- **EN:** "And now I can't get it started"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi çalıştıramıyorum bile.'

### 1992. `RD_ALifeLine_0bab8612546cc17a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Maybe it just needs a little time'
- **TR:** 'Belki biraz zamana ihtiyacı vardır.'

### 1993. `RD_ALifeLine_1a346f37776230f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Maybe it needs gas'
- **TR:** 'Belki benzine ihtiyacı vardır.'

### 1994. `RD_ALifeLine_389201e663de6f91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0048*
- **EN:** 'I rode all the way to Sturgis once on a bad clutch'
- **TR:** "Bir keresinde bozuk debriyajla Sturgis'e kadar sürdüm."

### 1995. `RD_ALifeLine_57efdb4a321197b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0048*
- **EN:** 'Fourteen hundred miles, I had a rope tied to my left hand'
- **TR:** 'İki bin iki yüz elli kilometre, sol elime de ip bağlamıştım.'
- *Not: 1400 miles -> 2250 km*

### 1996. `RD_ALifeLine_2320d24d2f93cc53` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0048*
- **EN:** "You're lying, but go on"
- **TR:** 'Yalan söylüyorsun ama devam et.'

### 1997. `RD_ALifeLine_6493636e567619f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0048*
- **EN:** "Let him lie, it's better than the quiet"
- **TR:** 'Bırak yalan söylesin, sessizlikten iyidir.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 1998. `RD_ALifeLine_45695e2218b0afc2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'Road captain says we move at dawn'
- **TR:** 'Yol kaptanı şafakta çıkıyoruz diyor.'

### 1999. `RD_ALifeLine_75911bd30c6f1741` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0049*
- **EN:** "He didn't say, he said dawn"
- **TR:** 'Hangi yöne demedi, sadece şafak dedi.'

### 2000. `RD_ALifeLine_6e05d3681c11db6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0049*
- **EN:** "That's all you get from him, dawn"
- **TR:** 'Ondan alacağın tek laf bu zaten, şafak.'

### 2001. `RD_ALifeLine_317c46e05edac898` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0049*
- **EN:** "Dawn's good enough"
- **TR:** 'Şafak yeter.'

### 2002. `RD_ALifeLine_08370bec3dd4463b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0050*
- **EN:** 'I earned that patch'
- **TR:** 'O armayı hak ettim ben.'

### 2003. `RD_ALifeLine_1a1ba7691d30f810` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0050*
- **EN:** 'I know you did'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, ettin.'

### 2004. `RD_ALifeLine_45cbcf5e57b6d162` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0050*
- **EN:** 'I earned it with a broken wrist and a ticket in three states'
- **TR:** 'Kırık bir bilek, üç eyalette yediğim cezalar... Hak ettim ben onu.'

### 2005. `RD_ALifeLine_3ab7d3e35e3c81ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0050*
- **EN:** "Nobody's taking your patch"
- **TR:** 'Armanı kimse almıyor.'

### 2006. `RD_ALifeLine_6a44e6cc742353a0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0050*
- **EN:** "Anybody tries, we'll have a conversation"
- **TR:** 'Deneyen çıkarsa bir güzel konuşuruz.'

### 2007. `RD_ALifeLine_0f4679846db828b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0051*
- **EN:** 'Anybody got a bandana?'
- **TR:** 'Kimde bandana var?'

### 2008. `RD_ALifeLine_2564d42521259ce3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0051*
- **EN:** 'My nose, the smell in here'
- **TR:** 'Burnuma takacağım, buranın kokusundan.'

### 2009. `RD_ALifeLine_731a3cbf6ac9647c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0051*
- **EN:** "It's your smell"
- **TR:** 'O senin kokun.'

### 2010. `RD_ALifeLine_0671e88026437a65` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0051*
- **EN:** "It's his smell and mine and everybody's, take it up with God"
- **TR:** "Senin kokun, benim kokum, herkesin kokusu. Derdin varsa git Allah'a anlat."

### 2011. `RD_ALifeLine_30a2c475032f8965` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'Used to be nothing like a Sunday run, forty bikes and the road open'

### 2012. `RD_ALifeLine_7786485d7e26e843` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'Quit it'
- **TR:** 'Kes şunu.'

### 2013. `RD_ALifeLine_18518d1e5c4de3b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0052*
- **EN:** "I'm just saying, I can still hear it"
- **TR:** 'Bir şey demiyorum, hala kulağımda ama.'

### 2014. `RD_ALifeLine_27473ce75fe463f6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0078*
- **EN:** 'Two liters'
- **TR:** 'İki litre.'

### 2015. `RD_ALifeLine_1f32b97c55e07f53` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0078*
- **EN:** 'Rotation'
- **TR:** 'Nöbet düzeni.'

### 2016. `RD_ALifeLine_6ffde63178d11b9c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0078*
- **EN:** 'Four on, four off, same as yesterday'

### 2072. `RD_ALifeLine_141e084272ca4164` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0108*
- **EN:** "Can't hear a thing, just shout it"
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şey duyamıyorum, sen bağır gitsin.'

### 2073. `RD_ALifeLine_36d230025bebb275` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0109*
- **EN:** "In '37 the river came up to Main Street, my daddy rowed a boat down Fourth"
- **TR:** "'37'de nehir Main Street'e kadar yükseldi, babam Dördüncü Cadde'den kayıkla geçti."

### 2074. `RD_ALifeLine_3b0682e5096f4577` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0109*
- **EN:** 'You told me'
- **TR:** 'Anlatmıştın.'

### 2075. `RD_ALifeLine_6d8b7e58180da4ef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0109*
- **EN:** "I'm telling you again, he rowed past the drugstore, past the movie house"
- **TR:** 'Yine anlatıyorum, eczanenin önünden geçti, sinemanın önünden geçti.'

### 2076. `RD_ALifeLine_2a248a2d31a9c70b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0109*
- **EN:** 'Tell it, I like it'
- **TR:** 'Anlat, hoşuma gidiyor.'

### 2077. `RD_ALifeLine_5af334b37bb94c71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0109*
- **EN:** 'I like it too, every time'
- **TR:** 'Benim de, her seferinde.'

### 2078. `RD_ALifeLine_75ad247b3b98a191` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0110*

### 2079. `RD_ALifeLine_0d309282419c1415` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0110*
- **EN:** 'Four?'

### 2080. `RD_ALifeLine_73c426e701e95679` — Claude (yeni)

- **EN:** 'Four if she takes half'
- **TR:** 'Yarım içerse dört.'

### 2081. `RD_ALifeLine_1d49d1a5496e982c` — Claude (yeni)

### 2082. `RD_ALifeLine_75faab58715e0e74` — Claude (yeni)

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0111*

### 2086. `RD_ALifeLine_1b83ecef5f53e27c` — Claude (yeni)

- **TR:** "MTV'yi mi özledin!"

### 2089. `RD_ALifeLine_7aa55aa1530af4dd` — Claude (yeni)

- **TR:** 'Yirmi altı yaşındayım ben.'

### 2094. `RD_ALifeLine_515c2c3372d87126` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0114*
- **EN:** 'You took my turn'
- **TR:** 'Sıramı aldın.'

### 2095. `RD_ALifeLine_598eb3dd0c3b9faa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0114*
- **EN:** 'I was next, you cut'
- **TR:** 'Sıra bendeydi, sen araya girdin.'

### 2096. `RD_ALifeLine_5668ed66496c9be6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0114*
- **EN:** "You're not the boss because you're older"
- **TR:** 'Yaşça büyüksün diye patron olmuyorsun.'

### 2097. `RD_ALifeLine_32b84cad44799a5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0114*
- **EN:** "I'm the boss because I'm louder"
- **TR:** 'Ben daha çok bağırdığım için patronum.'

### 2098. `RD_ALifeLine_7a84746848cda584` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0115*
- **EN:** "Daddy's chair is empty, nobody sits in it"
- **TR:** 'Babamın sandalyesi boş, kimse oturmuyor.'

### 2099. `RD_ALifeLine_2dc0792a3a6df679` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0115*
- **EN:** "It's a chair"
- **TR:** 'Sandalye işte.'

### 2100. `RD_ALifeLine_6f715e5021687e7b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0115*
- **EN:** "It's HIS chair, it's got the shape of him in it!"
- **TR:** 'ONUN sandalyesi, içinde onun şekli duruyor!'

### 2101. `RD_ALifeLine_3724f9ff34d4aa2c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0115*
- **EN:** '...okay, nobody sits in it'
- **TR:** '...Tamam, kimse oturmuyor.'

### 2102. `RD_ALifeLine_7f02cb262b407d45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0116*
- **EN:** 'I used to dread Sunday dinner, forty relatives and a ham'
- **TR:** 'Pazar yemeğinden nefret ederdim, kırk akraba ve bir jambon.'

### 2103. `RD_ALifeLine_408762351c5e457a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0116*
- **EN:** "Now I'd give a kidney for the ham"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi o jambon için böbreğimi veririm.'

### 2104. `RD_ALifeLine_5eaab9017937f6f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0116*
- **EN:** "Take mine, I've got two"
- **TR:** 'Al benimkini, bende iki tane var.'

### 2105. `RD_ALifeLine_46ec971c3e39ec7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0117*
- **EN:** "I'm bored"
- **TR:** 'Sıkıldım.'

### 2106. `RD_ALifeLine_2c64bbe02ff397d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0117*
- **EN:** 'Read a book'
- **TR:** 'Kitap oku.'

### 2107. `RD_ALifeLine_7c5ac7a5211c4b07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0117*
- **EN:** "There's no books"
- **TR:** 'Kitap yok ki.'

### 2108. `RD_ALifeLine_7f7d28b846966a48` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0117*
- **EN:** 'Read a cereal box'
- **TR:** 'Gevrek kutusunu oku.'

### 2109. `RD_ALifeLine_23e75a3628dd176b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0117*
- **EN:** "I did, it's got a maze"
- **TR:** 'Okudum, labirent var.'

### 2110. `RD_ALifeLine_6966ed9a3020ef70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0117*
- **EN:** 'Do the maze'
- **TR:** 'Labirenti çöz o zaman.'

### 2111. `RD_ALifeLine_109c4c0e2a9ceb8f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0118*
- **EN:** "Grandpa's pocketknife, he carried it every day for sixty years"
- **TR:** 'Dedemin çakısı, altmış yıl boyunca her gün yanında taşıdı.'

### 2112. `RD_ALifeLine_446caa1c78d051fe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0118*
- **EN:** "And now it's yours"
- **TR:** 'Ve şimdi senin.'

### 2113. `RD_ALifeLine_04399e8f345fb0a2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0118*
- **EN:** "It's got his thumb in the handle, there's a groove"
- **TR:** 'Sapında onun başparmağının izi var, bir oyuk oluşmuş.'

### 2114. `RD_ALifeLine_6772da71770b31dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0118*
- **EN:** "Don't lose it..."
- **TR:** 'Sakın kaybetme...'

### 2115. `RD_ALifeLine_6441d96b1c1079f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0119*
- **EN:** "This quilt's the warmest thing I own"
- **TR:** 'Bu yorgan sahip olduğum en sıcak şey.'

### 2116. `RD_ALifeLine_0aa734de736fd2a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0119*
- **EN:** "It's too hot for a quilt"
- **TR:** 'Yorgan için hava çok sıcak.'

### 2117. `RD_ALifeLine_47678f724968e58a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0119*
- **EN:** "It's for the smell, the heat's got nothing to do with it"
- **TR:** 'Kokusu için sarılıyorum, sıcakla alakası yok.'

### 2118. `RD_ALifeLine_56a993766750c158` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0119*
- **EN:** 'What does it smell like'
- **TR:** 'Ne kokuyor peki?'

### 2119. `RD_ALifeLine_4bce25230152c61d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0120*
- **EN:** "Who's got the baby"
- **TR:** 'Bebek kimde?'

### 2120. `RD_ALifeLine_201284c95241617d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0120*
- **EN:** 'I gave her to you'
- **TR:** 'Ben sana verdim.'

### 2121. `RD_ALifeLine_78664b62470086a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0120*
- **EN:** 'You gave her to Mama'
- **TR:** 'Sen anneme verdin.'

### 2122. `RD_ALifeLine_03019aba43fa2fe1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0120*
- **EN:** "Mama has her, Mama's had her for an hour, relax"
- **TR:** 'Bebek annemde, bir saattir annemde, sakin ol.'

### 2123. `RD_ALifeLine_212b25e42d03ec9c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0121*
- **EN:** 'I turned seventy-nine last month'
- **TR:** 'Geçen ay yetmiş dokuz yaşına girdim.'

### 2124. `RD_ALifeLine_087e4a4933b1035d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0121*
- **EN:** 'Happy birthday'
- **TR:** 'Doğum günün kutlu olsun.'

### 2125. `RD_ALifeLine_0cd9205111072c0e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0121*
- **EN:** 'I was gonna have a cake, a little one, with one candle'
- **TR:** 'Bir pasta yapacaktım, küçük, tek mumlu.'

### 2126. `RD_ALifeLine_535950246672a2fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0121*
- **EN:** 'Next year'
- **TR:** 'Seneye.'

### 2127. `RD_ALifeLine_538cf0e51cd84581` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0121*
- **EN:** 'Next year, sure'
- **TR:** 'Seneye, tabii.'

### 2128. `RD_ALifeLine_4f3f02c33992bb74` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0122*
- **EN:** 'Somebody likes somebody here'
- **TR:** 'Burada biri birine tutulmuş.'

### 2129. `RD_ALifeLine_6b79cae75faa28b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0122*
- **EN:** "I'm not saying, I'm just saying somebody keeps looking"
- **TR:** 'Söylemiyorum ben, sadece biri durmadan bakıyor diyorum.'

### 2130. `RD_ALifeLine_5bef73103e631227` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0122*
- **EN:** "It's the cat, you idiot"
- **TR:** 'Kedi o, salak.'

### 2131. `RD_ALifeLine_2bc1786e24a3ab56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0123*
- **EN:** 'Chores'
- **TR:** 'Ev işleri.'

### 2132. `RD_ALifeLine_098a55eb7220860f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0123*
- **EN:** "There's no chores"
- **TR:** 'İş yok ki.'

### 2133. `RD_ALifeLine_182898db50d1380e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0123*
- **EN:** "There's always chores, somebody's gotta do the dishes"
- **TR:** 'İş hep vardır, birinin bulaşığı yıkaması lazım.'

### 2134. `RD_ALifeLine_23da59cf0108b947` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0123*
- **EN:** 'What dishes'
- **TR:** 'Ne bulaşığı?'

### 2135. `RD_ALifeLine_22816acc02d4bca2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0123*
- **EN:** 'The can lids, stack them in the corner'
- **TR:** 'Konserve kapakları, onları köşeye diz.'

### 2136. `RD_ALifeLine_1c39f3574acde506` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0124*
- **EN:** 'Tell the one about when you were little'
- **TR:** 'Küçükken başına gelen şeyi anlat.'

### 2137. `RD_ALifeLine_1307b6672875cb52` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0124*
- **EN:** 'The one with the chicken'
- **TR:** 'Tavuklu olanı.'

### 2138. `RD_ALifeLine_337195a85fe1e9c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0124*
- **EN:** 'I was five, there was a chicken in the kitchen, it had gotten in'
- **TR:** 'Beş yaşındaydım, mutfakta bir tavuk vardı, içeri girmişti.'

### 2139. `RD_ALifeLine_51a2bc46504405e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0124*
- **EN:** "And it was Mama's good chicken, the one she was saving for Easter"
- **TR:** 'Hem de annemin Paskalya için sakladığı o güzel tavuktu.'

### 2140. `RD_ALifeLine_0c3f81871e79de80` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0053*
- **EN:** 'Boss said we hold this block'
- **TR:** 'Patron bu bloğu tutacağız dedi.'

### 2141. `RD_ALifeLine_38cc21856551caea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0053*
- **EN:** "There's no block, there's a house"
- **TR:** 'Blok yok, bir ev var.'

### 2142. `RD_ALifeLine_12e73fca099d54dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0053*
- **EN:** 'Then we hold the house'
- **TR:** 'O zaman evi tutarız.'

### 2143. `RD_ALifeLine_48f8ddd9041f0b1a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0053*
- **EN:** "It's not ours"
- **TR:** 'Bizim değil.'

### 2144. `RD_ALifeLine_37c275d6767262dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0053*
- **EN:** "It's ours now, I don't care"
- **TR:** 'Artık bizim, umurumda değil.'

### 2145. `RD_ALifeLine_0412a5e50c965a66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0053*
- **EN:** "It's got a roof, it's ours"
- **TR:** 'Çatısı var, bizim.'

### 2146. `RD_ALifeLine_35315be872f36ec1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0054*
- **EN:** 'We used to run the whole east side'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden doğu yakasının hepsini biz yönetirdik.'

### 2147. `RD_ALifeLine_5906254c436d1e83` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0054*
- **EN:** 'We had a corner store, a car wash, a little bit of everything'
- **TR:** 'Bir bakkalımız vardı, bir oto yıkamamız, biraz da her şeyden.'

### 2148. `RD_ALifeLine_12da720d2ec2c916` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0054*
- **EN:** "I know, I was there, now we've got a bottle"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, oradaydım. Şimdi elimizde bir şişe var.'

### 2149. `RD_ALifeLine_639264d3191d4b7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0054*
- **EN:** "A bottle's something..."
- **TR:** 'Bir şişe de bir şeydir...'

### 2150. `RD_ALifeLine_0dd6d8860792b6aa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0055*
- **EN:** "The old man's gonna want his cut"
- **TR:** 'İhtiyar payını isteyecek.'

### 2151. `RD_ALifeLine_524d5c5332321b0a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0055*
- **EN:** "There's no cut, there's no old man"
- **TR:** 'Pay yok, ihtiyar da yok.'

### 2152. `RD_ALifeLine_2095de7954b3cdf8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0055*
- **EN:** "He's alive, I know it"
- **TR:** 'Hayatta, biliyorum.'

### 2153. `RD_ALifeLine_76ef3f3d34f1ae9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0055*
