- **EN:** "Then he's running late, and when he arrives he'll want his cut"
- **TR:** 'O zaman geç kalıyor, gelince de payını ister.'

### 2154. `RD_ALifeLine_6623ef6c611a0812` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0055*
- **EN:** "Then we'd better have a cut"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir payımız olsa iyi olur.'

### 2155. `RD_ALifeLine_570cc7f60ad3d4d9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0056*
- **EN:** 'I do business with anybody'
- **TR:** 'Ben herkesle iş yaparım.'

### 2156. `RD_ALifeLine_50cef36c5b572537` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0056*
- **EN:** "There's nobody left to do business with"
- **TR:** 'İş yapacak kimse kalmadı ki.'

### 2157. `RD_ALifeLine_386e0a2a7b6ed485` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0056*
- **EN:** "There's always somebody, there's a guy down the road with six chickens"
- **TR:** 'Hep biri vardır, yolun aşağısında altı tavuklu bir adam var.'

### 2158. `RD_ALifeLine_19d447625b71083e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0056*
- **EN:** 'What do we have to trade?'
- **TR:** 'Takas edecek neyimiz var?'

### 2159. `RD_ALifeLine_7d0866811adef9f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0056*
- **EN:** 'A story about chickens'
- **TR:** 'Tavuk hakkında bir hikaye.'

### 2160. `RD_ALifeLine_5094c1c04e5f8933` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0056*
- **EN:** "I'd buy a chicken story"
- **TR:** 'Ben tavuk hikayesi alırım.'

### 2161. `RD_ALifeLine_48323e8f5a1bb9db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'Funny how nobody calls the cops now'
- **TR:** 'Ne komik, artık kimse polisi aramıyor.'

### 2162. `RD_ALifeLine_1844505505755e2e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'I miss that, I miss being a little bit worried'
- **TR:** 'Onu özledim, biraz tedirgin olmayı özledim.'

### 2163. `RD_ALifeLine_771bdd474565c698` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'You were never worried'
- **TR:** 'Sen hiç tedirgin olmazdın.'

### 2164. `RD_ALifeLine_017712274e302a50` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'I was worried about my mother, finding out'
- **TR:** 'Annem öğrenecek diye tedirgin olurdum.'

### 2165. `RD_ALifeLine_4145f54242f77c9f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0058*
- **EN:** "This crew isn't my family"
- **TR:** 'Bu ekip benim ailem değil.'

### 2166. `RD_ALifeLine_65febc016455763e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0058*
- **EN:** "It's all you've got"
- **TR:** 'Elindeki tek şey bu.'

### 2167. `RD_ALifeLine_3d267318185f9f5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0058*
- **EN:** "It's all I've got, that's what I'm saying, it's not enough"
- **TR:** 'Elimdeki tek şey bu, ben de onu diyorum, yetmiyor.'

### 2168. `RD_ALifeLine_4c4eadf879b7f59c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0058*
- **EN:** "It's enough for tonight..."
- **TR:** 'Bu gece için yeter...'

### 2169. `RD_ALifeLine_5f3ed9f315283bef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0059*
- **EN:** 'I ran booze into dry counties for six years'
- **TR:** 'Altı yıl boyunca içkinin yasak olduğu ilçelere kaçak içki soktum.'

### 2170. `RD_ALifeLine_272b8e7b6b8923c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0059*
- **EN:** 'Hardin was dry, you could drive a truck of Beam through at three in the morning and the sheriff waved'
- **TR:** "Hardin'de içki yasaktı, sabaha karşı üçte kamyon dolusu Beam geçirirdin, şerif el sallardı."

### 2171. `RD_ALifeLine_7744b05e6b5b346c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0059*
- **EN:** 'The sheriff was in the truck'
- **TR:** 'Şerif kamyonun içindeydi.'

### 2172. `RD_ALifeLine_31e34fcf7389d070` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'You owe me a favor'
- **TR:** 'Bana bir iyiliğin borcu var.'

### 2173. `RD_ALifeLine_159c827635b3c9c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0060*
- **EN:** "I-- I don't owe you a damn thing"
- **TR:** 'Ben-- Sana kahrolası bir şey borçlu değilim.'

### 2174. `RD_ALifeLine_0668f1c128baceb1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'You owe me one for the thing at the quarry'
- **TR:** 'Taş ocağındaki iş için bir borcun var.'

### 2175. `RD_ALifeLine_50dbc72a4012766c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0060*
- **EN:** "That's a favor from two years ago"
- **TR:** 'O iki yıl önceki bir iyilik.'

### 2176. `RD_ALifeLine_3dc0c0265f813cfa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0060*
- **EN:** "Favors don't expire, ask my cousin"
- **TR:** 'İyiliğin son kullanma tarihi olmaz, kuzenime sor.'

### 2177. `RD_ALifeLine_2ab90cb201fd81e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, s13_law_school_cafeteria_042*
- **EN:** "They're putting families in the high school cafeteria and asking us to keep order"
- **TR:** 'Aileleri lise kafeteryasına yerleştiriyorlar, bir de düzeni bizden sağlamamızı istiyorlar.'

### 2178. `RD_ALifeLine_429587a751efca2e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, s13_law_school_cafeteria_042*
- **EN:** 'With our faces, I guess, four of us and two hundred and something of them'
- **TR:** 'Suratlarımızla herhalde. Biz dört kişi, onlar iki yüz küsur.'

### 2179. `RD_ALifeLine_54d8eedb482cfffb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, s13_law_school_cafeteria_042*
- **EN:** "Two hundred and sixty, I counted at the door 'cause somebody ought to have the number"
- **TR:** 'İki yüz altmış. Kapıda saydım, birinin elinde sayı olmalı.'

### 2180. `RD_ALifeLine_67ba2dab7cacd515` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0001*
- **EN:** 'Nothing on the net since Sunday'
- **TR:** 'Pazardan beri telsizde tık yok.'

### 2181. `RD_ALifeLine_17bdb1b109929b46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0001*
- **EN:** 'I tried at noon, not even static'
- **TR:** 'Öğlen denedim, parazit bile yok.'

### 2182. `RD_ALifeLine_12167a823e4148a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0001*
- **EN:** "Static I could take, static means somebody's carrier is up, this is fucking nothing"
- **TR:** 'Parazite razıyım, parazit demek biri yayında demek. Bu ise koca bir hiç, siktir.'

### 2183. `RD_ALifeLine_6ec99e3f2b3a2b1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0001*
- **EN:** "It's dead air, dead fucking dead"
- **TR:** 'Hat ölü, siktiğimin ölüsü.'

### 2184. `RD_ALifeLine_0d9d32f9459a3b47` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0002*
- **EN:** "Who's keeping the log?"
- **TR:** 'Kayıt defterini kim tutuyor?'

### 2185. `RD_ALifeLine_3c093b2c2fab65cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0002*
- **EN:** 'What are you putting down for today?'
- **TR:** 'Bugün için ne yazıyorsun?'

### 2186. `RD_ALifeLine_4006ed2a3fd1f8a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0002*
- **EN:** 'Nothing happened'
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şey olmadı.'

### 2187. `RD_ALifeLine_352f94a8508301a2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0002*
- **EN:** 'Put something'
- **TR:** 'Bir şey yaz.'

### 2188. `RD_ALifeLine_70c089820fd9f8e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0002*
- **EN:** "Fine, 'still here'"
- **TR:** 'Tamam, "hala buradayız".'

### 2189. `RD_ALifeLine_3f73063864a8a1fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0002*
- **EN:** 'Put the time, too, the Sheriff always wanted the goddamn time'
- **TR:** 'Saati de yaz, Şerif o lanet saati hep isterdi.'

### 2190. `RD_ALifeLine_060196d958b4982d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0003*
- **EN:** "Take your vest off, you're dripping"
- **TR:** 'Yeleğini çıkar, damlıyorsun.'

### 2191. `RD_ALifeLine_4d8c0d522014d461` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0003*
- **EN:** 'Not allowed'
- **TR:** 'Yasak.'

### 2192. `RD_ALifeLine_790d62d978ccdebd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0003*
- **EN:** "Who's gonna write you up, the fucking Sheriff?"
- **TR:** 'Seni kim tutanağa geçirecek, siktiğimin Şerifi mi?'

### 2193. `RD_ALifeLine_1f28dd1b7c85aac0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0003*
- **EN:** "Habit, I put it on in the morning like putting on pants, I'd feel naked"
- **TR:** 'Alışkanlık. Sabah pantolon giyer gibi giyiyorum, çıplak hissederim.'

### 2194. `RD_ALifeLine_2341d2402e931d83` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0003*
- **EN:** "Habit's not an order. If you need a break, say so before you overheat"
- **TR:** 'Alışkanlık emir değil. Mola gerekiyorsa aşırı ısınmadan söyle.'

### 2195. `RD_ALifeLine_514a533038912a3c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0004*
- **EN:** "I was supposed to be off Friday, I was supposed to be at a fuckin' ballgame"
- **TR:** 'Cuma izinli olacaktım, bir maça gidecektim, siktir.'

### 2196. `RD_ALifeLine_7e73af6116ea1990` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'Everybody was supposed to be off Friday'
- **TR:** 'Herkes cuma izinli olacaktı.'

### 2197. `RD_ALifeLine_48d88d227c9365ec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'I traded shifts for a ballgame'
- **TR:** 'Maç için vardiya değiştirmiştim.'

### 2198. `RD_ALifeLine_7fb5745252e6d7ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'Did you go'
- **TR:** 'Gittin mi?'

### 2199. `RD_ALifeLine_58b4047839bd0eec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'Never made it, got called in at four'
- **TR:** 'Hiç gidemedim, dörtte göreve çağırdılar.'

### 2200. `RD_ALifeLine_0b4852130e718fdf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'Reds game?'
- **TR:** 'Reds maçı mıydı?'

### 2201. `RD_ALifeLine_5282a73601a9074f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0005*
- **EN:** 'How many we got?'
- **TR:** 'Kaç tane var?'

### 2202. `RD_ALifeLine_6ef1dfad2823b714` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0005*
- **EN:** "Six, I'm not counting that one"
- **TR:** 'Altı, şunu saymıyorum.'

### 2203. `RD_ALifeLine_4610173836e63610` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0005*
- **EN:** "That one's got a name, it's Wendell"
- **TR:** 'Onun bir adı var, Wendell.'

### 2204. `RD_ALifeLine_664091dc1ccba9c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0006*
- **EN:** 'Sheriff said hold at the substation'
- **TR:** 'Şerif alt karakolda bekleyin dedi.'

### 2205. `RD_ALifeLine_28641b8540098358` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0006*
- **EN:** 'Sheriff stopped answering Saturday'
- **TR:** 'Şerif cumartesiden beri cevap vermiyor.'

### 2206. `RD_ALifeLine_678137670156b375` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0006*
- **EN:** "So who's giving the orders now?"
- **TR:** 'Peki emirleri kim veriyor şimdi?'

### 2207. `RD_ALifeLine_6006cf0a5e1d6b93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0006*
- **EN:** 'Nobody, so we hold'
- **TR:** 'Kimse, o yüzden bekliyoruz.'

### 2208. `RD_ALifeLine_108987ac31c57db4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0006*
- **EN:** 'Hold what, exactly'
- **TR:** 'Neyi tutuyoruz tam olarak?'

### 2209. `RD_ALifeLine_4a7050a76ec644c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'I was gonna put in my papers in September'
- **TR:** 'Eylülde emeklilik dilekçemi verecektim.'

### 2210. `RD_ALifeLine_1ff05c883a485cdb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Retire?'
- **TR:** 'Emekli mi?'

### 2211. `RD_ALifeLine_6cf089c4125d4f60` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Thirty-one years in September'
- **TR:** 'Eylülde otuz bir yıl.'

### 2212. `RD_ALifeLine_638037f04de6bd07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Thirty-one, damn'
- **TR:** 'Otuz bir, vay be.'

### 2213. `RD_ALifeLine_2ae6354921864451` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Yeah, I was gonna buy a boat'
- **TR:** 'Evet, bir tekne alacaktım.'

### 2214. `RD_ALifeLine_15c5d42f048cc940` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'A boat, where, Nolin Lake?'
- **TR:** "Tekne mi, nerede, Nolin Gölü'nde mi?"

### 2215. `RD_ALifeLine_578c9d856a1928c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0008*
- **EN:** "Station coffee tasted like tar and I'd kill for a goddamn cup"
- **TR:** 'Karakol kahvesi katran gibiydi, şimdi lanet bir fincanı için adam öldürürüm.'

### 2216. `RD_ALifeLine_506c440430c7f93f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0008*
- **EN:** 'It tasted like a tire'
- **TR:** 'Lastik tadındaydı.'

### 2217. `RD_ALifeLine_44e7efbd30ce73d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0008*
- **EN:** "A tire that's been in a fire, yeah, don't stop"
- **TR:** 'Yanmış bir lastik gibi, evet, devam et.'

### 2218. `RD_ALifeLine_0750bd52155cbff7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0008*
- **EN:** "I'd drink it cold, out of a boot"
- **TR:** 'Ben soğuk içerdim, çizmeden bile.'

### 2219. `RD_ALifeLine_333231fa2a5266ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0009*
- **EN:** "I've got keys to thirty cells and nobody to put in them"
- **TR:** 'Otuz hücrenin anahtarı bende, içine koyacak kimse yok.'

### 2220. `RD_ALifeLine_5fa92c5f14b8e6be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0009*
- **EN:** 'Keep the keys'
- **TR:** 'Anahtarları tut.'

### 2221. `RD_ALifeLine_6dcafcd707568456` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0009*
- **EN:** "I keep checking my belt for them, like I'll forget how to open a door"
- **TR:** 'Sürekli kemerimi yokluyorum, sanki kapı açmayı unutacakmışım gibi.'

### 2222. `RD_ALifeLine_1da077c367b31915` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0010*
- **EN:** "I count everybody every hour, it's a habit"
- **TR:** 'Her saat herkesi sayıyorum, alışkanlık.'

### 2223. `RD_ALifeLine_1fdeb8f37a37d9e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Eleven years at the jail?'
- **TR:** 'Hapishanede on bir yıl mı?'

### 2224. `RD_ALifeLine_46a69f2a54833fb9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Eleven years, you count heads at four in the morning in your sleep'
- **TR:** 'On bir yıl. Sabah dörtte uykunda bile kafa sayarsın.'

### 2225. `RD_ALifeLine_40ba9d6852f1a86b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'How many are we'
- **TR:** 'Kaç kişiyiz?'

### 2226. `RD_ALifeLine_4221577f7ee1f7f7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Five, right now, five and one asleep, so six'
- **TR:** 'Şu an beş kişiyiz, bir de uyuyan var, altı eder.'

### 2227. `RD_ALifeLine_723a46c34bd72fcc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0010*
- **EN:** "Five and one asleep, that's six, quit saying it"
- **TR:** 'Beş, bir de uyuyan, altı eder. Bırak artık söylemeyi.'

### 2228. `RD_ALifeLine_2884e1585a512f45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0011*
- **EN:** 'Wrote some asshole a ticket Thursday for a taillight'
- **TR:** 'Perşembe bir göt herife stop lambası yüzünden ceza yazdım.'

### 2229. `RD_ALifeLine_11173028559668f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0011*
- **EN:** 'I did, and he was polite about it, said thank you, can you believe that'
- **TR:** 'Yazdım, hem de nazik davrandı, teşekkür etti, inanabiliyor musun?'

### 2230. `RD_ALifeLine_4c7a17680494bbb0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0011*
- **EN:** "Wonder if he's still got the ticket"
- **TR:** 'Merak ediyorum, ceza hala duruyor mu?'

### 2231. `RD_ALifeLine_04f295a8719aa1a3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0011*
- **EN:** 'I hope so, he paid in advance'
- **TR:** 'Umarım, peşin ödemişti zaten.'

### 2232. `RD_ALifeLine_4e98f65361f07afb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0011*
- **EN:** 'Ticket for a taillight, God, remember tickets'
- **TR:** "Stop lambası cezası... Allah'ım, ceza kesmek de vardı bir zamanlar."

### 2233. `RD_ALifeLine_167dbee06be6a06b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0012*
- **EN:** 'If one of them walks up, do we warn first?'
- **TR:** 'Biri yaklaşırsa önce uyarıyor muyuz?'

### 2234. `RD_ALifeLine_3835d6ff111332d6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0012*
- **EN:** 'Warn first'
- **TR:** 'Önce uyar.'

### 2235. `RD_ALifeLine_64b3b8de1d4e771d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0012*
- **EN:** "And if they don't stop?"
- **TR:** 'Ya durmazsa?'

### 2236. `RD_ALifeLine_4fc405194f3046e7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0012*
- **EN:** 'Then we decide, but warn first'
- **TR:** 'O zaman karar veririz, ama önce uyar.'

### 2237. `RD_ALifeLine_07560b6314eab6a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0012*
- **EN:** "That's the order, that's what the Sheriff said, warn first, shoot second"
- **TR:** 'Emir bu, Şerif böyle dedi: önce uyar, sonra ateş et.'

### 2238. `RD_ALifeLine_67a2b66423200e45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0013*
- **EN:** "Domestic call, that's the only call I'd take right now"
- **TR:** 'Aile kavgası ihbarı, şu an alacağım tek çağrı bu.'

### 2239. `RD_ALifeLine_1226e3cb1fc669f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0013*
- **EN:** "Because it's two people yelling about a remote control, remember that"
- **TR:** 'Çünkü iki kişi uzaktan kumanda yüzünden bağırıyor, hatırlıyor musun?'

### 2240. `RD_ALifeLine_3bc6be3871f8ac1d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0013*
- **EN:** "I'd give my badge for a remote control"
- **TR:** 'Uzaktan kumanda için rozetimi veririm.'

### 2241. `RD_ALifeLine_589ebd6353e4f742` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0013*
- **EN:** "I'd give your badge for a remote control"
- **TR:** 'Ben senin rozetini veririm uzaktan kumanda için.'

### 2242. `RD_ALifeLine_0a83164479952c21` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0014*
- **EN:** "The guy who's snoring"
- **TR:** 'Horlayan adama.'

### 2243. `RD_ALifeLine_4125b30e23cf5d1d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0014*
- **EN:** "He doesn't need rights, he needs a pillow"
- **TR:** 'Haklara değil, yastığa ihtiyacı var.'

### 2244. `RD_ALifeLine_32c0ee2e55a0e29f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0014*
- **EN:** "He's got my pillow"
- **TR:** 'Yastığım onda.'

### 2245. `RD_ALifeLine_18fa5145374bdd75` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0014*
- **EN:** 'I thought that was a pillow'
- **TR:** 'Ben onu yastık sanmıştım.'

### 2246. `RD_ALifeLine_3d776f8d7621b8f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0015*
- **EN:** "Who's got the Remington?"
- **TR:** 'Remington kimde?'

### 2247. `RD_ALifeLine_2df8824c2e7f9548` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0015*
- **EN:** "It's by the door"
- **TR:** 'Kapının yanında.'

### 2248. `RD_ALifeLine_464a587c09524c80` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0015*
- **EN:** "It's by the door or it's in your goddamn hand?"
- **TR:** 'Kapının yanında mı, yoksa lanet elinde mi?'

### 2249. `RD_ALifeLine_78c4689b16a59be4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0015*
- **EN:** "It's by the door, it's in my hand later"
- **TR:** 'Kapının yanında, birazdan elimde olur.'

### 2250. `RD_ALifeLine_4a6c6ea01d364e0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0016*
- **EN:** "Eleven rounds, that's it"
- **TR:** 'On bir mermi, o kadar.'

### 2251. `RD_ALifeLine_403cf3366854005c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0016*
- **EN:** "That's what you've got?"
- **TR:** 'Elindeki bu mu?'

### 2252. `RD_ALifeLine_13c5f8e6529527db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0016*
- **EN:** "That's what the group's got, I counted, you want to count?"
- **TR:** 'Grubun elindeki bu, saydım. Sen de saymak ister misin?'

### 2253. `RD_ALifeLine_77b8b04e59fbfc11` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0016*
- **EN:** "I'll count"
- **TR:** 'Ben sayarım.'

### 2254. `RD_ALifeLine_2caaee6a1408ea7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0016*
- **EN:** "Count it twice, we've been wrong before"
- **TR:** 'İki kere say, daha önce yanıldık.'

### 2255. `RD_ALifeLine_3cd5426c32ba21ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0017*
- **EN:** "My shirt's so fucking wet it's see-through!"
- **TR:** 'Gömleğim o kadar ıslak ki içi görünüyor, siktir!'

### 2256. `RD_ALifeLine_229e246c18814f6f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'I know, I looked away'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, ben başımı çevirdim.'

### 2257. `RD_ALifeLine_74465f225a38d2c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'Well, thank you for that much'
- **TR:** 'Neyse, bu kadarına bile sağ ol.'

### 2258. `RD_ALifeLine_3f5b01bd3d82bcc3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'I looked away so hard I hit the table'
- **TR:** 'Başımı öyle çevirdim ki masaya çarptım.'

### 2259. `RD_ALifeLine_208cdaa13cd328b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0018*
- **EN:** "Last I knew, my daughter was at her grandmother's in Elizabethtown"
- **TR:** "Bildiğim kadarıyla kızım Elizabethtown'da büyükannesinin yanındaydı."

### 2260. `RD_ALifeLine_5524bd6c37e81efd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0018*
- **EN:** "That's close"
- **TR:** 'Yakın sayılır.'

### 2261. `RD_ALifeLine_4a1485982d1323e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0018*
- **EN:** "Used to be fifteen miles from home. Might as well be Mars when I don't know where she went"
- **TR:** 'Eskiden evden yirmi dört kilometreydi. Nereye gittiğini bilmeyince Mars kadar uzak.'
- *Not: 15 miles -> 24 km*

### 2262. `RD_ALifeLine_2ee0bd16072429bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0018*
- **EN:** "You'd get there if you could"
- **TR:** 'Gidebilsen giderdin.'

### 2263. `RD_ALifeLine_47a51ce439aba6ba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0018*
- **EN:** "I'd get there if I could, Lord"
- **TR:** "Gidebilsem giderdim, Allah'ım."

### 2264. `RD_ALifeLine_078bc60352d89c3d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0018*
- **EN:** "I hope you get news. I won't pretend I can promise the trip"
- **TR:** 'Umarım haber alırsın. Ama oraya gidebileceğine söz veremem.'

### 2265. `RD_ALifeLine_08cbe723757de1c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0019*
- **EN:** 'Stopped a man on Dixie Friday morning, said his wife was sick'
- **TR:** "Cuma sabahı Dixie'de bir adamı durdurdum, karısı hasta dedi."

### 2266. `RD_ALifeLine_316bca4c3fb7515f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0019*
- **EN:** 'I told him to take her to the hospital, wrote nothing'
- **TR:** 'Hastaneye götür dedim, hiçbir şey yazmadım.'

### 2267. `RD_ALifeLine_52ab895e64c5ace7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0019*
- **EN:** 'You did the right thing'
- **TR:** 'Doğru olanı yaptın.'

### 2268. `RD_ALifeLine_76c8f0115e4159b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0019*
- **EN:** 'I keep wondering if I did'
- **TR:** 'Doğru muydu diye hala kafa yoruyorum.'

### 2269. `RD_ALifeLine_6222332619342d84` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0019*
- **EN:** "Quit it, you can't wonder at everybody"
- **TR:** 'Bırak şunu, her olay için kafa yoramazsın.'

### 2270. `RD_ALifeLine_63d4e3c3702a55be` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0020*
- **EN:** 'My training officer used to say sit still and let the radio come to you'
- **TR:** 'Eğitmen memurum derdi ki, otur, telsiz sana gelsin.'

### 2271. `RD_ALifeLine_0762c5493fe431a5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0020*
- **EN:** "The radio's not coming"
- **TR:** 'Telsiz gelmiyor.'

### 2272. `RD_ALifeLine_66ee1e751dfef561` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0020*
- **EN:** "I know, I'm still doing it"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, yine de yapıyorum.'

### 2273. `RD_ALifeLine_4538471750514996` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0020*
- **EN:** 'Keep doing it'
- **TR:** 'Yapmaya devam et.'

### 2274. `RD_ALifeLine_03359e957461d12d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0021*
- **EN:** "I heard the Guard's at the bridge on Rolling Fork"
- **TR:** "Ulusal Muhafız'ın Rolling Fork köprüsünde olduğunu duydum."

### 2275. `RD_ALifeLine_23046f607b9b5306` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0021*
- **EN:** "A guy in the road, he said they've got a roadblock, but he was a little off"
- **TR:** 'Yolda bir adamdan, barikat kurmuşlar dedi ama biraz tuhaftı.'

### 2276. `RD_ALifeLine_005a2c135c83f0fd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0021*
- **EN:** 'Off how?'
- **TR:** 'Nasıl tuhaf?'

### 2277. `RD_ALifeLine_5adfa85274f4170d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0021*
- **EN:** 'He kept licking his lips'
- **TR:** 'Dilini dudaklarında gezdirip duruyordu.'

### 2278. `RD_ALifeLine_1436fedf25a30a61` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0021*
- **EN:** "Then it's not a roadblock"
- **TR:** 'O zaman barikat değil.'

### 2279. `RD_ALifeLine_3aa49d022110eef8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0022*
- **EN:** "I never thought I'd miss reporters"
- **TR:** 'Gazetecileri özleyeceğimi hiç düşünmezdim.'

### 2280. `RD_ALifeLine_2aa9e53e4db75e6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0022*
- **EN:** 'Reporters?'
- **TR:** 'Gazeteciler mi?'

### 2281. `RD_ALifeLine_210a0ea7776189c4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0022*
- **EN:** 'A bunch of idiots with microphones, in my face, at a wreck'
- **TR:** 'Mikrofonlu bir sürü ahmak, kazada suratıma doluşurlardı.'

### 2282. `RD_ALifeLine_077e64ff560c4d5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0022*
- **EN:** "You'd talk to a reporter right now?"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi bir gazeteciyle konuşur muydun?'

### 2283. `RD_ALifeLine_17d8fda7476c81e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0022*
- **EN:** "I'd give one an exclusive"
- **TR:** 'Birine özel röportaj verirdim.'

### 2284. `RD_ALifeLine_564aded111ea10d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0022*
- **EN:** "I'd give one a ride and a sandwich, the sorry bastards"
- **TR:** 'Ben birine yolculuk ve sandviç ısmarlardım, o zavallı piçlere.'

### 2285. `RD_ALifeLine_0b50537869a4e114` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0023*
- **EN:** "I'm 10-7 until morning"
- **TR:** "Sabaha kadar 10-7'deyim."

### 2286. `RD_ALifeLine_0349f12c3c882132` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0023*
- **EN:** 'Out of service'
- **TR:** 'Hizmet dışı.'

### 2287. `RD_ALifeLine_1f16222c36d8a747` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0023*
- **EN:** 'Wake me when somebody needs a deputy'
- **TR:** 'Biri şerif yardımcısına ihtiyaç duyarsa uyandır beni.'

### 2288. `RD_ALifeLine_75dc20cf790ccb23` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0023*
- **EN:** "That's anybody, that's everybody"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin ihtiyacı var, herkesin.'

### 2289. `RD_ALifeLine_178a372a4ae995bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0023*
- **EN:** 'Then wake me gently'
- **TR:** 'O zaman nazikçe uyandır.'

### 2290. `RD_ALifeLine_6d6deb5804f6e8fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0023*
- **EN:** "I'll wake you like a daisy"
- **TR:** 'Seni papatya gibi uyandırırım.'

### 2291. `RD_ALifeLine_2a95543b0d15aebb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0024*
- **EN:** 'Four hours of sleep since Friday'
- **TR:** 'Cumadan beri dört saat uyudum.'

### 2292. `RD_ALifeLine_559a365c1db3bab2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0024*
- **EN:** "That's all I've had too"
- **TR:** 'Bende de o kadar.'

### 2293. `RD_ALifeLine_53a97f17128b7041` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0024*
- **EN:** "You don't look like you've had four"
- **TR:** 'Dört saat uyumuş gibi durmuyorsun.'

### 2294. `RD_ALifeLine_485a9e4b7661385a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0024*
- **EN:** "You don't look like you've had one"
- **TR:** 'Sen de bir saat uyumuş gibi durmuyorsun.'

### 2295. `RD_ALifeLine_4ccc0be81efcee87` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Gear check'
- **TR:** 'Teçhizat kontrolü.'

### 2296. `RD_ALifeLine_14fe008565714e71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Plates?'
- **TR:** 'Plakalar?'

### 2297. `RD_ALifeLine_204a74461954a764` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Both in, tight'
- **TR:** 'İkisi de takılı, sıkı.'

### 2298. `RD_ALifeLine_5007ac2d68b79f0e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Mask, light, spare mag'
- **TR:** 'Maske, ışık, yedek şarjör.'

### 2299. `RD_ALifeLine_065f24df5c16f1d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Done, done, done'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, tamam, tamam.'

### 2300. `RD_ALifeLine_61c6a37526add582` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0026*
- **EN:** 'Slow week'
- **TR:** 'Bu hafta ortalık sakin.'

### 2301. `RD_ALifeLine_794aad9c5288e5e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0026*
- **EN:** "What, it's been slow"
- **TR:** 'Ne var, sakindi işte.'

### 2302. `RD_ALifeLine_683ce0863c6b5147` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0026*
- **EN:** "Don't, you're gonna jinx it"
- **TR:** 'Yapma, nazar değdirirsin şimdi.'

### 2303. `RD_ALifeLine_18b18fa859f532f6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0026*
- **EN:** "It can't get any worse"
- **TR:** 'Bundan beteri olamaz ki.'

### 2304. `RD_ALifeLine_62cac3345016e9b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0026*
- **EN:** "Now you've done it"
- **TR:** 'Al işte, oldu şimdi.'

### 2305. `RD_ALifeLine_060c567e08052c8a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0027*
- **EN:** 'I miss predictable assholes'
- **TR:** 'Tahmin edilebilir göt herifleri özledim.'

### 2306. `RD_ALifeLine_0fb43b304db3d7bb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0027*
- **EN:** 'Like who?'
- **TR:** 'Mesela kimi?'

### 2307. `RD_ALifeLine_7142ff291599a279` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0027*
- **EN:** 'The guy at the jail who spit every Tuesday at nine'
- **TR:** 'Hapishanedeki şu herif, her salı saat dokuzda tükürürdü.'

### 2308. `RD_ALifeLine_0eb3c0a339b72645` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0027*
- **EN:** "He'd be here right now"
- **TR:** 'O olsa şimdi burada olurdu.'

### 2309. `RD_ALifeLine_31c53cca0e9d9fd8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0027*
- **EN:** "I'd hand him a roll of paper towels, honestly"
- **TR:** 'Valla ona bir rulo kağıt havlu uzatırdım.'

### 2310. `RD_ALifeLine_5fdfa89024e63a12` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0027*
- **EN:** "You'd hand him a mop"
- **TR:** 'Sen ona paspas uzatırdın.'

### 2311. `RD_ALifeLine_0d15fb354d2a6b16` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0028*
- **EN:** 'Which one of you sons of bitches ate MY crackers!'
- **TR:** 'Hanginiz yedi BENİM krakerlerimi, orospu çocukları!'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*

### 2312. `RD_ALifeLine_2fdb0cc544096814` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0028*
- **EN:** "I'm not looking, I'm smelling, you've got salt on your breath, you lying prick"
- **TR:** 'Bakmıyorum, kokluyorum. Nefesin tuz kokuyor, yalancı pislik.'

### 2313. `RD_ALifeLine_5f26577b56010035` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0028*
- **EN:** "Everybody's got salt on their breath, it's a hundred goddamn degrees"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin nefesi tuz kokar, ortalık lanet olası otuz sekiz derece!'
- *Not: 100 °F -> ~38 °C*

### 2314. `RD_ALifeLine_0e8be008614b7360` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Who here still knows how to write a citation by hand?'
- **TR:** 'Hanginiz hala elle ceza tutanağı yazmayı biliyor?'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*

### 2315. `RD_ALifeLine_31e59d1f23bc8519` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Excessive heat, a fine of one cold beer'
- **TR:** 'Aşırı sıcak, ceza bir soğuk bira.'

### 2316. `RD_ALifeLine_4f4ddc56333e0339` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Pay it in installments'
- **TR:** 'Taksitle ödesin.'

### 2317. `RD_ALifeLine_2038fae24aaf9e0b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0030*
- **EN:** 'My old partner used to hum when he drove'
- **TR:** 'Eski ortağım araba sürerken mırıldanırdı.'

### 2318. `RD_ALifeLine_69901d78396da774` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0030*
- **EN:** 'You miss it?'
- **TR:** 'Özledin mi?'

### 2319. `RD_ALifeLine_1eacde5438e7c390` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0030*
- **EN:** "I miss it more than I'd say out loud"
- **TR:** 'Sesli söylemeye bile çekinirim, o kadar çok.'

### 2320. `RD_ALifeLine_7623f3621ed30e9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'I almost asked one for license and registration'
- **TR:** 'Az kalsın birinden ehliyet ruhsat isteyecektim.'

### 2321. `RD_ALifeLine_7db30edd4c16d28a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'It was standing in the road, hands at its sides, my mouth was already open'
- **TR:** 'Yolun ortasında dikiliyordu, kolları iki yanında. Ağzım bile açılmıştı.'

### 2322. `RD_ALifeLine_022c31346735471f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'What would you have done with the registration?'
- **TR:** 'Ruhsatı ne yapacaktın?'

### 2323. `RD_ALifeLine_4ee1a0d67c37a56b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'Written it up, probably'
- **TR:** 'Muhtemelen tutanak tutardım.'

### 2324. `RD_ALifeLine_1fb3fcf0586ec6ef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0032*
- **EN:** "Pager's still on my belt, I keep checking it"
- **TR:** 'Çağrı cihazı hala kemerimde, hep bakıp duruyorum.'

### 2325. `RD_ALifeLine_1e5d3f8470071da4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'Anybody call?'
- **TR:** 'Arayan oldu mu?'

### 2326. `RD_ALifeLine_224ace27245cd7e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0032*
- **EN:** "It's just a little black box, I keep looking at it like it's gonna talk"
- **TR:** 'Küçücük bir kara kutu, ama sanki konuşacakmış gibi bakıyorum ona.'

### 2327. `RD_ALifeLine_668eff5907c1ee63` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0032*
- **EN:** "It's been dead since Saturday"
- **TR:** 'Cumartesiden beri ölü.'

### 2328. `RD_ALifeLine_58f727ba5947447e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'I know, I look anyway...'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, yine de bakıyorum...'

### 2329. `RD_ALifeLine_1f0b31952219df35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'I check mine too...'
- **TR:** 'Ben de kendiminkine bakıyorum...'

### 2330. `RD_ALifeLine_7881c26a2a3a54a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0033*
- **EN:** "I miss Gail's voice on the radio"
- **TR:** "Gail'in telsizdeki sesini özledim."

### 2331. `RD_ALifeLine_75c7aa4a38e86860` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'Dispatch Gail?'
- **TR:** 'Sevk görevlisi Gail mi?'

### 2332. `RD_ALifeLine_5862597e4abf9b9f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0033*
- **EN:** "Twelve years of 'all units' in that voice, calm as coffee"
- **TR:** 'On iki yıl boyunca "tüm birimler" dedi o sesle, kahve gibi sakin.'

### 2333. `RD_ALifeLine_7339ac3367638308` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'She always sounded like she was smiling'
- **TR:** 'Hep gülümsüyormuş gibi gelirdi sesi.'

### 2334. `RD_ALifeLine_36c54cc77582df71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'She was, she was the only one'
- **TR:** 'Gülümsüyordu da zaten, bir tek o.'

### 2335. `RD_ALifeLine_70b237003fc92158` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0034*
- **EN:** "I don't miss the paperwork"
- **TR:** 'Evrak işlerini özlemiyorum.'

### 2336. `RD_ALifeLine_5376f4ca6202d94f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0034*
- **EN:** 'Okay, I miss a little of the paperwork'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, evrak işlerini azıcık özlüyorum.'

### 2337. `RD_ALifeLine_64b62f1c2953e0ea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0034*
- **EN:** 'You miss the stamp, admit it'
- **TR:** 'Damgayı özlüyorsun, kabul et.'

### 2338. `RD_ALifeLine_2a2dbcd0107d2de7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0034*
- **EN:** 'I miss the stamp'
- **TR:** 'Ben damgayı özlüyorum.'

### 2339. `RD_ALifeLine_347faba5092eda9a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0035*
- **EN:** 'Sheriff Dunn was up for election in November'
- **TR:** 'Şerif Dunn kasımda seçime girecekti.'

### 2340. `RD_ALifeLine_65834f917bc07e31` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0035*
- **EN:** "He'd have won"
- **TR:** 'Kazanırdı.'

### 2341. `RD_ALifeLine_6816058c597824de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0035*
- **EN:** "Third term, he'd have won by a mile"
- **TR:** 'Üçüncü dönem, açık farkla kazanırdı.'

### 2342. `RD_ALifeLine_55e53f664584da9a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0035*
- **EN:** "He'd have hated this, he never even liked the Army"
- **TR:** 'Bunu görse çıldırırdı. Ordudan bile hiç hoşlanmazdı.'

### 2343. `RD_ALifeLine_0e3e62f210f27bdd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0035*
- **EN:** "He never liked anybody who wasn't a cousin"
- **TR:** 'Kuzeni olmayan kimseden hoşlanmadı ki.'

### 2344. `RD_ALifeLine_621bd5e0108e5c9b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0036*
- **EN:** "I didn't join to do this"
- **TR:** 'Ben bunun için polis olmadım.'

### 2345. `RD_ALifeLine_4731f6cd78672382` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'Nobody joined to do this'
- **TR:** 'Kimse bunun için olmadı.'

### 2346. `RD_ALifeLine_7d071f4a2f89a2cb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'I joined to direct traffic at the school and tell tourists where the bathrooms are'
- **TR:** 'Ben okul önünde trafiği yönlendireyim, turistlere tuvaletin yerini söyleyeyim diye girmiştim.'

### 2347. `RD_ALifeLine_73fd95c033ad801b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0036*
- **EN:** "That's the job, that's what I signed up for"
- **TR:** 'İş buydu zaten, ben de bunun için imza attım.'

### 2348. `RD_ALifeLine_151b5b6a79964e07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'I miss telling people where the bathrooms are'
- **TR:** 'İnsanlara tuvaletin yerini söylemeyi özledim.'

### 2349. `RD_ALifeLine_29f982d109c93909` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'Behind the courthouse, to the left'
- **TR:** 'Adliyenin arkasında, solda.'

### 2350. `RD_ALifeLine_350a633d68ca1690` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0037*
- **EN:** 'Longest I ever went without a call was four hours, on a Wednesday'
- **TR:** 'En uzun çağrısız kaldığım süre dört saatti, bir çarşamba günü.'

### 2351. `RD_ALifeLine_3b29fa2b26ecd3ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0037*
- **EN:** "I keep waiting for it to ring, for somebody to say Deputy, there's a cow in the road"
- **TR:** 'Hala çalmasını bekliyorum, biri çıkıp "Şerif Yardımcısı, yolda bir inek var" desin diye.'

### 2352. `RD_ALifeLine_5a28d0aa68da8f13` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'Evidence locker had a whole box of cigarettes'
- **TR:** 'Delil dolabında bir kutu dolusu sigara vardı.'

### 2353. `RD_ALifeLine_6abec5e9774cbf29` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0038*
- **EN:** "Nobody's, from a case, they were never gonna be claimed"
- **TR:** 'Kimsenin. Bir davadan kalma, sahibi hiç çıkmayacaktı zaten.'

### 2354. `RD_ALifeLine_675cdb1005b53b33` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'Do you still have the key?'
- **TR:** 'Anahtar hala sende mi?'

### 2355. `RD_ALifeLine_5a12603a5bacda7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'Do I still have the key, what is wrong with you, of course I do'
- **TR:** 'Anahtar hala bende mi, ne biçim soru bu? Tabii ki bende!'

### 2356. `RD_ALifeLine_549768e63665573f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'Is the locker far'
- **TR:** 'Dolap uzak mı?'

### 2357. `RD_ALifeLine_681243fc0279904f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0039*
- **EN:** "Been writing letters to people's families"
- **TR:** 'İnsanların ailelerine mektup yazıyorum.'

### 2358. `RD_ALifeLine_271b40c05cfd2e9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0039*
- **EN:** "You can't mail them"
- **TR:** 'Postalayamazsın ki.'

### 2359. `RD_ALifeLine_658f1f5e46e67045` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0039*
- **EN:** 'I know, I keep them in my vest, I figure someday'
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, yelekte tutuyorum, bir gün lazım olur diye.'

### 2360. `RD_ALifeLine_2796f8df14799e40` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0039*
- **EN:** 'How many have you got?'
- **TR:** 'Kaç tane oldu?'

### 2361. `RD_ALifeLine_7469ac6c6b3bbcd5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0039*
- **EN:** "Four, and I don't wanna do the fifth"
- **TR:** 'Dört tane. Beşincisini yazmak istemiyorum.'

### 2362. `RD_ALifeLine_1d4454ca1ec58573` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0039*
- **EN:** "Don't do the fifth tonight..."
- **TR:** 'Beşincisini bu gece yazma...'

### 2363. `RD_ALifeLine_7df731075ae336f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0040*
- **EN:** 'I can sleep standing up, learned it at the academy'
- **TR:** 'Ayakta uyuyabilirim, akademide öğrendim.'

### 2364. `RD_ALifeLine_5ce4dfe341a74235` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0040*
- **EN:** "It's a skill, you lean on something and go limp"
- **TR:** 'Bu bir beceri, bir şeye yaslanıp gevşiyorsun.'

### 2365. `RD_ALifeLine_0eb8c2de6fe9e722` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0040*
- **EN:** "Hold on, I'm busy being awake"
- **TR:** 'Dur, şu an uyanık olmakla meşgulüm.'

### 2366. `RD_ALifeLine_2620d09c6121a35e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0040*
- **EN:** "I'll hold the wall"
- **TR:** 'Duvarı ben tutarım.'

### 2367. `RD_ALifeLine_7c05565738480ab4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'Our K-9 is at the kennel'
- **TR:** 'K-9 köpeğimiz barınakta kaldı.'

### 2368. `RD_ALifeLine_7c8bc3f55cf80465` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'Rex?'
- **TR:** 'Rex mi?'

### 2369. `RD_ALifeLine_6a1773ee2133087e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0041*
- **EN:** "Rex, I fed him Thursday, and I don't know, I just don't know"
- **TR:** 'Rex... Perşembe günü mamasını verdim. Bilmiyorum, hiç bilmiyorum...'

### 2370. `RD_ALifeLine_3a15a4d12e9a16b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0041*
- **EN:** "He's a police dog, he'll be fine, he'll be the best-behaved dog in Kentucky"
- **TR:** "Polis köpeği o, bir şeyi olmaz. Kentucky'nin en uslu köpeği olur."

### 2371. `RD_ALifeLine_169594027c30cf4c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0041*
- **EN:** "He'll be eating the kennel door"
- **TR:** 'Barınağın kapısını yemiştir şimdiye.'

### 2372. `RD_ALifeLine_46f658ea22abf6da` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'I know every back road in this county, every one of them'
- **TR:** 'Bu ilçedeki her ara yolu bilirim, hepsini.'

### 2373. `RD_ALifeLine_07d91e18329a99bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0042*
- **EN:** "That's why we're here"
- **TR:** 'İşte bu yüzden buradayız.'

### 2374. `RD_ALifeLine_05a9adce417f197f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0042*
- **EN:** "And I'm still lost, I look at a road I've driven a thousand times and I don't know where it goes"
- **TR:** 'Ama yine de kayboluyorum. Bin kere sürdüğüm yola bakıyorum, nereye çıktığını bilmiyorum.'

### 2375. `RD_ALifeLine_0753a61e3e80a65f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0042*
- **EN:** "That's normal now"
- **TR:** 'Artık normal bu.'

### 2376. `RD_ALifeLine_1f3fea443193d2b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0043*
- **EN:** "It's low"
- **TR:** 'Azalmış.'

### 2377. `RD_ALifeLine_41af882a00d043bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Pass it anyway...'
- **TR:** 'Yine de ver...'

### 2378. `RD_ALifeLine_41ff0aa21b76e9d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Everybody gets a swallow, not a gulp'
- **TR:** 'Herkese bir yudum, kana kana içmek yok.'

### 2379. `RD_ALifeLine_7fcfbf59234fba8b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0043*
- **EN:** "I know what a swallow is, I'm a professional"
- **TR:** 'Yudumun ne olduğunu biliyorum, ben profesyonelim.'

### 2380. `RD_ALifeLine_21e06bfa2402ee9d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Then swallow professionally'
- **TR:** 'O zaman profesyonelce yudumla.'

### 2381. `RD_ALifeLine_1ac216733abd7da0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0044*
- **EN:** 'Nine months of training for a door'
- **TR:** 'Bir kapı için dokuz ay eğitim aldım.'

### 2382. `RD_ALifeLine_582dc2fc1e1242c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0044*
- **EN:** "Don't start"
- **TR:** 'Başlama.'

### 2383. `RD_ALifeLine_2eb6aa1a647743e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0044*
- **EN:** "Nine months of training and the door's open, there's no fucking door"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz ay eğitim aldım, kapı da açık duruyor. Ortada siktiğimin kapısı bile yok!'

### 2384. `RD_ALifeLine_7cb7db5640172df5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0044*
- **EN:** 'Call it a retirement'
- **TR:** 'Emekli oldun say.'

### 2385. `RD_ALifeLine_67b12d8026c8b09f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0044*
- **EN:** 'A very early retirement'
- **TR:** 'Çok erken bir emeklilik.'

### 2386. `RD_ALifeLine_54a31ed73dba5e26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0045*
- **EN:** "Cap's snoring again"
- **TR:** 'Yüzbaşı yine horluyor.'

### 2387. `RD_ALifeLine_05b578620242d89f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0045*
- **EN:** "It's a good snore, it means he's sleeping"
- **TR:** 'İyi horlama o, uyuyor demek.'

### 2388. `RD_ALifeLine_1f53a5c256456ec1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0045*
- **EN:** "It's a terrible snore, it sounds like a duck being sat on by a bear"
- **TR:** 'Berbat bir horlama, sanki ayının altında kalmış bir ördek.'

### 2389. `RD_ALifeLine_6cadb1346b2263a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0045*
- **EN:** "Let the man sleep, he's earned a duck"
- **TR:** 'Bırak uyusun adam, bir ördeği hak etti.'

### 2390. `RD_ALifeLine_7c466c38754df99e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0045*
- **EN:** 'Somebody cover the duck'
- **TR:** 'Biri ördeğin üstünü örtsün.'

### 2391. `RD_ALifeLine_7811172f5e2c20c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'My shift partner was Ray, eleven years'
- **TR:** "Vardiya ortağım Ray'di, on bir yıl."

### 2392. `RD_ALifeLine_0177154a2500ae04` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'Where is he?'
- **TR:** 'Şimdi nerede?'

### 2393. `RD_ALifeLine_29663154790d30c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0046*
- **EN:** 'He took the Brandenburg road Saturday to check on his mother'
- **TR:** 'Annesine bakmak için cumartesi Brandenburg yoluna girdi.'

### 2394. `RD_ALifeLine_5e36986c262051b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0046*
- **EN:** "I don't know, he's not on the net, and the net's not on"
- **TR:** 'Bilmiyorum. Telsiz ağında yok, ağ da kapalı.'

### 2395. `RD_ALifeLine_22b6da71392a1aa6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0046*
- **EN:** "He'll turn up..."
- **TR:** 'Çıkar gelir...'

### 2396. `RD_ALifeLine_31f232ba02c7c1ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'We should clear the next room before we sleep in it'
- **TR:** 'Yatmadan önce yan odayı temizleyelim.'

### 2397. `RD_ALifeLine_551e598a510a180f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'We cleared it twice'
- **TR:** 'İki kere temizledik.'

### 2398. `RD_ALifeLine_5cd6a2d27b4d3c96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Clear it again'
- **TR:** 'Bir daha temizle.'

### 2399. `RD_ALifeLine_1ad5efce33b5350d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0047*
- **EN:** "It's a closet, there's a goddamn mop in it!"
- **TR:** 'Dolap o be, içinde kahrolası bir paspas var!'

### 2400. `RD_ALifeLine_057dd1e662883d14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0047*
- **EN:** "A closet's a room, a mop's got hands"
- **TR:** 'Dolap da odadır, paspasın da elleri var.'

### 2401. `RD_ALifeLine_07e35e95678fd70f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0047*
- **EN:** 'Clear the mop'
- **TR:** 'Paspası da temizleyelim.'

### 2402. `RD_ALifeLine_046d28b1195fb3dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0048*
- **EN:** "Smokey hat's in the other bag, where the fuck's my other bag?"
- **TR:** 'Smokey şapkam öbür çantada, siktiğimin öbür çantası nerede?'

### 2403. `RD_ALifeLine_4581e4712d9a707b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0048*
- **EN:** "You don't need the hat"
- **TR:** 'Şapkaya ihtiyacın yok.'

### 2404. `RD_ALifeLine_20847e3579c15b88` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0048*
- **EN:** 'I need the hat'
- **TR:** 'Şapkaya ihtiyacım var.'

### 2405. `RD_ALifeLine_6df0f9f922a973d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0048*
- **EN:** "It's a hat"
- **TR:** 'Şapka bu, şapka.'

### 2406. `RD_ALifeLine_3cba9b2a6238cf77` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'Seven years and I never fired my weapon off the range'
- **TR:** 'Yedi yıl boyunca poligon dışında silahımı hiç ateşlemedim.'

### 2407. `RD_ALifeLine_0da0228f2bb771b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'Lucky'
- **TR:** 'Şanslısın.'

### 2408. `RD_ALifeLine_696958c5586ef94a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0049*
- **EN:** "Now it's twice a day, and I hate it"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi günde iki kere, hem de nefret ediyorum bundan.'

### 2409. `RD_ALifeLine_2b7eefb61f4120c2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0049*
- **EN:** 'At least you hit what you aim at'
- **TR:** 'En azından nişan aldığın şeye vuruyorsun.'

### 2410. `RD_ALifeLine_44276cc925a9f902` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0049*
- **EN:** "That's the part I don't like"
- **TR:** 'Hoşuma gitmeyen kısım da o.'

### 2411. `RD_ALifeLine_5734741c2f74ae76` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0049*
- **EN:** "That's the part you'll get used to"
- **TR:** 'Alışacağın kısım da o.'

### 2412. `RD_ALifeLine_5128f3621820f751` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0050*
- **EN:** "You think they've got a plan, the Guard, the Army?"
- **TR:** "Sence onların bir planı var mı, Ulusal Muhafız'ın, Ordu'nun?"

### 2413. `RD_ALifeLine_0daaa4d572536bea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0050*
- **EN:** "I think somebody's got a plan"
- **TR:** 'Birinin planı var bence.'

### 2414. `RD_ALifeLine_303b6c3c673a76e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0050*
- **EN:** "I think the plan is a map with a circle on it, and we're inside the circle"
- **TR:** 'Bence plan, üstünde daire çizili bir harita, biz de dairenin içindeyiz.'

### 2415. `RD_ALifeLine_5c55a67d79021f07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0050*
- **EN:** "Then we're in the plan"
- **TR:** 'O zaman biz de planın içindeyiz.'

### 2416. `RD_ALifeLine_6246f98f767109ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0050*
- **EN:** "That's the county in one sentence"
- **TR:** 'İlçenin tek cümlelik özeti bu.'

### 2417. `RD_ALifeLine_04fde9df36831ac9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0051*
- **EN:** "I keep thinking I should've stayed on the road"
- **TR:** 'Yolda kalmalıydım diye düşünüp duruyorum.'

### 2418. `RD_ALifeLine_1fc1aaa3648162ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0051*
- **EN:** 'Which road?'
- **TR:** 'Hangi yolda?'

### 2419. `RD_ALifeLine_5af0bcc26f810620` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0051*
- **EN:** "Dixie, Friday, when it started, there were people everywhere, I should've stayed"
- **TR:** "Dixie'de, cuma günü, her şey başladığında. Her yerde insan vardı, kalmalıydım."

### 2420. `RD_ALifeLine_57ad11e56d708b73` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0051*
- **EN:** "You couldn't have stayed"
- **TR:** 'Kalamazdın.'

### 2421. `RD_ALifeLine_1604a76d0c286286` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0051*
- **EN:** "I know, I know I couldn't have"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, biliyorum, kalamazdım.'

### 2422. `RD_ALifeLine_7b0efcda6b62d0c2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0051*
- **EN:** "Nobody could've..."
- **TR:** 'Kimse kalamazdı...'

### 2423. `RD_ALifeLine_2bd647bd4d77daea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'I got a speeding ticket once'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde hız cezası yedim.'

### 2424. `RD_ALifeLine_68ae4cea5cbaf65d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0052*
- **EN:** 'Seventy in a fifty-five, from a trooper, he was a prick about it, wrote me for ninety'
- **TR:** 'Saatte 88 kilometre sınırında 113 ile giderken bir eyalet polisi yakaladı. Pislik gibi davrandı, doksan dolar yazdı.'
- *Not: 70 mph -> 113 km/h, 55 mph -> 88 km/h*

### 2425. `RD_ALifeLine_32ebf7d7238bbf95` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0052*
- **EN:** "And now you're a cop"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi de polis olmuşsun.'

### 2426. `RD_ALifeLine_18636ab767326d53` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0052*
- **EN:** "I bought him a coffee at the courthouse, years later, he didn't remember me"
- **TR:** 'Yıllar sonra adliyede ona bir kahve ısmarladım. Beni hatırlamadı bile.'

### 2427. `RD_ALifeLine_7ed8212735f5ce9f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0052*
- **EN:** "That's a trooper for you..."
- **TR:** 'Eyalet polisi böyle işte...'

### 2428. `RD_ALifeLine_04dfdcf658e56354` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0053*
- **EN:** "Who's got first watch"
- **TR:** 'İlk nöbet kimde?'

### 2429. `RD_ALifeLine_7e7fb9d02327a20b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0053*
- **EN:** 'I had it last night'
- **TR:** 'Dün gece bendeydi.'

### 2430. `RD_ALifeLine_6dcffc1e3ca70b4e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0053*
- **EN:** "Somebody's lying, and it's a very small room"
- **TR:** 'Biri yalan söylüyor, hem de oda bu kadar küçükken.'

### 2431. `RD_ALifeLine_0181eed418fd79ca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0110*
- **EN:** "Nineteen years a cop and I never once drew on anybody, and now I'm doing it twice a day"
- **TR:** 'On dokuz yıllık polisim, hiç kimseye silah çekmedim. Şimdi günde iki kere çekiyorum.'

### 2432. `RD_ALifeLine_367ee9116d13ef9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0110*
- **EN:** "That's the part that scares me, I'm getting good at it"
- **TR:** 'Beni korkutan da o, gittikçe iyi oluyorum.'

### 2433. `RD_ALifeLine_07b0e6c775be1f9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0110*
- **EN:** 'Somebody has to be...'
- **TR:** 'Birinin olması lazım...'

### 2434. `RD_ALifeLine_11437e1b5bf0f0e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0111*
- **EN:** "The Sheriff's wife called the station Friday, crying, and I put her on hold"
- **TR:** "Şerif'in karısı cuma günü karakolu aradı, ağlıyordu. Ben de onu beklemeye aldım."

### 2435. `RD_ALifeLine_5d2b60aa1bee9446` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0111*
- **EN:** "You didn't know"
- **TR:** 'Bilemezdin.'

### 2436. `RD_ALifeLine_5e8cf6ec7c182bea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0111*
- **EN:** 'I put her on hold to take a call about a barking dog, a goddamn dog'
- **TR:** 'Havlayan bir köpeğin ihbarına bakmak için bekletmeye aldım, kahrolası bir köpek için!'

### 2437. `RD_ALifeLine_268b346147216f36` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0112*
- **EN:** 'Where the hell is the National Guard?'
- **TR:** 'Ulusal Muhafız hangi cehennemde?'

### 2438. `RD_ALifeLine_7d3ed0c652289711` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0112*
- **EN:** "Coming from where, Frankfort? Frankfort's a two-hour drive!"
- **TR:** "Nereden geliyorlar, Frankfort'tan mı? Frankfort iki saatlik yol!"

### 2439. `RD_ALifeLine_058470601946fa3e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0112*
- **EN:** "It's been a week, they could've walked"
- **TR:** 'Bir hafta oldu, yürüyerek bile gelirlerdi.'

### 2440. `RD_ALifeLine_0d5622f53905fc32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0113*
- **EN:** 'I gave a woman a flare and told her to stay with her car'
- **TR:** 'Bir kadına işaret fişeği verdim, arabasının yanında kalmasını söyledim.'

### 2441. `RD_ALifeLine_10ad52ba40d575a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0113*
- **EN:** 'And I left, I had a call, I left her there'
- **TR:** 'Sonra gittim. Bir çağrım vardı, onu orada bıraktım.'

### 2442. `RD_ALifeLine_67d525797292eacb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0113*
- **EN:** "You couldn't have known..."
- **TR:** 'Bilemezdin...'

### 2443. `RD_ALifeLine_001543b53021912a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0114*
- **EN:** "We're supposed to protect and serve, and I'm sitting in somebody's basement counting crackers"
- **TR:** 'Koruyup kollamakla yükümlüyüz, ben ise birinin bodrumunda kraker sayıyorum.'

### 2444. `RD_ALifeLine_6fc4d5905d5888cf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0114*
- **EN:** "It's a lot of crackers"
- **TR:** 'Bir sürü kraker o.'

### 2445. `RD_ALifeLine_0e06302273e35b81` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0114*
- **EN:** "It's seventeen crackers, I counted"
- **TR:** 'On yedi tane, saydım.'

### 2446. `RD_ALifeLine_574759f06670981d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0114*
- **EN:** "Seventeen's good, hold onto them"
- **TR:** 'On yedi iyidir, iyi sakla onları.'

### 2447. `RD_ALifeLine_35ee49a5424798c5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0115*
- **EN:** 'My badge feels heavy, like somebody put a brick behind it'
- **TR:** 'Rozetim ağır geliyor, arkasına biri tuğla koymuş gibi.'

### 2448. `RD_ALifeLine_4159694d68c2331f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0115*
- **EN:** "That's the bullet-resistant vest"
- **TR:** 'O kurşun geçirmez yelek.'

### 2449. `RD_ALifeLine_34c2c0ef2dfaa848` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0115*
- **EN:** 'No, I mean the badge, specifically'
- **TR:** 'Yok, yelek değil, rozetten bahsediyorum, özellikle rozetten.'

### 2450. `RD_ALifeLine_76e18414048239b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0116*
- **EN:** 'All I wanted was one normal Saturday night, a drunk, a fight, a guy who forgot where he parked'
- **TR:** 'Tek istediğim normal bir cumartesi gecesiydi: bir sarhoş, bir kavga, arabasını nereye park ettiğini unutan bir adam.'

### 2451. `RD_ALifeLine_7fc01cb55cdd9659` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0116*
- **EN:** 'That sounds like heaven'
- **TR:** 'Cennet gibi geliyor.'

### 2452. `RD_ALifeLine_01b923a53bb40541` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0116*
- **EN:** "I'd pull over a thousand drunks for one normal Saturday"
- **TR:** 'Tek bir normal cumartesi için bin sarhoşu kenara çekerdim.'

### 2453. `RD_ALifeLine_438b825e57d4e998` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0116*
- **EN:** "I'd arrest every one of them personally"
- **TR:** 'Hepsini şahsen ben tutuklardım.'

### 2454. `RD_ALifeLine_7789d0a575c2d947` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0117*
- **EN:** "If the Sheriff told me to hold the line I'd hold it, but there's no line"
- **TR:** 'Şerif bana hattı tut deseydi tutardım, ama ortada hat yok.'

### 2455. `RD_ALifeLine_7c295f942af9f22a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0117*
- **EN:** 'Then hold your spot'
- **TR:** 'O zaman yerini tut.'

### 2456. `RD_ALifeLine_24ebca5130a5010e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0117*
- **EN:** "My spot's a chair with a lean"
- **TR:** 'Benim yerim, yan yatmış bir sandalye.'

### 2457. `RD_ALifeLine_65781f161ead7039` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0117*
- **EN:** "It's a good lean, it's historic"
- **TR:** 'Güzel eğim, tarihi eser gibi.'

### 2458. `RD_ALifeLine_53c0de9246034f5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0118*
- **EN:** "I'm so tired I put my coffee in my pocket and tried to drink my radio"
- **TR:** 'O kadar yorgunum ki kahveyi cebime koydum, telsizden içmeye kalktım.'

### 2459. `RD_ALifeLine_1dc7e80527dbf300` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0118*
- **EN:** 'I held it up to my mouth and almost said ten-four to a cup'
- **TR:** 'Ağzıma götürdüm, az kalsın bir bardağa "anlaşıldı" diyecektim.'

### 2460. `RD_ALifeLine_4eabd31452cf4c98` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0119*
- **EN:** 'I used to hate the night shift'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden gece vardiyasından nefret ederdim.'

### 2461. `RD_ALifeLine_722519840173c176` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0119*
- **EN:** "You didn't hate it, you were scared of it"
- **TR:** 'Nefret etmiyordun, ondan korkuyordun.'

### 2462. `RD_ALifeLine_5d7e3cc75250ef65` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0119*
- **EN:** "I hated it, and I'd give a lot for it right now"
- **TR:** 'Nefret ederdim, ama şimdi o gece vardiyası için çok şey verirdim.'

### 2463. `RD_ALifeLine_4a3bdf202f2183f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0119*
- **EN:** "It's just a longer night"
- **TR:** 'Sadece biraz daha uzun bir gece.'

### 2464. `RD_ALifeLine_6625705a56daf458` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0120*
- **EN:** 'I hate being in charge, I hate every second of it, nobody ever teaches you how'
- **TR:** 'Başta olmaktan nefret ediyorum, her saniyesinden. Kimse nasıl yapılacağını öğretmiyor ki.'

### 2465. `RD_ALifeLine_39847e0a1055f40d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0120*
- **EN:** "You're doing fine"
- **TR:** 'İyi gidiyorsun.'

### 2466. `RD_ALifeLine_60aff88c70a5878e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0120*
- **EN:** "I'm doing something, I'll give you that, but it's sure as hell not fine"
- **TR:** 'Bir şeyler yapıyorum, orası doğru. Ama iyi falan değil, hiç değil.'

### 2467. `RD_ALifeLine_11d52f483e85c5ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0121*
- **EN:** 'Anybody got a pen that works'
- **TR:** 'Çalışan bir kalemi olan var mı?'

### 2468. `RD_ALifeLine_2999a828744aae95` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0121*
- **EN:** "I've got a pen"
- **TR:** 'Bende kalem var.'

### 2469. `RD_ALifeLine_4b3077df11eecc61` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0121*
- **EN:** "I've got a pencil"
- **TR:** 'Bende kurşun kalem var.'

### 2470. `RD_ALifeLine_2dc1e3b02eb0fd6a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0121*
- **EN:** "I've got a crayon"
- **TR:** 'Bende mum boya var.'

### 2471. `RD_ALifeLine_518981af54db3583` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0121*
- **EN:** 'Where did you get a crayon'
- **TR:** 'Mum boyayı nereden buldun?'

### 2472. `RD_ALifeLine_6b0a49222c2da851` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0121*
- **EN:** "It's a long story"
- **TR:** 'Uzun hikaye.'

### 2473. `RD_ALifeLine_0818e9fd2aa327e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0122*
- **EN:** "The first thing they teach you is don't take it personally"
- **TR:** 'Sana ilk öğrettikleri şey, işi kişisel algılama.'

### 2474. `RD_ALifeLine_72f3bb8f237af7a5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0122*
- **EN:** "And I took every second of it personally, and I'd do it again"
- **TR:** 'Ben her saniyesini kişisel algıladım, yine olsa yine öyle yaparım.'

### 2475. `RD_ALifeLine_206ff529613de5cf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0123*
- **EN:** "We're out of coffee and we're out of cigarettes"
- **TR:** 'Kahvemiz de bitti, sigaramız da.'

### 2476. `RD_ALifeLine_0abe78cb50b2d986` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0123*
- **EN:** 'And excuses'
- **TR:** 'Bir de bahanelerimiz.'

### 2477. `RD_ALifeLine_6a6a635753181848` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0123*
- **EN:** "And excuses, yeah, that's the entire stockpile"
- **TR:** 'Bir de bahaneler, evet, bütün stokumuz bu.'

### 2478. `RD_ALifeLine_5001810139041d7a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0123*
- **EN:** "That's a very bad stockpile"
- **TR:** 'Çok kötü bir stok.'

### 2479. `RD_ALifeLine_3d233c9718feb39f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0124*
- **EN:** "Don't tell the new guy what we did at the school"
- **TR:** 'Yeni elemana okulda ne yaptığımızı anlatma.'

### 2480. `RD_ALifeLine_425fb8435e99b71f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0124*
- **EN:** "He won't ask"
- **TR:** 'Sormaz.'

### 2481. `RD_ALifeLine_367a0be172fa59a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0124*
- **EN:** "He'll ask, and I don't want to answer"
- **TR:** 'Sorar, ben de cevap vermek istemiyorum.'

### 2482. `RD_ALifeLine_612994384a5bcf6f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0125*
- **EN:** 'Somebody left the radio on and it ate the battery'
- **TR:** 'Biri telsizi açık bırakmış, pili bitirmiş.'

### 2483. `RD_ALifeLine_7b6f7bab58184ba9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0125*
- **EN:** "It wasn't me"
- **TR:** 'Ben değilim.'

### 2484. `RD_ALifeLine_444811957100afec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0125*
- **EN:** 'It was somebody, and it was somebody with hands'
- **TR:** 'Biri yaptı, hem de elleri olan biri.'

### 2485. `RD_ALifeLine_6dc8d91451715842` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0125*
- **EN:** "It was probably you, you've got hands too"
- **TR:** 'Büyük ihtimalle sensin, senin de elin var.'

### 2486. `RD_ALifeLine_19286031439495d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0126*
- **EN:** 'I never liked the jail, you know'
- **TR:** 'Hapishaneyi hiç sevmedim, biliyor musun.'

### 2487. `RD_ALifeLine_290c5d8d229852ea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0126*
- **EN:** 'Nobody likes the jail'
- **TR:** 'Hapishaneyi kimse sevmez.'

### 2488. `RD_ALifeLine_3b9df3e32e14aa13` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0126*
- **EN:** "The noise, mostly, the doors, I'd lie awake and hear the doors"
