- **TR:** 'Daha çok gürültüsü, kapılar. Yatağa uzanıp kapıları dinlerdim.'

### 2489. `RD_ALifeLine_43defba26d2d45f3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0126*
- **EN:** "And now it's quiet"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi ise sessiz.'

### 2490. `RD_ALifeLine_2644af2b03244f7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0126*
- **EN:** "And now I can't sleep"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi de uyuyamıyorum.'

### 2491. `RD_ALifeLine_510cabb425ea2847` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0179*
- **EN:** "Goddamn it, I've been awake for sixty hours!"
- **TR:** 'Lanet olsun, altmış saattir uyanığım!'

### 2492. `RD_ALifeLine_795edcd5551345e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0179*
- **EN:** 'Go to sleep'
- **TR:** 'Uyu.'

### 2493. `RD_ALifeLine_16cabaac31da14a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0179*
- **EN:** "I can't, every time I close my eyes I hear the radio"
- **TR:** 'Uyuyamıyorum, gözümü kapatsam telsiz sesi geliyor.'

### 2494. `RD_ALifeLine_3e83b0336e3ec715` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0179*
- **EN:** "There's no radio, that's the problem"
- **TR:** 'Telsiz yok ki, sorun da o.'

### 2495. `RD_ALifeLine_40f6ae2b19ba8fe1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0180*
- **EN:** "If I see that asshole from the gas station again I'm gonna arrest him for being an asshole"
- **TR:** 'O benzinlikteki göt herifi bir daha görürsem göt herif olmaktan tutuklarım.'

### 2496. `RD_ALifeLine_183d869c15b2549b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'Is that a crime?'
- **TR:** 'Bu suç mu?'

### 2497. `RD_ALifeLine_258d0c5c1c967f29` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0180*
- **EN:** 'It is today'
- **TR:** 'Bugün öyle.'

### 2498. `RD_ALifeLine_2e8d8524681d1a5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0181*
- **EN:** 'What the hell is that smell?'
- **TR:** 'Bu lanet koku da ne böyle?'

### 2499. `RD_ALifeLine_5f3bf62b027d0187` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0181*
- **EN:** "It's the corner"
- **TR:** 'Köşeden geliyor.'

### 2500. `RD_ALifeLine_7003206e1f93e3b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0181*
- **EN:** "There's a dead mouse in the corner, or a live one that's been in the same shirt for a week"
- **TR:** 'Köşede ya ölü bir fare var, ya da bir haftadır aynı gömleği giyen diri bir fare.'

### 2501. `RD_ALifeLine_528110f0505dd190` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0181*
- **EN:** "Shit, it's the Sheriff's old jacket!"
- **TR:** "Hay sıçayım, Şerif'in eski ceketi bu!"

### 2502. `RD_ALifeLine_0d801e431897665c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0182*
- **EN:** "Twenty-two years and I never been this scared on a call, and I've been shot at"
- **TR:** 'Yirmi iki yıldır görevdeyim, bana ateş de ettiler ama hiçbir çağrıda bu kadar korkmadım.'

### 2503. `RD_ALifeLine_403c148a4b96fa6b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0182*
- **EN:** "I'd rather be shot at, you know where the shot is coming from"
- **TR:** 'Bana ateş etsinler, daha iyi. Kurşunun nereden geldiğini bilirsin.'

### 2504. `RD_ALifeLine_2337836949f871e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0183*
- **EN:** "Get this shit off the table, somebody's gonna spill it"
- **TR:** 'Şu zımbırtıyı masadan kaldırın, biri dökecek.'

### 2505. `RD_ALifeLine_321bfd4b0f1aae07` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0183*
- **EN:** "It's a can of beans"
- **TR:** 'Bir kutu fasulye bu.'

### 2506. `RD_ALifeLine_3d01ab2d6d03df35` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0183*
- **EN:** "It's a can of beans on a map, we'll never find the road"
- **TR:** 'Haritanın üstünde bir kutu fasulye, yolu hiç bulamayız.'

### 2507. `RD_ALifeLine_41468abf684bf183` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0183*
- **EN:** "We'll never find the road anyway..."
- **TR:** 'Zaten yolu hiçbir zaman bulamayacağız...'

### 2508. `RD_ALifeLine_764d2dd02f2eadca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0184*
- **EN:** 'I told dispatch four times I needed backup'
- **TR:** 'Merkeze dört kere destek lazım dedim.'

### 2509. `RD_ALifeLine_4b6f9f3f70930162` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0184*
- **EN:** "And she said ten-four, and then nothing, ten-fucking-four, that's all anybody says"
- **TR:** 'O da "anlaşıldı" dedi, sonra hiçbir şey. Siktiğimin "anlaşıldı"sı, herkes sadece bunu söylüyor!'

### 2510. `RD_ALifeLine_07a0e52c171b41d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0185*
- **EN:** 'The only thing worse than not having a plan is having a plan nobody follows'
- **TR:** 'Planın olmamasından beter tek şey, kimsenin uymadığı bir planın olması.'

### 2511. `RD_ALifeLine_696ebedd4a556b30` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0185*
- **EN:** 'We have both'
- **TR:** 'İkisi de var bizde.'

### 2512. `RD_ALifeLine_4ff7a47144b82294` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0185*
- **EN:** "Great, we're overachievers"
- **TR:** 'Harika, çalışkanlıkta tavan yapmışız.'

### 2513. `RD_ALifeLine_71b0808047cf4a0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0186*
- **EN:** 'Quit tapping that goddamn pen!'
- **TR:** 'Şu lanet kalemi şaklatmayı kes!'

### 2514. `RD_ALifeLine_28857c396c6be992` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0186*
- **EN:** "Tap, tap, tap, it's been an hour, I'm gonna lose my mind"
- **TR:** 'Tık tık tık, bir saattir, aklımı kaçıracağım.'

### 2515. `RD_ALifeLine_727cbcd159a159b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0186*
- **EN:** "That's the clock, genius"
- **TR:** 'O saat, dahi çocuk.'

### 2516. `RD_ALifeLine_450aa2c9313ded8e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0187*
- **EN:** "I've been a cop for nineteen years and I still can't parallel park"
- **TR:** 'On dokuz yıllık polisim ve hala paralel park edemiyorum.'

### 2517. `RD_ALifeLine_5778fdad76805d11` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0187*
- **EN:** "You don't have to now"
- **TR:** 'Artık etmene gerek yok.'

### 2518. `RD_ALifeLine_2e5698e459a85bdf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0187*
- **EN:** 'Best thing about the end of the world'
- **TR:** 'Dünyanın sonunun en güzel tarafı bu.'

### 2519. `RD_ALifeLine_55260609776f7476` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0188*
- **EN:** "Radio's got nothing"
- **TR:** 'Telsizde ses yok.'

### 2520. `RD_ALifeLine_1dd3a3e924ec2c47` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0188*
- **EN:** 'You sign the log yet'
- **TR:** 'Defteri imzaladın mı?'

### 2521. `RD_ALifeLine_6e46c7823bdd8933` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0188*
- **EN:** 'Try channel two'
- **TR:** 'İkinci kanalı dene.'

### 2522. `RD_ALifeLine_4126723510ed3dcf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0188*
- **EN:** "Not yet, I'll do it at the change"
- **TR:** 'Henüz değil, vardiya değişiminde yaparım.'

### 2523. `RD_ALifeLine_499790bd0b3a9e70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0188*
- **EN:** "Channel two's got nothing either"
- **TR:** 'İkinci kanalda da ses yok.'

### 2524. `RD_ALifeLine_05da5c1b2cc6bebe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0188*
- **EN:** 'Do it now, the Sheriff always wanted the time'
- **TR:** 'Şimdi yap, Şerif hep saati isterdi.'

### 2525. `RD_ALifeLine_3b99ba1463b6346d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0189*
- **EN:** "I don't like how quiet it got"
- **TR:** 'Ortalığın bu kadar sessizleşmesinden hoşlanmadım.'

### 2526. `RD_ALifeLine_4f7d54f30eb40ae9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0189*
- **EN:** "Cap's been in the back an hour"
- **TR:** 'Yüzbaşı bir saattir arkada.'

### 2527. `RD_ALifeLine_43c3bd1a0b7be02a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0189*
- **EN:** "Quiet's fine"
- **TR:** 'Sessizlik iyidir.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 2528. `RD_ALifeLine_4d157de0449e4f91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0189*
- **EN:** "It's not quiet, there's a hum"
- **TR:** 'Sessiz değil ki, bir uğultu var.'

### 2529. `RD_ALifeLine_5d0bcb891608d099` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0189*
- **EN:** "There's always a hum, that's the hum"
- **TR:** 'Hep bir uğultu var, o uğultu işte.'

### 2530. `RD_ALifeLine_6cbd977a27c5605f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0189*
- **EN:** "I'll check on the Captain"
- **TR:** "Ben Yüzbaşı'ya bir bakayım."

### 2531. `RD_ALifeLine_519efe0d2cefabe6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0190*
- **EN:** "Take the shotgun to the back, I'll take the front"
- **TR:** 'Pompalıyı arkaya götür, ben öne bakarım.'

### 2532. `RD_ALifeLine_3702053a2e73b706` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0190*
- **EN:** 'Anybody hear that'
- **TR:** 'Duyan oldu mu?'

### 2533. `RD_ALifeLine_18fb3ebf260be0f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0190*
- **EN:** "I'll take the shotgun, you take the front"
- **TR:** 'Pompalıyı ben alırım, sen öne geç.'

### 2534. `RD_ALifeLine_67dfd46345c66a0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0190*
- **EN:** 'Switch if it gets loud'
- **TR:** 'Ortalık gürültülü olursa yer değiştiririz.'

### 2535. `RD_ALifeLine_2c55b4d901bb5df8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0190*
- **EN:** 'Nothing, I thought I heard a truck'
- **TR:** 'Yok bir şey, bir kamyon sesi duydum sandım.'

### 2536. `RD_ALifeLine_2cfe44df62f09f1a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0001*
- **EN:** "Y'all eat anything today?"
- **TR:** 'Bugün bir şey yediniz mi?'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 2537. `RD_ALifeLine_7f4eb00444028910` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0001*
- **EN:** 'Half a can of beans'
- **TR:** 'Yarım kutu fasulye.'

### 2538. `RD_ALifeLine_0d661ad54171b3e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0001*
- **EN:** 'I had a cracker'
- **TR:** 'Ben bir kraker yedim.'

### 2539. `RD_ALifeLine_4739c3391040b019` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0001*
- **EN:** "Here, I've got a Snickers in my vest, split it three ways"
- **TR:** 'Buyur, yeleğimde bir Snickers var, üçe bölelim.'

### 2540. `RD_ALifeLine_358cd6681084ab91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0002*
- **EN:** "Don't sit with your back to the door"
- **TR:** 'Sırtını kapıya dönüp oturma.'

### 2541. `RD_ALifeLine_2e37056a3a633ba0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0002*
- **EN:** 'Habit, you want to see who comes in'
- **TR:** 'Alışkanlık, kimin girdiğini görmek istersin.'

### 2542. `RD_ALifeLine_333737ae5a8d4156` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0002*
- **EN:** 'Who comes in?'
- **TR:** 'Kim girecek ki?'

### 2543. `RD_ALifeLine_2489e47c32927f52` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0002*
- **EN:** "Nobody, that's the habit"
- **TR:** 'Kimse, alışkanlık dediğim bu.'

### 2544. `RD_ALifeLine_205944e922ffc634` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0005*
- **EN:** 'Which one of you knows how to drive a stick?'
- **TR:** 'Hanginiz manuel vites kullanmayı biliyor?'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*

### 2545. `RD_ALifeLine_59a97e0b4b9eaa05` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0005*
- **EN:** "Good, we may need that, I'll show you the road"
- **TR:** 'Güzel, lazım olabilir. Yolu ben gösteririm.'

### 2546. `RD_ALifeLine_2d4e70f30d877fbe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0010*
- **EN:** 'You got any real coffee?'
- **TR:** 'Gerçek kahven var mı?'

### 2547. `RD_ALifeLine_2d347d49134a195d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0010*
- **EN:** "MRE coffee, it's tan water"
- **TR:** 'MRE kahvesi var, kahverengi su.'

### 2548. `RD_ALifeLine_001b845563764ed7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0010*
- **EN:** "I'll take tan water"
- **TR:** 'Kahverengi suya razıyım.'

### 2549. `RD_ALifeLine_0f60be1a05d27839` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0010*
- **EN:** "It's an acquired taste, you won't acquire it"
- **TR:** 'Alışılacak bir tat, sen alışamazsın.'

### 2550. `RD_ALifeLine_69b1af7148a02099` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0011*
- **EN:** "We'll take the first watch"
- **TR:** 'İlk nöbeti biz alırız.'

### 2551. `RD_ALifeLine_170750e46f657142` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0011*
- **EN:** 'And you'
- **TR:** 'Bir de sen.'

### 2552. `RD_ALifeLine_0b8807b6172d8795` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0011*
- **EN:** 'Me?'
- **TR:** 'Ben mi?'

### 2553. `RD_ALifeLine_7060951d6b9636e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0011*
- **EN:** "Just sleep, we've got it"
- **TR:** 'Sen uyu, biz hallederiz.'

### 2554. `RD_ALifeLine_30a9af0f64f697ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0011*
- **EN:** "Sleep, it's an order"
- **TR:** 'Uyu, emir bu.'

### 2555. `RD_ALifeLine_489626a5182eeed0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0011*
- **EN:** 'Fine, fine'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, tamam.'

### 2556. `RD_ALifeLine_59bb1f3a0b33030b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0054*
- **EN:** 'Beef stew, or the omelet'
- **TR:** 'Etli güveç ya da omlet.'

### 2557. `RD_ALifeLine_3078b1203724b2e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0054*
- **EN:** "The omelet's the one that looks like a fever dream and tastes like ass"
- **TR:** 'Omlet mi, ateşli bir kabus gibi görünüp bok gibi tat veren şey.'

### 2558. `RD_ALifeLine_0a10a74479afbe6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0054*
- **EN:** 'We call it the vomlette'
- **TR:** 'Biz ona kusmlet diyoruz.'

### 2559. `RD_ALifeLine_4dc526c27923076d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0054*
- **EN:** 'Beef stew it is'
- **TR:** 'Etli güveç olsun o zaman.'

### 2560. `RD_ALifeLine_0c91f3f76bd13d3e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0054*
- **EN:** "Beef stew's the only one that doesn't fight back"
- **TR:** 'Karşılık vermeyen tek yemek etli güveç.'

### 2561. `RD_ALifeLine_763eb5d904451759` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0058*
- **EN:** 'Commo check'
- **TR:** 'Telsiz kontrolü.'

### 2562. `RD_ALifeLine_3db73a7c0186dc1e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0058*
- **EN:** 'Nothing but hiss'
- **TR:** 'Hışırtıdan başka bir şey yok.'

### 2563. `RD_ALifeLine_5e78fe3779385bc0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0058*
- **EN:** 'Try the other freq'
- **TR:** 'Öbür frekansı dene.'

### 2564. `RD_ALifeLine_56638e61158766df` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0058*
- **EN:** 'Tried it, tried the other other one, tried yelling'
- **TR:** 'Denedim, öbür öbürünü de denedim, bağırmayı da denedim.'

### 2565. `RD_ALifeLine_5081cd4724fd67a0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0058*
- **EN:** "Yelling's not a freq"
- **TR:** 'Bağırmak frekans değil.'

### 2566. `RD_ALifeLine_72476aa0482e3f0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0058*
- **EN:** 'It works on the Lieutenant'
- **TR:** "Teğmen'de işe yarıyor."

### 2567. `RD_ALifeLine_53e461ff136f1f20` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0062*
- **EN:** "Nobody fucking told me we'd be doing this shit"
- **TR:** 'Kimse bana, siktir, bu boku yapacağımızı söylemedi!'

### 2568. `RD_ALifeLine_1103fa1d2438c203` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0062*
- **EN:** "Nobody told fuckin' anybody"
- **TR:** 'Kimse kimseye bir halt söylemedi ki.'

### 2569. `RD_ALifeLine_4b3d37433d145203` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0062*
- **EN:** "Nobody told the Sergeant, he's just better at not looking surprised"
- **TR:** "Çavuş'a bile söyleyen olmadı. O sadece şaşırmamış gibi yapmakta bizden usta."

### 2570. `RD_ALifeLine_4efba6250644c33f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0067*
- **EN:** 'Six weeks in the Army, and then this'
- **TR:** 'Orduda altı haftam oldu, sonra bu.'

### 2571. `RD_ALifeLine_2e69b29b7e33c92d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0067*
- **EN:** "I haven't done anything, I've stood where they told me"
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şey yapmadım ki, bana söyledikleri yerde durdum.'

### 2572. `RD_ALifeLine_663616a2171b6881` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0067*
- **EN:** "That's most of it, standing where they tell you"
- **TR:** 'İşin çoğu bu zaten, sana söyledikleri yerde durmak.'

### 2573. `RD_ALifeLine_7353c89612ef2efd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0067*
- **EN:** "That's the Army, that's all of it, I'm not lying"
- **TR:** 'Ordu bu, hepsi bu, yalan söylemiyorum.'

### 2574. `RD_ALifeLine_15e995dc350c6325` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0074*
- **EN:** 'Hurry up and wait'
- **TR:** 'Acele et ve bekle.'

### 2575. `RD_ALifeLine_4f1cacec44598a6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0074*
- **EN:** "We've been waiting three days"
- **TR:** 'Üç gündür bekliyoruz.'

### 2576. `RD_ALifeLine_28e45f6d2c51175d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0074*
- **EN:** "We've hurried up for three days, nobody said what for"
- **TR:** 'Üç gündür acele ediyoruz, kimse ne için olduğunu söylemedi.'

### 2577. `RD_ALifeLine_14fd852c109c1efe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0074*
- **EN:** "That's the Army, start to finish"
- **TR:** 'Ordu dediğin baştan sona bu.'

### 2578. `RD_ALifeLine_5d01b38555c4bde5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0074*
- **EN:** "Wait, it's finishing?"
- **TR:** 'Dur bir dakika, bitiyor mu?'

### 2579. `RD_ALifeLine_2773d70d23da1100` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0088*
- **EN:** "I'm supposed to be on the range tomorrow"
- **TR:** 'Yarın poligonda olmam gerekiyor.'

### 2580. `RD_ALifeLine_7d749d9438172ca0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0088*
- **EN:** "There's no range"
- **TR:** 'Poligon yok.'

### 2581. `RD_ALifeLine_6fc8f8801f295a75` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0088*
- **EN:** "I know, it's just, that's where I'm supposed to be, I've got a schedule"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, sadece orada olmam gerekiyor, bir programım var.'

### 2582. `RD_ALifeLine_1f662cce069e2f55` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0088*
- **EN:** 'Keep the schedule, it helps'
- **TR:** 'Programı bırakma, iyi gelir.'

### 2583. `RD_ALifeLine_1c5b58293fd77aff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'Airborne'
- **TR:** 'Airborne!'

### 2584. `RD_ALifeLine_03d8f4cb06259912` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'All the way'
- **TR:** 'Sonuna kadar!'

### 2585. `RD_ALifeLine_2265a7a50f393536` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0091*
- **EN:** "I've been waiting to say that for two days"
- **TR:** 'İki gündür bunu demek için bekliyordum.'

### 2586. `RD_ALifeLine_69b0cc740358336d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0091*
- **EN:** "I'm not saying it, it's too hot to yell"
- **TR:** 'Ben demiyorum, bağırmak için çok sıcak.'

### 2587. `RD_ALifeLine_58c6ee7b2d519d6c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'Airborne, all the way, shut up'
- **TR:** 'Airborne, sonuna kadar. Şimdi kapa çeneni.'

### 2588. `RD_ALifeLine_6c157eda12595b9f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0097*
- **EN:** 'New guy'
- **TR:** 'Yeni çocuk.'

### 2589. `RD_ALifeLine_4c07d29d3e6aa6b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0097*
- **EN:** "It's been three weeks"
- **TR:** 'Üç hafta oldu ya.'

### 2590. `RD_ALifeLine_542dfa017bb840b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0097*
- **EN:** 'Still the new guy'
- **TR:** 'Hala yeni çocuk.'

### 2591. `RD_ALifeLine_369767080851b0f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0097*
- **EN:** 'Until somebody newer shows up'
- **TR:** 'Daha yenisi gelene kadar.'

### 2592. `RD_ALifeLine_4737085e5c359593` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0097*
- **EN:** "And who's gonna show up?"
- **TR:** 'Kim gelecek ki?'

### 2593. `RD_ALifeLine_33e854496aae21a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0097*
- **EN:** 'Fine, I take it back'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, geri alıyorum.'

### 2594. `RD_ALifeLine_5e2a66e8100d6896` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0127*
- **EN:** 'Fuck the Army, fuck the line, fuck all of it!'
- **TR:** 'Siktir git Ordu, siktir git hat, siktir git her şey!'

### 2595. `RD_ALifeLine_692dbadd2191f5ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0127*
- **EN:** 'You done?'
- **TR:** 'Bitti mi?'

### 2596. `RD_ALifeLine_2f6c2a452a516656` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0127*
- **EN:** 'Almost, give me a minute'
- **TR:** 'Neredeyse, bir dakika ver.'

### 2597. `RD_ALifeLine_6562200862f53baa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0127*
- **EN:** "Take your time, I'm gonna sit here and agree with every word"
- **TR:** 'Rahat ol, ben burada oturup her kelimene katılacağım.'

### 2598. `RD_ALifeLine_2c35a79117e96d0d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0128*
- **EN:** 'My CO told us hold the line, and then there was no line, and then there was no CO'
- **TR:** 'Birlik komutanımız hattı tutun dedi, sonra hat kalmadı, sonra komutan da kalmadı.'

### 2599. `RD_ALifeLine_32904e0c0cddb419` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0128*
- **EN:** "I'm still holding it, it's a spot on the floor, I'm very loyal"
- **TR:** 'Ben hala tutuyorum. Yerde bir nokta, ama ben sadığımdır, ne yaparsın.'

### 2600. `RD_ALifeLine_47fd94e70dac80b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0130*
- **EN:** 'They gave me an M16 and no fucking orders, what the hell am I supposed to do with that'
- **TR:** 'Elime M16 verdiler, tek bir emir yok, siktir! Ben bununla ne halt edeceğim?'

### 2601. `RD_ALifeLine_1545ff63435ceccd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0130*
- **EN:** "I've cleaned it nine times, there's nothing left to clean"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz kere temizledim, temizleyecek bir şey kalmadı.'

### 2602. `RD_ALifeLine_42d0ab934d1813de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0130*
- **EN:** "Then clean it a tenth, it's the only thing we know how to do"
- **TR:** 'O zaman onuncu kere temizle, bildiğimiz tek iş bu.'

### 2603. `RD_ALifeLine_3f14179b34cf05a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0131*
- **EN:** 'I miss basic, at least in basic somebody yelled at me, shit, I miss being yelled at'
- **TR:** 'Temel eğitimi özledim. En azından biri bana bağırırdı. Hay sıçayım, bana bağırılmasını özlemişim!'

### 2604. `RD_ALifeLine_2cb47a614f837a6a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0131*
- **EN:** 'I can yell at you'
- **TR:** 'Ben bağırabilirim.'

### 2605. `RD_ALifeLine_12145a6e06e005e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0131*
- **EN:** "It's not the same, you don't have the voice"
- **TR:** 'Aynı şey değil, sende o ses yok.'

### 2606. `RD_ALifeLine_794ee491745987dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0133*
- **EN:** "Sergeant's the only one keeping us sane"
- **TR:** 'Çavuş bizi aklı başında tutan tek kişi.'

### 2607. `RD_ALifeLine_67979f246333e8c5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0133*
- **EN:** "If he goes, we're done, I mean that"
- **TR:** 'Giderse biteriz, ciddiyim.'

### 2608. `RD_ALifeLine_085c6cc80fb65918` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0133*
- **EN:** 'Not going anywhere, stubborn as a mule'
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir yere gitmez, katır gibi inatçı.'

### 2609. `RD_ALifeLine_28fd25170dcb5b82` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0136*
- **EN:** "If I hear one more person say 'the Army will handle it' I'm gonna lose my shit"
- **TR:** 'Bir kişi daha "Ordu halleder" derse kafayı yiyeceğim.'

### 2610. `RD_ALifeLine_0a9b19ad0e164b70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0136*
- **EN:** 'We are the Army'
- **TR:** 'Ordu biziz.'

### 2611. `RD_ALifeLine_209410b168ed45a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0136*
- **EN:** "I know we are, that's what I'm saying, we are, and we're not handling it"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum biziz, ben de onu diyorum, biziz ve halletmiyoruz.'

### 2612. `RD_ALifeLine_72402d5d72362f6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0136*
- **EN:** 'Then we need a plan'
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir plan lazım.'

### 2613. `RD_ALifeLine_087f44f1522f517f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0161*
- **EN:** "This is some bullshit, we're sitting here like targets"
- **TR:** 'Bu tam bir boktan iş, burada hedef tahtası gibi oturuyoruz.'

### 2614. `RD_ALifeLine_2a870a5467918526` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0161*
- **EN:** "It's the order"
- **TR:** 'Emir bu.'

### 2615. `RD_ALifeLine_29b38a5e1a31046e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0161*
- **EN:** 'What order? Who gave it? Show me the order!'
- **TR:** 'Hangi emir? Kim verdi? Göster bana emri!'

### 2616. `RD_ALifeLine_112178c812839633` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0161*
- **EN:** "There's no order, that's the order"
- **TR:** 'Emir yok, emir de bu.'

### 2617. `RD_ALifeLine_36ab2c1d105f3529` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0163*
- **EN:** "Sergeant says if I touch my face one more time he's gonna cut my hands off"
- **TR:** 'Çavuş yüzüme bir daha dokunursam ellerimi keseceğini söylüyor.'

### 2618. `RD_ALifeLine_43090e400911c562` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0163*
- **EN:** 'What were you touching it for'
- **TR:** 'Niye dokunuyordun?'

### 2619. `RD_ALifeLine_0e7c46324540b1ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0163*
- **EN:** "It itches, it's a goddamn beard"
- **TR:** 'Kaşınıyor, kahrolası sakal bu.'

### 2620. `RD_ALifeLine_1c8eb597358ca5ad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0163*
- **EN:** "You've got three whiskers"
- **TR:** 'Üç tel kılın var.'

### 2621. `RD_ALifeLine_6378c0ee4d83addc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0165*
- **EN:** 'Who the fuck lost the radio battery?'
- **TR:** 'Kim siktiğimin telsiz pilini kaybetti?'

### 2622. `RD_ALifeLine_130431a42126700a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0165*
- **EN:** "It's in the radio"
- **TR:** 'Telsizin içinde.'

### 2623. `RD_ALifeLine_603977471beae198` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0165*
- **EN:** "The radio's dead, I need the battery"
- **TR:** 'Telsiz ölü, bana pil lazım.'

### 2624. `RD_ALifeLine_1b7da70a13de9d79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0165*
- **EN:** "It's the battery that's dead, you moron"
- **TR:** 'Ölü olan pil, geri zekalı!'

### 2625. `RD_ALifeLine_0ab310277ba6d066` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0165*
- **EN:** "Both of you shut up, I'm trying to hear the squelch"
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de kapayın çenenizi, parazit sesini dinlemeye çalışıyorum.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 2626. `RD_ALifeLine_2e1fde2f7ce140af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0167*
- **EN:** "I got somebody back in Georgia, they're gonna think I'm dead"
- **TR:** "Georgia'da beni bekleyen biri var, öldüğümü sanacak."

### 2627. `RD_ALifeLine_2e71deef712745b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0167*
- **EN:** "You're not dead"
- **TR:** 'Ölmedin.'

### 2628. `RD_ALifeLine_40a2a23953084d9d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0167*
- **EN:** "I-- I don't know that, how do I know that, I don't know a goddamn thing anymore"
- **TR:** 'Ben-- bilmiyorum, nereden bileyim ki? Artık hiçbir şeyi bilmiyorum, lanet olsun.'

### 2629. `RD_ALifeLine_59ef3b077fd854ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0169*
- **EN:** 'The Army never taught me how to make fire with wet wood'
- **TR:** 'Ordu bana ıslak odunla ateş yakmayı hiç öğretmedi.'

### 2630. `RD_ALifeLine_6d757a4c358ec135` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0169*
- **EN:** 'They did, it was Tuesday, you slept through it'
- **TR:** 'Öğretti ya, salı günüydü. Sen uyuyordun.'

### 2631. `RD_ALifeLine_1154387c50f5ac24` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0169*
- **EN:** "I slept through everything, that's how I got through basic"
- **TR:** 'Ben her şeyi uyuyarak geçtim, temel eğitimi de öyle atlattım.'

### 2632. `RD_ALifeLine_527ecf805ceca229` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0171*
- **EN:** 'They were kids, some of them, you know that'
- **TR:** 'Çocuktular bazıları, biliyorsun.'

### 2633. `RD_ALifeLine_6353e8590149acce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0171*
- **EN:** 'I try not to look at their faces'
- **TR:** 'Yüzlerine bakmamaya çalışıyorum.'

### 2634. `RD_ALifeLine_62489c7d020d92fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0171*
- **EN:** "Then don't look, and don't talk about it"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bakma. Bir de konuşma bunu.'

### 2635. `RD_ALifeLine_450805711f2301c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0173*
- **EN:** 'What the fuck is that smell?'
- **TR:** 'Siktir, bu ne koku böyle?'

### 2636. `RD_ALifeLine_095207bc6c6e4f8e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0173*
- **EN:** "It's me, I haven't showered in nine days"
- **TR:** 'Benim. Dokuz gündür duş almadım.'

### 2637. `RD_ALifeLine_4fd04bba3fe35866` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0173*
- **EN:** "Nine? I'm at ten and you smell worse"
- **TR:** 'Dokuz mu? Ben onuncu günümdeyim, sen benden beter kokuyorsun.'

### 2638. `RD_ALifeLine_43dae746768a4995` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0173*
- **EN:** 'You both smell like a wet goat in a Humvee'
- **TR:** "İkiniz de Humvee'nin içindeki ıslak keçi gibi kokuyorsunuz."
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 2639. `RD_ALifeLine_1a55ce030df949a2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0175*
- **EN:** 'Anybody got a pen? I wanna write the Lieutenant a nasty letter'
- **TR:** 'Kimde kalem var? Teğmene pis bir mektup yazacağım.'

### 2640. `RD_ALifeLine_278417fd297bdf27` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0175*
- **EN:** "You can't write"
- **TR:** 'Sen yazı yazamazsın ki.'

### 2641. `RD_ALifeLine_3681ec085c031c32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0175*
- **EN:** "I can write 'fuck you' and I can spell it"
- **TR:** 'Siktir git" yazarım, imlasını da bilirim.'

### 2642. `RD_ALifeLine_3c94053c408078a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0055*
- **EN:** 'Remember trading crackers for jelly?'
- **TR:** 'Krakerleri reçelle takas ettiğimiz günleri hatırlıyor musun?'

### 2643. `RD_ALifeLine_3494aa44252f2e6b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0055*
- **EN:** 'What kind of jelly?'
- **TR:** 'Ne reçeli?'

### 2644. `RD_ALifeLine_685875897c2ab886` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0055*
- **EN:** 'Grape. It was always grape'
- **TR:** 'Üzüm. Hep üzümdü.'

### 2645. `RD_ALifeLine_30b8e68a249d9d08` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0055*
- **EN:** "You'd ask for the cheese spread too, wouldn't you?"
- **TR:** 'Sen peynir ezmesini de isterdin tabii, değil mi?'

### 2646. `RD_ALifeLine_4b5f6ae61f09b9b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0055*
- **EN:** "Don't even pretend-trade with him. He'll swindle you"
- **TR:** 'Onunla şaka bile olsa takasa girme, seni soyar.'

### 2647. `RD_ALifeLine_0636f6723423917c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0056*
- **EN:** "Heater's still warm"
- **TR:** 'Isıtıcı hâlâ sıcak.'

### 2648. `RD_ALifeLine_3f751dd836ef1f5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0056*
- **EN:** "Don't sit on it"
- **TR:** 'Üstüne oturma.'

### 2649. `RD_ALifeLine_052be780479df3b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0056*
- **EN:** "I wasn't gonna sit on it"
- **TR:** 'Oturacak değildim.'

### 2650. `RD_ALifeLine_31b7aefb662c8ff7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0056*
- **EN:** 'You were looking at it like a pillow'
- **TR:** 'Yastık gibi bakıyordun ona.'

### 2651. `RD_ALifeLine_59295af00e0f5527` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0056*

- **TR:** 'Herkes yastık gibi bakıyor ona.'

### 2652. `RD_ALifeLine_78c199671a6b8733` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0057*
- **EN:** 'Pound cake, who opened the pound cake?'
- **TR:** 'Kim açtı şu keki?'

### 2653. `RD_ALifeLine_1a38898955dab79d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0057*
- **EN:** "It's gone, somebody's got crumbs on their lip, I'm not saying who"
- **TR:** 'Bitmiş. Birinin dudağında kırıntı var, kim olduğunu söylemiyorum.'

### 2654. `RD_ALifeLine_79a90b870cfcd729` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0057*
- **EN:** "Don't look at me, look at the new guy"

### 2655. `RD_ALifeLine_6914c0b23c6c15dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0059*
- **EN:** "Net's been dead since Sunday"

### 2656. `RD_ALifeLine_1bed4cc83837c921` — Claude (yeni)

- **EN:** 'Dead or jammed?'
- **TR:** 'Ölü mü, karıştırılıyor mu?'

### 2657. `RD_ALifeLine_241b2f494cae1374` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0059*
- **EN:** 'Jammed has a shape, this has no shape'
- **TR:** 'Parazitin bir deseni olur. Bunda desen falan yok, bomboş.'

### 2658. `RD_ALifeLine_36c7999e6ab5170b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0059*
- **EN:** "Then it's down"
- **TR:** 'O zaman kapanmış.'

### 2659. `RD_ALifeLine_20bc98b8674205d9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0059*

- **TR:** 'Ya da biri kapattı...'

### 2660. `RD_ALifeLine_09c758da1bf1e7de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'Hold position until further fucking notice'
- **TR:** 'Yeni bir emre kadar yerini koru, siktiğimin emri gelene kadar.'

### 2661. `RD_ALifeLine_36d3e95a75905fd0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0060*
- **EN:** 'How long is further notice?'
- **TR:** 'O yeni emir ne zaman gelir peki?'

### 2662. `RD_ALifeLine_72043e4766b7294e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0060*
- **EN:** "The Sergeant didn't say"
- **TR:** 'Çavuş söylemedi.'

### 2663. `RD_ALifeLine_7fa39c9262325681` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0060*
- **EN:** "It's been three days of further goddamn notice"
- **TR:** 'Üç gündür lanet olası yeni emri bekliyoruz.'

### 2664. `RD_ALifeLine_6c20e51d4d2cfd8c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0060*
- **EN:** "That's a lot of further"
- **TR:** 'Uzun bir yeni emir bu.'

### 2665. `RD_ALifeLine_783eed1566b9f3ea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0061*
- **EN:** "Where's the Lieutenant?"
- **TR:** 'Teğmen nerede?'

### 2666. `RD_ALifeLine_4fe8ccd021a78236` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0061*
- **EN:** 'At the CP'
- **TR:** 'Komuta noktasında.'

### 2667. `RD_ALifeLine_72d89d4760b781b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0061*
- **EN:** "There's no CP"
- **TR:** 'Ortada komuta noktası yok ki.'

### 2668. `RD_ALifeLine_5de87cf256db8905` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0061*
- **EN:** "Then he's at the ghost of one"
- **TR:** 'O zaman hayaletinin başında.'

### 2669. `RD_ALifeLine_62c5313424b0ebe4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0061*
- **EN:** "The ghost's got a better view than us"
- **TR:** 'Hayaletin manzarası bizimkinden iyi.'

### 2670. `RD_ALifeLine_05aa92487662a062` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0063*
- **EN:** "If the Sergeant goes down, who's next?"
- **TR:** 'Çavuş düşerse sıra kimde?'

### 2671. `RD_ALifeLine_58f794344f66b652` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0063*
- **EN:** "You're senior, by two weeks"
- **TR:** 'Sen kıdemlisin, iki hafta farkla.'

### 2672. `RD_ALifeLine_38de835822c11adf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0063*
- **EN:** "That's not a good enough reason to put me in charge of anything"
- **TR:** 'Bu, beni bir şeyin başına geçirmek için yeterli bir sebep değil.'

### 2673. `RD_ALifeLine_47ca333547149be0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0063*
- **EN:** "It's the Army, it's the only fucking reason"
- **TR:** 'Burası Ordu, geçerli tek siktiğimin sebebi de bu.'

### 2674. `RD_ALifeLine_3c76827b2b8f2ef9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0064*
- **EN:** 'Ever get stuck with a whole week of fire watch?'
- **TR:** 'Hiç bir hafta boyunca yangın nöbetine kaldın mı?'

### 2675. `RD_ALifeLine_4d92b0722623f614` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0064*
- **EN:** "I thought they couldn't do that two nights running"
- **TR:** 'Üst üste iki gece vermezler sanıyordum.'

### 2676. `RD_ALifeLine_518e3f9c028f9602` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0064*
- **EN:** 'They could if you were the only one there to argue'
- **TR:** 'Verirler, itiraz eden tek kişi sensen verirler.'

### 2677. `RD_ALifeLine_01bb40fd3b2af715` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0064*
- **EN:** 'Who gave you the week?'
- **TR:** 'Haftayı sana kim verdi?'

### 2678. `RD_ALifeLine_663e15af1f44daf1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0064*
- **EN:** "The one who didn't show up"
- **TR:** 'Gelmeyen adam.'

### 2679. `RD_ALifeLine_775386d36e4e9af6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0064*
- **EN:** "That would've been the Lieutenant, the asshole"
- **TR:** 'Teğmendi o, göt herif.'

### 2680. `RD_ALifeLine_4ca02fd841d6aa7b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0065*
- **EN:** 'My spouse is on post at Campbell'
- **TR:** "Eşim Campbell'de görevde."

### 2681. `RD_ALifeLine_1664451658b7bfe3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0065*
- **EN:** "That's an hour"
- **TR:** 'Bir saatlik yol.'

### 2682. `RD_ALifeLine_7e9321b35ac58b7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0065*
- **EN:** "Hour and ten, if you don't stop, and I can't"
- **TR:** 'Bir saat on dakika, hiç durmazsan. Benim gitmem mümkün değil.'

### 2683. `RD_ALifeLine_39e754ac44904599` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0065*
- **EN:** "You'd go if you could"
- **TR:** 'Gidebilsen giderdin.'

### 2684. `RD_ALifeLine_56eeddde16e3daf6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0065*
- **EN:** "I'd go if I could, that's my whole sentence"
- **TR:** 'Gidebilsem giderdim, derdim bu işte.'

### 2685. `RD_ALifeLine_3daf8d6160413554` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0065*
- **EN:** "Nobody here's stopping you, go"
- **TR:** 'Seni tutan yok burada, git.'

### 2686. `RD_ALifeLine_2a52aa9172cb3adb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0066*
- **EN:** "I'm from Georgia, I never saw so much corn in my life"
- **TR:** "Ben Georgia'lıyım, hayatımda bu kadar mısır görmedim."

### 2687. `RD_ALifeLine_131b542a3112c6ba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0066*
- **EN:** "It's Kentucky, that's what it is"
- **TR:** 'Burası Kentucky, böyle bir yer.'

### 2688. `RD_ALifeLine_54c6e05006aeb37b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0066*
- **EN:** 'Corn and bourbon and dogs'
- **TR:** 'Mısır, burbon, köpekler.'

### 2689. `RD_ALifeLine_06a4cbd2161b4b38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0066*
- **EN:** "That's about right"
- **TR:** 'Aşağı yukarı öyle.'

### 2690. `RD_ALifeLine_5ba241242f0110cb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0066*
- **EN:** 'And basketball'
- **TR:** 'Bir de basketbol.'

### 2691. `RD_ALifeLine_0d8f632937aa141d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'At Benning I did pushups for sneezing'
- **TR:** "Benning'de hapşırdım diye şınav çektirirlerdi."

### 2692. `RD_ALifeLine_4c505b235f890ade` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0068*
- **EN:** "That's true"
- **TR:** 'Doğru.'

### 2693. `RD_ALifeLine_7964e2d04d4b7342` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0068*
- **EN:** "Drill sergeant would've had our asses for this shit"
- **TR:** 'Eğitmen çavuş bu haltı görse kıçımıza tekmeyi basardı.'

### 2694. `RD_ALifeLine_2d7f32b367f31cf4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0068*
- **EN:** "He'd have had our asses and then he'd have had a plan"
- **TR:** 'Önce kıçımıza tekmeyi basar, sonra da bir plan çıkarırdı.'

### 2695. `RD_ALifeLine_12d4f1e475cd6fc3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'And a clipboard'
- **TR:** 'Bir de elinde dosya tahtasıyla.'

### 2696. `RD_ALifeLine_0440da01419a285b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'I still find sand in my boots from Kuwait'
- **TR:** "Kuveyt'ten kalma kum hâlâ botlarımdan çıkıyor."

### 2697. `RD_ALifeLine_04d950f741793b91` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0069*
- **EN:** "It's been two years"
- **TR:** 'İki yıl oldu.'

### 2698. `RD_ALifeLine_7c8198860476b676` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0069*
- **EN:** "I shake them out and some falls on the floor, every time, I don't know where it comes from"
- **TR:** 'Silkeleyince her seferinde biraz yere dökülüyor, nereden geliyor bilmiyorum.'

### 2699. `RD_ALifeLine_5589ed2f2c7553cb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'The same place you do'
- **TR:** 'Senin geldiğin yerden.'

### 2700. `RD_ALifeLine_279a510120fc5ab0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0069*
- **EN:** "Mine's in my fucking ears"
- **TR:** 'Benimki kulaklarımda, siktir.'

### 2701. `RD_ALifeLine_1cbd321558205830` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0070*
- **EN:** "Haven't had mail since Friday"
- **TR:** 'Cumadan beri mektup gelmedi.'

### 2702. `RD_ALifeLine_431459d77bf6842f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0070*
- **EN:** "I know there's no mail, I still look"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, posta yok. Yine de bakıyorum.'

### 2703. `RD_ALifeLine_296960265fcca991` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0071*
- **EN:** "I'm owed three weeks of fucking pay"
- **TR:** 'Üç haftalık maaş alacağım var, siktir.'

### 2704. `RD_ALifeLine_430617c642091ea2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0071*
- **EN:** "Who's gonna pay you?"
- **TR:** 'Kim ödeyecek ki sana?'

### 2705. `RD_ALifeLine_3b37d3177ef95125` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'The finance office, when it opens'
- **TR:** 'Mali işler birimi, açılınca.'

### 2706. `RD_ALifeLine_5d4e832211193ddd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'Put it on a form'
- **TR:** 'Forma yaz.'

### 2707. `RD_ALifeLine_5712e0e856c15d68` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'Put it on three fucking forms'
- **TR:** 'Üç tane siktiğimin formuna yaz.'

### 2708. `RD_ALifeLine_37e5c3e74a2a4b1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0072*
- **EN:** 'I was due to re-up in August'
- **TR:** 'Ağustosta sözleşmemi uzatacaktım.'

### 2709. `RD_ALifeLine_5777ed303fd6c77e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0072*
- **EN:** "They'll make you do the paperwork, and the paperwork's the only fucking thing left that works"
- **TR:** 'Kâğıt işi yaptıracaklar, hem de çalışan tek şey o kaldı, siktir.'

### 2710. `RD_ALifeLine_699b549c4b075001` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0072*
- **EN:** "Re-up, I'll be your witness, you dumb bastard"
- **TR:** 'Uzat hadi, şahidin olurum, aptal piç.'

### 2711. `RD_ALifeLine_10ffb29b200cee28` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0073*
- **EN:** 'I spent my whole career training to fight Russians'
- **TR:** 'Bütün kariyerimi Ruslarla savaşmak için eğitim yaparak geçirdim.'

### 2712. `RD_ALifeLine_47b35b5a7964c499` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0073*
- **EN:** 'So did I'
- **TR:** 'Ben de.'

### 2713. `RD_ALifeLine_67f8144a3a22584f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0073*
- **EN:** 'And here we are in Kentucky, and not a Russian in sight'
- **TR:** "Şimdi Kentucky'deyiz, ortada tek Rus yok."

### 2714. `RD_ALifeLine_595f012d0bd30bf8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0073*
- **EN:** "I'd take the Russians"
- **TR:** 'Ben Rusları tercih ederdim.'

### 2715. `RD_ALifeLine_240728516b06ad15` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0073*
- **EN:** "I'd take the Russians, at least they'd be organized"
- **TR:** 'Ben de, hiç olmazsa derli toplu olurlardı.'

### 2716. `RD_ALifeLine_0d0e1b43618103f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0075*
- **EN:** 'This is FUBAR'
- **TR:** 'Bu tam bir FUBAR.'

### 2717. `RD_ALifeLine_6d0c32d8330928cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0075*
- **EN:** "It's SNAFU"
- **TR:** 'SNAFU bu.'

### 2718. `RD_ALifeLine_166e59550bd58056` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0075*
- **EN:** "One's a situation, one's a state of being, I forget which"
- **TR:** 'Biri durum, biri bir hâl, hangisi hangisi unuttum.'

### 2719. `RD_ALifeLine_28720a9c2c5d16d0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0075*
- **EN:** "It's FUBAR, I was there for the goddamn invention"
- **TR:** 'FUBAR bu, icat edilirken ben oradaydım, lanet olsun.'

### 2720. `RD_ALifeLine_6bc6555d7c465243` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0076*
- **EN:** "I'm not scared"
- **TR:** 'Korkmuyorum.'

### 2721. `RD_ALifeLine_7454822977008836` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0076*
- **EN:** "It's a different kind of cold"
- **TR:** 'Başka cins bir soğuk bu.'

### 2722. `RD_ALifeLine_3c192af067c8b81b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0077*
- **EN:** "Breaking in these boots is a joke, they're from 1990 and they hate me"
- **TR:** "Şu botları ayağıma alıştırmak tam bir şaka, 1990'dan kalmalar, benden nefret ediyorlar."

### 2723. `RD_ALifeLine_3b65876c31be0dbc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0077*
- **EN:** 'They fit'
- **TR:** 'Ayağına tam oturuyorlar.'

### 2724. `RD_ALifeLine_7c9e89f6726a17d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0077*
- **EN:** "They fit like somebody else's feet"
- **TR:** 'Başka birinin ayağına göre yapılmış gibi oturuyorlar.'

### 2725. `RD_ALifeLine_7c68e7834f45754e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0077*
- **EN:** "Somebody else's feet had a better time, the lucky bastards"
- **TR:** 'O başka ayaklar daha çok keyif almıştır, şanslı piçler.'

### 2726. `RD_ALifeLine_202a8426665fb821` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0085*
- **EN:** "I'd give a month's pay for a cold Coke"
- **TR:** 'Soğuk bir Coca-Cola için bir aylık maaşımı verirdim.'

### 2727. `RD_ALifeLine_1f6769a40cc04327` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0085*
- **EN:** 'Which month'
- **TR:** 'Hangi ayı?'

### 2728. `RD_ALifeLine_2f5542fb03c30b52` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0085*
- **EN:** 'Any, all of them, I never spent it anyway...'
- **TR:** 'Hangisi olursa, hepsini. Zaten hiç harcamadım...'

### 2729. `RD_ALifeLine_646ac5a10f7c6de2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0085*
- **EN:** "Take mine too, I've got a lot of months"
- **TR:** 'Benimkini de al, bir sürü ayım var.'

### 2730. `RD_ALifeLine_4214888c5739ccb7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0086*
- **EN:** "Somebody drew a mustache on the Lieutenant's photo, with a Sharpie, the sons of bitches"
- **TR:** 'Biri Teğmenin fotoğrafına Sharpie ile bıyık çizmiş, orospu çocukları.'

### 2731. `RD_ALifeLine_7ba41be528e257de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0086*
- **EN:** "Where's the photo?"
- **TR:** 'Fotoğraf nerede?'

### 2732. `RD_ALifeLine_363ae33f50c6e779` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0086*
- **EN:** 'His wallet, he was showing the platoon his kid'
- **TR:** 'Cüzdanında. Müfrezeye çocuğunu gösteriyordu.'

### 2733. `RD_ALifeLine_7f48bc424ac8180a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0086*
- **EN:** "It's a very good mustache"
- **TR:** 'Çok iyi bir bıyık olmuş.'

### 2734. `RD_ALifeLine_24253b8046eac665` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0087*
- **EN:** 'I counted our MREs, eleven'
- **TR:** "MRE'leri saydım, on bir tane."

### 2735. `RD_ALifeLine_188a6256535e0efc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0087*
- **EN:** 'Counted again, ten'
- **TR:** 'Tekrar saydım, on.'

### 2736. `RD_ALifeLine_24ed1d6d22650df9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0087*
- **EN:** "Count a third time and you'll have none"
- **TR:** 'Bir üçüncü kez sayarsan hiç kalmaz.'

### 2737. `RD_ALifeLine_420745f5283cee29` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0089*
- **EN:** 'Fort Knox taught me to drive a sixty-ton tank'
- **TR:** 'Fort Knox bana altmış tonluk tank sürmeyi öğretti.'

### 2738. `RD_ALifeLine_22f4a9aa2e4ed10f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0089*
- **EN:** 'And not one damn thing about this!'
- **TR:** 'Ama bunun hakkında hiçbir şey öğretmedi, hiçbir şey!'

### 2739. `RD_ALifeLine_44ec92c85025f125` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0089*
- **EN:** "They don't make a field manual for this shit!"
- **TR:** 'Bu halt için saha kılavuzu bile yok, siktir!'

### 2740. `RD_ALifeLine_08c7bcff7ab891a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0090*
- **EN:** "Who's got dip?"
- **TR:** 'Kimde çiğneme tütünü var?'

### 2741. `RD_ALifeLine_4e1b7c3d0a13cdff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0090*
- **EN:** 'Skoal, wintergreen'
- **TR:** 'Skoal, nane aromalı.'

### 2742. `RD_ALifeLine_67fd9154075efa7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0090*
- **EN:** "I'll trade you a pack of gum"
- **TR:** 'Sana bir paket sakızla takas ederim.'

### 2743. `RD_ALifeLine_11f4bd8346dcec62` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0090*
- **EN:** 'Spit in a can, not on the goddamn floor'
- **TR:** 'Kutuya tükür, şu lanet yere değil.'

### 2744. `RD_ALifeLine_2a556a385e4cd49b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0090*
- **EN:** "It's a floor, it's already got everything on it"
- **TR:** 'Yer burası, zaten her halt dökülmüş üstüne.'

### 2745. `RD_ALifeLine_0dcf1ca954515cc6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0090*
- **EN:** "That's not a reason"
- **TR:** 'Bu bir sebep değil.'

### 2746. `RD_ALifeLine_27eeebd80c0e3387` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'My first jump, I closed my eyes the whole way down'
- **TR:** 'İlk atlayışımda inene kadar gözlerimi kapattım.'

### 2747. `RD_ALifeLine_308266ee4b76882a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'Everybody does'
- **TR:** 'Herkes öyle yapar.'

### 2748. `RD_ALifeLine_23de947b60b10915` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'The sergeant said open them or break your legs, I opened them'
- **TR:** 'Çavuş "Gözünü aç, yoksa bacağını kırarsın" dedi, açtım.'

### 2749. `RD_ALifeLine_3b89e02b2d896dc8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'Good decision'
- **TR:** 'İyi karar.'

### 2750. `RD_ALifeLine_55f482b942cc4133` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'I opened mine at the last second, it was a good surprise'
- **TR:** 'Ben gözlerimi son saniyede açtım, hoş bir sürpriz oldu.'

### 2751. `RD_ALifeLine_426086f33d5f45e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0093*
- **EN:** "Mama's gonna kill me, I haven't called in a month"
- **TR:** 'Annem beni öldürecek, bir aydır aramadım.'

### 2752. `RD_ALifeLine_27dd551437d76020` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0093*
- **EN:** "There's no phones"
- **TR:** 'Telefon yok ki.'

### 2753. `RD_ALifeLine_734e790431be8f8d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0093*
- **EN:** "I know, I'm saying when this is over she's gonna kill me"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, diyorum ki bu iş bitince beni öldürecek.'

### 2754. `RD_ALifeLine_06f53c146d3241a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0093*
- **EN:** "When it's over she'll be too happy to kill you"
- **TR:** 'Bitince öyle sevinecek ki seni öldürmeyi aklına bile getirmez.'

### 2755. `RD_ALifeLine_574d5c47724f702b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0093*
- **EN:** "She'll kill you after..."
- **TR:** 'Sonra yine öldürür seni...'

### 2756. `RD_ALifeLine_0c4e425d30646410` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'Ranger school, I ate a rat'
- **TR:** 'Ranger okulunda bir fare yedim.'

### 2757. `RD_ALifeLine_032168444f895388` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'It was a very small rat and a man was watching'
- **TR:** 'Çok küçük bir fareydi, üstelik adamın biri izliyordu.'

### 2758. `RD_ALifeLine_4464d69b6f6162b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'Did it taste like chicken?'
- **TR:** 'Tavuk gibi miydi tadı?'

### 2759. `RD_ALifeLine_3d2bba5f2f6588e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'It tasted like rat, with hope'
- **TR:** 'Fare tadındaydı, umutla birlikte.'

### 2760. `RD_ALifeLine_3266b51547cc5207` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'Everything tastes like chicken with enough hope and a lot of salt'
- **TR:** 'Yeterince umut ve bol tuzla her şey tavuk gibi olur.'

### 2761. `RD_ALifeLine_5afd0c197a430a13` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0095*
- **EN:** 'Count the rounds, then count them again'
- **TR:** 'Mermileri say, sonra bir daha say.'

### 2762. `RD_ALifeLine_41f31d7f606c2803` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0095*
- **EN:** "I'll count them a third time"
- **TR:** 'Ben bir üçüncü kez sayarım.'

### 2763. `RD_ALifeLine_605f55bb1f45d62c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0095*
- **EN:** 'Because the number keeps changing...'
- **TR:** 'Çünkü sayı sürekli değişiyor...'

### 2764. `RD_ALifeLine_3383b3ec12aa274f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0096*
- **EN:** "Lieutenant's not sleeping"
- **TR:** 'Teğmen uyumuyor.'

### 2765. `RD_ALifeLine_6318d0215d4c1cba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0096*
- **EN:** 'He sits up with the map every night'
- **TR:** 'Her gece haritayla oturuyor.'

### 2766. `RD_ALifeLine_413700035e1a8101` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0096*
- **EN:** "He's got nothing on that map, it's an old county road map from a gas station"
- **TR:** 'O haritada bir şey yok ki, benzin istasyonundan alınma eski bir ilçe yol haritası.'

### 2767. `RD_ALifeLine_5dc2a98604a741e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0096*
- **EN:** "That's what I'm saying"
- **TR:** 'Ben de onu diyorum.'

### 2768. `RD_ALifeLine_271fb47526d2401a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0098*
- **EN:** 'Take the corner seat, you can see both doors'
- **TR:** 'Köşedeki koltuğa otur, iki kapıyı da görürsün.'

### 2769. `RD_ALifeLine_0b61ba3f02871246` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0098*
- **EN:** 'I like the corner'
- **TR:** 'Köşeyi severim.'

### 2770. `RD_ALifeLine_3278d7fc74c79350` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0098*
- **EN:** "Everybody likes the corner, that's why it's a good corner"
- **TR:** 'Köşeyi herkes sever, iyi bir köşe olmasının sebebi de o.'

### 2771. `RD_ALifeLine_02801bcc357a0c32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0098*
- **EN:** 'Take turns'
- **TR:** 'Sırayla olsun.'

### 2772. `RD_ALifeLine_35124f6650c16bd3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0098*
- **EN:** "I'll take the other corner"
- **TR:** 'Ben öbür köşeyi alırım.'

### 2773. `RD_ALifeLine_3c3f802a2deafc09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0099*
- **EN:** "Boots off, feet up, you're no good to me with blisters"
- **TR:** 'Botları çıkar, ayaklarını uzat, su toplamış ayaklarla hiçbir işime yaramazsın.'

### 2774. `RD_ALifeLine_58565a9b3a9a6d89` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0099*
- **EN:** "Boots off, that's an order"
- **TR:** 'Botları çıkar, bu bir emir.'

### 2775. `RD_ALifeLine_7eb3eca1722841f0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0099*
- **EN:** "From me, I'm the oldest"
- **TR:** 'Benden, en yaşlı benim.'

### 2776. `RD_ALifeLine_6941229838af194b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0099*
- **EN:** "It's a good order, take 'em off"
- **TR:** 'İyi bir emir, çıkar şunları.'

### 2777. `RD_ALifeLine_683ca6eb1675a538` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0100*
- **EN:** "What's the first thing you do when you get home?"
- **TR:** 'Eve dönünce ilk ne yaparsın?'

### 2778. `RD_ALifeLine_0d549fcf64b561c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0100*
- **EN:** 'Home?'
- **TR:** 'Eve mi?'

### 2779. `RD_ALifeLine_14dc2c2e3cb9cd85` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0100*
- **EN:** 'After all this, when the Army says we can go'
- **TR:** 'Bütün bunlar bitince, Ordu gidebiliriz dediğinde.'

### 2780. `RD_ALifeLine_18b7158f6fc38ab9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0100*
- **EN:** 'Sleep, in my own bed, with the window open and a fan going'
- **TR:** 'Uyurum, kendi yatağımda, pencere açık, vantilatör çalışırken.'

### 2781. `RD_ALifeLine_666af5073ac7da70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0100*
- **EN:** 'And a glass of tea so cold it hurts your teeth'
- **TR:** 'Bir de dişini sızlatacak kadar soğuk bir bardak çay.'

### 2782. `RD_ALifeLine_7628fe8e3dd97be0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0101*
- **EN:** "I thought it'd be louder"
- **TR:** 'Daha gürültülü olur sanmıştım.'

### 2783. `RD_ALifeLine_20c259bc00c2d2d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0101*
- **EN:** "The end of things, I thought there'd be more noise"
- **TR:** 'Dünyanın sonu için diyorum, daha çok gürültü olur sanmıştım.'

### 2784. `RD_ALifeLine_49cdd41062a62616` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0101*
- **EN:** 'There was noise, in the beginning'
- **TR:** 'Gürültü vardı, başlarda.'

### 2785. `RD_ALifeLine_3a952a8d796b14da` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0101*
- **EN:** "I mean now, it's just quiet and hot"
- **TR:** 'Şimdiyi diyorum, sadece sessizlik ve sıcak.'

### 2786. `RD_ALifeLine_40559b8f07afd7d5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0101*
- **EN:** "It's the quiet that gets you"
- **TR:** 'Seni asıl çökerten o sessizlik.'

### 2787. `RD_ALifeLine_169bae9e4c36656b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0104*
- **EN:** 'Rations'
- **TR:** 'Erzak?'

### 2788. `RD_ALifeLine_7e636bce458524d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0104*
- **EN:** 'Four days'
- **TR:** 'Dört günlük.'

### 2789. `RD_ALifeLine_15880a371c9ddc00` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0104*
- **EN:** 'If we stretch'
- **TR:** 'İdare edersek.'

### 2790. `RD_ALifeLine_0180abb723868c2c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0104*
- **EN:** "Five, six if somebody doesn't eat"
- **TR:** 'Beş, biri yemezse altı.'

### 2791. `RD_ALifeLine_5f159aad699e3cae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0104*
- **EN:** 'Everybody eats...'
- **TR:** 'Herkes yer...'

### 2792. `RD_ALifeLine_1df95cae52bd7963` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0105*
- **EN:** 'You write home?'
- **TR:** 'Eve yazıyor musun?'

### 2793. `RD_ALifeLine_27f75129365f2fc7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0105*
- **EN:** 'What do you put?'
- **TR:** 'Ne yazıyorsun?'

### 2794. `RD_ALifeLine_474bdeef0f53d850` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0105*
- **EN:** "Weather, mostly, it's hot, it's still hot"
- **TR:** 'Çoğunlukla hava durumu, sıcak, hâlâ sıcak.'

### 2795. `RD_ALifeLine_136468742a07264d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0105*
- **EN:** "That's what I put too"
- **TR:** 'Ben de onu yazıyorum.'

### 2796. `RD_ALifeLine_1e94cd92613083d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0105*
- **EN:** "Nobody's reading it, but we write it"
- **TR:** 'Okuyan yok ama biz yine de yazıyoruz.'

### 2797. `RD_ALifeLine_6dc6c2d7227226d9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0106*
- **EN:** 'I miss chow hall pizza'
- **TR:** 'Yemekhane pizzasını özledim.'

### 2798. `RD_ALifeLine_6ba28b627a533f13` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0106*
- **EN:** 'Nobody misses chow hall pizza'
- **TR:** 'Yemekhane pizzasını kimse özlemez.'

### 2799. `RD_ALifeLine_6704e8074c7fd32c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0106*
- **EN:** "I'd eat a whole pan, cold, on the floor of the latrine"
- **TR:** 'Bir tepsisini yerdim, soğuk soğuk, tuvaletin zemininde.'

### 2800. `RD_ALifeLine_56f5334d123c94e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0106*
- **EN:** 'Okay, that one I believe'
- **TR:** 'Tamam, buna inandım.'

### 2801. `RD_ALifeLine_6918d2b631269e78` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0106*
- **EN:** "I'd eat the pan"
- **TR:** 'Ben tepsiyi de yerdim.'

### 2802. `RD_ALifeLine_19a052085d7e7750` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0107*
- **EN:** 'You ever been this far from base?'
- **TR:** 'Üsten hiç bu kadar uzak kaldın mı?'

### 2803. `RD_ALifeLine_70e957954b03f54a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0107*
- **EN:** "It's thirty miles"
- **TR:** 'Kırk sekiz kilometre var.'
- *Not: 30 miles -> 48 km*

### 2804. `RD_ALifeLine_732d674a376ad3dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0107*
- **EN:** 'Thirty miles feels like thirty hundred'
- **TR:** 'Kırk sekiz kilometre değil de dört bin sekiz yüz kilometre gibi.'
- *Not: 30 miles -> 48 km*

### 2805. `RD_ALifeLine_0196616d0d8a6777` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0107*
- **EN:** "It's the longest thirty miles in the world"
- **TR:** 'Dünyanın en uzun kırk sekiz kilometresi bu.'
- *Not: 30 miles -> 48 km*

### 2806. `RD_ALifeLine_3603204a4a57d2ac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0108*
- **EN:** "My hands haven't stopped shaking since the last one"
- **TR:** 'Ellerim son seferden beri titremeyi kesmedi.'

### 2807. `RD_ALifeLine_0395babb51fcfed1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0108*
- **EN:** "Don't, it'll make it worse"
- **TR:** 'Yapma, daha da kötü olacak.'

### 2808. `RD_ALifeLine_08e5a54978663627` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0108*
- **EN:** "It's okay, I'm just looking"
- **TR:** 'Sorun değil, sadece bakıyorum.'

### 2809. `RD_ALifeLine_0f3db6a3317004b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0108*
- **EN:** "It's the cold, ha"
- **TR:** 'Soğuktan, ha.'

### 2810. `RD_ALifeLine_4aea463510aad04b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0108*
- **EN:** "There's no cold..."
- **TR:** 'Soğuk yok ki...'

### 2811. `RD_ALifeLine_7c0ab7875311e636` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0109*
- **EN:** "Somebody's snoring on watch"
- **TR:** 'Biri nöbette horluyor.'

### 2812. `RD_ALifeLine_09fc2bf87692fbc8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0109*
- **EN:** "It's me, and I'm not sorry"
- **TR:** 'Benim, pişman da değilim.'

### 2813. `RD_ALifeLine_6a003f3c55160bd3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0109*
- **EN:** 'Wake up and be sorry'
- **TR:** 'Uyan da pişman ol.'

### 2814. `RD_ALifeLine_6f1c3f4461bb3859` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0129*
- **EN:** 'I used to think I was tough, and then I smelled it'
- **TR:** 'Kendimi sert sanırdım, sonra kokuyu aldım.'

### 2815. `RD_ALifeLine_3c0f8aa34553a917` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0129*
- **EN:** 'Smelled what'
- **TR:** 'Hangi kokuyu?'

### 2816. `RD_ALifeLine_32f4011029ca3149` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0129*
- **EN:** 'The first one, up close, in the heat, and my whole body just said no'
- **TR:** 'İlkini, yakından, sıcakta... Bütün vücudum hayır dedi.'

### 2817. `RD_ALifeLine_75620eab06639df0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0129*
- **EN:** 'It does that to everybody...'
- **TR:** 'Herkese bunu yapar...'

### 2818. `RD_ALifeLine_14a9884f1054beed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0132*
- **EN:** "I've got a letter in my pocket, I wrote it to my mother, and I'm not gonna send it"
- **TR:** 'Cebimde annem için yazdığım bir mektup var, göndermeyeceğim.'

### 2819. `RD_ALifeLine_571fb7ed3338a513` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0132*
- **EN:** "Because then she'd know where I am, and I don't want her coming"
- **TR:** 'Çünkü o zaman nerede olduğumu öğrenir, gelmesini de istemem.'

### 2820. `RD_ALifeLine_56bf9bce6782b44a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0134*
- **EN:** "What's the worst thing you ate in the service?"
- **TR:** 'Askerlikte yediğin en kötü şey neydi?'

### 2821. `RD_ALifeLine_200acb8a582d2de8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0134*
- **EN:** 'Powdered eggs'
- **TR:** 'Toz yumurta.'

### 2822. `RD_ALifeLine_251b2a7b117f3228` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0134*
- **EN:** 'Gray, gray eggs, with the texture of a sponge'
- **TR:** 'Gri, gri yumurtalar, sünger gibi bir dokuyla.'

### 2823. `RD_ALifeLine_64857a4a0b55d9bb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0134*
- **EN:** "I had a goat once, in Kuwait, don't ask"
- **TR:** "Ben bir keresinde keçi yemiştim, Kuveyt'te, sorma."

### 2824. `RD_ALifeLine_25a6d5ea24c5fbaf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0135*
- **EN:** "I keep checking my watch, and then I remember there's nowhere to be"
- **TR:** 'Sürekli saatime bakıyorum, sonra gidecek bir yer olmadığını hatırlıyorum.'

### 2825. `RD_ALifeLine_3420cff05b927db4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0135*
- **EN:** "That's the worst part"
- **TR:** 'En kötü kısmı bu.'

### 2826. `RD_ALifeLine_7e00396b591d245c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0135*
- **EN:** "It's the worst part of every day"
- **TR:** 'Her günün en kötü kısmı bu.'

### 2827. `RD_ALifeLine_7ce71dbf69f9aefd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0137*
- **EN:** "The first thing I'm doing when this is over is sleeping for three days"
- **TR:** 'Bu iş bitince ilk yapacağım şey üç gün uyumak.'

### 2828. `RD_ALifeLine_412f6dcf01aa37c1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0137*
- **EN:** "And then I'm complaining about how I slept too much"
- **TR:** 'Sonra da çok uyudum diye şikâyet edeceğim.'

### 2829. `RD_ALifeLine_53288e470f934c01` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0137*
