- **EN:** "I'll complain with you"
- **TR:** 'Ben de seninle beraber şikâyet ederim.'

### 2830. `RD_ALifeLine_0bfb41b95bdd1f5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0162*
- **EN:** "I'm so fucking tired of MREs I could cry!"
- **TR:** "Şu MRE'lerden öyle bıktım ki, siktir, ağlayacağım!"

### 2831. `RD_ALifeLine_3a9e6e4c70a673a0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0162*
- **EN:** 'Then cry'
- **TR:** 'Ağla o zaman.'

### 2832. `RD_ALifeLine_67a27f294cffd5e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0162*
- **EN:** "I don't have the fucking water!"
- **TR:** 'Ağlayacak kadar su bile yok, siktir!'

### 2833. `RD_ALifeLine_67df8c3215aceaa8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0164*
- **EN:** "I saw something in the road this morning I'm not gonna tell you about"
- **TR:** 'Bu sabah yolda bir şey gördüm, sana anlatmayacağım.'

### 2834. `RD_ALifeLine_5c037d4c17455213` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0164*
- **EN:** "Then don't"
- **TR:** 'O zaman anlatma.'

### 2835. `RD_ALifeLine_58b8c32a1093170b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0164*
- **EN:** "I'm just saying, I'm not the same as Friday"
- **TR:** 'Sadece diyorum ki, Cuma günkü ben değilim artık.'

### 2836. `RD_ALifeLine_011ff6413c8fb7cb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0166*
- **EN:** "Man, I'd sell my own mother for a cold Coke and a hot shower"
- **TR:** 'Ya, soğuk bir Coca-Cola ve sıcak bir duş için annemi satardım.'

### 2837. `RD_ALifeLine_2849e8c850fbb749` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0166*
- **EN:** "You'd kill for a cold Coke, you'd negotiate the shower"
- **TR:** 'Soğuk Cola için adam öldürürsün, duş için de pazarlığa oturursun.'

### 2838. `RD_ALifeLine_589fc0ea24019ad4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0166*
- **EN:** "Fuck you, I'd kill for both!"
- **TR:** 'Siktir git, ikisi için de öldürürüm!'

### 2839. `RD_ALifeLine_4bf5be1e267b12b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0166*
- **EN:** "I'd take a bathroom with a door"
- **TR:** 'Ben kapısı olan bir banyoyla yetinirdim.'

### 2840. `RD_ALifeLine_5ab51eeb416e444b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0168*
- **EN:** "If the Army doesn't give me a plan by Friday I'm going home"
- **TR:** 'Ordu cumaya kadar bir plan vermezse eve gidiyorum.'

### 2841. `RD_ALifeLine_39471dd800c9d624` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0168*
- **EN:** 'Home where?'
- **TR:** 'Hangi eve?'

### 2842. `RD_ALifeLine_0f74aea14a29761e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0168*
- **EN:** "Wherever home is, I'll find it"
- **TR:** 'Neresiyse, bulurum.'

### 2843. `RD_ALifeLine_3004e9742e55b47c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0168*
- **EN:** "You'll find it at the bottom of a ditch, shut the hell up"
- **TR:** 'Bir hendeğin dibinde bulursun, kes sesini artık.'

### 2844. `RD_ALifeLine_3c36e4790d96f361` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0170*
- **EN:** "I'm so fucking dirty I could grow potatoes in my armpits"
- **TR:** 'Öyle pisim ki siktir, koltuk altımda patates yetiştirebilirim.'

### 2845. `RD_ALifeLine_0da96dd82c3aabc7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0170*
- **EN:** "Seriously, there's a little farm back there"
- **TR:** 'Ciddiyim, şurada ufak bir çiftlik var.'

### 2846. `RD_ALifeLine_0145ac6e45937fe7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0170*
- **EN:** 'Plant something useful, a tomato'
- **TR:** 'Faydalı bir şey ek, mesela domates.'

### 2847. `RD_ALifeLine_32dcec15372a0d3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0172*
- **EN:** "Sergeant's an asshole"
- **TR:** 'Çavuş göt herifin teki.'

### 2848. `RD_ALifeLine_1accb69b78c99918` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0172*
- **EN:** "Sergeant's the only thing between us and chaos"
- **TR:** 'Çavuş, bizimle kaos arasındaki tek şey.'

### 2849. `RD_ALifeLine_599554123df3adc4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0172*
- **EN:** "That's what I said, he's an asshole, a necessary asshole"
- **TR:** 'Ben de onu dedim, göt herif, ama gerekli bir göt herif.'

### 2850. `RD_ALifeLine_183f51604d95355f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0174*
- **EN:** "The thing I can't get over is nobody told us, we were just standing at a gate one day"
- **TR:** 'Hâlâ aklım almıyor, kimse bize bir şey söylemedi, bir gün kapıda dikilip durduk.'

### 2851. `RD_ALifeLine_39b6123f61a5a1db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0174*
- **EN:** "That's the Army"
- **TR:** 'Ordu işte.'

### 2852. `RD_ALifeLine_3a818a2b66f273e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0174*
- **EN:** "That's every goddamn thing..."
- **TR:** 'Her lanet şey öyle...'

### 2853. `RD_ALifeLine_1af0b47d5e7b52c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0176*
- **EN:** "I'd give my left arm to be back in Fort Knox, eating bad pizza"
- **TR:** "Sol kolumu verirdim şimdi Fort Knox'ta kötü pizza yiyor olmak için."

### 2854. `RD_ALifeLine_66914d3a3c88e510` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0176*
- **EN:** "It's thirty miles through hell, I'm not walking it"
- **TR:** 'Cehennemin içinden kırk sekiz kilometre, yürümem.'
- *Not: 30 miles -> 48 km*

### 2855. `RD_ALifeLine_4549367e3562da6d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0176*
- **EN:** 'Then enjoy the pizza in your head'
- **TR:** 'O zaman kafanda pizza ye.'

### 2856. `RD_ALifeLine_5bc7e5873b1ec2af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0177*
- **EN:** 'Who touched my rifle?'
- **TR:** 'Tüfeğime kim dokundu?'

### 2857. `RD_ALifeLine_7891ec2f2f7e9104` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0177*
- **EN:** "It's been moved, it was on the left side of my bag"
- **TR:** 'Yeri değişmiş, çantamın sol tarafındaydı.'

### 2858. `RD_ALifeLine_7f68dff36dd5cc9c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0177*
- **EN:** 'I moved it, it was in my way, calm down'
- **TR:** 'Ben aldım, yoluma çıkıyordu, sakin ol.'

### 2859. `RD_ALifeLine_51a032814dbb5f38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0191*
- **EN:** "I don't think we're getting extracted"
- **TR:** 'Bizi tahliye edecekler gibi durmuyor.'

### 2860. `RD_ALifeLine_31ef6ffa6eef12f6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0191*
- **EN:** 'Too hot to talk about anything'
- **TR:** 'Bu sıcakta hiçbir şey konuşulmaz.'

### 2861. `RD_ALifeLine_7400d947747a292b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0191*
- **EN:** 'Shut up'
- **TR:** 'Kapa çeneni.'

### 2862. `RD_ALifeLine_71e96b2145324a7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0191*
- **EN:** "Don't talk about it"
- **TR:** 'Bunu konuşma.'

### 2863. `RD_ALifeLine_62e62cea5a07642f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0191*
- **EN:** "I'm just saying what everybody's thinking"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin aklından geçeni söylüyorum sadece.'

### 2864. `RD_ALifeLine_52b11ffe05e0fe4d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0191*
- **EN:** "Then keep thinking it, don't say it"
- **TR:** 'O zaman düşünmeye devam et ama sakın söyleme.'

### 2865. `RD_ALifeLine_560d3bb47dd97165` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0008*
- **EN:** "What do you say when somebody asks for water and you can't spare any?"
- **TR:** 'Biri su istediğinde ve ona ayıracak suyun yoksa ne dersin?'

### 2866. `RD_ALifeLine_6370c22961ef8638` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0008*
- **EN:** 'The truth, I hope'
- **TR:** 'Doğrusunu, umarım.'

### 2867. `RD_ALifeLine_2d6161577bf02706` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0008*
- **EN:** 'Even when they only want a swallow?'
- **TR:** 'Bir yudum bile isteyen birine mi?'

### 2868. `RD_ALifeLine_7222169b74789464` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0008*
- **EN:** "Especially then. Don't make them wait on water you haven't got"
- **TR:** 'Özellikle de o zaman. Elinde olmayan su için kimseyi bekletme.'

### 2869. `RD_ALifeLine_4e69bf34156a8423` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0001*
- **EN:** "You're cheating"
- **TR:** 'Hile yapıyorsun.'

### 2870. `RD_ALifeLine_7bea84be0e081f37` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0001*
- **EN:** "You had that ace up your fuckin' sleeve, I seen it"
- **TR:** 'Koluna as saklamıştın, siktiğimin, gördüm.'

### 2871. `RD_ALifeLine_391f541c0712b4b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0001*
- **EN:** 'Go on, show your sleeve'
- **TR:** 'Hadi, kolunu göster.'

### 2872. `RD_ALifeLine_40361d2a0380a12d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0001*
- **EN:** "I ain't showing nobody my sleeve, back off"
- **TR:** 'Kimseye kolumu göstermem, geri çekil.'

### 2873. `RD_ALifeLine_77a4814c0043e3d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0002*
- **EN:** "I've been waiting my fuckin' turn for ten minutes, you're hogging it"
- **TR:** 'On dakikadır siktiğimin sırasını bekliyorum, sen elinden bırakmıyorsun.'

### 2874. `RD_ALifeLine_66c22a445d695376` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0002*
- **EN:** "It's a bottle, you can hog it later"
- **TR:** 'Şişe bu, sonra tutarsın.'

### 2875. `RD_ALifeLine_34349c7450c5632c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0002*
- **EN:** "It's empty, you two dumbasses been fighting over fumes"
- **TR:** 'Boş o be, iki salak koku için kapışıyorsunuz.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 2876. `RD_ALifeLine_14beaef33336c93f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0003*
- **EN:** 'Who gets the boots?'
- **TR:** 'Çizmeler kimin olacak?'

### 2877. `RD_ALifeLine_04254ebb2bb055c0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0003*
- **EN:** "I found 'em"
- **TR:** 'Ben buldum.'

### 2878. `RD_ALifeLine_29d241b33d3eb49d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0003*
- **EN:** "You found 'em on my side of the fuckin' room"
- **TR:** 'Siktiğimin odasında benim tarafta buldun.'

### 2879. `RD_ALifeLine_35f35526294719f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0003*
- **EN:** "It's a room, there's no sides, flip a coin for the damn things"
- **TR:** 'Oda burası, taraf falan yok, yazı tura atılsın şu lanet çizmelere.'

### 2880. `RD_ALifeLine_30fdffac702997f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'New guy, make coffee'
- **TR:** 'Çaylak, kahve yap.'

### 2881. `RD_ALifeLine_24f067502629a57d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'We got no coffee'
- **TR:** 'Kahve yok ki.'

### 2882. `RD_ALifeLine_3b6b71063d0d3013` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'Then make something that looks like coffee'
- **TR:** 'O zaman kahveye benzeyen bir şey yap.'

### 2883. `RD_ALifeLine_4440f64e611f2bca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0004*
- **EN:** 'What do I make it out of?'
- **TR:** 'Neyden yapayım?'

### 2884. `RD_ALifeLine_6965765d155c8e16` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0004*
- **EN:** "Dirt, I don't give a shit, just make it hot"
- **TR:** 'Topraktan olur, bok umurumda, sıcak olsun yeter.'

### 2885. `RD_ALifeLine_230378c565306465` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0004*
- **EN:** "And don't spit in it, I'm watching"
- **TR:** 'Bir de içine tükürme, izliyorum.'

### 2886. `RD_ALifeLine_1d45fc3f070b8e64` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0005*
- **EN:** "Got four thousand goddamn bucks in my pocket and I can't buy a cheeseburger"
- **TR:** 'Cebimde dört bin lanet dolar var, bir peynirli burger alamıyorum.'

### 2887. `RD_ALifeLine_301b7d1d158038aa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0005*
- **EN:** "Put it on the fire, at least it'll burn"
- **TR:** 'Ateşe at, en azından yanar.'

### 2888. `RD_ALifeLine_572cd57f13b6d256` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0005*
- **EN:** "It's all anybody's got, it's worthless"
- **TR:** 'Herkeste o var, değeri yok.'

### 2889. `RD_ALifeLine_504032102aca8cc4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0005*
- **EN:** "It's good for starting a fire, tell me I'm wrong"
- **TR:** 'Ateş yakmaya yarar, yanlış mı söylüyorum?'

### 2890. `RD_ALifeLine_79657c9879307e5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0006*
- **EN:** "Your breath could kill a fuckin' horse!"
- **TR:** 'Nefesin bir atı öldürür, siktir!'

### 2891. `RD_ALifeLine_2c0fcd823c960c8f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0006*
- **EN:** "It's a gift"
- **TR:** 'Yetenek bu.'

### 2892. `RD_ALifeLine_7f55408a0177aa95` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0006*
- **EN:** "It's a gift to horses everywhere, they'd be safe"
- **TR:** 'Dünyadaki bütün atlar için hediye bu, hepsi güvende olur.'

### 2893. `RD_ALifeLine_2eb66ff75303d714` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0006*
- **EN:** "Blame the teeth, the breath's just along for the ride"
- **TR:** 'Suçu dişlerde ara, nefes sadece onlara eşlik ediyor.'

### 2894. `RD_ALifeLine_42ab23bf5652eff5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0007*
- **EN:** "Who's got a smoke?"
- **TR:** 'Kimde sigara var?'

### 2895. `RD_ALifeLine_0e4ebe02623e1589` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'If I had one, not for you'
- **TR:** 'Olsa bile sana yok.'

### 2896. `RD_ALifeLine_2de884167d9486e0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'You owe me from yesterday'
- **TR:** 'Dünden borcun var bana.'

### 2897. `RD_ALifeLine_289df46010eb06a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0007*
- **EN:** 'Yesterday you owed me, I called it even'
- **TR:** 'Dün sen bana borçluydun, ben de denkleşti saydım.'

### 2898. `RD_ALifeLine_18d822c60bcb5048` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0007*
- **EN:** "You're both full of shit. Arguing over a pack neither of you has shown me"
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de bok atıyorsunuz. Hiçbirinizin göstermediği bir paket için kavga ediyorsunuz.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 2899. `RD_ALifeLine_6985886b6cc3ae79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0008*
- **EN:** "Don't touch my shit"
- **TR:** 'Şeylerime dokunma.'

### 2900. `RD_ALifeLine_404c86f219c03f72` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0008*
- **EN:** 'You were looking at it'
- **TR:** 'Ona bakıyordun.'

### 2901. `RD_ALifeLine_410a138150892442` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0008*
- **EN:** "I look at everything, it's a free country"
- **TR:** 'Ben her şeye bakarım, özgür ülke.'

### 2902. `RD_ALifeLine_3443ff0252d233b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0008*
- **EN:** "It's a room, you jackass"
- **TR:** 'Burası ülke değil, oda, salak.'

### 2903. `RD_ALifeLine_197a7d2978395e96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0008*
- **EN:** "It's a shithole is what it is"
- **TR:** 'Oda dediğin bok çukuru işte.'

### 2904. `RD_ALifeLine_5c6c59b40e19e2f1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0009*
- **EN:** 'Go shoot something'
- **TR:** 'Git bir şey vur.'

### 2905. `RD_ALifeLine_08ad911f0f2008cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0009*
- **EN:** "There's nothing to shoot"
- **TR:** 'Vuracak bir şey yok.'

### 2906. `RD_ALifeLine_5076505a0a7ee428` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0009*
- **EN:** 'Then daydream about shooting a can'
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir kutuyu vurduğunu hayal et.'

### 2907. `RD_ALifeLine_050e7bcc42b112ab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0009*
- **EN:** "I'd rather you didn't even joke about doing that inside"
- **TR:** 'İçeride bunun şakasını bile yapmazsan sevinirim.'

### 2908. `RD_ALifeLine_2340ccec1c96cc71` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0009*
- **EN:** 'Fine. Imaginary can, imaginary bottle, very safe imaginary boredom'
- **TR:** 'Tamam. Hayali kutu, hayali şişe, çok güvenli hayali can sıkıntısı.'

### 2909. `RD_ALifeLine_6b4e5e7501a74710` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Somebody took my seat'
- **TR:** 'Biri benim yerimi aldı.'

### 2910. `RD_ALifeLine_587c95b517d6f649` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Nobody took your seat, you got up'
- **TR:** 'Kimse almadı, sen kalktın.'

### 2911. `RD_ALifeLine_788b4507755ebbe2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0010*
- **EN:** "Then somebody's sitting in my seat"
- **TR:** 'O zaman biri benim yerimde oturuyor.'

### 2912. `RD_ALifeLine_70d8531445f2fd04` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Sit somewhere else'
- **TR:** 'Başka yere otur.'

### 2913. `RD_ALifeLine_2dbc8ec316233c06` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0010*
- **EN:** "It's my seat, it's got my ass in it!"
- **TR:** 'Benim yerim, götümün izi var içinde!'

### 2914. `RD_ALifeLine_1e3e09470270d538` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0010*
- **EN:** 'Sit on my lap then, jackass'
- **TR:** 'O zaman kucağıma otur, salak.'

### 2915. `RD_ALifeLine_485ed3ab19f16999` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0011*
- **EN:** 'Arm wrestle, a can of beans'
- **TR:** 'Bilek güreşi, bir kutu fasulye.'

### 2916. `RD_ALifeLine_52ce28a1580c7825` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0011*
- **EN:** "It's what we've got, it's the only currency"
- **TR:** 'Elimizdeki bu, tek para birimi.'

### 2917. `RD_ALifeLine_12280f974af70fcd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0011*
- **EN:** 'Fine, but if I win I want peaches'
- **TR:** 'Olur, ama ben kazanırsam şeftali isterim.'

### 2918. `RD_ALifeLine_2b16547a4a2884f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0011*
- **EN:** "I'll referee, for a cut"
- **TR:** 'Ben hakemlik yaparım, pay karşılığı.'

### 2919. `RD_ALifeLine_31af758f6fc5701d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0012*
- **EN:** "Somebody's lying about the haul"
- **TR:** 'Biri ganimet konusunda yalan söylüyor.'

### 2920. `RD_ALifeLine_1b40915224c75be5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0012*
- **EN:** "I counted it, we're short two cans and a fuckin' lighter"
- **TR:** 'Saydım, iki kutu ve lanet bir çakmak eksik.'

### 2921. `RD_ALifeLine_4a4867d73738aff5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0012*
- **EN:** 'Maybe it fell out of the bag'
- **TR:** 'Belki çantadan düşmüştür.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 2922. `RD_ALifeLine_158b55715bd6b7a4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0012*
- **EN:** 'Maybe somebody has sticky fingers'
- **TR:** 'Belki birinin elleri yapışkandır.'

### 2923. `RD_ALifeLine_659c633531ea4c9a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_4.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0012*
- **EN:** "Everybody here's got sticky fingers, that's why we're in this shithole"
- **TR:** 'Buradaki herkesin elleri yapışkan, o yüzden bu bok çukurundayız.'

### 2924. `RD_ALifeLine_1091d6c7377bacd3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0013*
- **EN:** "You snore, I'll put a sock in it"
- **TR:** 'Horluyorsun, ağzına çorap tıkarım.'

### 2925. `RD_ALifeLine_1fd3cc8d760fa2e6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0013*
- **EN:** 'Which sock?'
- **TR:** 'Hangi çorap?'

### 2926. `RD_ALifeLine_5279a37e2afee083` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0013*
- **EN:** "Yours, it's the one that's been in your boot for a week"
- **TR:** 'Seninki, bir haftadır botunda duran.'

### 2927. `RD_ALifeLine_172893a8638051b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0013*
- **EN:** "I'd rather be gagged by a dead rat"
- **TR:** 'Ölü bir fareyle tıkanmayı tercih ederim.'

### 2928. `RD_ALifeLine_6262e0eb006b5916` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0013*
- **EN:** 'That can be arranged'
- **TR:** 'O da ayarlanabilir.'

### 2929. `RD_ALifeLine_53cc964456340d30` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0014*
- **EN:** 'Boss said keep moving'
- **TR:** 'Patron durmayın dedi.'

### 2930. `RD_ALifeLine_195dff751ea475ba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0014*
- **EN:** 'Moving where?'
- **TR:** 'Nereye?'

### 2931. `RD_ALifeLine_71be97501b0c744f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0014*
- **EN:** "He didn't say where, just keep walking"
- **TR:** 'Nereye olduğunu söylemedi, sadece yürümeye devam dedi.'

### 2932. `RD_ALifeLine_677364a35c7d61e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0014*
- **EN:** "Then we're moving somewhere, shut up"
- **TR:** 'O zaman bir yere gidiyoruz, kes sesini.'

### 2933. `RD_ALifeLine_320a96d7671cedb8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0014*
- **EN:** "Somewhere's the one place we haven't been"
- **TR:** 'Bir yer dediğin, gitmediğimiz tek yer.'

### 2934. `RD_ALifeLine_31a449a14a158d64` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0015*
- **EN:** 'I used to have a pool'
- **TR:** 'Eskiden bir havuzum vardı.'

### 2935. `RD_ALifeLine_62193cac2a4deaeb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0015*
- **EN:** 'A pool?'
- **TR:** 'Havuz mu?'

### 2936. `RD_ALifeLine_6fca33111c3a2ead` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0015*
- **EN:** 'In the backyard, above ground, with a ladder'
- **TR:** 'Arka bahçede, yerüstü, merdivenli.'

### 2937. `RD_ALifeLine_661d3355435f0563` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0015*
- **EN:** 'Why are you telling me this?'
- **TR:** 'Bunu bana niye anlatıyorsun?'

### 2938. `RD_ALifeLine_535c543157085329` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0015*
- **EN:** "I'd give my left nut to jump in it, ladder and all"
- **TR:** 'Sol taşağımı verirdim içine atlamak için, merdiveniyle birlikte.'

### 2939. `RD_ALifeLine_3b6692e577677aa4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0015*
- **EN:** "I'd drown you in it"
- **TR:** 'Ben seni içinde boğardım.'

### 2940. `RD_ALifeLine_1c1932b9210395ad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0016*
- **EN:** 'Anybody got a better gun?'
- **TR:** 'Daha iyi silahı olan var mı?'

### 2941. `RD_ALifeLine_7221509515dc7eaa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0016*
- **EN:** "That's a pretty good gun"
- **TR:** 'Bu gayet iyi bir silah.'

### 2942. `RD_ALifeLine_5d00f7753524dd5e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0016*
- **EN:** 'It jams every third shot'
- **TR:** 'Her üçüncü atışta tutukluk yapıyor.'

### 2943. `RD_ALifeLine_79ee6de260293e31` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0016*
- **EN:** 'Then shoot three at a time'
- **TR:** 'O zaman üçer üçer ateş et.'

### 2944. `RD_ALifeLine_7adf34021a0c875c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0016*
- **EN:** 'Shoot it at the guy who sold it to you'
- **TR:** 'Onu sana satan herife ateş et.'

### 2945. `RD_ALifeLine_5840e472787c1f8d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** "Where the fuck'd you get that watch?"
- **TR:** 'Siktir, o saati nereden buldun?'

### 2946. `RD_ALifeLine_0f5327d71669829d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'Found it'
- **TR:** 'Buldum.'

### 2947. `RD_ALifeLine_7caa726219a8aff9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'You found it on a guy'
- **TR:** 'Bir adamın üstünde buldun.'

### 2948. `RD_ALifeLine_3458651a03138a2d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** "He didn't need it"
- **TR:** 'Adamın ihtiyacı yoktu.'

### 2949. `RD_ALifeLine_6e35877f534b5b2c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'Is it set to the right time?'
- **TR:** 'Doğru saate mi ayarlı?'

### 2950. `RD_ALifeLine_2ae33e851ec5744c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** 'I never know, no one does'
- **TR:** 'Bilmem, kimse bilmiyor.'

### 2951. `RD_ALifeLine_1e41012e606adb43` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0017*
- **EN:** "It's always Friday on that watch"
- **TR:** 'O saatte hep cuma.'

### 2952. `RD_ALifeLine_224842ef3bcf4447` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0018*
- **EN:** "Somebody's talking to the Army"
- **TR:** 'Biri Orduyla konuşuyor.'

### 2953. `RD_ALifeLine_2b28c01f6cee33b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0018*
- **EN:** "I don't know, but somebody knows where we are"
- **TR:** 'Bilmiyorum ama biri nerede olduğumuzu biliyor.'

### 2954. `RD_ALifeLine_25a5fd1216213a44` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0018*
- **EN:** "That's just you being a scared little bitch again"
- **TR:** 'Yine ödlek bir kancık gibi konuşuyorsun.'

### 2955. `RD_ALifeLine_335c4b9644d6f2cf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0018*
- **EN:** "I'm not scared, I'm paranoid, there's a fucking difference"
- **TR:** 'Korkmuyorum, paranoyağım, ikisi arasında siktiğimin bir farkı var.'

### 2956. `RD_ALifeLine_4b2c1ab9520c2957` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0019*
- **EN:** "You're not the fuckin' boss of me"
- **TR:** 'Sen benim patronum falan değilsin be!'

### 2957. `RD_ALifeLine_4a913ec949af970e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0019*
- **EN:** "I'm not trying to be"
- **TR:** 'Olmaya da çalışmıyorum.'

### 2958. `RD_ALifeLine_39a07c9f16ec68ea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0019*
- **EN:** 'You keep telling me what to do'
- **TR:** 'Sürekli bana ne yapacağımı söyleyip duruyorsun.'

### 2959. `RD_ALifeLine_240b0e7c7a4bd9c7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0019*
- **EN:** 'Because YOU keep fucking it up, you dumb shit!'
- **TR:** 'Çünkü SEN sürekli bokunu çıkarıyorsun, seni salak!'

### 2960. `RD_ALifeLine_587220bc508d4534` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0020*
- **EN:** 'Draw on his face while he sleeps'
- **TR:** 'Uyurken suratına bir şeyler çizelim.'

### 2961. `RD_ALifeLine_2f2535723fa187a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0020*
- **EN:** 'A burnt match'
- **TR:** 'Yanmış kibritle.'

### 2962. `RD_ALifeLine_2b42d52300d86984` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0020*
- **EN:** "Do it, I'll hold the light"
- **TR:** 'Yap bakalım, ışığı ben tutarım.'

### 2963. `RD_ALifeLine_00c10b686a1cbcd5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0021*
- **EN:** "Don't get soft"
- **TR:** 'Yumuşamaya kalkma.'

### 2964. `RD_ALifeLine_0e15af307fa921c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0021*
- **EN:** "I'm not getting soft, quit looking at me"
- **TR:** 'Yumuşamıyorum, bakıp durma şöyle bana!'

### 2965. `RD_ALifeLine_7b9096d82f945a42` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0021*
- **EN:** "Say it again and I'll cut your coffee"
- **TR:** 'Bir daha söylersen kahveni keserim.'

### 2966. `RD_ALifeLine_14a56c93320d255e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0021*
- **EN:** "What fuckin' coffee"
- **TR:** 'Ne kahvesi ulan?'

### 2967. `RD_ALifeLine_148a76ea6d91ce03` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0022*
- **EN:** 'I hate this heat'
- **TR:** 'Şu sıcaktan nefret ediyorum.'

### 2968. `RD_ALifeLine_1cc114af658b75b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0022*
- **EN:** 'Take your shirt off'
- **TR:** 'Gömleğini çıkar.'

### 2969. `RD_ALifeLine_31b41b744017dc57` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0022*
- **EN:** 'Not around you'
- **TR:** 'Senin yanında olmaz.'

### 2970. `RD_ALifeLine_2d836db30d886ec8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0022*
- **EN:** "What's that supposed to mean?"
- **TR:** 'Bu da ne demek şimdi?'

### 2971. `RD_ALifeLine_4c6b9da169493222` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0022*
- **EN:** 'It means nobody wants to see that shit!'
- **TR:** 'Şu rezaleti kimse görmek istemez, onu diyorum!'

### 2972. `RD_ALifeLine_1354b76f37bee273` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0023*
- **EN:** 'One time I stole a whole truck of Pepsi'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde koca bir kamyon dolusu Pepsi çaldım.'

### 2973. `RD_ALifeLine_4c843e0020ecaaad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0023*
- **EN:** 'A whole semi, drove it to Indiana, sold it in three days'
- **TR:** "Koca bir tır, Indiana'ya kadar sürdüm, üç günde sattım."

### 2974. `RD_ALifeLine_3d9d8769585d6ba5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0023*
- **EN:** 'You smell like a Pepsi truck even now'
- **TR:** 'Şimdi bile Pepsi kamyonu gibi kokuyorsun.'

### 2975. `RD_ALifeLine_691d63250d8e974e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0023*
- **EN:** "It's the cleanest he's ever been"
- **TR:** 'Hayatında en temiz hali bu.'

### 2976. `RD_ALifeLine_4fe2e8b76498f6f6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0024*
- **EN:** 'My granddaddy ran shine in Hart County'
- **TR:** "Dedem Hart County'de kaçak içki yapıp satardı."

### 2977. `RD_ALifeLine_38d4dfa21910acea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0024*
- **EN:** "Everybody's granddaddy ran shine"
- **TR:** 'Herkesin dedesi kaçak içki satardı.'

### 2978. `RD_ALifeLine_4ed76f9520f5595a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0024*
- **EN:** 'Mine got caught once, did a year, said it was the best year of his life'
- **TR:** 'Benimki bir kere yakalandı, bir yıl yattı, hayatının en güzel yılı dedi.'

### 2979. `RD_ALifeLine_22e2ef51199edc7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0024*
- **EN:** 'No wife yelling at him, three meals a day'
- **TR:** 'Başında bağıran karı yok, günde üç öğün yemek var.'

### 2980. `RD_ALifeLine_4b67737f6b83908d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0024*
- **EN:** "That's every one of us right now"
- **TR:** 'Şu an hepimizin hali bu.'

### 2981. `RD_ALifeLine_105780e913b9f568` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Who the fuck ate my beans?!'
- **TR:** 'Kim yedi lan fasulyemi?!'

### 2982. `RD_ALifeLine_53876fbf53bca610` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0025*
- **EN:** "There was a whole can, and it's licked clean"
- **TR:** 'Koca bir kutu vardı, yalayıp yalayıp bırakmışlar!'

### 2983. `RD_ALifeLine_3fe4ee4f4cf03de4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0025*
- **EN:** 'Look at your chin'
- **TR:** 'Çenene bak.'

### 2984. `RD_ALifeLine_09c5286465c9c29a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0025*
- **EN:** "I didn't, I'm just saying look at your chin"
- **TR:** 'Ben yemedim... Sadece çenene bak diyorum.'

### 2985. `RD_ALifeLine_11402d48035ff155` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0026*
- **EN:** 'I robbed a bank once with a spatula'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde bir spatulayla banka girdim.'

### 2986. `RD_ALifeLine_35a6f9d10639645a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0026*
- **EN:** 'A spatula'
- **TR:** 'Spatula mı?'

### 2987. `RD_ALifeLine_7195fc6768cc2f7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0026*
- **EN:** "It was in my pocket, the guy didn't know it wasn't a gun"
- **TR:** 'Cebimde duruyordu, herif silah olmadığını anlamadı.'

### 2988. `RD_ALifeLine_670403e0250dcaee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0026*
- **EN:** 'How much'
- **TR:** 'Ne kadar aldın?'

### 2989. `RD_ALifeLine_13f728a017664673` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0026*
- **EN:** 'Four hundred dollars and a free toaster'
- **TR:** 'Dört yüz dolar, bir de bedava tost makinesi.'

### 2990. `RD_ALifeLine_75bb243c2b8e924b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0026*
- **EN:** 'Tell it right, the toaster was the whole point'
- **TR:** 'Doğru anlat, olayın bütün esprisi tost makinesi.'

### 2991. `RD_ALifeLine_0932b8d325e3a7fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0027*
- **EN:** "Bourbon's gone"
- **TR:** 'Burbon bitti.'

### 2992. `RD_ALifeLine_13dbe57454e8dbc3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0027*
- **EN:** "I'm drinking mouthwash"
- **TR:** 'Gargara içiyorum.'

### 2993. `RD_ALifeLine_6021285b0c87d1c5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0027*
- **EN:** 'We have mouthwash?'
- **TR:** 'Bizde gargara mı vardı?'

### 2994. `RD_ALifeLine_4cfa39130c2a9aea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0027*
- **EN:** 'We had mouthwash'
- **TR:** 'Vardı.'

### 2995. `RD_ALifeLine_1eb9c10637705f3e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0027*
- **EN:** 'The mouthwash was mine'
- **TR:** 'O gargara benimdi.'

### 2996. `RD_ALifeLine_42b4865569e95d3c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0028*
- **EN:** "I'm the best shot here"
- **TR:** 'Burada en iyi nişancı benim.'

### 2997. `RD_ALifeLine_1e11cb712cb373a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0028*
- **EN:** "You couldn't hit a fuckin' barn!"
- **TR:** 'Ahıra bile vuramazsın be!'

### 2998. `RD_ALifeLine_31bbb65c5b37cbd3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0028*
- **EN:** 'I hit the barn'
- **TR:** 'Ahıra vurdum.'

### 2999. `RD_ALifeLine_695391b629031d2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0028*
- **EN:** "You hit the side of it, sideways, you fuckin' blind bat"
- **TR:** 'Kenarına vurdun, hem de yan yan, seni kör yarasa!'

### 3000. `RD_ALifeLine_05e13f63410b8dc6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0028*
- **EN:** "It's a big barn, it counts"
- **TR:** 'Koca ahır, sayılır.'

### 3001. `RD_ALifeLine_20da455964eb970f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0029*
- **EN:** "Fair share, that's all I want"
- **TR:** 'Hakkıma düşen pay, tek istediğim bu.'

### 3002. `RD_ALifeLine_120fe98466dc79d7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'Fair according to who?'
- **TR:** 'Kime göre hak?'

### 3003. `RD_ALifeLine_1dcb29e17376ce0e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0029*
- **EN:** 'According to me'
- **TR:** 'Bana göre.'

### 3004. `RD_ALifeLine_392b0168013c7d98` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0029*
- **EN:** "That's not how fair works"
- **TR:** 'Adalet öyle işlemez.'

### 3005. `RD_ALifeLine_060d850546ab9293` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0029*
- **EN:** "It's how fair works in this room, I've got the gun"
- **TR:** 'Bu odada öyle işler, silah bende.'

### 3006. `RD_ALifeLine_33ef495b53e7753b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0030*
- **EN:** 'You cry in your sleep'
- **TR:** 'Uykunda ağlıyorsun.'

### 3007. `RD_ALifeLine_2bad8031608c9b05` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0030*
- **EN:** 'You called for your mama'
- **TR:** 'Annene sesleniyordun.'

### 3008. `RD_ALifeLine_7b98a01849ed27f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0030*
- **EN:** "I called for my mama's pie, there's a difference"
- **TR:** 'Annemin turtasına sesleniyordum, fark var.'

### 3009. `RD_ALifeLine_5feddafa0a7af5bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0030*
- **EN:** "There's no difference"
- **TR:** 'Fark yok.'

### 3010. `RD_ALifeLine_369f2a7a7f9da684` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0031*
- **EN:** "Look at 'em, all holed up in their little houses with their little curtains, the lucky bastards"
- **TR:** 'Şunlara bak, küçük evlerinde, küçük perdeleriyle kapanmışlar, şanslı piçler.'

### 3011. `RD_ALifeLine_2eb520c4157fcb64` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0031*
- **EN:** "I'm just looking, I never had a house"
- **TR:** 'Sadece bakıyorum, benim hiç evim olmadı.'

### 3012. `RD_ALifeLine_73cfdfe21b549513` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0031*
- **EN:** 'Nobody here had a house'
- **TR:** 'Burada kimsenin evi olmadı.'

### 3013. `RD_ALifeLine_2b84425f661c3a08` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0032*
- **EN:** "New fish, you're on the door"
- **TR:** 'Çaylak, kapı nöbeti senin.'

### 3014. `RD_ALifeLine_5d4ee0302e1d1366` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'The door?'
- **TR:** 'Kapı mı?'

### 3015. `RD_ALifeLine_163e4dbc422206a9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'Stand there and look scary, jackass'
- **TR:** 'Dikil orada, korkutucu görün, hıyar.'

### 3016. `RD_ALifeLine_55346c62381a19e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0032*
- **EN:** "I don't know how to look scary"
- **TR:** 'Korkutucu görünmeyi bilmiyorum.'

### 3017. `RD_ALifeLine_0d23fc94677aaf55` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0032*
- **EN:** 'Think about your taxes'
- **TR:** 'Vergilerini düşün.'

### 3018. `RD_ALifeLine_79cc0ebf2fd401a8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0067*
- **EN:** "That's the second time you've kicked my boot"
- **TR:** 'Botuma ikinci kez tekme attın.'

### 3019. `RD_ALifeLine_0cd45cd95078e7d6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0067*
- **EN:** "It's in the way"
- **TR:** 'Yolumun üstünde duruyor.'

### 3020. `RD_ALifeLine_2d0661b244ceb78a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0067*
- **EN:** "It's a boot, it doesn't have a way"
- **TR:** 'Bot o be, kendi yolu mu olur!'

### 3021. `RD_ALifeLine_5609d9f113cdd211` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0067*
- **EN:** "Both of you shut the fuck up, I'm trying to sleep"
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de kapatın çenenizi ulan, uyumaya çalışıyorum!'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): gerçek çoğul: kaynakta gruba hitap sözcüğü var*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 3022. `RD_ALifeLine_3a52a51f1adbc2e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0068*
- **EN:** "You ever wonder if they're looking for us?"
- **TR:** 'Hiç merak ettin mi, bizi arıyorlar mı?'

### 3023. `RD_ALifeLine_5a10e6b855d78f38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'Anybody, the Army, the sheriff, our mothers'
- **TR:** 'Herhangi biri, ordu, şerif, annelerimiz.'

### 3024. `RD_ALifeLine_17a1ce7e027685d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0068*
- **EN:** 'Mothers are always looking'
- **TR:** 'Anneler hep arar.'

### 3025. `RD_ALifeLine_5fefffe51ed3a00f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0068*
- **EN:** "Mine's not..."
- **TR:** 'Benimki aramaz...'

### 3026. `RD_ALifeLine_394c9e073c483d48` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** "I can't sleep, I hear stuff"
- **TR:** 'Uyuyamıyorum, bir şeyler duyuyorum.'

### 3027. `RD_ALifeLine_479fd8f67f2423fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'What stuff?'
- **TR:** 'Ne gibi?'

### 3028. `RD_ALifeLine_36e75e7a550e888f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'Walking, outside, up and down'
- **TR:** 'Yürüyüş sesi, dışarıda, bir aşağı bir yukarı.'

### 3029. `RD_ALifeLine_3323969a012ccd89` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** "That's me, I'm walking"
- **TR:** 'O benim, yürüyorum.'

### 3030. `RD_ALifeLine_7cfd887b4e97202c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** "Why're you walking?"
- **TR:** 'Sen neden yürüyorsun?'

### 3031. `RD_ALifeLine_0910e3b93edd8f5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** "Same reason you can't sleep"
- **TR:** 'Senin uyuyamadığın sebepten.'

### 3032. `RD_ALifeLine_56a5f94f67de6d95` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0069*
- **EN:** 'I thought that was the guard'
- **TR:** 'Ben nöbetçi sanmıştım.'

### 3033. `RD_ALifeLine_0a019f1b3e3f3962` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0070*
- **EN:** "If anybody says we're gonna be fine I'm gonna break their fuckin' jaw"
- **TR:** 'Biri bize bir şey olmaz derse çenesini kırarım.'

### 3034. `RD_ALifeLine_7c1a095e10ebc798` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0070*
- **EN:** "We're gonna be fine"
- **TR:** 'Bize bir şey olmaz.'

### 3035. `RD_ALifeLine_69084bc334692b65` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0070*
- **EN:** "That's it"
- **TR:** 'Tamam, işte bu kadar!'

### 3036. `RD_ALifeLine_325c736c160eac55` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0070*
- **EN:** "Keep your hands to yourself, you've got a cigarette to finish"
- **TR:** 'Ellerini sakla, bitirmen gereken bir sigaran var.'

### 3037. `RD_ALifeLine_59e4c4134f2d66b0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0071*
- **EN:** "I'm so fucking hungry I'd eat my own boot"
- **TR:** 'Açlıktan öldüm, kendi botumu yiyeceğim.'

### 3038. `RD_ALifeLine_33fc1bbc694fa169` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0071*
- **EN:** "You'd have to cook it first"
- **TR:** 'Önce pişirmen lazım.'

### 3039. `RD_ALifeLine_1566b02f5ac7e737` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0071*
- **EN:** "It's leather, practically a steak"
- **TR:** 'Deri o, neredeyse biftek.'

### 3040. `RD_ALifeLine_2b39256855627f65` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0071*
- **EN:** 'Practically a steak, with a sole'
- **TR:** 'Neredeyse biftek, tabanlı bir biftek.'

### 3041. `RD_ALifeLine_7e6f627231add067` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0072*
- **EN:** 'Somebody left the door open'
- **TR:** 'Biri kapıyı açık bırakmış.'

### 3042. `RD_ALifeLine_4cb3e672738e0b64` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0072*
- **EN:** "I don't know, but if I find out"
- **TR:** 'Bilmiyorum, ama bir öğrenirsem...'

### 3043. `RD_ALifeLine_5d5881a70a75b5b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0072*
- **EN:** "You'll what, kill 'em"
- **TR:** 'Ne yapacaksın, öldürecek misin?'

### 3044. `RD_ALifeLine_454370ed24a710a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0072*
- **EN:** "I'll tell 'em to shut it, I'm not a monster"
- **TR:** 'Kapatmasını söylerim, canavar değilim ya.'

### 3045. `RD_ALifeLine_6a7a6c302533dae3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0073*
- **EN:** "My old man used to say you can't outrun your own shadow"
- **TR:** 'Babam derdi ki, kimse kendi gölgesinden kaçamaz.'

### 3046. `RD_ALifeLine_2f14f31223f89f2f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0073*
- **EN:** 'He was drunk, but he was right'
- **TR:** 'Sarhoştu ama haklıydı.'

### 3047. `RD_ALifeLine_02c924ad4fab1e51` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0074*
- **EN:** "We took that whole fuckin' truck of Pepsi and the driver cried like a baby"
- **TR:** 'O koca Pepsi kamyonunu aldık, şoför bebek gibi ağladı.'

### 3048. `RD_ALifeLine_57ac8ea4320d26d3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0074*
- **EN:** 'You told that one already'
- **TR:** 'Bunu anlattın zaten.'

### 3049. `RD_ALifeLine_6dc45db201f32622` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0074*
- **EN:** "I'm telling it again, the guy cried, I felt bad for a second"
- **TR:** 'Yine anlatıyorum, adam ağladı, bir an içim sızladı.'

### 3050. `RD_ALifeLine_28dbd78f08d2df93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0074*
- **EN:** "A second, that's generous"
- **TR:** 'Bir an, ne cömertsin!'

### 3051. `RD_ALifeLine_52d2aad9355b8ef2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0075*
- **EN:** "That prick wouldn't know a plan if it bit him in the ass"
- **TR:** 'O hergele planı kıçını ısırsa tanımaz!'

### 3052. `RD_ALifeLine_3cf891f04d9e0f51` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0075*
- **EN:** 'Keep your voice down'
- **TR:** 'Sesini alçalt.'

### 3053. `RD_ALifeLine_136b3754562276f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0075*
- **EN:** "Why? He's not even here, he's off pissing somewhere!"
- **TR:** 'Niye? Burada bile değil, bir yerlerde işiyordur!'

### 3054. `RD_ALifeLine_627d0e225c22888c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0075*
- **EN:** "He's right behind you"
- **TR:** 'Tam arkanda duruyor.'

### 3055. `RD_ALifeLine_3071934b7da1229b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0076*
- **EN:** "You bet my fuckin' boots, you piece of shit!"
- **TR:** 'Botlarımı kumara yatırdın, seni bok parçası!'

### 3056. `RD_ALifeLine_4f9270323ae63876` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0076*
- **EN:** 'I was gonna win them back'
- **TR:** 'Geri kazanacaktım.'

### 3057. `RD_ALifeLine_70e566e56b233a96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0076*
- **EN:** 'You lost my boots on a pair of fours!'
- **TR:** 'Botlarımı bir çift dörtlüye kaybettin!'

### 3058. `RD_ALifeLine_60aaa21c2259e68c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0077*
- **EN:** 'Did you hear that? Shut up and listen'
- **TR:** 'Duydun mu? Kapa çeneni de dinle.'

### 3059. `RD_ALifeLine_14b30045260190cb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0077*
- **EN:** "It's the wind"
- **TR:** 'Rüzgar.'

### 3060. `RD_ALifeLine_0ed5d9462cae6896` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0077*
- **EN:** "Wind doesn't make that noise, it's something with feet"
- **TR:** 'Rüzgar öyle ses çıkarmaz, ayakları olan bir şey bu.'

### 3061. `RD_ALifeLine_1db21c823b82b203` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0077*
- **EN:** "It's a fuckin' possum, you pussies"
- **TR:** 'Bir possum o be, korkak herifler!'

### 3062. `RD_ALifeLine_2e90994d763c8c7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0078*
- **EN:** "I don't trust the boss, I think he's gonna sell us out"
- **TR:** 'Patrona güvenmiyorum, bizi satacak gibi.'

### 3063. `RD_ALifeLine_50d2dd0a1de38b89` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0078*
- **EN:** "To who? There's nobody to sell us to"
- **TR:** 'Kime? Satacak kimse yok ki.'

### 3064. `RD_ALifeLine_5bf35e101c9a9275` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0078*
- **EN:** "That's what he wants us to think"
- **TR:** 'Bunu düşünmemizi istiyor zaten.'

### 3065. `RD_ALifeLine_5e80c6b07905b82c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0079*
- **EN:** "Did you see that guy's face when I pointed the gun at him? He pissed himself"
- **TR:** 'Silahı doğrulttuğumda herifin suratını gördün mü? Altına işedi.'

### 3066. `RD_ALifeLine_46a5e0ba6e32b66b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0079*
- **EN:** 'He was seventy'
- **TR:** 'Yetmiş yaşındaydı.'

### 3067. `RD_ALifeLine_4011a3bd5572d2fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0079*
- **EN:** 'He was seventy and he pissed himself, I rest my case'
- **TR:** 'Yetmiş yaşında olsa ne, altına işedi, dava kapanmıştır.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (dir): konuşmada doğal tahmin/genelleme kalıbı (-mıştır, iyidir, demektir), resmi dil değil*

### 3068. `RD_ALifeLine_69f0821f5b515432` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0079*
- **EN:** "You're a hell of a guy!"
- **TR:** 'Vay be, adam gibi adamsın!'

### 3069. `RD_ALifeLine_268223322cb3056c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0080*
- **EN:** 'Once saw a cord running from a house to a generator'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde bir evden jeneratöre uzanan kablo görmüştüm.'

### 3070. `RD_ALifeLine_4ce37e665dd041cd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0080*
- **EN:** "I told myself that meant power, food, whiskey. Didn't actually know any of that"
- **TR:** 'Kendi kendime bu elektrik, yemek, viski demek dedim. Aslında hiçbirini bilmiyordum.'

### 3071. `RD_ALifeLine_3c37c1ff035ae37a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0080*
- **EN:** 'Good reason to look before barging in, not a reason to expect a feast'
- **TR:** 'İçeri dalmadan önce bakmak için iyi sebep, ziyafet beklemek için değil.'

### 3072. `RD_ALifeLine_13989004093c7f2b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0081*
- **EN:** "It's ninety-five fuckin' degrees and you want me to wear that jacket"
- **TR:** 'Hava 35 derece lan, sen de bana şu montu giy diyorsun.'
- *Not: 95 F -> 35 C*

### 3073. `RD_ALifeLine_27ab99ec0e099809` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0081*
- **EN:** "It's got a hood"
- **TR:** 'Kapüşonu var.'

### 3074. `RD_ALifeLine_785744d221617c48` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0081*
- **EN:** "I-- I don't need a hood, I need a fucking pool"
- **TR:** 'Ben... kapüşon istemiyorum, havuz istiyorum!'

### 3075. `RD_ALifeLine_11d6015237f21dac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0082*
- **EN:** "If I get stuck eating cold beans again, I'm gonna dream about steak"
- **TR:** 'Yine soğuk fasulye yemek zorunda kalırsam rüyamda biftek göreceğim.'

### 3076. `RD_ALifeLine_39273ba95593492d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0082*
- **EN:** "I'd dream about a fuckin' fork first"
- **TR:** 'Ben önce çatal görürdüm.'

### 3077. `RD_ALifeLine_7549d11713fdc5e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0082*
- **EN:** 'You ever eat beans without a fork?'
- **TR:** 'Sen hiç çatalsız fasulye yedin mi?'

### 3078. `RD_ALifeLine_60f2c368480e35c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0082*
- **EN:** "Once with a spoon Lonnie had licked. I haven't forgiven him"
- **TR:** "Bir keresinde Lonnie'nin yaladığı kaşıkla yedim. Onu hala affetmedim."

### 3079. `RD_ALifeLine_4478f7274475a305` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0083*
- **EN:** "I'm not sleeping next to Rusty, he farts"
- **TR:** "Rusty'nin yanında yatmam, osuruyor."

### 3080. `RD_ALifeLine_40ec004f3ee649c8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0083*
- **EN:** 'Everybody farts'
- **TR:** 'Herkes osurur.'

### 3081. `RD_ALifeLine_0b026215653a81c2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0083*
- **EN:** "Not like that, it's like a goddamn tornado"
- **TR:** 'Öyle değil, resmen kasırga gibi!'

### 3082. `RD_ALifeLine_2bd8d4f13e46ab82` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0083*
- **EN:** 'Then sleep outside'
- **TR:** 'O zaman dışarıda yat.'

### 3083. `RD_ALifeLine_34b579ea085421c5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0084*
- **EN:** "I'm done with this shit, I'm heading to Indiana"
- **TR:** "Bıktım bu boktan, Indiana'ya gidiyorum."

### 3084. `RD_ALifeLine_5f1be22b3d137322` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0084*
- **EN:** 'Indiana?'
- **TR:** 'Indiana mı?'

### 3085. `RD_ALifeLine_0eee7a747c57e2b3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0084*
- **EN:** "My sister's got a place, she'd take me in, I think"
- **TR:** 'Kardeşimin bir yeri var, beni alır herhalde.'

### 3086. `RD_ALifeLine_6a4b5856397bf181` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0084*
- **EN:** 'You think'
- **TR:** 'Herhalde.'

### 3087. `RD_ALifeLine_64f6f7916a6bef77` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0085*
- **EN:** 'Tell the one about when you robbed the church'
- **TR:** 'Kiliseyi nasıl soyduğunu anlat.'

### 3088. `RD_ALifeLine_4e6f33da7f063a41` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0085*
- **EN:** "I didn't rob it, I borrowed a collection plate"
- **TR:** 'Soymadım, bir bağış tabağını ödünç aldım.'

### 3089. `RD_ALifeLine_00bd401628832b14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0085*
- **EN:** "He borrowed a plate and the whole fuckin' congregation chased him"
- **TR:** 'Tabak ödünç almış, bütün cemaat peşine düşmüş!'

### 3090. `RD_ALifeLine_5ecdae30503a2e0e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0085*
- **EN:** 'I ran faster than any man in the choir'
- **TR:** 'Koroda hiçbir adam benden hızlı koşamazdı.'

### 3091. `RD_ALifeLine_5b4920215478a7fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0086*
- **EN:** "I got a dog, I'm keeping the little fucker!"
- **TR:** 'Bir köpeğim var, bu küçük piçi tutuyorum!'

### 3092. `RD_ALifeLine_3aa9ce84090a7ff9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0086*
- **EN:** "Mine, he followed me home, he's got one ear"
- **TR:** 'Benim, peşimden eve geldi, tek kulağı var.'

### 3093. `RD_ALifeLine_437f5e3a699ed935` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0087*
- **EN:** "Mine's bigger"
- **TR:** 'Benimki daha büyük.'

### 3094. `RD_ALifeLine_54e2c0ae61e3010b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0087*
- **EN:** "It's a shotgun, dumbass, everybody's is bigger!"
- **TR:** 'Pompalı o, mal, herkesinki daha büyük!'

### 3095. `RD_ALifeLine_6f4492553cfff1e5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0087*
- **EN:** "It's got a longer barrel, that's all I'm saying"
- **TR:** 'Namlusu daha uzun, ben sadece bunu diyorum.'

### 3096. `RD_ALifeLine_7bb11fce223f333c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0087*
- **EN:** 'Rusty, put it away'
- **TR:** 'Rusty, kaldır onu.'

### 3097. `RD_ALifeLine_0ed3e1fe534f2a5a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0088*
- **EN:** "Those church people with their casseroles, all holier than thou, I hope they choke on a fuckin' noodle"
- **TR:** 'Şu güveçli kilise güruhu, hepsi bir kendini aziz sanan, umarım bir makarna lokmasında boğulurlar!'

### 3098. `RD_ALifeLine_556d4cb47edbbf36` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0088*
- **EN:** "They've never done anything to you"
- **TR:** 'Sana hiçbir şey yapmadılar.'

### 3099. `RD_ALifeLine_0ad795db07e5bf79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0088*
- **EN:** "They looked at me funny, in the parking lot, in '85"
- **TR:** "Bana yan baktılar, otoparkta, 85'te."

### 3100. `RD_ALifeLine_5e11a44a0fff5e09` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0089*
- **EN:** 'You still scared of the dark, pussy?'
- **TR:** 'Hala karanlıktan korkuyor musun, ödlek?'

### 3101. `RD_ALifeLine_32d9a12765a16c9e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0089*
- **EN:** 'Fuck you'
- **TR:** 'Siktir git.'

### 3102. `RD_ALifeLine_09ea176115524494` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0089*
- **EN:** 'You slept with a night-light in the cell, I saw it'
- **TR:** 'Hücrede gece lambasıyla yatıyordun, gördüm.'

### 3103. `RD_ALifeLine_08a14e293fcdff90` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0089*
- **EN:** 'It was a flashlight, you ignorant fuck!'
- **TR:** 'El fenerliydi, cahil herif!'

### 3104. `RD_ALifeLine_0aa9d4ef17f0e89a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0090*
- **EN:** "Don't ever fuckin' touch my bag again!"
- **TR:** 'Bir daha sakın çantama dokunma!'

### 3105. `RD_ALifeLine_6e8be0c701617eea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0090*
- **EN:** 'I was looking for a spoon'
- **TR:** 'Kaşık arıyordum.'

### 3106. `RD_ALifeLine_1d6a150b26285633` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0090*
- **EN:** 'Ask me, you piece of shit, you ask!'
- **TR:** 'Bana soracaksın, seni pislik, soracaksın!'

### 3107. `RD_ALifeLine_2ded7a8b0c8ea7b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0091*
- **EN:** 'Pass the goddamn bottle'
- **TR:** 'Şu lanet şişeyi uzat.'

### 3108. `RD_ALifeLine_3a817fee7045d8cf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0091*
- **EN:** "It's not empty, I can hear it, there's a good swallow"
- **TR:** 'Boş değil, duyuyorum, bir yudum var.'

### 3109. `RD_ALifeLine_1f842429421909c5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0091*
- **EN:** "There's a good swallow of spit, Cecil's been backwashing"
- **TR:** 'Bir yudum tükürük var, Cecil şişeden içip geri akıtmış.'

### 3110. `RD_ALifeLine_76056b78057008a4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0092*
- **EN:** "My head's fucking killing me!"
- **TR:** 'Kafam çatlıyor be!'

### 3111. `RD_ALifeLine_55c8953b56263e05` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0092*
- **EN:** "That's the bourbon"
- **TR:** 'Burbon yüzünden.'

### 3112. `RD_ALifeLine_1fae1c0637a311d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0092*
- **EN:** "Blame the bourbon all you want, it's your face doing it"
- **TR:** 'İstediğin kadar burbonu suçla, asıl bunu yapan senin suratın.'

### 3113. `RD_ALifeLine_17e81e512c9a5b1e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0092*
- **EN:** 'Both can be true'
- **TR:** 'İkisi de olabilir.'

### 3114. `RD_ALifeLine_05cd72990fdd43aa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0093*
- **EN:** "I'm gonna shoot the next bird I see"
- **TR:** 'Göreceğim ilk kuşu vuracağım.'

### 3115. `RD_ALifeLine_390fd37a5775794e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0093*
- **EN:** "I'm bored, and it'll make a noise, and I'll feel something"
- **TR:** 'Sıkıldım, ses çıkar, ben de bir şey hissederim.'

### 3116. `RD_ALifeLine_20cc2e8033013021` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0094*
- **EN:** "I told you we should've hit the pharmacy"
- **TR:** 'Sana eczaneyi vurmalıyız dedim.'

### 3117. `RD_ALifeLine_6d2e8dd94926cbb2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0094*
- **EN:** 'You told me nothing'
- **TR:** 'Bana hiçbir şey demedin.'

### 3118. `RD_ALifeLine_45066a9752bb5710` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0094*
- **EN:** "I said the pharmacy, in the fuckin' truck, on the way here"
- **TR:** 'Eczane dedim, kamyonda, buraya gelirken.'

### 3119. `RD_ALifeLine_23ff56922cc10c96` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0094*
- **EN:** "You said 'pharmacy' and then you threw up"
- **TR:** 'Eczane dedin, sonra kustun.'

### 3120. `RD_ALifeLine_5e7c5cca234a4421` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0095*
- **EN:** "Don't you go telling anybody, but I'm scared. Fuck, I'm scared"
- **TR:** 'Sakın kimseye söyleme ama korkuyorum. Siktir, korkuyorum.'

### 3121. `RD_ALifeLine_406909bf1d803435` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0095*
- **EN:** "Don't, I mean it, I'll know"
- **TR:** 'Söyleme, ciddiyim, öğrenirim.'

### 3122. `RD_ALifeLine_37b4de3d526574bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0095*
- **EN:** "I won't tell a soul. Everybody's scared shitless, it's not just you"
- **TR:** 'Kimseye söylemem. Herkesin ödü patlamış, sadece sen değilsin.'

### 3123. `RD_ALifeLine_6c0c1d82260da220` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0096*
- **EN:** "I used to steal cars and now I'd kill for one that runs"
- **TR:** 'Eskiden araba çalardım, şimdi çalışan bir tane için adam öldürürdüm.'

### 3124. `RD_ALifeLine_6ca49cf23b7ef52c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0096*
- **EN:** 'Hotwire one'
- **TR:** 'Kontağını kır.'

### 3125. `RD_ALifeLine_1f14c2bf0fcb3b7e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0096*
- **EN:** "The ones I tried wouldn't run. Doesn't mean every damn car is dead"
- **TR:** 'Denediklerim çalışmadı. Her lanet arabanın ölü olduğu anlamına gelmez.'

### 3126. `RD_ALifeLine_16dc54106bb822fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0096*
- **EN:** "I used to know a guy with a tractor. Haven't seen him in a while"
- **TR:** 'Traktörü olan bir adam tanırdım. Bir süredir görmedim.'

### 3127. `RD_ALifeLine_6c90a3c9237a5fba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0097*
- **EN:** "I saw a crow on the way in, that's bad luck"
- **TR:** 'Gelirken bir karga gördüm, uğursuzluk.'

### 3128. `RD_ALifeLine_052bcdab7ca28760` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0097*
- **EN:** "There's no such thing as luck"
- **TR:** 'Uğursuzluk diye bir şey yok.'

### 3129. `RD_ALifeLine_1bd770a232515c5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0097*
- **EN:** "There's a crow on a fence post and a dead man's shoe in the road, that's bad luck"
- **TR:** 'Çit direğinde bir karga, yolda da ölü bir adamın ayakkabısı var, bu uğursuzluk.'

### 3130. `RD_ALifeLine_7592ff2d2d5de9fe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0098*
- **EN:** 'Who the fuck put him in charge?'
- **TR:** 'Lanet olası, kim geçirdi bunu başa?'

### 3131. `RD_ALifeLine_54d30722330eda2e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0098*
- **EN:** 'Nobody, he just started talking'
- **TR:** 'Kimse, kendi konuşmaya başladı.'

### 3132. `RD_ALifeLine_2872ad7626ecf61e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0098*
- **EN:** "That's how it works, you talk loud and people shut up"
- **TR:** 'İş böyle yürür, yüksek sesle konuşursun, millet susar.'

### 3133. `RD_ALifeLine_69b4f8a048a0cb5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0098*
- **EN:** "That's the dumbest way to run anything"
- **TR:** 'Bir şeyi yönetmenin en aptalca yolu bu.'

### 3134. `RD_ALifeLine_4385b87758253483` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0099*
- **EN:** "You'd leave me, wouldn't you? If it got bad"
- **TR:** 'Beni bırakıp giderdin, değil mi? İş kötüye giderse.'

### 3135. `RD_ALifeLine_38b2353839601866` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0099*
- **EN:** "Yeah, me too, I'd leave you in a second"
- **TR:** 'Evet, ben de seni bir saniyede bırakırım.'

### 3136. `RD_ALifeLine_267461887b07ce2f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0099*
- **EN:** "Good, that's honest"
- **TR:** 'İyi, bu dürüstçe.'

### 3137. `RD_ALifeLine_587984bf22efcc2a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0100*
- **EN:** "Where'd you get that fuckin' hat?"
- **TR:** 'O lanet şapkayı nereden buldun?'

### 3138. `RD_ALifeLine_0caa8b1d5f3de8dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0100*
- **EN:** 'A guy'
- **TR:** 'Bir herifte.'

### 3139. `RD_ALifeLine_2a4bd5ae60ebfca6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0100*
- **EN:** 'A guy took off his hat and said take it?'
- **TR:** 'Bir herif şapkasını çıkarıp al mı dedi?'

### 3140. `RD_ALifeLine_6dd64cb900d4adc1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0100*
- **EN:** "A guy took off his hat and said please don't hurt me, and he left it"
- **TR:** 'Bir herif şapkasını çıkarıp bana zarar verme dedi, bırakıp gitti.'

### 3141. `RD_ALifeLine_165f2d921edce90c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0101*
- **EN:** "I haven't seen a woman in a month, I'd marry a mailbox"
- **TR:** 'Bir aydır kadın yüzü görmedim, posta kutusuyla evlenirim.'

### 3142. `RD_ALifeLine_6eef82a40eadd58d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0101*
- **EN:** "There's a mailbox out front"
- **TR:** 'Ön tarafta bir posta kutusu var.'

### 3143. `RD_ALifeLine_15d91e9f0a7441e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0101*
- **EN:** "I'm gonna go talk to it"
- **TR:** 'Gidip konuşacağım onunla.'

### 3144. `RD_ALifeLine_4dc8198d0f5232c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0102*
- **EN:** 'I heard the boss is skimming'
- **TR:** 'Patronun kıyıdan köşeden götürdüğünü duydum.'

### 3145. `RD_ALifeLine_71b22b814cfa7f26` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0102*
- **EN:** 'Skimming what?'
- **TR:** 'Neyi götürüyormuş?'

### 3146. `RD_ALifeLine_190556c47d72ba46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0102*
- **EN:** "The good stuff, he's got a stash, in his bag"
- **TR:** 'İyi malları, bir zulası var, çantasında.'

### 3147. `RD_ALifeLine_0375f31a6a7b7c7b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0102*
- **EN:** "If that's true I'm gonna cut his bag open"
- **TR:** 'Doğruysa çantasını yırtıp açarım.'

### 3148. `RD_ALifeLine_60c5ecf42a2a6327` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0103*
- **EN:** "If you die I'm taking your boots"
- **TR:** 'Ölürsen botlarını ben alıyorum.'

### 3149. `RD_ALifeLine_54c13b9970410d7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0103*
- **EN:** "If I die you can have 'em"
- **TR:** 'Ölürsem senin olsun.'

### 3150. `RD_ALifeLine_71e56ea75c346a99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0103*
- **EN:** "Good, they're nice boots, I've been looking at them for a month"
- **TR:** 'Güzel, hoş botlar, bir aydır gözüm üstünde.'

### 3151. `RD_ALifeLine_35821e5405038d70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0104*
- **EN:** "You think anybody's gonna come after us?"
- **TR:** 'Sence bizi arayan olur mu?'

### 3152. `RD_ALifeLine_282e43b60336f812` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0104*
- **EN:** 'For what we did'
- **TR:** 'Yaptıklarımız için.'

### 3153. `RD_ALifeLine_106272344168fd59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0104*
- **EN:** "Nobody's left to come"
- **TR:** 'Gelecek kimse kalmadı.'

### 3154. `RD_ALifeLine_200d516e1e1e2f88` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0105*
- **EN:** 'Who was supposed to be watching the door?'
- **TR:** 'Kapıyı kim gözleyecekti?'

### 3155. `RD_ALifeLine_0925dc4a685a2a31` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0105*
- **EN:** 'Ask them'
- **TR:** 'Onlara sor.'

### 3156. `RD_ALifeLine_5a80cf9a71e9b849` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0105*
- **EN:** "They said they'd take my watch"
- **TR:** 'Nöbetimi alacaklarını söylediler.'

### 3157. `RD_ALifeLine_1e329977713bc233` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0105*
- **EN:** 'The first one. Not every damn one'
- **TR:** 'İlk nöbeti. Her lanet nöbeti değil.'

### 3158. `RD_ALifeLine_4f971dd42e6db261` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0105*
- **EN:** "Good. We're all awake now, aren't we?"
- **TR:** 'İyi. Hepimiz uyanığız şimdi, değil mi?'

### 3159. `RD_ALifeLine_50c3877601808cc0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0106*
- **EN:** "My old man used to say you'd go to hell for stealing, and I figured, then I'll see you there"
- **TR:** 'Babam derdi ki hırsızlık yapan cehennemlik olur, ben de dedim ki, orada görüşürüz.'

### 3160. `RD_ALifeLine_6b1d2f8d6f72dc8c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0106*
- **EN:** 'You gonna see him?'
- **TR:** 'Onu görecek misin?'

### 3161. `RD_ALifeLine_6cbc2fc661480bb4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0106*
- **EN:** 'I hope so, I owe him a beating'
- **TR:** 'Umarım, bir dayak borcum var ona.'

### 3162. `RD_ALifeLine_386cb9340a0b06ce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0107*
- **EN:** "Stop fuckin' talking about food"
- **TR:** 'Yemekten bahsetmeyi kes artık.'

### 3163. `RD_ALifeLine_354097af72a2b268` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0107*
- **EN:** "We're all hungry, shut up about it"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz açız, kes şunu.'

### 3164. `RD_ALifeLine_4ad7ef4d21d0287a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0108*
- **EN:** "That sound you hear is my stomach, it's trying to leave"
- **TR:** 'Duyduğun ses midem, gitmeye çalışıyor.'

### 3165. `RD_ALifeLine_7949964b0c2be4e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0108*
- **EN:** 'Tell it to wait for me'
- **TR:** 'Beni beklesin de ona söyle.'

### 3166. `RD_ALifeLine_40b8b46f30f78088` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0108*
- **EN:** "It's getting a ride with the fuckin' beans"
- **TR:** 'Kahrolası fasulyelerle birlikte gidiyor.'

### 3167. `RD_ALifeLine_76d8cbf6590a3cb0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0141*
- **EN:** "I'll break your fuckin' hand if you touch my bottle again"
- **TR:** 'Şişeme bir daha dokunursan elini kırarım, siktir!'

### 3168. `RD_ALifeLine_49dc887e753fe616` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0141*
- **EN:** 'It was empty'
- **TR:** 'Boştu.'

### 3169. `RD_ALifeLine_1558f0600d2b71f6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0141*
- **EN:** "It's not empty if I say it's got a swallow in it"
- **TR:** 'İçinde bir yudum var diyorsam, boş değildir.'

### 3170. `RD_ALifeLine_7d6f430771c75670` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0141*
- **EN:** "There's a swallow in it, I can hear it slosh"
- **TR:** 'Bir yudum var, çalkalandığını duyabiliyorum.'

### 3171. `RD_ALifeLine_6d0044c46dfddf15` — Claude (yeni)
