*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0142*
- **EN:** "Don't you fucking look at me like that"
- **TR:** 'Sakın bana öyle bakma.'

### 3172. `RD_ALifeLine_14f2e08542acb134` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0142*
- **EN:** "Like I'm something you stepped in"
- **TR:** 'Ayağına bulaşmış bir pislikmiş gibi.'

### 3173. `RD_ALifeLine_13af375a7745d543` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0142*
- **EN:** "You're something I'd step over, that's different"
- **TR:** 'Sen bastığım değil, üstünden atladığım şeysin, fark var.'

### 3174. `RD_ALifeLine_14202131551bfe01` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0143*
- **EN:** 'You owe me a lot, you owe me for the truck'
- **TR:** 'Bana çok borcun var, kamyonun borcu var.'

### 3175. `RD_ALifeLine_4a4c678958d4af5d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0143*
- **EN:** 'The truck was mine'
- **TR:** 'Kamyon benimdi.'

### 3176. `RD_ALifeLine_38a604f64e8d5830` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0143*
- **EN:** 'The truck was OURS, and you wrecked it!'
- **TR:** 'Kamyon BİZİMDİ, sen de mahvettin!'

### 3177. `RD_ALifeLine_35325d940dc07d92` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0143*
- **EN:** 'You both wrecked it, I was in the back'
- **TR:** 'İkiniz de mahvettiniz, ben arkadaydım.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 3178. `RD_ALifeLine_288d9e2d36c7a169` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0144*
- **EN:** "I'd like to meet the son of a bitch who started this"
- **TR:** 'Şu işi başlatan piçle tanışmak isterdim.'

### 3179. `RD_ALifeLine_240a4e112c83dbe3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0144*
- **EN:** 'And do what'
- **TR:** 'Ve ne yapacaksın?'

### 3180. `RD_ALifeLine_5d979172002482d8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0144*
- **EN:** 'Shake his hand, and then break it'
- **TR:** 'Elini sıkarım, sonra kırarım.'

### 3181. `RD_ALifeLine_4a65c0542d28b223` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0145*
- **EN:** "I've never been so goddamn bored in my life"
- **TR:** 'Hayatımda hiç bu kadar lanet sıkılmamıştım.'

### 3182. `RD_ALifeLine_7f4890116444a791` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0145*
- **EN:** 'I counted everything, I counted the floorboards, I counted my teeth'
- **TR:** 'Her şeyi saydım, döşeme tahtalarını, dişlerimi.'

### 3183. `RD_ALifeLine_1e6da8e75d052ef8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0145*
- **EN:** 'How many teeth'
- **TR:** 'Kaç diş?'

### 3184. `RD_ALifeLine_23fda1e112568c5c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0146*
- **EN:** "I used to rob people for a living, and now I'm hiding from the same people I used to rob"
- **TR:** 'Eskiden insanları soyardım, şimdi soyduğum insanlardan saklanıyorum.'

### 3185. `RD_ALifeLine_20c21a0b36d1fff7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0146*
- **EN:** "They're not the same people"
- **TR:** 'Aynı insanlar değil.'

### 3186. `RD_ALifeLine_15d7475c571e6782` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0146*
- **EN:** 'They were exactly those people, I recognized one'
- **TR:** 'Tam da o insanlardı, birini tanıdım.'

### 3187. `RD_ALifeLine_4b408832515d3baf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0147*
- **EN:** "I got a plan, we hit the gas station off 31W, they've got a whole cooler"
- **TR:** "Bir planım var, 31W'deki benzin istasyonunu basarız, koca bir soğutucuları var."

### 3188. `RD_ALifeLine_542239c56d29881d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0147*
- **EN:** "It's been a week, the cooler's off"
- **TR:** 'Bir hafta oldu, soğutucu kapalı.'

### 3189. `RD_ALifeLine_38d5285c4b5ae262` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0147*
- **EN:** "It's a very full cooler, I'm telling you, something's in there"
- **TR:** 'Çok dolu bir soğutucu, sana söylüyorum, içinde bir şey var.'

### 3190. `RD_ALifeLine_6b99925979166102` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0147*
- **EN:** "Something's in there for sure, that's the problem"
- **TR:** 'İçinde bir şey olduğu kesin, sorun da bu.'

### 3191. `RD_ALifeLine_42b0d6766498f62e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0148*
- **EN:** "I'm so tired of this stupid group"
- **TR:** 'Bu aptal gruptan bıktım.'

### 3192. `RD_ALifeLine_24803b1f50468bfc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0148*
- **EN:** "We're all tired of this stupid group"
- **TR:** 'Hepimiz bıktık bu aptal gruptan.'

### 3193. `RD_ALifeLine_1db76f776ca5ef73` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0148*
- **EN:** "I'm leaving"
- **TR:** 'Gidiyorum.'

### 3194. `RD_ALifeLine_35465a713bac6c59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0148*
- **EN:** "Leave, you'll be back by dinner"
- **TR:** 'Git, akşam yemeğine dönersin.'

### 3195. `RD_ALifeLine_4bd9883f529b7db1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0149*
- **EN:** "You're a coward"
- **TR:** 'Sen korkaksın.'

### 3196. `RD_ALifeLine_60c0ec6d4c684c75` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0149*
- **EN:** "I'm careful"
- **TR:** 'Ben temkinliyim.'

### 3197. `RD_ALifeLine_2f42803d56d4cb2f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0149*
- **EN:** "And I'm alive, you're welcome"
- **TR:** 'Ve hayattayım, rica ederim.'

### 3198. `RD_ALifeLine_7c49ff5e561f32e8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0150*
- **EN:** 'I miss my dog, I never told anybody that'
- **TR:** 'Köpeğimi özlüyorum, bunu hiç kimseye söylemedim.'

### 3199. `RD_ALifeLine_6609362040ce484b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0150*
- **EN:** 'What was his name?'
- **TR:** 'Adı neydi?'

### 3200. `RD_ALifeLine_54aa3d8071b8a355` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0150*
- **EN:** 'Duke, he was a mutt, he ate a whole ham once and never apologized'
- **TR:** 'Duke, melezdi, bir keresinde bütün bir jambonu yedi ve özür bile dilemedi.'

### 3201. `RD_ALifeLine_6e9838f34c68ae87` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0151*
- **EN:** 'Prison was better, at least they fed us'
- **TR:** 'Hapishane daha iyiydi, hiç değilse karnımızı doyuruyorlardı.'

### 3202. `RD_ALifeLine_0b77899975e7cf51` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0151*
- **EN:** 'They fed you gruel'
- **TR:** 'Sana sulu lapa yediriyorlardı.'

### 3203. `RD_ALifeLine_115155382e08ce8f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0151*
- **EN:** 'It was hot gruel, with a spoon, on a tray'
- **TR:** 'Sıcak lapaydı, kaşıkla, tepside.'

### 3204. `RD_ALifeLine_4b1657786a1d2ce2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0151*
- **EN:** "Gruel's better than this"
- **TR:** 'Lapa buradakinden iyi.'

### 3205. `RD_ALifeLine_7c3e025c49818835` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0152*
- **EN:** "Let's draw straws"
- **TR:** 'Kısa çöp çekelim.'

### 3206. `RD_ALifeLine_02dbd3194d4da7b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0152*
- **EN:** 'For what'
- **TR:** 'Ne için?'

### 3207. `RD_ALifeLine_34fd3a8611245a32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0152*
- **EN:** 'For who sleeps in the good corner'
- **TR:** 'İyi köşede kim yatacak diye.'

### 3208. `RD_ALifeLine_64455b7d5c6bd02c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0152*
- **EN:** "There's no good corner"
- **TR:** 'İyi köşe diye bir şey yok.'

### 3209. `RD_ALifeLine_6a1156a5520a5ebc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0153*
- **EN:** 'Whose turn to watch?'
- **TR:** 'Nöbet kimde?'

### 3210. `RD_ALifeLine_51767fd5702ef5a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0153*
- **EN:** 'Not mine, you lying prick'
- **TR:** 'Bende değil, yalancı puşt!'

### 3211. `RD_ALifeLine_1fedc5ef3d5c0759` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0153*
- **EN:** 'It was yours last night'
- **TR:** 'Dün gece sendeydi.'

### 3212. `RD_ALifeLine_7408468600df2c65` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0153*
- **EN:** "It was nobody's last night, we all slept, that's why we're still alive"
- **TR:** 'Dün gece kimsede değildi, hepimiz uyuduk, hala hayatta olmamızın sebebi de bu.'

### 3213. `RD_ALifeLine_21486071762df9b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0154*
- **EN:** 'I fucking hate the sound of crickets'
- **TR:** 'Cırcır böceklerinin sesinden nefret ediyorum, siktir.'

### 3214. `RD_ALifeLine_4d45067b2d9a4ee2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0154*
- **EN:** "They're just crickets"
- **TR:** 'Sadece cırcır böceği.'

### 3215. `RD_ALifeLine_035f5d6b2db2583b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0154*
- **EN:** "They're just crickets with a plan"
- **TR:** 'Planı olan cırcır böcekleri onlar.'

### 3216. `RD_ALifeLine_26c55ee02c415502` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0155*
- **EN:** 'I heard the boss say something about moving us out'
- **TR:** 'Patronun bizi buradan çıkarmaktan bahsettiğini duydum.'

### 3217. `RD_ALifeLine_0eb19fa22f079367` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0155*
- **EN:** 'Moving us where?'
- **TR:** 'Nereye çıkaracakmış?'

### 3218. `RD_ALifeLine_759990080e7549bc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0155*
- **EN:** "He didn't say, he said soon"
- **TR:** 'Söylemedi, yakında dedi.'

### 3219. `RD_ALifeLine_3e54a44b0411c384` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0155*
- **EN:** "Soon's a long time when you're counting"
- **TR:** 'Yakında, saydıkça uzun bir zaman.'

### 3220. `RD_ALifeLine_16adbe3170b08000` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0156*
- **EN:** "Anybody got a cigarette that isn't wet"
- **TR:** 'Kimsede ıslak olmayan bir sigara var mı?'

### 3221. `RD_ALifeLine_69b1ba2305ca0408` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0156*
- **EN:** "I got one that's damp"
- **TR:** 'Bende nemli bir tane var.'

### 3222. `RD_ALifeLine_0e778b3d2649bb0e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0156*
- **EN:** "Damp's a start"
- **TR:** 'Nemli de iyi bir başlangıç.'

### 3223. `RD_ALifeLine_36f3686a47bd8e3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0156*
- **EN:** "Damp's a gamble, it's a gamble with your mouth"
- **TR:** 'Nemli olan riskli, ağzınla kumar oynuyorsun.'

### 3224. `RD_ALifeLine_04bccbf719e92a0b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0157*
- **EN:** "You steal one more thing from me and I'll tie your boots together"
- **TR:** 'Benden bir şey daha çalarsan botlarını birbirine bağlarım.'

### 3225. `RD_ALifeLine_7039efbd69330ae8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0157*
- **EN:** "I didn't steal it"
- **TR:** 'Çalmadım.'

### 3226. `RD_ALifeLine_0ca080996a321631` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0157*
- **EN:** "It's in your pocket"
- **TR:** 'Cebinde duruyor.'

### 3227. `RD_ALifeLine_0c6d550251913c27` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0157*
- **EN:** "It's in the left pocket, I saw it, it's a lighter"
- **TR:** 'Sol cebinde, gördüm, çakmak.'

### 3228. `RD_ALifeLine_684b306478063e74` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0158*
- **EN:** "What's your favorite thing about the end of the world?"
- **TR:** 'Dünyanın sonunun en sevdiğin yanı ne?'

### 3229. `RD_ALifeLine_7d67a00d7757ca3f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0158*
- **EN:** 'No cops'
- **TR:** 'Polis yok.'

### 3230. `RD_ALifeLine_2b87b5bd3a7775e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0158*
- **EN:** 'No taxes'
- **TR:** 'Vergi yok.'

### 3231. `RD_ALifeLine_175efc423f211379` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0158*
- **EN:** 'No one telling me what to do, besides the three of you'
- **TR:** 'Kimse bana ne yapacağımı söylemiyor, sizler hariç.'
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 3232. `RD_ALifeLine_209a1b8350b32865` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0159*
- **EN:** "I-- I never thought I'd say it, but I miss working"
- **TR:** 'Bunu diyeceğimi hiç düşünmezdim ama çalışmayı özlüyorum.'

### 3233. `RD_ALifeLine_388091ef63a4a189` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'Working?'
- **TR:** 'Çalışmayı mı?'

### 3234. `RD_ALifeLine_7098851d055bc313` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'I had a job once, at a tire place, I liked the smell of the rubber'
- **TR:** 'Bir zamanlar bir lastikçide işim vardı, kauçuk kokusunu severdim.'

### 3235. `RD_ALifeLine_229d307309243359` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0159*
- **EN:** 'I liked the smell of the rubber too'
- **TR:** 'Ben de kauçuk kokusunu severim.'

### 3236. `RD_ALifeLine_3e5b067330cdc9b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0160*
- **EN:** "Don't talk to me about God"
- **TR:** "Bana Tanrı'dan bahsetme."

### 3237. `RD_ALifeLine_2354953c11035cef` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_6.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0160*
- **EN:** "Good, because I've got a lot to say to Him, and it's not nice"
- **TR:** 'İyi, çünkü ona söyleyecek çok şeyim var ve hiç de hoş şeyler değil.'

### 3238. `RD_ALifeLine_39e54d3e2389379f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0193*
- **EN:** "Who's winning"
- **TR:** 'Kim kazanıyor?'

### 3239. `RD_ALifeLine_40a555733f48cbcc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0193*
- **EN:** "It's got a swallow"
- **TR:** 'İçinde bir yudum var.'

### 3240. `RD_ALifeLine_5a1bec927f5f8b79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0193*
- **EN:** "A swallow's not empty, ask any drunk"
- **TR:** 'Bir yudum varsa boş sayılmaz, herhangi bir ayyaşa sor.'

### 3241. `RD_ALifeLine_3d6c149c11506ff8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0194*
- **EN:** "Don't deal me that"
- **TR:** 'Bana onu verme.'

### 3242. `RD_ALifeLine_7652e2a014d98231` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0194*
- **EN:** "What's in the bag"

### 3243. `RD_ALifeLine_3ca0577f30b877c3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0194*
- **EN:** 'A bunch of nothing'
- **TR:** 'Bir sürü hiç.'

### 3244. `RD_ALifeLine_67eac7f0424a90e7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0194*
- **EN:** "I'm folding"

### 3245. `RD_ALifeLine_757fca4e4a74b68e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0194*
- **EN:** "Don't fold, you're the only one who knows how to count"

### 3246. `RD_ALifeLine_585ca9e165924456` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0195*
- **EN:** 'Is that your sock'

### 3247. `RD_ALifeLine_29e1139a48cb3119` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0195*
- **EN:** "It's the floor"
- **TR:** 'Burası zemin.'

### 3248. `RD_ALifeLine_6f3d60f87e73d2ed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0195*
- **EN:** "It's his sock"
- **TR:** 'Onun çorabı.'

### 3249. `RD_ALifeLine_77bd220f71c0deb4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0195*
- **EN:** 'Then move your sock'

### 3250. `RD_ALifeLine_581e569c65f59782` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_7.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_lawmil_1_0195*
- **EN:** "It's a very ripe sock"
- **TR:** 'Kokusu iyice oturmuş bir çorap.'

### 3251. `RD_ALifeLine_243f9452035dca46` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0033*
- **EN:** 'I keep waiting for count'
- **TR:** 'Hâlâ sayıma çıkmayı bekliyorum.'

### 3252. `RD_ALifeLine_478be9d876325f7e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0033*
- **EN:** "Count time, four o'clock, you stand by your bunk, and I'm standing by a couch"
- **TR:** 'Sayım saati, dörtte, ranzanın yanında dikilirsin, ben de bir kanepenin yanında dikiliyorum.'

### 3253. `RD_ALifeLine_3c5d081e3cbe6d89` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0033*
- **EN:** "Nobody's counting you"
- **TR:** 'Seni sayan yok.'

### 3254. `RD_ALifeLine_0359729844176408` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0033*
- **EN:** "I know, that's the thing, nobody's counting me"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum, mesele de bu, beni sayan yok.'

### 3255. `RD_ALifeLine_68a024817006c5db` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0033*
- **EN:** "I'll count you, one..."
- **TR:** 'Ben seni sayarım, bir...'

### 3256. `RD_ALifeLine_58a4b06220ecd26f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0034*
- **EN:** "I'm never going back"
- **TR:** 'Bir daha asla geri dönmeyeceğim.'

### 3257. `RD_ALifeLine_07ee4cf4035d3d9c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0034*
- **EN:** "Never's a long time"
- **TR:** 'Asla uzun bir süre.'

### 3258. `RD_ALifeLine_778fc6105b9b1ba4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0034*
- **EN:** "I'd shoot myself first"
- **TR:** 'Önce kendimi vururum.'

### 3259. `RD_ALifeLine_39c79a83688d5505` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0034*
- **EN:** "It's a figure of speech..."
- **TR:** 'Mecazen söylüyorum...'

### 3260. `RD_ALifeLine_66e2d52f35a0e740` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0035*
- **EN:** 'My cellie snored like a diesel truck at Eddyville'
- **TR:** "Eddyville'de hücre arkadaşım dizel kamyon gibi horlardı."

### 3261. `RD_ALifeLine_767b7fe923aa4562` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0035*
- **EN:** 'At least he was reliable'
- **TR:** 'Hiç değilse güvenilirdi.'

### 3262. `RD_ALifeLine_227a0d970bbab227` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0035*
- **EN:** 'He was reliable at three in the morning'
- **TR:** 'Sabahın üçünde güvenilirdi.'

### 3263. `RD_ALifeLine_5cf8528644b7af4b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0035*
- **EN:** 'My cellie sang, in the dark, off-key, every night'
- **TR:** 'Benimki karanlıkta şarkı söylerdi, her gece, yanlış yanlış.'

### 3264. `RD_ALifeLine_052b281a762996a6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'I miss the yard'
- **TR:** 'Avluyu özlüyorum.'

### 3265. `RD_ALifeLine_5ec3406c1cdd8bbb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'The yard?'
- **TR:** 'Avluyu mu?'

### 3266. `RD_ALifeLine_4400f33b0b35f3b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'A big flat field, with a fence, you could walk in a circle and think'
- **TR:** 'Büyük düz bir alan, çitle çevrili, daire çizip yürüyüp düşünebilirdin.'

### 3267. `RD_ALifeLine_506bf422399bd15c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0036*
- **EN:** 'You can walk in a circle here'
- **TR:** 'Burada da daire çizip yürüyebilirsin.'

### 3268. `RD_ALifeLine_13fe4ed22d74c18f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0036*
- **EN:** "It's not the same, there's no fence"
- **TR:** 'Aynısı değil, çit yok.'

### 3269. `RD_ALifeLine_52ff762779f10c56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0036*
- **EN:** "I'll build you a fence..."
- **TR:** 'Sana bir çit yaparım...'

### 3270. `RD_ALifeLine_35d57f27307f929a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0037*

- **TR:** 'Bir araba teybi için sekiz yıl.'

### 3271. `RD_ALifeLine_607c9e1302b3e110` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0037*
- **EN:** 'It was a very nice stereo'
- **TR:** 'Çok güzel bir teypti.'

### 3272. `RD_ALifeLine_6b6016c849c2c4e9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0037*

### 3273. `RD_ALifeLine_16e3078a28595b58` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0037*

### 3274. `RD_ALifeLine_1d2665597e5f887d` — Claude (yeni)

- **EN:** "Eight years, and you didn't even keep the stereo"

### 3275. `RD_ALifeLine_170697ec29c29603` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0038*

### 3276. `RD_ALifeLine_0827ef7b6847ef59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0038*

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'The pudding was terrible'
- **TR:** 'Puding berbattı.'

### 3278. `RD_ALifeLine_6cf14e796680591b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0038*
- **EN:** 'A reliable kind of terrible'

### 3279. `RD_ALifeLine_70e719ed538e78d9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0038*
- **EN:** "I'd kill for a pudding"

### 3280. `RD_ALifeLine_59b3bba475b3587c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0039*

### 3284. `RD_ALifeLine_49efcd7734077d06` — Claude (yeni)

### 3285. `RD_ALifeLine_4018012432411356` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0040*

### 3286. `RD_ALifeLine_3190ce42604ebc59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0040*

### 3287. `RD_ALifeLine_43149b1461be89e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0040*
- **EN:** 'Mine never wrote at all'
- **TR:** 'Benimkisi hiç yazmadı bile.'

### 3288. `RD_ALifeLine_7285da44665780ae` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'I had eleven days left on my sentence'
- **TR:** 'Cezamın bitmesine on bir gün kalmıştı.'

### 3289. `RD_ALifeLine_1622529b610e7d5b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'And you came out anyway...'
- **TR:** 'Yine de çıkmışsın ha...'

### 3290. `RD_ALifeLine_644881435b758099` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0041*
- **EN:** 'It was a very short walk'
- **TR:** 'Çok kısa bir yürüyüştü.'

### 3291. `RD_ALifeLine_20f2c3ae5fbaa935` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0041*
- **EN:** "Sometimes the door's just open"
- **TR:** 'Bazen kapı açık kalır işte.'

### 3292. `RD_ALifeLine_2763bde56c9fe3bb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0041*
- **EN:** "You just walked out, I'm so jealous"
- **TR:** 'Öylece çıkıp gitmişsin, çok kıskandım.'

### 3293. `RD_ALifeLine_6f41fed0159df890` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'They count us in the morning, the guards'
- **TR:** 'Sabahları sayarlardı bizi, gardiyanlar.'

### 3294. `RD_ALifeLine_1faf02d707ce582f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'They counted us, you mean'
- **TR:** 'Sayarlardı, evet. Geçmiş zaman.'

### 3295. `RD_ALifeLine_6e69c3502c29c23b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'I counted myself in the mirror this morning, one'
- **TR:** 'Bu sabah aynada kendimi saydım. Bir.'

### 3296. `RD_ALifeLine_1a1f45aa48d0b9fa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0042*
- **EN:** "It's the one number that doesn't change"
- **TR:** 'Değişmeyen tek sayı bu.'

### 3297. `RD_ALifeLine_3e2c5a2b61b4041e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0042*
- **EN:** 'It changes, every day, I keep losing'
- **TR:** 'Değişiyor, her gün değişiyor. Ben hep kaybediyorum.'

### 3298. `RD_ALifeLine_5d3bc30d65ba7a18` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Anybody here ever been in the hole?'
- **TR:** 'Hiç hücre cezası yatan var mı aranızda?'

### 3299. `RD_ALifeLine_0e3217c3386b4d8e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Twice'
- **TR:** 'İki kere.'

### 3300. `RD_ALifeLine_68413f143ff6944d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0043*
- **EN:** 'Three times, one was a clerical error'
- **TR:** 'Üç kere. Biri evrak hatasıydı.'

### 3301. `RD_ALifeLine_155e83937d7eca2e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0061*
- **EN:** 'We found a truckload of copper'
- **TR:** 'Bir kamyon dolusu bakır bulduk.'

### 3302. `RD_ALifeLine_66bbe3d6393b6c3b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0061*
- **EN:** 'What do we do with copper?'
- **TR:** 'Bakırı ne yapacağız ki?'

### 3303. `RD_ALifeLine_6d6e0758724245b1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0061*
- **EN:** "I don't know, but it was heavy, it felt important"
- **TR:** 'Bilmem, ama ağırdı, önemli bir şey gibi geldi.'

### 3304. `RD_ALifeLine_3a464afb3b93c1d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0061*
- **EN:** "Nobody's buying"
- **TR:** 'Alan yok.'

### 3305. `RD_ALifeLine_31fdf67c462677da` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0061*
- **EN:** "It's pretty, we can look at it"

*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0062*

### 3307. `RD_ALifeLine_112290ec7036fcbe` — Claude (yeni)

### 3308. `RD_ALifeLine_099fe91260a24749` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0062*

### 3314. `RD_ALifeLine_3157756d436d54b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0063*

### 3315. `RD_ALifeLine_6317efa934c176f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0063*

### 3316. `RD_ALifeLine_69b160b33bdc390f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0064*

### 3317. `RD_ALifeLine_2b81bf8d3a8ab0b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0064*
- **EN:** "Name's Tyler, haven't seen him in four years"
- **TR:** 'Adı Tyler, dört yıldır görmedim.'

### 3318. `RD_ALifeLine_4de75cd640c8fc14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0065*
- **EN:** 'Eleven years, union, a good plumber, I could fix anything'
- **TR:** 'On bir yıl, sendikalı, iyi bir tesisatçıydım. Her şeyi tamir ederdim.'

### 3319. `RD_ALifeLine_5b32af5d58cb4114` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0065*
- **EN:** 'Fix the end of the world'
- **TR:** 'Dünyanın sonunu tamir et bakalım.'

### 3320. `RD_ALifeLine_7211adcb24709ba8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0065*
- **EN:** 'Give me a wrench'
- **TR:** 'Bir anahtar ver bana.'

### 3321. `RD_ALifeLine_6392155c78309b32` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0065*
- **EN:** "I'd give you a wrench and a bad day"
- **TR:** 'Sana anahtarı da veririm, bir de kötü bir gün.'

### 3322. `RD_ALifeLine_3ab7f89f37f0ecde` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0066*
- **EN:** "Don't look at me like that"

### 3323. `RD_ALifeLine_03831e6203897293` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0066*

- **TR:** 'Sanki bir şey söylemek istiyorsun gibi.'

### 3324. `RD_ALifeLine_303e5042260db5dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0066*
- **EN:** 'I do wanna say something'
- **TR:** 'İstiyorum zaten.'

### 3325. `RD_ALifeLine_7e791ab130f103de` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0066*
- **EN:** 'Then say it'
- **TR:** 'O zaman söyle.'

### 3326. `RD_ALifeLine_47180783271b50e7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_5.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_crim_1_0066*

- **TR:** 'Senin bir pislik olduğunu söylemek istiyor.'

### 3327. `RD_ALifeLine_66f8572576764ac0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0137*
- **EN:** "I'm out of gauze"
- **TR:** 'Gazlı bezim bitti.'

### 3328. `RD_ALifeLine_52962fce0545a71a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0137*
- **EN:** "There's some in the second bag"
- **TR:** 'İkinci çantada biraz var.'

### 3329. `RD_ALifeLine_78bc51f131419b0b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0137*
- **EN:** "That's the last of it, I've been rationing it"
- **TR:** 'O son kalanlar, idareli kullanıyordum.'

### 3330. `RD_ALifeLine_7ea3e23b60ce84b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0137*
- **EN:** "I'll find more, there's got to be a pharmacy"
- **TR:** 'Ben daha bulurum, bir yerlerde eczane vardır mutlaka.'

### 3331. `RD_ALifeLine_4ee5115e56f49fa8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0138*
- **EN:** 'I keep smelling smoke'
- **TR:** 'Hâlâ duman kokusu geliyor burnuma.'

### 3332. `RD_ALifeLine_7d8d4f664b40cb4a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0138*
- **EN:** "There's no smoke"
- **TR:** 'Duman yok ki.'

### 3333. `RD_ALifeLine_65d03f3d6bae062b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0138*
- **EN:** "I know there's no smoke, I smell it every night, since the station"
- **TR:** 'Biliyorum yok. Ama her gece kokusunu alıyorum, istasyondan beri.'

### 3334. `RD_ALifeLine_2b3d02234fd1f003` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0139*
- **EN:** "I'm out of sermons"
- **TR:** 'Vaazlarım bitti.'

### 3335. `RD_ALifeLine_6ae4a9d10f8d1afc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0139*
- **EN:** "You've been preaching for three days"
- **TR:** 'Üç gündür vaaz veriyorsun.'

### 3336. `RD_ALifeLine_79fdc0a975347193` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0139*
- **EN:** "I've said everything I know, and some things I don't"
- **TR:** 'Bildiğim ne varsa söyledim, bilmediklerimi de.'

### 3337. `RD_ALifeLine_0f8cdf6f293a23f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0139*
- **EN:** "Just sit with us, that's a sermon"
- **TR:** 'Yanımıza otur yeter, o da bir vaaz.'

### 3338. `RD_ALifeLine_71c9c7c112fba50f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0140*
- **EN:** 'Pray with me'
- **TR:** 'Benimle dua et.'

### 3339. `RD_ALifeLine_1f4403120f3a6380` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0140*
- **EN:** 'Just say hello, and then say help'
- **TR:** 'Sadece merhaba de, sonra da yardım et de.'

### 3340. `RD_ALifeLine_1f5ae8b0518dfce9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0140*
- **EN:** 'Hello, help'
- **TR:** 'Merhaba... yardım et.'

### 3341. `RD_ALifeLine_42f1b3f961433216` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0141*
- **EN:** "I never thought I'd miss a house fire"
- **TR:** 'Ev yangınını özleyeceğimi hiç düşünmezdim.'

### 3342. `RD_ALifeLine_63513ca8217c252c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0141*
- **EN:** 'At least there was a plan, a hose, a ladder, a way to do it'
- **TR:** 'En azından bir plan vardı: hortum, merdiven, bir yol.'

### 3343. `RD_ALifeLine_493981b501a211d4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0141*
- **EN:** 'This is a fire without a hose'
- **TR:** 'Bu hortumsuz bir yangın.'

### 3344. `RD_ALifeLine_5059c70a7c0843fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0142*
- **EN:** 'I used to hold their hands, in the ambulance'
- **TR:** 'Ambulansta ellerini tutardım eskiden.'

### 3345. `RD_ALifeLine_3ca992fb34db6d79` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0142*
- **EN:** "And I don't know where to put my hands now"
- **TR:** 'Şimdi ellerimi nereye koyacağımı bilmiyorum.'

### 3346. `RD_ALifeLine_4415cabe340b5ca3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0142*
- **EN:** "Put 'em here, hold mine, it's all right"
- **TR:** 'Buraya koy, benimkini tut. Sorun yok.'

### 3347. `RD_ALifeLine_252cc9804fd2577f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0012*
- **EN:** 'Let me see that arm'
- **TR:** 'Şu koluna bakayım.'

### 3348. `RD_ALifeLine_430a03c833055a56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0012*
- **EN:** 'Let me see it anyway...'
- **TR:** 'Yine de bakayım...'

### 3349. `RD_ALifeLine_02a9dbd55468dab0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0012*
- **EN:** "...it's a little red"
- **TR:** '...biraz kızarmış.'

### 3350. `RD_ALifeLine_6fdacd4836492cd5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0012*
- **EN:** "It's more than a little red, we'll clean it again"
- **TR:** 'Biraz değil, epey kızarmış. Bir daha temizleyeceğiz.'

### 3351. `RD_ALifeLine_4f67829b0ad58679` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0013*
- **EN:** 'Nobody sleeps in their boots, take them off, air out your feet'
- **TR:** 'Kimse botuyla uyumaz. Çıkarın şunları, ayaklarınız hava alsın.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*
- *Not: gerçek çoğul*

### 3352. `RD_ALifeLine_3c3810b404ed8099` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0013*
- **EN:** "Then you'll run in socks"
- **TR:** 'O zaman çorapla koşarsınız.'
- *Gerekçeli üslup istisnası (Claude) (hitap): 3-4 kişilik sahne, gruba hitap*

### 3353. `RD_ALifeLine_1ef4d5483450fb10` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_3_0013*
- **EN:** "That's why you have boots, now take them off"
- **TR:** 'Bot bunun için var, ama şimdi çıkarın.'

### 3354. `RD_ALifeLine_111a31852ee56ef8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0130*
- **EN:** 'I was on overtime at the plant when it started'
- **TR:** 'Salgın başladığında fabrikada fazla mesaideydim.'

### 3355. `RD_ALifeLine_1552cc6a6afc1ce4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0130*
- **EN:** 'Time and a half?'
- **TR:** 'Bir buçuk mu veriyorlar?'

### 3356. `RD_ALifeLine_6f8418bb677eaaa8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0130*
- **EN:** 'Double, it was Friday'
- **TR:** 'Çift. Cumaydı.'

### 3357. `RD_ALifeLine_4ff43ba752cf5563` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0130*
- **EN:** "You'll never see it"
- **TR:** 'O parayı hiç görmeyeceksin.'

### 3358. `RD_ALifeLine_1a52054145057cb8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0131*
- **EN:** 'Foreman said finish the shift'
- **TR:** 'Ustabaşı vardiyayı bitir dedi.'

### 3359. `RD_ALifeLine_2a14e192230a9887` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0131*
- **EN:** 'Finish the shift?'
- **TR:** 'Vardiyayı mı bitir?'

### 3360. `RD_ALifeLine_55160f7f76587d67` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0131*
- **EN:** 'Finish the shift, I did, I clocked out and everything'
- **TR:** 'Vardiyayı bitir dedi, bitirdim de. Çıkış kartını bile bastım.'

### 3361. `RD_ALifeLine_691a74317df82aa7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0131*
- **EN:** 'Of course you did'
- **TR:** 'Tabii ki basmışsındır.'

### 3362. `RD_ALifeLine_6121036c584e4d9f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0132*
- **EN:** "Payday's Friday"
- **TR:** 'Maaş günü Cuma.'

### 3363. `RD_ALifeLine_3d740a00617daddf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0132*
- **EN:** 'It was Friday'
- **TR:** 'Cumaydı.'

### 3364. `RD_ALifeLine_1ccf6b303557f140` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0132*
- **EN:** "I know, I mean next Friday, I keep thinking I'm gonna get paid"
- **TR:** "Biliyorum, gelecek Cuma'yı diyorum. Hâlâ maaş alacağımı sanıyorum."

### 3365. `RD_ALifeLine_5a92a76e47a119a1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0132*
- **EN:** "Check's in the mail"
- **TR:** 'Maaşın postada, yolda.'

### 3366. `RD_ALifeLine_5826650600cb1a56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0133*
- **EN:** 'I fixed a radiator with duct tape and spite once'
- **TR:** 'Bir keresinde radyatörü bantla ve inatla tamir etmiştim.'

### 3367. `RD_ALifeLine_2c8f783a401b9bed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0133*
- **EN:** 'Did it hold'
- **TR:** 'Dayandı mı?'

### 3368. `RD_ALifeLine_3ae094a708c9d861` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0133*
- **EN:** 'Eleven miles, it held eleven miles'
- **TR:** 'On sekiz kilometre, tam on sekiz kilometre dayandı.'
- *Not: 11 miles -> ~18 km*

### 3369. `RD_ALifeLine_6b3c712533b3a0e3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0133*
- **EN:** "That's all spite's good for"
- **TR:** 'İnat da ancak buna yarar.'

### 3370. `RD_ALifeLine_0d4e63d535b370fe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0134*
- **EN:** "Union said we'd have protection"
- **TR:** 'Sendika koruma sağlayacağını söylemişti.'

### 3371. `RD_ALifeLine_69ac38e10986d5b4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0134*
- **EN:** 'From what'
- **TR:** 'Neye karşı?'

### 3372. `RD_ALifeLine_0a9fc601495484ff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0134*
- **EN:** "From the company, I don't know, from anything"
- **TR:** 'Şirkete karşı, bilmem, her şeye karşı.'

### 3373. `RD_ALifeLine_0041c22419e5af14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0134*
- **EN:** "Union's got a lot of explaining to do"
- **TR:** 'Sendikanın açıklaması gereken çok şey var.'

### 3374. `RD_ALifeLine_1e608c2f3ec7a471` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0135*
- **EN:** "My hands haven't been this clean in thirty years"
- **TR:** 'Ellerim otuz yıldır bu kadar temiz olmamıştı.'

### 3375. `RD_ALifeLine_61e0f7ed642bd7f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0135*
- **EN:** "Because you're not working"
- **TR:** 'Çünkü çalışmıyorsun.'

### 3376. `RD_ALifeLine_655a1add4400ae0f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0135*
- **EN:** 'I miss the grease, I feel naked'
- **TR:** 'Gresi özledim, çıplak hissediyorum.'

### 3377. `RD_ALifeLine_4fa563f8753362c2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_scenes_shelter_3.lua, fw_ld6_shelter_civ_2_0135*
- **EN:** 'You are naked, you have no gloves'
- **TR:** 'Çıplaksın zaten, eldivenin yok ya.'
